Extensive experiments show that many existing methods remain vulnerable under these attacks, indicating that current approaches often hide rather than fully remove target knowledge. Our study provides new insights into the robustness and limitations of current VLM unlearning methods and highlights the need for more reliable multimodal unlearning strategies. Code is available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/VLM-UnL-Attack.

</details>


### [74] [ChartAct: A Benchmark for Dynamic Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26994)
*Muye Huang,Wu Lin,Lingling Zhang,Hang Yan,Zhiyuan Wang,Yumeng Fu,Zesheng Yang,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChartAct是一个面向动态图表理解的新型交互式基准测试，包含673个动态图表和1,440个问答样本，评估了11个多模态模型和GUI智能体，发现最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7的成功率为84.5%，多数模型低于60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表理解基准主要针对静态图表，但真实世界的图表通常是动态和交互式的，关键信息需要通过悬停、点击、缩放、拖拽等操作才能获取，因此需要一个能够评估模型在交互式环境中理解动态图表能力的基准。

Method: 从8个真实图表网站收集和筛选673个动态图表，涵盖7种常见图表类型，构建1,440个高质量的问答样本。每个样本在两个环境（Dynamic Chart和Dashboard Chart）中实例化，以评估不同上下文下的动态图表理解能力。

Result: 基于ChartAct评估了11个先进的多模态模型和GUI智能体，最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7的平均成功率为84.5%，而大多数模型低于60%。

Conclusion: 现有模型在动态图表理解方面仍存在明显局限性，ChartAct为研究真实交互环境中的图表理解提供了新的基准。

Abstract: Charts are widely used to present complex data for analysis and decision making. Existing chart understanding benchmarks mainly focus on static charts, but real-world charts are often dynamic and interactive. Key information may only appear after actions such as hovering, clicking, zooming, or dragging. Dynamic chart understanding therefore requires models to identify visible content, choose proper interactions, and reason over changing chart states. To evaluate this ability, we propose ChartAct, an interactive benchmark for dynamic chart understanding. ChartAct collects and filters 673 dynamic charts from 8 real chart websites, covers 7 common chart types, and constructs 1,440 high-quality question-answer samples. Each sample is instantiated in two environments, Dynamic Chart and Dashboard Chart, to evaluate dynamic chart understanding under different contexts. Based on ChartAct, we systematically evaluate 11 advanced multimodal models and GUI agents. Experimental results show that existing models still have clear limitations in dynamic chart understanding. The strongest model, Claude-Opus-4.7, achieves an average success rate of 84.5\%, while most models remain below 60\%. We also conduct detailed failure attribution and case analysis. ChartAct provides a new benchmark for studying chart understanding in real interactive environments. Codes at https://github.com/wulin-wulin/OSWorld_Chart

</details>


### [75] [Timestep-Aware SVDQuant-GPTQ for W4A4 Quantization of Wan2.2-I2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27003)
*Junhao Wu,Dezhong Yao,Hai Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对Wan2.2-I2V的MoE视频扩散Transformer，提出一种结合SVDQuant低秩异常补偿、GPTQ感知重建权重量化和逐专家时间步分箱裁剪搜索的后训练量化框架，在W4A4精度下实现59.3%的显存节省，仅带来0.9%的VBench分数下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W4A4量化视频扩散Transformer面临两大挑战：激活值存在稀疏大幅异常值，以及去噪轨迹中激活分布强依赖于时间步。此外，Wan2.2-I2V的双专家MoE设计使得高噪声和低噪声专家具有不同的量化敏感性，单一全局校准策略无法覆盖。

Method: 提出后训练量化框架，包含：(1) SVDQuant低秩异常补偿处理激活异常值；(2) GPTQ感知重建残差权重量化；(3) 针对每个专家独立进行时间步分箱的逐层激活裁剪比搜索。

Result: 在OpenS2V-Eval基准上，相比BF16基线减少59.3%的峰值GPU显存，VBench平均分仅下降0.9%，成像质量下降2.3%。

Conclusion: 专家感知和时间步感知的校准策略对于MoE视频DiT的高保真W4A4推理至关重要。

Abstract: W4A4 quantization of large video diffusion Transformers offers substantial memory savings but is hindered by two main challenges: sparse large-magnitude activation outliers, and strongly timestep-dependent activation distributions across the multi-step denoising trajectory. These difficulties are compounded by Wan2.2-I2V's two-expert Mixture-of-Experts DiT design, whose high-noise and low-noise experts exhibit distinct quantization sensitivities that a single global calibration policy cannot capture. We propose a post-training quantization framework combining SVDQuant-based low-rank outlier compensation, GPTQ-based reconstruction-aware residual weight quantization, and timestep-bin-wise per-layer activation clipping-ratio search conducted independently for each expert. On the OpenS2V-Eval benchmark, our method reduces peak GPU memory by 59.3\% relative to the BF16 baseline while incurring only a 0.9\% drop in VBench average score and a 2.3\% drop in Imaging Quality, demonstrating that expert- and timestep-aware calibration is essential for high-fidelity W4A4 inference on MoE video DiTs.

</details>


### [76] [Black-box Membership Inference Attacks on the Pre-training Data of Image-generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27020)
*Tao Qi,Huili Wang,Yuanhong Huang,Wendan Wang,Lianchao Zhao,Jinrui Wang,Zichen Qin,Shangguang Wang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SD-MIA的黑盒成员推理攻击框架，通过分析扩散模型对目标图像及对应扰动文本指令的去噪差异来检测预训练数据中的成员身份，在公开和自建数据集上均取得了优于现有方法（包括可访问内部模型特征的基线）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推理攻击方法在检测扩散模型的预训练数据时效果不佳，因为它们过度依赖模型对数据的记忆程度，且多数方法需要访问模型内部特征，这在主流闭源图像生成平台中不可行。

Method: 提出SD-MIA黑盒攻击框架，核心是跨模态数据扰动机制：同时扰动目标图像和对应文本指令，通过分析黑盒扩散模型对两者的去噪差异来提取更具区分度的成员线索。

Result: 在公开基准数据集和自建数据集上进行了大量实验，两个数据集均包含分布一致的预训练成员和非成员样本。SD-MIA在性能上优于所有现有基线方法，包括那些可访问内部模型特征的不公平优势基线。

Conclusion: 分析黑盒扩散模型对目标图像和扰动文本指令的去噪行为能够揭示更具区分性的成员线索，SD-MIA框架有效实现了对扩散模型预训练数据的黑盒成员推理攻击。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of diffusion-based image generation models has raised serious concerns regarding potential copyright and privacy infringements involving human-created data. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have emerged as a promising tool for identifying unauthorized data usage during model training. Existing methods typically assess the ability of model to denoise perturbed suspect images as an indicator of membership status. However, the discriminative power of such features is highly dependent on the degree of model memorization and deteriorates significantly when applied to less exposed data (e.g., pre-training data). Although several methods attempt to enhance detection by leveraging internal model features, these features are generally inaccessible in mainstream closed-source image generation platforms, limiting their practicality. In this paper, we demonstrate that analyzing how a black-box diffusion model denoises a target image and corresponding perturbed textual instructions can reveal more distinctive membership cues. Based on this insight, we propose a black-box membership inference attack framework (named SD-MIA) that leverages a cross-modal data perturbation mechanism to detect pre-training data in diffusion models. We conduct extensive experiments on both a public benchmark dataset and a newly constructed dataset, each comprising pre-training membership and non-membership samples with identical distributions. Experimental results demonstrate that SD-MIA achieves superior performance compared to existing baselines, including those with the unfair advantage of accessing internal model features.

</details>


### [77] [NeR-SC: Adapting Neural Video Representation to Screen Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27024)
*Ruohan Shi,Jiaoyan Zhao,Haogang Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NeR-SC，一种专为屏幕内容视频设计的神经表示框架。针对屏幕内容视频的锐利边缘、有限调色板和强时间冗余等特性，在SNeRV主干上引入三个专用模块：可学习调色板、多门控密集融合模块和嵌入级帧跳过策略。实验表明NeR-SC在DSCVC和VCD数据集上分别达到40.32dB和41.73dB平均PSNR，优于现有神经视频表示方法，且在低比特率下超越H.264和H.265，跳过策略实现了无损质量的实时解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示方法针对自然视频设计，缺乏利用屏幕内容视频特有统计特性（锐利边缘、有限调色板、强时间冗余）的机制，导致压缩效率存在较大提升空间。

Method: 基于SNeRV主干，NeR-SC包含三个核心模块：(i) 可学习调色板，通过将低频子带约束到学习到的颜色集来建模离散颜色结构；(ii) 多门控密集融合模块，用密集的注意力门控跨阶段交互替代顺序特征融合；(iii) 嵌入级帧跳过策略，跳过静态帧的冗余解码器调用，且零训练开销。

Result: 在DSCVC和VCD数据集上分别达到40.32dB和41.73dB平均PSNR，优于代表性神经视频表示方法；在低比特率下超越传统编码标准H.264和H.265；跳过策略实现实时解码且无质量损失。

Conclusion: NeR-SC有效利用屏幕内容的离散颜色特性，通过专用模块设计和帧跳过机制，在压缩性能和解码速度上均取得显著提升，验证了针对特定视频内容类型定制神经表示方法的有效性。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations have emerged as a promising paradigm for video compression, with recent methods achieving competitive performance on natural video. However, screen content video -- common in remote desktop, online education, and cloud gaming -- exhibits distinct statistics: sharp edges, limited color palettes, and strong temporal redundancy. Existing neural representation methods, designed for natural scenes, lack mechanisms to exploit these properties, leaving substantial room for improvement. In this paper, we propose NeR-SC, a neural representation framework tailored for screen content video. Building on the SNeRV backbone, NeR-SC introduces three screen-content-specific modules: (i) a learnable color palette that models the discrete color structure of screen content by restricting the low-frequency sub-band to a learned color set; (ii) a multi-gate dense fusion module that replaces sequential feature fusion with dense, attention-gated cross-stage interaction; and (iii) an embedding-level frame skip strategy that bypasses redundant decoder invocations for static frames, with zero training overhead. Experiments on DSCVC and VCD show that NeR-SC achieves 40.32~dB and 41.73~dB average PSNR, outperforming representative neural video representation methods and, at low bitrates, surpassing H.264 and H.265. The skip strategy enables real-time decoding with no loss in quality.

</details>


### [78] [BEAT: Rhythm-Elastic Alignment for Agentic Music-guided Movie Trailer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27067)
*Yutong Wang,Yunke Wang,Xinyuan Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEAT框架通过MuVA（基于Sinkhorn正则化两阶段学习的紧凑音视频对齐编码器）和Bar-DP（能量自适应动态规划算法实现弹性的多对一音视频对齐），结合五阶段智能体流程，在TrailerArena基准上实现了电影预告片自动生成的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预告片生成方法要么将音乐对齐降级为后处理，要么强制使用刚性的逐镜头-音乐一对一映射，忽略了专业剪辑中快速剪辑对应高能量段落、长镜头对应舒缓节拍的弹性节奏特性。

Method: 提出BEAT框架，包含：(1) MuVA模块——基于Sinkhorn正则化两阶段学习的紧凑音视频对齐编码器；(2) Bar-DP模块——能量自适应动态规划算法，实现遵循音乐动态的弹性多对一对齐；(3) 五阶段智能体流程——将核心对齐建立在跨模态特征上，通过结构化文本信号协调高层创意决策。同时构建TrailerArena基准（20+指标，4个互补维度）。

Result: 在TrailerArena基准上，BEAT在镜头选择、镜头排序和感知质量三个维度均达到SOTA性能，并能端到端生成完整的预告片。

Conclusion: 提出的弹性对齐策略（允许多个镜头匹配同一音乐小节）更符合专业剪辑实践，五阶段智能体流程有效整合了低层跨模态对齐与高层创意决策，为自动预告片生成设立了新的性能标准。

Abstract: Automatic movie trailer generation must select shots from a full-length film and synchronize them with background music. Existing methods either relegate music alignment to post-processing or enforce rigid one-to-one shot-music mappings, overlooking that professional editing rhythm is elastic: rapid cuts accompany high-energy passages while sustained shots span quieter bars. We introduce BEAT, a framework that addresses this gap with two core components: MuVA, a compact music-visual alignment encoder trained with Sinkhorn-regularized two-stage learning, and Bar-DP, an energy-adaptive dynamic programming algorithm that produces elastic many-to-one alignments following musical dynamics. These components are integrated into a five-phase agentic pipeline that grounds the core alignment in learned cross-modal features while coordinating higher-level creative decisions through structured text signals. To support comprehensive evaluation, we also introduce TrailerArena, a benchmark with 20+ metrics across four complementary dimensions. On TrailerArena, BEAT achieves state-of-the-art performance across shot selection, ordering, and perceptual quality, while producing fully composed trailers end-to-end.

</details>


### [79] [IPIBench: Evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under Continuous Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27074)
*Jinzhao Li,Yinuo Chen,Wenxuan Song,Yijia Lei,Yichi Zhang,Honglei Yan,Panwang Pan,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IPIBench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在流式视频场景下的交互式主动智能，并设计IPI-Agent框架以改进模型在主动触发和多轮交互协调方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要研究单轮场景下的反应式或主动式交互，忽略了流式视频中用户可能添加、修改或取消主动请求并与反应式查询交织的动态多轮场景。

Method: 提出IPIBench基准（涵盖主动监控、主动任务管理、交错反应-主动请求），并设计IPI-Agent无训练智能体框架，包含交互控制策略和时间门控机制。

Result: 代表性MLLM在IPIBench上表现出主动触发不稳定和反应-主动行为协调弱的局限；IPI-Agent在所有基准设置上一致提升了现有MLLM的性能。

Conclusion: IPIBench有效揭示了MLLM在流式视频中主动智能的不足，IPI-Agent无需额外训练即可显著提升多模态模型在复杂交互场景下的表现。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on reactive question answering, but real-world streaming assistants require proactive reasoning over continuous visual inputs. Existing benchmarks mainly study reactive or proactive interactions in isolated single-turn settings, overlooking dynamic multi-turn scenarios where users may add, modify, or cancel proactive requests alongside interleaved reactive queries. To address this gap, we introduce IPIBench, the first benchmark for evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under streaming video settings. IPIBench covers proactive monitoring, proactive task management, and interleaved reactive-proactive requests. Evaluations on representative MLLMs reveal two major limitations: unstable proactive triggering and weak coordination between reactive and proactive behaviors. We further propose IPI-Agent, a training-free agentic framework with an interaction-control policy and a temporal-gating mechanism for stabilizing proactive triggering and coordinating multi-turn interactions. Experiments show that IPI-Agent consistently improves existing MLLMs across all benchmark settings.

</details>


### [80] [SoftCap: Soft-Budget Control for Diffusion Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27075)
*Yuhang Zhang,Junxiang Qiu,Huixia Ben,Zhenhua Tang,Shuo Wang,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftCap是一种针对扩散Transformer（DiT）的免训练控制层，通过轨迹漂移观测器和软预算PI控制器动态调整缓存策略，在几乎相同计算量下提升图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT虽能生成高质量视觉内容，但迭代去噪过程需要大量昂贵的Transformer计算。现有免训练加速方法（如缓存、预测、验证）虽能降低成本，但决定何时执行完整计算步骤的运行时策略通常依赖固定调度或手工设定的阈值，缺乏动态适应性。

Method: SoftCap包含两个核心组件：1) 轨迹漂移观测器，通过轻量级隐藏状态统计量估计局部缓存风险；2) 软预算PI控制器，根据相对于固定参考配置的实际计算量调整完整步骤触发阈值。预算作为软上限，调节阈值但不强制要求运行消耗指定数量的完整评估。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上，SoftCap在中等计算量下相比SpeCa实现提升：ImageReward从0.967提升至0.981，LPIPS-Full从0.518降至0.498，且FLOPs几乎相同。目标扫描诊断显示，随着预算放宽，软上限行为如预期表现。

Conclusion: SoftCap通过动态、自适应的缓存控制机制，在免训练条件下有效平衡了DiT推理的计算效率与生成质量，其软预算设计避免了硬性约束带来的性能损失。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve strong visual quality, but their iterative denoising process requires many costly Transformer evaluations. Training-free acceleration methods reduce this cost by caching, forecasting, or verifying intermediate features, yet the runtime decision of when to execute a Full step is often driven by fixed schedules or hand-tuned thresholds. We propose \textbf{SoftCap}, a training-free control layer for cache-based DiT inference. SoftCap couples a Trajectory Drift Observer, which estimates local cache risk from lightweight hidden-state statistics, with a Soft-Budget PI Controller, which adjusts the Full-triggering threshold from realized compute relative to a fixed reference profile. The budget is a soft ceiling: it shapes the threshold but does not require a run to spend a prescribed number of Full evaluations. On FLUX.1-dev, SoftCap improves over SpeCa at a comparable middle-compute operating point, raising ImageReward from 0.967 to 0.981 and reducing LPIPS-Full from 0.518 to 0.498 at nearly identical FLOPs, while target-sweep diagnostics show the intended soft-ceiling behavior as the budget is relaxed.

</details>


### [81] [Semi-Supervised Gaze Estimation via Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27080)
*Qida Tan,Hongyu Yang,Wenchao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解耦子空间对比学习的半监督外观注视估计框架(DSCL)，通过雅可比正则化将特征解耦为俯仰角和偏航角的判别性子空间，并利用子空间内的序数关系进行对比学习，仅需5%-20%的标注数据即可在域内和跨域场景下达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于外观的注视估计因标注样本有限和数据集多样性不足而泛化能力差；现有弱监督方法虽可生成大规模伪标签数据缓解域偏移，但仍依赖大量人工标注。本文旨在通过半监督学习利用无标注数据增强域泛化，减少对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 提出解耦子空间对比学习(DSCL)框架：(1) 通过雅可比正则化将特征表示解耦为俯仰角和偏航角对应的判别性子空间；(2) 在每个子空间内利用固有的序数关系进行对比学习，使模型能从少量标注样本和大量无标注样本中学习鲁棒的注视表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了DSCL的即插即用特性，在域内和跨域评估下仅用20%、10%甚至5%的标注数据即可达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: DSCL框架通过特征解耦和子空间序数对比学习，有效利用无标注数据提升了注视估计的域泛化能力，显著降低了对人工标注的依赖。

Abstract: Appearance-based gaze estimation always suffers from poor generalization due to limited annotated samples and insufficient dataset diversity. Leading approaches adopt weakly supervised learning to generate large-scale pseudo-labeled data from unconstrained real-world scenarios, aiming to mitigate the domain shifts. In this work, we devise a simple yet effective semi-supervised learning architecture that leverages unlabeled data to enhance domain generalization, thereby reducing reliance on labor-intensive manual annotations. Our key insight is to impose Jacobian regularization to disentangle feature representations into discriminative subspaces dedicated to specific gaze components, such as pitch and yaw angles. We further exploit the intrinsic ordinal ranking within each subspace for contrastive learning, enabling the model to learn robust gaze representations from a small set of labeled samples and an abundance of unlabeled ones. This ultimately yields our Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning (DSCL) framework. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks verify that the proposed DSCL is plug-and-play, achieving competitive performance using only 20\%, 10\%, and even 5\% of the annotated data under both in-domain and cross-domain evaluation settings. The public code is available at \href{https://github.com/da60266/DSCL}{https://github.com/da60266/DSCL}.

</details>


### [82] [Pop-Up Distractions Reveal Bag-of-Events Behavior in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27101)
*Oscar Chew,Serhii Honcharenko,Qian-Hui Chen,Patricia Lu,Dishant Zaveri,Khoa D. Doan,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DistractionBench基准，通过向视频中插入无关广告片段来测试VideoLLMs是否会将不同片段中的主体和事件错误关联。实验发现11种主流模型普遍存在"事件袋（BoE）"行为，即把视频当作无序事件集合而非时间序列处理，导致频繁幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VideoLLMs虽然具备强大的视频理解能力，但它们是否能真正可靠地将主体与事件在时间维度上进行关联尚不清楚。作者希望通过受控干扰来揭示模型在这一能力上的缺陷。

Method: 设计DistractionBench基准，通过在长视频中插入短广告片段作为干扰，构造主体与事件跨片段错位的测试样本。通过分析模型是否将广告中的动作错误归属于主视频的主体，来量化"事件袋（BoE）"行为程度。

Result: 11种主流VideoLLMs均表现出显著的BoE行为，即模型倾向于将视频处理为无序事件集合，无法有效区分不同时间片段中的主体-事件关联，导致系统性的跨片段幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前VideoLLMs缺乏可靠的时间接地（temporal grounding）机制，无法稳健地建立跨时间的主体-事件关联。未来需要开发具有更强时间结构化能力的模型。

Abstract: A key capability for video understanding is reliably linking subjects to events across time, yet whether Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) actually achieve this remains unclear. In this work, we introduce DistractionBench to evaluate whether VideoLLMs can robustly link subjects and events in the presence of unrelated video segments. Through controlled interventions, such as inserting short advertisement clips into longer videos, we show that VideoLLMs frequently hallucinate interactions between entities from different segments, incorrectly attributing actions from injected advertisements to subjects in the main video. We characterize this systematic hallucination as bag-of-events (BoE) behavior, where models process videos as collections of events rather than temporally structured sequences. Evaluating 11 popular VideoLLMs, we find that all models exhibit substantial BoE behavior. Our findings suggest that VideoLLMs lack reliable mechanisms for temporal grounding and motivate the development of models with more robust subject-event association.

</details>


### [83] [COVD: Continual Open-Vocabulary Object Detection with Novel Concept Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27116)
*Yupeng Zhang,Ruize Han,Yuzhong Feng,Zixin Ren,Yuntong Tian,Liang Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了持续开放词汇目标检测（COVD）任务及Novel-114基准，并设计了无需额外参数的NoIn-Det框架，通过冻结视觉编码器、仅更新文本分支少量参数来实现新概念的高效注入，同时保持旧知识和开放词汇能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVD模型无法适应持续涌现的新类别，重复全量训练成本过高，且预训练视觉编码器已具备对新概念的感知能力，主要瓶颈在于视觉表示与文本概念之间缺乏稳定的语义对齐。

Method: 提出NoIn-Det框架：冻结视觉编码器；仅用常见概念和已注入概念的文本维持文本表示空间；通过仅更新文本分支中有利于新概念学习的少量参数来注入新概念，无需额外参数。

Result: NoIn-Det能够有效学习新概念、保持旧知识，在无需引入额外参数的情况下，持续优于现有的VLM持续学习方法。

Conclusion: NoIn-Det验证了通过稳定语义对齐和极小参数更新即可实现高效持续概念注入，为OVD模型适应动态类别空间提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) has made significant progress, enabling detectors to generalize from seen to unseen categories. However, real-world category spaces continually evolve, and existing OVD models still struggle with newly emerging concepts, while repeated full retraining is prohibitively expensive. To this end, we introduce a new task setting, termed Continual OVD with Novel Concept Injection (COVD), where models sequentially learn incoming novel concept groups while preserving prior concepts and original open-vocabulary knowledge, along with a new benchmark, Novel-114. Our key observation is that pretrained visual encoders often already perceive and represent many novel concepts, and the main bottleneck lies in the lack of stable semantic alignment between visual representations and textual concepts. Based on this, we propose NoIn-Det, an efficient continual injection framework without additional parameters. NoIn-Det freezes the visual encoder, preserves the text representation space using only texts of common concepts and previously injected concepts, and injects novel concepts by updating only a small subset of text-branch parameters beneficial to novel concept learning. Extensive experiments show that NoIn-Det effectively learns novel concepts, preserves old knowledge, and consistently outperforms existing continual learning methods for VLMs without introducing additional parameters.Novel-114 and the code will be released.

</details>


### [84] [Image Thresholding: Understanding Bias of Evaluation Metrics towards Specific Evaluation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27132)
*Eslam Hegazy,Mohamed Gabr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现多级图像阈值分割中，Otsu类间方差与质量指标（SSIM/PSNR）高度相关，而Kapur熵相关性较弱且不稳定，揭示了固有的指标-目标函数偏差，需要更中立的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究在优化图像阈值分割目标函数（如Otsu和Kapur）时，默认SSIM和PSNR是分割质量的无偏度量。本文旨在检验这一假设是否成立，探究阈值目标函数与质量指标之间是否存在固有的相关性偏差。

Method: 在BSDS500数据集上，对所有可能的阈值组合，分析了Otsu类间方差和Kapur熵这两个经典目标函数与SSIM、PSNR质量指标之间的相关性。

Result: Otsu准则与SSIM和PSNR均保持高度一致的相关性；Kapur熵的相关性较弱且波动更大。Otsu在所有图像上与PSNR的相关性均优于Kapur，在超过91%的图像上与SSIM的相关性也优于Kapur。

Conclusion: 存在固有的指标-目标函数偏差（尤其是Otsu与SSIM/PSNR之间），这暗示以往基于这些指标的评价可能高估了Otsu的性能。未来需要构建更中立的评估框架，并扩展至更多阈值准则和应用领域。

Abstract: Multilevel image thresholding is widely used for segmentation in applications ranging from medical imaging to remote sensing. Classical objective functions, such as Otsu's between-class variance and Kapur's entropy, are often optimized using metaheuristic algorithms, with performance evaluated via metrics like Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) and Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR). These evaluations implicitly assume that SSIM and PSNR provide unbiased measures of segmentation quality. In this study, we examine this assumption by analyzing the correlation between thresholding objective functions and quality metrics across all possible thresholds for images in the BSDS500 dataset. Results show that Otsu's criterion consistently exhibits high correlation with both SSIM and PSNR, while Kapur's entropy demonstrates weaker and more variable correlation. Otsu outperforms Kapur in correlation with PSNR for all images and with SSIM for over 91%. Our findings reveal an inherent metric-objective-function bias. This work highlights the need for more neutral evaluation frameworks and motivates extending the analysis to additional thresholding criteria and domains. Source code of this paper can be found at https://w3id.org/met-dp/icpr26-95

</details>


### [85] [Leveraging Visual Signals for Robust Token-Level Uncertainty in Vision-Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27136)
*Joseph Hoche,David Brellmann,Gianni Franchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIG-TUQ框架，通过将视觉接地分数整合到令牌级语言不确定性中，来改进LVLM的不确定性量化，实验表明该方法在多种架构上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM不确定性量化方法多从LLM领域直接迁移，主要关注语言模态，而视觉信息对不确定性的贡献尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过分析视觉特征整合后的隐藏表示，发现高置信度预测更依赖于视觉内容。基于此，提出VIG-TUQ，一种无需训练的不确定性估计框架，通过视觉接地分数加权令牌级语言不确定性。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种LVLM架构（早期融合、晚期融合、原生融合）上，VIG-TUQ在令牌级不确定性方法上取得了改进。

Conclusion: 视觉信息在LVLM不确定性估计中具有重要作用，显式引入视觉接地可以有效提升不确定性量化性能。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) remains a critical challenge in Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) for reliable predictions and real-world deployment. However, most existing methods are adapted from the LLM literature and primarily focus on the language modality, leaving the contribution of visual information to LVLM uncertainty largely underexplored. In this paper, we investigate how LVLMs process visual information and whether this process can be used to improve uncertainty estimation. By analyzing hidden representations after the integration of visual features during the generation process, we observe that high-confidence predictions rely more heavily on visual content than uncertain ones. Building on this insight, we propose Visual-Grounded Token UQ (VIG-TUQ), a training-free framework that explicitly incorporates visual grounding into uncertainty estimation by weighting token-level language uncertainty with visual grounding scores. We evaluate VIG-TUQ on multiple datasets and across diverse LVLM architectures, including early-fusion, late-fusion, and native-fusion models. Results indicate that our method often improves upon existing token-level uncertainty approaches. Code and data will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [86] [Is an Image Also Worth 16x16=256 Superpixels? A Framework for Attentional Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27144)
*Pedro Henrique da Costa Avelar,Anderson R. Tavares,Luís C. Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Superpixel Transformers (SPT)框架，统一了超像素图像分类与Vision Transformers，通过多维正弦-余弦位置编码和 enriched patch 数据结构，在CIFAR10、FashionMNIST和Imagenette等数据集上超越基于超像素的GNN方法，并与ViT保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN、超像素与Transformer三者之间的协同连接尚未被探索，且现有超像素GNN方法（如SICGAT）存在像素聚合过程中的信息损失等问题。

Method: 提出SPT框架，泛化SICGAT和ViT，支持任意超像素分块策略、连接图和位置编码；引入多维正弦-余弦位置编码和包含超像素形状与颜色信息的 enriched patch 数据结构。

Result: 在CIFAR10、FashionMNIST和Imagenette上，SPT优于先前基于超像素的GNN方法，与ViT性能相当；证明受约束的图连接可增强ViT性能。

Conclusion: SPT弥合了超像素模型与Transformer模型之间的差距，为混合注意力框架的跨域泛化和未来创新开辟了道路。

Abstract: Superpixel-based image classification has traditionally leveraged graph neural networks (GNNs) for processing irregular image representations. Recent advances in computer vision, driven by Vision Transformers (ViTs), have introduced new paradigms in self-attentional models, surpassing convolutional neural networks (CNNs) in various tasks. However, a synergistic connection between GNNs, superpixels, and transformers remains unexplored. In this work, we propose Superpixel Transformers (SPT), a novel framework that unifies superpixel-based image classification and ViTs. SPT generalizes the Superpixel Image Classification with Graph Attention Networks (SICGAT) model and ViT to support arbitrary superpixel-based chunking strategies, connectivity graphs, and positional encodings. We introduce refinements including a multidimensional sine-cosine positional encoding and an enriched patch data structure that fully incorporates superpixel shape and color information. By testing SPT across datasets such as CIFAR10, FashionMNIST, and Imagenette, with various superpixel generation and graph connectivity strategies, we demonstrate that SPT achieves superior performance compared to previous superpixel-based GNN methods and remains competitive with ViTs. Notably, our approach addresses the limitations of SICGAT, such as information loss during pixel aggregation, and shows how constrained graph connectivity can enhance ViT performance. SPT bridges the gap between superpixel-based and transformer models, opening avenues for cross-domain generalization and future innovations in hybrid attentional frameworks, and showing that an image can also be worth $16\times16$ superpixels.

</details>


### [87] [Touch-R1: Reinforcing Touch Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27154)
*Yingxin Lai,Yafei Zhou,Fucai Zhu,Siyu Zhu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TouchReason-1M大规模触觉多模态数据集和TouchReason-Bench评估框架，并基于此训练Touch-R1触觉推理多模态大语言模型，通过触觉增强GRPO目标函数实现基于物理接触的推理，在TouchReason-Bench上超越Octopi-13B和GPT-4o。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉-语言模型依赖监督或对比学习目标，难以基于物理证据进行推理或纠正错误视觉先验，且触觉推理面临物理属性序数性和跨传感器分布偏移两大挑战。

Method: 构建包含4种传感器、超100万同步触觉对的TouchReason-1M数据集和评估基准TouchReason-Bench；提出Touch-R1模型，基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B，采用触觉增强GRPO目标函数，融合序数感知准确性、跨传感器物理一致性、结构化格式控制及输入侧触觉接地目标，其中触觉使用奖励仅在真实触觉输入优于反事实对照（移除、打乱或噪声掩蔽）时赋予信用。

Result: Touch-R1-7B在TouchReason-Bench上平均超越Octopi-13B 18.4%、超越GPT-4o 24.7%；结构化推理轨迹展现出探测、比较和修正等涌现行为。

Conclusion: R1式推理可以有效植根于物理触觉接触，验证了触觉增强GRPO训练策略在多模态触觉推理中的有效性。

Abstract: While rule-based reinforcement learning has recently catalyzed explicit reasoning in multimodal models, tactile reasoning remains largely underexplored. Existing tactile-language models primarily rely on supervised or contrastive objectives, which limits their capacity to ground predictions in physical evidence or rectify misleading visual priors. Tactile reasoning introduces two modality-specific challenges: the ordinal nature of physical attributes (e.g., hardness, roughness) and the cross-sensor distribution shifts inherent in optical tactile hardware. In this work, we introduce TouchReason-1M, a large-scale multimodal dataset comprising over 1M synchronized tactile pairs across four distinct sensors, and TouchReason-Bench, a rigorous framework for evaluating tactile perception and visual-tactile conflict resolution. Building upon these, we propose Touch-R1, a tactile reasoning MLLM based on Qwen2.5-VL-7B. Touch-R1 is trained via a tactile-grounded GRPO objective that combines ordinal-aware accuracy, cross-sensor physical consistency, structured-format control, and an input-side tactile grounding objective. Specifically, the tactile-use reward assigns credit only when authentic tactile inputs yield superior correctness relative to counterfactual controls where the tactile stream is removed, shuffled, or noise-masked. On TouchReason-Bench, Touch-R1-7B outperforms Octopi-13B by 18.4\% and GPT-4o by 24.7\% on average. Its structured reasoning traces reveal emergent behaviors of probing, comparison, and revision, demonstrating that R1-style reasoning can be effectively grounded in physical contact.

</details>


### [88] [Semantic Robustness Probing via Inpainting: An Interactive Tool for Safety-Critical Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27155)
*Nico Steckhan,Krutarth Prajapati,Weija Shao,Silvia Vock*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemProbe是一个面向安全关键领域目标检测器的语义鲁棒性探测工具，通过扩散模型控制修复技术生成语义探针，支持批量作业、参数配置、自动推理和结构化日志记录，以实现可追溯的鲁棒性评估证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键领域（如工业设备），传统基于像素级损坏的测试方法无法提供语义上有意义的探测，难以评估目标检测器在真实场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 用户上传部署图像，手动或自动创建掩码，选择操作设计域（ODD）因素或自定义提示，通过扩散模型进行受控修复。系统支持批量作业、并行种子/工作流变体以及可配置生成参数，输出后自动运行模型推理并展示带性能变化的注释前后对比。

Result: 所有探针作为结构化工件记录，实现可追溯的鲁棒性证据，并在手指检测（用于尺寸锯）场景中进行了演示，目标因素来自保险导向测试标准。

Conclusion: SemProbe为安全关键领域目标检测器提供了语义层面的鲁棒性测试工具，生成符合安全评估工作流的可追溯证据。

Abstract: Testing object detectors in safety-critical domains requires semantically meaningful probes beyond pixel-level corruptions. We present SemProbe, a tool for semantic robustness probing: users upload deployment images, create masks manually or automatically, select operational design domain-derived factors (or custom prompts), and run diffusion-based controlled inpainting. The system supports batch jobs, parallel seed/workflow variations, and configurable generation parameters. After each output, model inference runs automatically and displays annotated before/after comparisons with performance deltas. All probes are logged as structured artifacts, enabling traceable robustness evidence aligned with safety evaluation workflows. We demonstrate \textsc{SemProbe} on hand detection for dimension saws, targeting factors from insurance-oriented test criteria.

</details>


### [89] [Generative Animations: A Multi-Model Pipeline for Prompt-Driven Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27203)
*Mannat Khurana,Sanyam Jain,Rishav Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Generative Animations系统，通过LLM语义解析+SAM视觉定位的pipeline，将自然语言提示自动生成尊重场景几何、深度遮挡和3D透视变换的生产级动画运动路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建自定义运动路径十分繁琐，需要设计师手动选择预设、绘制贝塞尔点、配置时序属性，缺乏从自然语言到动画的自动化生成方法。

Method: 链式调用LLM进行语义解析，结合SAM模型进行视觉定位，构建自动生成运动路径的pipeline，处理场景几何关系、深度遮挡和3D透视变换。

Result: 通过三个用例验证系统能力：轮廓跟随轨迹、带z-order感知的轨道动画、变换对象上的透视对齐运动。

Conclusion: Generative Animations实现了从自然语言到生产级动画的端到端自动化生成，有效处理了复杂场景下的几何与透视约束。

Abstract: Animation elevates digital documents into immersive experiences, yet creating custom motion paths remains cumbersome, requiring designers to manually select presets, plot Bézier points, and configure timing properties. We introduce Generative Animations, a system that transforms natural language prompts into production-ready animations. By chaining Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic parsing with the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for visual grounding, our pipeline automatically generates motion paths that respect scene geometry, handle depth-based occlusions, and honor 3D perspective transforms. We demonstrate the system through three use cases: contour-following trajectories, orbital animations with z-order awareness, and perspective-aligned motion on transformed objects.

</details>


### [90] [MRT: Masked Region Transformer for Layered Image Generation and Editing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27235)
*Zhicong Tang,Zhao Zhang,Jingye Chen,Mohan Zhou,Yifan Pu,Yuchi Liu,Yalong Bai,Ethan Smith,Yuhui Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRT，一个200亿参数的掩码区域扩散模型，用于多层透明图像生成与编辑。该模型在超1000万多语言设计样本上训练，通过统一文本生成层、图像转层和层间编辑三种任务于共享框架，并引入溢出感知画布层处理边界不一致问题，采用扩散蒸馏实现8步实时生成。实验表明，MRT在三项任务上均显著超越现有最先进方法（包括商业系统），相比Qwen-Image-Layered在图像转层质量上更优，同时推理速度提升10-100倍，激活GPU内存降低50-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层图像生成与编辑是视觉内容创作的基础能力，可实现类似自然语言中词级编辑的灵活操作，但目前在大规模条件下尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1) 提出掩码区域扩散模型MRT（200亿参数），统一文本生成层（text-to-layers）、图像转层（image-to-layers）和层间编辑（layers-to-layers）三种任务；2) 引入溢出感知画布层（overflow-aware canvas layer），处理层边界溢出和不一致问题，支持半透明背景合成；3) 应用扩散蒸馏实现8步实时生成。

Result: 在三项任务上均显著超越现有SOTA方法及商业系统；相比Qwen-Image-Layered，图像转层质量更优，推理速度提升10-100倍，激活GPU内存降低50-90%。

Conclusion: MRT为多层透明图像生成建立了新基准，证明了大规模掩码区域扩散模型在分层视觉内容生成与编辑中的有效性。

Abstract: Layered image generation and editing is a fundamental capability that enables layer-wise reuse, editing, and composition of generated visual content, analogous to word-level editing in natural language. Despite its importance, this remains an underexplored area at scale. To address this gap, we present MRT, a 20B-parameter masked region diffusion model tailored for multi-layer transparent image generation and editing, trained on over 10M multilingual design samples spanning diverse aspect ratios and textual prompts. To fully leverage this scale, we make two key technical contributions. First, we unify three complementary tasks including text-to-layers, image-to-layers, and layers-to-layers within a shared masked region diffusion framework, where selective token masking enables flexible layer-wise generation and editing. Second, to enable overflow layer generation, we introduce an overflow-aware canvas layer that handles boundary inconsistencies and supports semi-transparent background synthesis, enabling complete editable layers extending beyond visible canvas boundaries. Additionally, we apply diffusion distillation to achieve 8-step, real-time multi-layer generation with minimal quality degradation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework substantially outperforms prior state-of-the-art approaches, including various commercial systems, across all three tasks, establishing a new benchmark for multi-layer transparent image generation. Notably, our model significantly outperforms the concurrent Qwen-Image-Layered model in image-to-layers quality according to user-study results, while achieving 10-100\times faster inference and reducing activation GPU memory consumption by 50-90\% during image-to-layer inference.

</details>


### [91] [Can Retrieval Heads See Images? Multimodal Retrieval Heads in Long-Context Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27243)
*Aaron Branson Cigres Li,Zhaowei Wang,Yu Zhao,Yiming Du,Haobo Li,Xiyu Ren,Ginny Wong,Simon See,Lishu Luo,Haodong Duan,Pasquale Minervini,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次在大型视觉-语言模型中发现多模态检索头（multimodal retrieval heads），这些头在跨文本和图像证据定位中具有稀疏性、固有性和因果重要性，并能直接用于视觉丰富的文档排序任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型需要处理包含交错文本和图像的长上下文，但先前基于纯文本的检索头检测方法因依赖复制机制而无法直接适用于图像证据。因此，需要一种通用的多模态检索头检测方法。

Method: 提出一种多模态检索头检测方法，通过计算问题token到文本或图像证据的注意力分数来识别检索头。在多个长上下文多模态基准上分析其稀疏性、跨模态共享性及因果重要性。

Result: 多模态检索头仅占4.4-10.2%，却贡献50%的正检索分数；屏蔽Top-5%检索头使MMLongBench-Doc和SlideVQA分别从48.2%降至5.7%、71.2%降至8.9%。在MMDocIR上，基于这些头的评分方法在页面和布局检索上分别提升Recall@1达7.7/7.4和6.3/6.8个点。

Conclusion: 多模态检索头是稀疏、固有且因果重要的，跨模态部分共享但模态内动态变化，且无需额外训练即可用于文档检索任务。

Abstract: Large vision-language models increasingly rely on long-context modeling to reason over documents, hour-level videos, and long-horizon agent trajectories, requiring them to locate relevant evidence across interleaved text and images. Prior work has studied this behavior using retrieval heads in large language models, but its copy-based criterion does not directly apply when evidence appears in images. We introduce a multimodal retrieval head detection method that scores attention from question tokens to textual or visual evidence. With this method, we show that multimodal retrieval heads are sparse, intrinsic, and causally important: only 4.4-10.2% of attention heads account for 50% of the positive retrieval-score mass, and masking the top-5% selected heads drops MMLongBench-Doc from 48.2% to 5.7% and SlideVQA from 71.2% to 8.9%, while random-head masking is far less damaging. Further analysis shows that these heads are partly shared across modalities yet remain dynamic within each modality, with image retrieval heads changing more than text retrieval heads as context length and haystack modality change. Without further training, we find that these heads can also be used directly to rank visually rich documents: on MMDocIR, Qwen3-VL-8B selected-head scoring improves Recall@1 by 7.7/7.4 macro/micro points for page retrieval and 6.3/6.8 points for layout retrieval over the strongest reported baseline.

</details>


### [92] [A Dynamic Programming Framework for Discovering Count and Values of Multilevel Image Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27287)
*Eslam Hegazy,Mohamed Gabr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动态规划算法和改进最小误差阈值（MET）准则的多级图像阈值化方法（MET-DP），该方法能自动确定合适的阈值数量，在高阈值数下计算效率显著优于传统动态规划方法，但图像质量（SSIM/PSNR）略低于人工指定阈值数的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数阈值化方法需要用户手动输入期望的阈值数量，缺乏自动确定合适阈值数量的能力。本文旨在提出一种能够从输入图像本身自动确定合适阈值数量的阈值化方法，以减少人工干预并提高实用性。

Method: 提出一种基于动态规划算法（DP）与修改后的最小误差阈值（MET）准则相结合的方法（MET-DP）。通过经验统计研究分析该方法优越性的原因，并在自然图像、卫星图像和医学图像的综合性测试集上与多种现有方法进行对比。

Result: MET-DP方法在高阈值数量下比传统动态规划阈值化方法耗时显著更少；能够为大多数不同类型测试图像自动检测出合适的阈值数量。然而，传统需人工输入阈值数量的方法在SSIM和PSNR指标上优于MET-DP。

Conclusion: MET-DP在计算效率（尤其是高阈值场景）和自动确定阈值数量方面具有优势，但在分割图像的结构相似性和峰值信噪比质量上不如人工指定阈值数的传统方法，存在图像质量与自动化程度之间的权衡。

Abstract: Multilevel Image thresholding is an important preprocessing algorithm in computer vision applications nowadays. Since most common thresholding methods take the desired count of thresholds as input by the user, thresholding methods that automatically determines a suitable count of thresholds from the input image itself are advantageous. In this article, a novel thresholding method based on a dynamic programming algorithm and a modification of Minimum Error Thresholding (MET) criterion is thoroughly presented. An empirical statistical study is performed to pinpoint why this proposed method is superior. Moreover, an extended comparison between this proposed method and other state-of-the-art methods is performed on a comprehensive set of natural, satellite and medical test images. The numerical results show that the proposed MET-DP method takes much less time than traditional dynamic programming thresholding methods when the number of thresholds is high. The proposed method can detect a suitable count of thresholds for most of tested images of different types. However, traditional methods that take the count of thresholds as input produce thresholded images of higher structural similarity index measure (SSIM) and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) values than MET-DP. Source code can be found on https://w3id.org/met-dp/article1-code

</details>


### [93] [Gemini Embedding 2: A Native Multimodal Embedding Model from Gemini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27295)
*Madhuri Shanbhogue,Zhe Li,Shanfeng Zhang,Gustavo Hernández Ábrego,Shih-Cheng Huang,Aashi Jain,Daniel Salz,Sonam Goenka,Chaitra Hegde,Ji Ma,Feiyang Chen,Jiaxing Wu,Tanmaya Dabral,Babak Samari,Kevin Poulet,Daniel Cer,Kaifeng Chen,Paul Suganathan,Hui Hui,Jovan Andonov,Philippe Schlattner,Jay Han,Iftekhar Naim,Wing Lowe,Vladimir Pchelin,Albert Yang,Yi-Ting Chen,Zhongli Ding,Grace Zhang,Georg Heigold,Yichang Chen,Antoine Reveillon,Brendan Mccloskey,Wenlei Zhou,Dahun Kim,Rui Meng,Emma Wang,Jack Zheng,Halley Fede,Zhen Yang,Keegan Mosley,Brian Potetz,Sahil Dua,Henrique Schechter Vera,Shen Gao,Hesen Zhang,Andreas Hess,Hengxuan Ying,Alberto Montes,Karan Gill,Min Choi,Sebastian Russo,Anja Hauth,Jinhyuk Lee,Michael Boratko,Megan Barnes,Vikram Rao,Claudiu Musat,Cyril Allauzen,Ehsan Variani,Shankar Kumar,Tom Bagby,Junyi Jiao,Yang Gu,Tengxin Li,Ayush Agrawal,Roberto Santana,Dev Nath,Stephen Karukas,Shuoxuan Han,Lucia Loher,Alice Twu,Nidhi Vyas,Siddharth Bhai,Frank Palma Gomez,Wangyuan Zhang,Chaoren Liu,Jizheng Yang,Steve Qiu,Shijie Zhang,Sujay Kulkarni,Sascha Rothe,Sean Nakamoto,Raphael Hoffmann,Zach Gleicher,Yunhsuan Sung,Qin Yin,Tom Duerig,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gemini Embedding 2 是一种原生多模态嵌入模型，能够将视频、音频、图像和文本模态统一到同一表示空间中，通过多任务多阶段对比学习训练，在多种检索基准上达到SOTA性能，超越专用模型，适用于RAG、推荐、搜索等下游任务，并展现出强大的零样本跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型通常只支持单一或少数模态，缺乏能统一表示视频、音频、图像和文本等多模态信息的通用嵌入模型，限制了跨模态检索和多模态下游应用的性能。

Method: 基于Gemini的多模态能力，采用大规模对比学习与多任务多阶段训练框架，将视频、音频、图像、文本等模态的任意交错组合输入映射到统一的表示空间。

Result: 在MSCOCO上R@1达62.9，Vatex上NDCG@10达68.8，MTEB多语言达69.9，MTEB Code达84.0，在多个跨模态与单模态检索任务上超越专用模型。

Conclusion: Gemini Embedding 2是一种统一、可靠且开箱即用的多模态嵌入模型，在RAG、推荐、搜索等下游场景中具有广阔应用前景，且在天文学、生物科学、艺术等多个专业领域也展现出优秀的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce Gemini Embedding 2, a native multimodal embedding model that allows embedding video, audio, image, and text modalities in a unified representation space. We leverage the multimodal capabilities of Gemini to produce embeddings for arbitrary combinations of interleaved inputs across all these modalities that generalize well across a wide variety of tasks. Applying large-scale contrastive learning in a multi-task multi-stage training setup, we achieve state-of-the-art performance on key embedding benchmarks including unimodal, cross-modal, and multimodal retrieval spanning a diverse set of tasks. We show that our embedding model demonstrates strong performance (with a score of 62.9 R@1 on MSCOCO, 68.8 NDCG@10 on Vatex, 69.9 on MTEB multilingual and 84.0 on MTEB Code) across a variety of tasks surpassing the performance of specialized models. These unified capabilities make Gemini Embedding 2 a promising candidate for downstream use cases such as RAG, recommendation and search. Furthermore, its robust zero-shot performance across distinct fields - from astronomy and bioscience to fine arts and the culinary arts - establishes it as a highly reliable, out-of-the-box representation even for specialized domains.

</details>


### [94] [How and What to Imagine? Visual Thinking in Unified Multimodal Models for Cross-View Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27310)
*Qian Yang,Ankur Sikarwar,Huy Le,Le Zhang,Zhuan Shi,Perouz Taslakian,Aishwarya Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉语言模型在跨视角空间推理中的弱点，本文提出View Dropout（VDrop）训练策略，强制模型利用中间思考图像而非仅依赖输入视图进行推理。通过对比三种思考图像变体（俯视图、全景图、点匹配渲染），发现全景视觉思考结合VDrop在五个真实世界域外基准测试中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在跨视角空间推理中存在弱点，它们倾向于用语言推理而丢失细粒度的几何信息。虽然"用图像思考"的方法通过生成中间思考图像来解决此问题，但现有工作表明模型常常忽略这些视觉线索。因此需要研究如何让视觉思考真正发挥作用，以及哪种视觉思考形式最有效。

Method: 提出View Dropout（VDrop）训练干预方法，在训练时隐藏输入视图的部分区域使其对抗答不可见，但对思考图像令牌保持可见，从而强制模型依赖思考图像进行回答。在此基础上，将视觉思考形式框架化为可学习性与信息性的权衡，比较三种思考图像变体：俯视图、全景图和点匹配渲染。

Result: 在合成场景上训练，在五个真实世界域外基准测试上评估，全景视觉思考结合VDrop是唯一兼具信息性和可学习性的配置，取得了最佳的域外泛化性能。

Conclusion: VDrop能有效促使模型真正利用视觉思考图像进行推理，而全景图作为中间思考表示在信息性与可学习性之间取得了最佳平衡，适用于跨视角空间推理任务。

Abstract: Cross-view spatial reasoning remains a weak spot for vision-language models (VLMs): they often reason in language and lose the fine-grained geometry needed for the task. Thinking with images aims to address this by generating an intermediate thinking image, but recent work shows that models often ignore the visual evidence in these traces. We therefore ask how to make visual thinking matter, and what kind of visual thinking works best. We study these questions in unified multimodal models (UMMs), which natively support interleaved image-text generation. For the first question, we propose View Dropout (VDrop), a training-time intervention that hides parts of one input view from the answer span while keeping them visible to the thinking-image tokens. This encourages the model to use the thinking image when answering, instead of relying only on the input views. Once the thinking image is used for answer prediction, we study which type of visual thinking is most effective. We frame this as a learnability-informativeness tradeoff and compare three thinking-image variants: top-down, panoramic, and point-matching renderings. Trained on synthetic scenes and evaluated on five real-world out-of-domain benchmarks, panoramic visual thinking with VDrop is the only configuration that is both informative and learnable, and it achieves the best out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [95] [Q-GeoMem: Question-Guided Geometric Memory for Video Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27318)
*Xianqiang Gao,Qizhi Chen,Delin Qu,Haoming Song,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题引导的几何记忆框架（ours），用于视频空间推理。该框架通过相机条件几何注入、细粒度上下文银行和语义-几何证据银行的双记忆结构，以及基于Q-Former的问题相关性和新颖性评分机制，实现了视频空间推理的最新性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间视频语言模型虽然提升了几何感知和长程上下文建模能力，但通常将记忆视为通用时间缓存，可能引入冗余或无关的几何信息，削弱了长程推理能力。

Method: 提出ours框架，包括：1) 将相机条件几何注入视觉token；2) 维护两种互补记忆：细粒度上下文银行（近期密集特征和相机状态）和语义-几何证据银行（紧凑长程证据）；3) 基于Q-Former的问题相关性和新颖性乘积计算候选帧评分，存储并复用于读取过程，采用容量替换规则保持银行紧凑；4) 推理时先读取再更新两种记忆，并与当前帧表示自适应融合。

Result: 在VSI-Bench和VSTI-Bench基准上，ours在评估的空间推理模型中取得了最优性能，消融实验验证了所提证据评分机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 问题引导的几何记忆框架有效提升了视频空间推理性能，验证了基于问题相关性引导记忆选择与更新的设计思路的有效性。

Abstract: Video spatial reasoning requires accumulating viewpoint-dependent evidence over time while retaining information useful to the question being asked. Existing spatial video-language models improve geometric perception and long-range context modeling, but often treat memory as a generic temporal cache, which can introduce redundant or irrelevant geometry and weaken long-horizon reasoning. We propose \textbf{\ours}, a question-guided geometric memory framework for video spatial reasoning. \ours injects camera-conditioned geometry into visual tokens and maintains two complementary memories: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent dense features and camera states, and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for compact long-range evidence. Each candidate frame is scored by the product of Q-Former-based question relevance and novelty with respect to the retained bank; this score is stored and reused during reading, while a capacity-based replacement rule keeps the bank compact. During reasoning, both memories are read before update and adaptively fused with the current frame representation. Experiments on VSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench show that \ours achieves state-of-the-art performance among evaluated spatial reasoning models, validating the effectiveness of question-guided geometric memory. Ablations further verify the contribution of the proposed evidence scoring mechanism.

</details>


### [96] [PARE: Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27336)
*Yutong Wang,Yunke Wang,Tianfan Xue,Yu Qiao,Yaohui Wang,Xinyuan Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PARE方法，通过结构感知的宽度剪枝（区分空间/时间注意力头）和输入自适应路由（基于去噪步长和视觉内容动态选择深度模块），联合压缩视频扩散Transformer的宽度和深度，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer（DiTs）生成高质量视频但计算成本极高，现有方法虽通过压缩宽度、深度或采样步数来降低开销，但都采用固定架构，无法根据单个输入或去噪阶段进行自适应调整。

Method: 1）宽度压缩：观察注意力头存在空间/时间角色专门化，设计区分性重要性评分，防止关键时间头被过早剪枝；2）深度压缩：训练轻量路由器，以去噪时间步和视觉内容为条件，动态选择每步执行哪些模块；3）渐进式流水线：先通过蒸馏恢复宽度剪枝质量，再联合优化学生模型和路由器以解耦两个学习目标。

Result: 在Wan2.1-14B模型上的图像到视频和文本到视频生成实验中，PARE在VBench各项指标上保持生成质量的同时显著减少了每步计算量，并能与步蒸馏组合实现进一步加速。

Conclusion: PARE通过联合宽度和深度压缩，以及输入自适应的动态路由策略，实现了视频DiTs的高效生成，且具有与现有步蒸馏方法正交组合的兼容性。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) generate high-quality videos but demand substantial compute due to wide blocks, deep architectures, and iterative sampling. Recent methods reduce cost by compressing width, depth, or sampling steps, but typically commit to a fixed architecture that cannot adapt to individual inputs or denoising stages. We propose PARE (Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient video generation), which jointly compresses width and depth with structure-aware pruning and input-adaptive routing. For width, we observe that attention heads specialize into spatial and temporal roles, and design importance scoring that accounts for this distinction to prevent motion-critical temporal heads from being pruned prematurely. For depth, we train a lightweight router conditioned on denoising timestep and visual content to dynamically select which blocks to execute at each step, enabling per-input compute adaptation rather than static block removal. A progressive pipeline first recovers width-pruned quality via distillation, then jointly optimizes the student and router to decouple the two learning objectives. Experiments on Wan2.1-14B for both image-to-video and text-to-video generation show that PARE substantially reduces per-step computation while preserving quality across VBench dimensions, and composes with step distillation for further acceleration.

</details>


### [97] [Towards Controllable Image Generation through Representation-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27343)
*Nithesh Chandher Karthikeyan,Jonas Unger,Gabriel Eilertsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了使用预训练自监督模型表征作为扩散模型的条件机制，该方法可改善无条件图像生成质量，并提供平滑且解缠的控制空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型依赖文本提示或语义图等条件机制，需要大量标注数据集，存在数据标注成本高的问题。

Method: 利用预训练自监督模型的表征作为扩散模型的条件，通过识别表征空间中的变化方向来实现对生成过程的控制。

Result: 自条件机制不仅提升了无条件图像生成的质量，还提供了可用于控制生成的表征空间，该空间在平滑性和解缠性方面表现出良好特性。

Conclusion: 基于自监督模型表征的条件机制是一种有前景的扩散模型控制方式，可减少对标注数据的依赖，同时提供高质量且可控的图像生成能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for high-quality image generation and editing, but guiding these models to produce specific outputs remains a challenge. Conventional approaches rely on conditioning mechanisms, such as text prompts or semantic maps, which require extensively annotated datasets. In this preliminary work, we explore diffusion models conditioned on representations from a pre-trained self-supervised model. The self-conditioning mechanism not only improves the quality of unconditional image generation, but also provides a representation space that can be used to control the generation. We explore this conditioning space by identifying directions of variations, and demonstrate promising properties in terms of smoothness and disentanglement.

</details>


### [98] [When Eyes Betray AI: Social Gaze Consistency as a Semantic Cue for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27348)
*Kim Jihyeon,Sohee Kim,Soosan Lee,Souhwan Jung,James Matthew Rehg,Hyesong Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出社会性凝视一致性（Social Gaze Consistency）作为高维语义线索用于检测生成图像，通过构建诊断数据集、块组合描述监督和跨架构验证，证明该线索能有效提升检测器性能且与现有方法正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在低层伪影（像素指纹、频率异常等）上的检测差距已大幅缩小，尤其在人脸中心和局部编辑场景中，被编辑区域小且被真实内容包围，使得低层线索失效。因此需要探索高层语义线索作为新的检测维度。

Method: (i) 构建控制诊断数据集，采用严格配对分组阻断生成器指纹记忆；(ii) 提出块组合描述监督（Block-Compositional Caption Supervision），固定5块推理骨架在1250个宏观组合描述中保持不变，解耦推理一致性与表面多样性；(iii) 跨架构验证，在视觉-语言骨干（FakeVLM）和纯视觉骨干（Effort）上评估。四步机制解释：配对编辑捷径阻断、难到易难度迁移、CLIP先验保持、扩散族在眼周结构的共享谱弱点。

Result: FakeVLM在COCOAI Interaction子集上平衡准确率从67.8提升至71.5（+3.7pp），在Person子集上从83.0提升至84.3（+1.3pp）；Effort骨干上也取得一致增益。真实类和伪造类召回率同步上升，排除了"预测所有为假"的伪影。单生成器（FLUX.1-Fill）训练可迁移至多生成器套件。

Conclusion: 社会性凝视一致性是一种骨干无关、与现有低层范式正交的高层语义检测线索，通过阻断配对编辑捷径和利用扩散模型在眼周结构的共享弱点，实现了跨生成器的有效检测。

Abstract: Recent generative models have largely closed the gap on low-level artifacts - pixel fingerprints, frequency anomalies, upsampling traces - particularly in person-centric and partial-edit settings where the manipulated region is small and surrounded by photometrically authentic content. We introduce Social Gaze Consistency, a high-level semantic cue defined as the mutual coherence of gaze direction, head-eye alignment, and pupil placement between interacting individuals, and show that it constitutes a previously underutilized detection axis orthogonal to existing low-level paradigms. We instantiate this insight through three coupled mechanisms: (i) a controlled diagnostic dataset with region-specific perturbations of gaze-consistent imagery, where strict pair-level grouping forecloses generator-fingerprint memorization as an optimization-time shortcut rather than relying on augmentation; (ii) Block-Compositional Caption Supervision, which holds a single 5-block reasoning skeleton invariant across 1,250 macro-combined captions, decoupling reasoning consistency from surface diversity; (iii) Cross-architecture validation showing the same supervision improves a vision-language backbone (FakeVLM) by +3.7 pp on the COCOAI Interaction subset (balanced accuracy 67.8 -> 71.5) and +1.3 pp on the COCOAI Person subset (83.0 -> 84.3), with consistent gains on a vision-only backbone (Effort), evidencing a backbone-agnostic cue. Real- and fake-class recalls rise simultaneously, ruling out a "predict-all-fake" artifact. A four-step mechanistic account - paired-edit shortcut blocking, hard-to-easy difficulty transfer, CLIP prior preservation, and diffusion-family shared spectral weakness in periocular structure - explains why training on a single inpainter (FLUX.1-Fill) transfers to multi-generator suites. We will release the code upon acceptance to facilitate reproducibility.

</details>


### [99] [SpatialBench: Is Your Spatial Foundation Model an All-Round Player?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27367)
*Haosong Peng,Hao Li,Jiaqi Chen,Yuhao Pan,Runmao Yao,Yalun Dai,Fushuo Huo,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Haozhao Wang,Dingwen Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialBench——一个跨范式、多领域的空间基础模型基准测试，包含19个数据集、546个场景，系统评估了6个范式的41个模型在4种输入密度下的表现。研究发现当前模型尚不具备全能泛化能力，并引入DA-Next-5M数据集和DA-Next基线模型以推动空间表示学习发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间基础模型评估局限于特定领域和范式，缺乏对模型在多样化下游任务、任意视角、场景域变化、输入密度变化及硬件约束下真正泛化能力的全面评估，亟需一个系统性、跨范式的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建SpatialBench基准，涵盖19个数据集和546个场景，涉及5个不同空间域；系统评估41个模型（6个范式）在5个任务套件、4种输入密度下的表现；引入DA-Next-5M大规模数据集和DA-Next基线模型。

Result: 当前模型尚未成为全能型玩家；全上下文注意力最大化准确性，有界记忆策略实现长序列可扩展性；在具身和以自我为中心的任务中，严格的领域对齐和高数据质量比简单扩大数据集规模更为关键。

Conclusion: 现有空间基础模型缺乏真正的跨域泛化能力，未来进步需关注领域对齐、数据质量和注意力机制设计，DA-Next-5M和DA-Next为空间表示学习提供了新的基准和方向。

Abstract: While spatial foundation models have demonstrated impressive performance on standard datasets, a critical question remains: are they truly all-round players capable of generalizing robustly across diverse downstream tasks, arbitrary viewpoints, shifting scene domains, varying input densities, and specific hardware constraints? Answering this overarching question requires a holistic assessment, yet current models are mainly evaluated on specific domains for which they were specifically designed or trained. Such evaluations are intrinsically limited by narrow paradigm coverage, limited scene domains, and arbitrary frame sampling, making it fundamentally difficult to assess their true generalization capabilities. To address this gap, we present SpatialBench, a cross-paradigm, domain-diverse benchmark for spatial foundation models with deterministic sampling. SpatialBench features unprecedented scale and rigorous deterministic design, comprising 19 datasets and 546 scenes across 5 diverse spatial domains. It comprehensively evaluates 41 models across 6 paradigms on 5 task suites under 4 different input density settings. Our extensive evaluation reveals that current models are not yet all-round players, and uncovers crucial insights for future advancement. Specifically, we demonstrate that full-context attention maximizes accuracy while bounded-memory strategies unlock long-sequence scalability. Moreover, our empirical evaluations in challenging embodied and egocentric tasks demonstrate that strict domain alignment and high data quality are far more critical to performance than simple dataset scaling. Furthermore, to address the largest data gap identified in our analysis, we go beyond evaluation by introducing a large-scale dataset, DA-Next-5M, and a strong baseline model, DA-Next, pushing the boundaries of spatial representation learning.

</details>


### [100] [G3T Up! Gravity Aligned Coordinate Frames Simplify Pointmap Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27372)
*Bharath Raj Nagoor Kani,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G3T模型，通过预测重力对齐的点图而非相机坐标点图，利用场景中的重力结构先验，减少帧间旋转自由度，提升3D重建精度。同时提出G3T-Long增量式子图重建流水线，进一步优化长序列重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法（如VGGT）预测相机中心坐标系中的像素对齐点图，但该坐标系并非最优选择。作者希望利用真实场景中普遍存在的重力结构线索，将预测转换到重力对齐的直立坐标系中，以降低跨视角配准的旋转自由度，提升重建性能。

Method: 从现有模型微调得到G3T（Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer），预测重力对齐的点图（直立点图）以及相机到重力的姿态。同时提出G3T-Long，一种基于子图的增量式3D重建流水线，利用直立帧降低的旋转自由度实现更优的重建精度。

Result: G3T在重力对齐的3D数据上产生高精度的重力感知预测；G3T-Long在长序列重建中显著提升了重建精度。

Conclusion: 使用重力对齐的坐标系（共享垂直轴）替代相机中心坐标系，能够有效降低帧间配准的旋转自由度，从而提升前馈3D重建方法的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods like VGGT predict pixel-aligned pointmaps in camera-centric coordinate frames. However, this choice of coordinate frame is not always optimal. We propose instead to predict pointmaps in upright, gravity-aligned frames that exploit strong structural cues present in many real-world scenes. Unlike camera-centric frames, gravity-aligned frames share a common vertical axis across viewpoints, reducing the rotational degrees of freedom needed to relate pointmaps to one another. To this end, we introduce the Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer (G3T), fine-tuned from existing models on gravity-aligned 3D data. G3T produces highly accurate gravity-aware predictions, including upright pointmaps and camera-to-gravity poses. We further introduce G3T-Long, a submap-based incremental 3D reconstruction pipeline that leverages the reduced rotational degrees of freedom afforded by upright frames to achieve significantly improved reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [101] [Learning GUI Grounding with Spatial Reasoning from Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21552)
*Yu Zhao,Wei-Ning Chen,Huseyin Atahan Inan,Samuel Kessler,Lu Wang,Lukas Wutschitz,Fangkai Yang,Chaoyun Zhang,Pasquale Minervini,Saravan Rajmohan,Robert Sim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GUI-Cursor，将GUI定位重定义为交互式搜索任务，通过多步强化学习训练模型在GUI中移动光标定位UI元素，取代传统坐标预测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在高分辨率、复杂布局的GUI图像上预测精确数值坐标时经常失败，传统坐标预测范式存在局限性。

Method: 将GUI定位重构为交互式搜索任务，模型每一步决定目标对象、评估光标与目标的空域关系、根据移动历史将光标移近目标；使用多步在线强化学习结合密集轨迹奖励函数训练。

Result: GUI-Cursor在GUI定位和智能体任务上超越强基线，使用相同基座模型且需要更少训练数据；模型能自适应地对困难样本执行更多步骤，并在分布外领域获得更好的空间推理能力。

Conclusion: 通过交互式搜索范式替代坐标预测，结合强化学习训练，能有效提升VLM在GUI定位任务上的表现和泛化能力。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding is commonly framed as a coordinate prediction task -- given a natural language instruction, generate on-screen coordinates for actions such as clicks and keystrokes. However, recent Vision Language Models (VLMs) often fail to predict accurate numeric coordinates when processing GUI images with high resolutions and complex layouts. To address this issue, we reframe GUI grounding as an interactive search task, where the VLM generates actions to move a cursor in the GUI to locate UI elements. At each step, the model determines the target object, evaluates the spatial relations between the cursor and the target, and moves the cursor closer to the target conditioned on the movement history. In this interactive process, the rendered cursor provides visual feedback to help the model align its predictions with the corresponding on-screen locations. We train our GUI grounding model, GUI-Cursor, using multi-step online reinforcement learning with a dense trajectory-based reward function. Experimental results demonstrate that GUI-Cursor surpasses strong baselines in GUI grounding and agentic tasks, achieving superior performance with the same base models while requiring less training data. Further analysis shows that GUI-Cursor learns to adaptively conduct more steps on more difficult examples, and it obtains better spatial reasoning capability on out-of-distribution domains.

</details>


### [102] [PRBench: A Standardized Probabilistic Robustness Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01724)
*Yi Zhang,Zheng Wang,Zhen Chen,Wenjie Ruan,Qing Guo,Siddartha Khastgir,Carsten Maple,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRBench是首个专门评估不同鲁棒性训练方法对概率鲁棒性（PR）提升效果的基准。通过系统比较对抗训练（AT）和PR定向训练方法，发现AT在提升AR和PR方面更通用，而PR定向方法在泛化误差和干净准确率上更有优势。基准包含229个模型、7个数据集和10种架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性（AR）研究聚焦于最坏情况下的确定性对抗样本，而概率鲁棒性（PR）从统计角度衡量随机扰动下预测正确的概率，被视为AR的实用补充。但针对PR的训练方法仍未充分探索，且存在三个局限：非可比评估协议、缺乏与强AT基线对比、缺乏统一框架比较泛化能力。

Method: 构建PRBench基准，系统比较多种AT方法和PR定向训练方法，使用全面指标（干净准确率、PR性能、AR性能、训练效率、泛化误差），并提供PR泛化误差的理论分析。

Result: AT方法在不同超参数设置下提升AR和PR方面更通用；PR定向训练方法始终具有更低的泛化误差和更高的干净准确率。基准包含229个训练模型、7个数据集和10种模型架构。

Conclusion: PRBench为PR训练方法提供了统一评估框架，揭示了不同鲁棒性训练方法各自的优势，有助于指导实际应用中的方法选择。

Abstract: Deep learning models are notoriously vulnerable to imperceptible perturbations. Most existing research centers on adversarial robustness (AR), which evaluates models under worst-case scenarios by examining the existence of deterministic adversarial examples (AEs). In contrast, probabilistic robustness (PR) adopts a statistical perspective, measuring the probability that predictions remain correct under stochastic perturbations. While PR is widely regarded as a practical complement to AR, dedicated training methods for improving PR are still relatively underexplored, albeit with emerging progress. Among the few PR-targeted training methods, we identify three limitations: i non-comparable evaluation protocols; ii limited comparisons to strong AT baselines despite anecdotal PR gains from AT; and iii no unified framework to compare the generalization of these methods. Thus, we introduce PRBench, the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating improvements in PR achieved by different robustness training methods. PRBench empirically compares most common AT and PR-targeted training methods using a comprehensive set of metrics, including clean accuracy, PR and AR performance, training efficiency, and generalization error (GE). We also provide theoretical analysis on the GE of PR performance across different training methods. Main findings revealed by PRBench include: AT methods are more versatile than PR-targeted training methods in terms of improving both AR and PR performance across diverse hyperparameter settings, while PR-targeted training methods consistently yield lower GE and higher clean accuracy. A leaderboard comprising 229 trained models across 7 datasets and 10 model architectures is publicly available at https://wellzline.github.io/PRBenchLeaderboard/.

</details>


### [103] [TAGRPO: Boosting GRPO on Image-to-Video Generation with Direct Trajectory Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05729)
*Jin Wang,Jianxiang Lu,Guangzheng Xu,Comi Chen,Haoyu Yang,Linqing Wang,Peng Chen,Mingtao Chen,Zhichao Hu,Longhuang Wu,Shuai Shao,Qinglin Lu,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAGRPO是一种针对图像到视频（I2V）模型的对比学习后训练框架，通过在中间潜在空间上应用GRPO损失（鼓励与高奖励轨迹对齐、远离低奖励轨迹），并结合记忆库提升多样性，显著优于DanceGRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接应用GRPO到I2V模型无法获得一致的奖励提升，现有方法存在局限性。

Method: 提出TAGRPO框架：1）利用相同初始噪声生成的rollout视频提供更优的优化引导；2）对中间潜在变量施加新颖的GRPO损失，对齐高奖励轨迹、远离低奖励轨迹；3）引入记忆库增强rollout视频多样性并降低计算开销。

Result: 在I2V生成任务上显著优于DanceGRPO。

Conclusion: TAGRPO是一种简单而有效的I2V模型后训练框架，通过对比学习思想解决了GRPO直接应用失效的问题。

Abstract: Recent studies have demonstrated the efficacy of integrating Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) into flow matching models, particularly for text-to-image and text-to-video generation. However, we find that directly applying these techniques to image-to-video (I2V) models often fails to yield consistent reward improvements. To address this limitation, we present TAGRPO, a robust post-training framework for I2V models inspired by contrastive learning. Our approach is grounded in the observation that rollout videos generated from identical initial noise provide superior guidance for optimization. Leveraging this insight, we propose a novel GRPO loss applied to intermediate latents, encouraging direct alignment with high-reward trajectories while maximizing distance from low-reward counterparts. Furthermore, we introduce a memory bank for rollout videos to enhance diversity and reduce computational overhead. Despite its simplicity, TAGRPO achieves significant improvements over DanceGRPO in I2V generation. The deliverables will be updated at https://tagrpo.github.io/ .

</details>


### [104] [Seeing vs. Believing: Evaluating the Language Bias of Open-Source MLLMs in Counter-Intuitive Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07737)
*Chen Ling,Tongwei Zhang,Hanqian Li,Nai Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAIT基准测试，评估多模态大模型在反常识视觉动作场景上的表现。研究发现开源指令微调模型因强语言先验而表现接近随机水平，虽可借助思维链提升准确性但会引发“过度思考”新问题，而针对性的微调和结构化提示能有效缓解这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在主流视觉理解任务上表现优异，但其处理违反日常常识的动作场景（如"兔子追老虎"）的能力尚未得到充分测试，亟需构建专门的基准来评估这一能力缺口。

Method: 构建包含400个高保真合成场景的CAIT基准，聚焦反直觉视觉动作；评估人类、商业闭源模型（Claude、Gemini）及14个代表性开源MLLMs；通过对比视觉输入与语言输出的不一致性，分析语言先验的影响。

Result: 人类准确率约0.95，闭源模型最高达0.88，而标准开源指令微调模型仅达随机水平；引入CoT可提升准确率但显著降低响应速度，并导致模型因过度推理而拒绝接受违反物理规律的视觉内容。

Conclusion: 开源MLLMs在反常识场景中失败的主要原因是强语言先验压倒视觉证据；通过针对性微调和结构化提示可有效缓解该问题，使模型基于实际视觉证据进行推理。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in mainstream visual understanding tasks, but their ability to process action scenes that contradict everyday common sense remains undertested. To address this gap, we introduce CAIT, a benchmark comprising 400 high-fidelity synthetic scenes focused on counter-intuitive visual actions, such as ``a rabbit is chasing a tiger'', where visual evidence explicitly contradicts common-sense expectations. We evaluate human, leading proprietary models (e.g., Claude and Gemini), and 14 representative open-source MLLMs. Humans achieve near-perfect performance (around 0.95 accuracy) and proprietary models demonstrate robust understanding (achieving up to 0.88 accuracy), standard open-source instruction-tuned models perform at the chance level. Further analysis demonstrates that this failure is driven by a strong language prior: rather than trusting the visual input, they automatically override the anomalous visual signals with statistically common text descriptions. Although introducing Chain-of-Thought reasoning mechanisms can improve accuracy, it significantly slows down the response and generates a new failure mode: models overthink the scenario and refuse to accept the actual visual content simply because it violates real-world physical laws. Finally, we demonstrate that targeted fine-tuning and structured prompting can effectively mitigate this reliance on language priors, enabling open-source models to accurately ground their reasoning in actual visual evidence.

</details>


### [105] [Source-Free Domain Adaptation for Geospatial Point Cloud Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08375)
*Yuan Gao,Di Cao,Xiaohuan Xi,Sheng Nie,Shaobo Xia,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LoGo的无源无监督域适应框架，用于3D地理空间点云的语义分割。该方法通过局部类平衡原型估计和全局最优传输分布对齐，结合双一致性伪标签过滤机制，在不访问源域数据的情况下有效应对域偏移，在跨场景和跨传感器基准上均取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D地理空间点云的语义分割在遥感应用中至关重要，但区域和采集方式差异导致的域偏移会降低模型性能。现有域适应方法通常需要访问源域数据，而由于隐私问题和监管政策这往往不可行。因此，需要一种无需源域数据的无源无监督域适应方法。

Method: 提出LoGo框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 局部层面：类平衡原型估计模块，为样本稀缺的尾部类生成鲁棒特征原型，缓解长尾分布导致的特征坍塌；(2) 全局层面：基于最优传输的全局分布对齐模块，将伪标签分配建模为全局优化问题，纠正局部贪心分配带来的头部类过度主导；(3) 双一致性伪标签过滤机制：仅保留局部多增广集成预测与全局最优传输分配一致的伪标签用于自训练。

Result: 在跨场景和跨传感器两个挑战性基准上，LoGo方法一致优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: LoGo框架有效解决了无源无监督域适应下3D点云语义分割的域偏移问题，通过局部-全局双一致性设计，在不访问源域数据的情况下实现了优异的域适应性能。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of 3D geospatial point clouds is fundamental to remote sensing applications, yet domain shifts caused by regional and acquisition-related variations often degrade model performance. Although domain adaptation can mitigate such shifts, existing methods typically require access to source-domain data, which is often infeasible due to privacy concerns and regulatory policies. To address this, we propose LoGo (Local-Global Dual-Consensus), a novel source-free unsupervised domain adaptation (SFUDA) framework requiring only a pretrained model and unlabeled target data. At the local level, we introduce a class-balanced prototype estimation module that ensures that robust feature prototypes can be generated even for sample-scarce tail classes, effectively mitigating the feature collapse caused by long-tailed distributions. At the global level, we introduce an optimal transport-based global distribution alignment module that formulates pseudo-label assignment as a global optimization problem, effectively correcting the over-dominance of head classes inherent in local greedy assignments, and thereby preventing model predictions from being severely biased towards majority classes. Finally, we propose a dual-consistency pseudo-label filtering mechanism that retains only high-confidence pseudo-labels where local multi-augmented ensemble predictions align with global optimal transport assignments for self-training. Extensive experiments on two challenging benchmarks, encompassing cross-scene and cross-sensor settings, demonstrate that LoGo consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/GYproject/LoGo-SFUDA.

</details>


### [106] [Left-Right Symmetry Breaking in CLIP-style Vision-Language Models Trained on Synthetic Spatial-Relation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12809)
*Takaki Yamamoto,Chihiro Noguchi,Toshihiro Tanizawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过可控的1D图像-文本测试平台，探究CLIP风格对比学习训练下Transformer视觉和文本编码器如何习得左右空间关系，发现标签多样性比布局多样性更关键，并通过注意力分解揭示位置嵌入与标记嵌入的交互产生水平注意力梯度，打破左右对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型的空间理解能力仍不明确，尤其是左右关系这类空间关系是否真正被模型习得、通过何种机制习得尚不清楚。

Method: 构建可控的1D图像-文本测试平台，训练轻量级Transformer视觉和文本编码器进行CLIP风格对比学习，系统改变标签多样性和布局多样性评估泛化性；使用注意力分解方法分析位置嵌入与标记嵌入的交互作用。

Result: 对比训练能学习左右关系；标签多样性比布局多样性更能驱动泛化；位置嵌入与标记嵌入的交互产生水平注意力梯度，打破左右对称性，消融该贡献会显著降低左右区分能力。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了CLIP风格模型何时以及如何获得关系能力的机制性见解。

Abstract: Spatial understanding remains a key challenge in vision-language models. Yet it is still unclear whether such understanding is truly acquired, and if so, through what mechanisms. We present a controllable 1D image-text testbed to probe how left-right relational understanding emerges in Transformer-based vision and text encoders trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective. We train lightweight Transformer-based vision and text encoders end-to-end on paired descriptions of one- and two-object scenes and evaluate generalization to unseen object pairs while systematically varying label and layout diversity. We find that contrastive training learns left-right relations and that label diversity, more than layout diversity, is the primary driver of generalization in this setting. To gain the mechanistic understanding, we perform an attention decomposition and show that interactions between positional and token embeddings induce a horizontal attention gradient that breaks left-right symmetry in the encoders; ablating this contribution substantially reduces left-right discrimination. Our results provide a mechanistic insight of when and how CLIP-style models acquire relational competence.

</details>


### [107] [LuxRemix: Lighting Decomposition and Remixing for Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15283)
*Ruofan Liang,Norman Müller,Ethan Weber,Duncan Zauss,Nandita Vijaykumar,Peter Kontschieder,Christian Richardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图像的光分解模型，可从多视图室内场景中分解出各光源并独立控制其开关、色温和强度，结合多视图光照协调和可重光照的3D高斯泼溅表示，实现实时交互式光照编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以从单次多视图场景捕获中实现对复杂室内场景中各独立光源的灵活编辑和控制。

Method: 1) 基于图像的生成式光分解模型，将室内场景光照分解为独立光源；2) 多视图光照协调，确保光照分解在所有视图间一致传播；3) 集成到可重光照的3D高斯泼溅表示中，实现实时交互控制。

Result: 在多种室内场景上实现了高度逼真的光照分解和重光照效果，并在合成和真实数据集上与最先进技术进行了定量和定性对比。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效实现室内场景中独立光源的实时交互编辑，达到高真实感的重光照效果。

Abstract: We present a novel approach for interactive light editing in indoor scenes from a single multi-view scene capture. Our method leverages a generative image-based light decomposition model that factorizes complex indoor scene illumination into its constituent light sources. This factorization enables independent manipulation of individual light sources, specifically allowing control over their state (on/off), chromaticity, and intensity. We further introduce multi-view lighting harmonization to ensure consistent propagation of the lighting decomposition across all scene views. This is integrated into a relightable 3D Gaussian splatting representation, providing real-time interactive control over the individual light sources. Our results demonstrate highly photorealistic lighting decomposition and relighting outcomes across diverse indoor scenes. We evaluate our method on both synthetic and real-world datasets and provide a quantitative and qualitative comparison to state-of-the-art techniques. For video results and interactive demos, see https://luxremix.github.io.

</details>


### [108] [Olaf-World: Orienting Latent Actions for Video World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10104)
*Yuxin Jiang,Yuchao Gu,Ivor W. Tsang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SeqΔ-REPA对齐目标，通过将潜在动作与冻结视频编码器的时序特征差异对齐，实现从无标签视频中学习可跨上下文迁移的动作控制接口，构建了Olaf-World世界模型预训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作学习方法缺乏跨上下文的动作语义对齐机制，导致学到的动作表征纠缠场景特定线索，无法在不同上下文中迁移和共享。

Method: 提出序列级控制-效果对齐目标（SeqΔ-REPA），将整合后的潜在动作与来自冻结自监督视频编码器的时序特征差异进行对齐。

Result: 学到的潜在动作空间更加结构化，在零样本动作迁移和新控制接口的数据高效适应方面显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 动作的语义效果可作为跨上下文的共享参考，通过时序特征差异对齐能够有效地从大规模无标签视频中预训练动作条件的世界模型。

Abstract: Scaling action-controllable world models is limited by the scarcity of action labels. While latent action learning promises to extract control interfaces from unlabeled video, learned latents often fail to transfer across contexts: they entangle scene-specific cues and lack a shared coordinate system. This occurs because standard objectives operate only within each clip, providing no mechanism to align action semantics across contexts. Our key insight is that although actions are unobserved, their semantic effects are observable and can serve as a shared reference. We introduce Seq$Δ$-REPA, a sequence-level control-effect alignment objective that anchors integrated latent action to temporal feature differences from a frozen, self-supervised video encoder. Building on this, we present Olaf-World, a pipeline that pretrains action-conditioned video world models from large-scale passive video. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method learns a more structured latent action space, leading to stronger zero-shot action transfer and more data-efficient adaptation to new control interfaces than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [109] [GS-CLIP: Zero-shot 3D Anomaly Detection by Geometry-Aware Prompt and Synergistic View Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19206)
*Zehao Deng,An Liu,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-CLIP框架，通过两阶段学习（几何感知提示生成和协同视图表示学习）解决零样本3D异常检测中投影几何细节丢失和单模态视觉理解不足的问题，在四个大型数据集上取得优异检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3D异常检测方法将3D点云投影为2D表示，导致几何细节丢失，且依赖单一2D模态难以全面检测多样化的异常类型。

Method: 提出GS-CLIP框架，包含两阶段：阶段1通过几何缺陷蒸馏模块（GDDM）动态生成嵌入3D几何先验的文本提示；阶段2采用协同视图表示学习架构并行处理渲染图和深度图，并通过协同精炼模块（SRM）融合双流特征的互补信息。

Result: 在四个大型公开数据集上的综合实验表明，GS-CLIP在零样本3D异常检测任务中取得了优越的性能。

Conclusion: GS-CLIP通过融合3D几何先验和多模态视觉信息有效解决了零样本3D异常检测中的关键挑战，代码已开源。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D Anomaly Detection is an emerging task that aims to detect anomalies in a target dataset without any target training data, which is particularly important in scenarios constrained by sample scarcity and data privacy concerns. While current methods adapt CLIP by projecting 3D point clouds into 2D representations, they face challenges. The projection inherently loses some geometric details, and the reliance on a single 2D modality provides an incomplete visual understanding, limiting their ability to detect diverse anomaly types. To address these limitations, we propose the Geometry-Aware Prompt and Synergistic View Representation Learning (GS-CLIP) framework, which enables the model to identify geometric anomalies through a two-stage learning process. In stage 1, we dynamically generate text prompts embedded with 3D geometric priors. These prompts contain global shape context and local defect information distilled by our Geometric Defect Distillation Module (GDDM). In stage 2, we introduce Synergistic View Representation Learning architecture that processes rendered and depth images in parallel. A Synergistic Refinement Module (SRM) subsequently fuses the features of both streams, capitalizing on their complementary strengths. Comprehensive experimental results on four large-scale public datasets show that GS-CLIP achieves superior performance in detection. Code can be available at https://github.com/zhushengxinyue/GS-CLIP.

</details>


### [110] [Axial-Centric Cross-Plane Attention for 3D Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21636)
*Doyoung Park,Jinsoo Kim,Lohendran Baskaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以轴向为中心的跨平面注意力架构（AC-XP Attention），用于3D医学图像分类。该方法利用预训练MedDINOv3提取轴向、冠状、矢状面特征，通过RICA块和跨平面Transformer实现轴向主导的非对称平面间依赖建模。在MedMNIST3D六个数据集上优于现有3D及多平面模型，轻量版AC-Tiny以更少参数取得竞争性能，消融实验验证了轴向中心查询、跨平面融合等设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D深度学习方法要么整体处理体积数据，要么对所有平面赋予同等重要性，未能反映临床CT诊断中以轴向平面为主要参考、辅助平面提供互补空间信息的不对称解读策略。

Method: 提出轴向中心跨平面注意力架构：使用大规模轴向CT图像预训练的MedDINOv3作为冻结特征提取器提取三平面特征；通过RICA块和平面内Transformer编码器捕获平面特定的位置与上下文信息；设计轴向中心跨平面Transformer编码器，使轴向表示有条件地选择辅助平面（冠状、矢状）的互补表示进行融合。

Result: 在MedMNIST3D六个数据集上，该方法在ACC和AUC指标上一致优于现有3D和多平面模型。轻量变体AC-Tiny以明显更少的可训练参数取得竞争性能。消融实验验证了轴向中心查询、QKV分配、定向跨平面融合、无残差交叉注意力及分类头设计的重要性。切片级Grad-CAM可视化显示模型能跨平面识别诊断相关区域。

Conclusion: 将架构设计与临床解读工作流对齐（轴向中心的不对称平面间依赖建模）对鲁棒的3D医学图像分析具有重要价值，表明架构设计对性能提升的贡献大于单纯增加模型规模。

Abstract: Abridged: Clinicians commonly interpret 3D medical images by examining multiple anatomical planes rather than relying on volumetric views. In clinical CT workflows, the axial plane often serves as the primary diagnostic reference, while the auxiliary planes provide complementary spatial context. However, many existing 3D deep learning approaches either process volumetric data holistically or assign equal importance to all planes, failing to reflect this asymmetric, axial-centric interpretation strategy.
