To address this, we propose an axial-centric cross-plane attention architecture for 3D medical image classification that models asymmetric dependencies between anatomical planes. The architecture employs large-scale axial CT images pretrained MedDINOv3 as a frozen feature extractor for axial, coronal, and sagittal planes. RICA blocks and intra-plane transformer encoders capture plane-specific positional and contextual information, while axial-centric cross-plane transformer encoders selectively condition axial representations on complementary auxiliary representations. Experiments on six datasets from the MedMNIST3D benchmark show that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing 3D and multi-plane models in ACC and AUC. A lightweight variant, AC-Tiny, achieves competitive performance with substantially fewer trainable parameters, suggesting that architectural design contributes more to performance gains than increased model scale. Ablation studies further validate the importance of axial-centric querying, QKV allocation, directional cross-plane fusion, residual-free cross-attention, and classification head design. Slice-level Grad-CAM visualizations demonstrate that the model identifies diagnostically relevant regions across all planes. These findings highlight the value of aligning architectural design with clinical interpretation workflows for robust 3D medical image analysis.

</details>


### [111] [LDP-Slicing: Local Differential Privacy for Images via Randomized Bit-Plane Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03711)
*Yuanming Cao,Chengqi Li,Wenbo He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LDP-Slicing框架，通过将像素值分解为二进制位平面，使本地差分隐私（LDP）能够高效应用于高维图像数据，在满足严格的像素级ε-LDP同时，保留了图像在下游任务中的高可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为LDP不适用于图像数据，因为像素空间的高维性导致严重的效用退化。本文试图证明这种效用损失并非LDP固有缺陷，而是源于将LDP应用于不恰当的数据表示方式。

Method: 提出LDP-Slicing框架，核心是将像素值分解为二进制位平面序列，将LDP机制直接应用于位级表示。此外集成感知混淆模块（减少人类可感知的信息泄露）和基于优化的隐私预算分配策略。该框架无需训练，计算开销小。

Result: 在人脸识别和图像分类任务上，LDP-Slicing在同等隐私预算下优于现有的DP/LDP基线方法，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LDP对图像数据的效用损失并非本质问题，通过合适的数据表示（位平面分解）可以有效解决这一领域不匹配问题，使LDP在图像隐私保护中变得实用。

Abstract: Local Differential Privacy (LDP) is the gold standard trust model for privacy-preserving machine learning by guaranteeing privacy at the data source. However, its application to image data has long been considered impractical due to the high dimensionality of pixel space. Canonical LDP mechanisms are designed for low-dimensional data, resulting in severe utility degradation when applied to high-dimensional pixel spaces. This paper demonstrates that this utility loss is not inherent to LDP, but from its application to an inappropriate data representation. We introduce LDP-Slicing, a lightweight, training-free framework that resolves this domain mismatch. Our key insight is to decompose pixel values into a sequence of binary bit-planes. This transformation allows us to apply the LDP mechanism directly to the bit-level representation. To further strengthen privacy and preserve utility, we integrate a perceptual obfuscation module that mitigates human-perceivable leakage and an optimization-based privacy budget allocation strategy. This pipeline satisfies rigorous pixel-level $\varepsilon$-LDP while producing images that retain high utility for downstream tasks. Extensive experiments on face recognition and image classification demonstrate that LDP-Slicing outperforms existing DP/LDP baselines under comparable privacy budgets, with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [112] [GeoSolver: Scaling Test-Time Reasoning in Remote Sensing with Fine-Grained Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09551)
*Lang Sun,Ronghao Fu,Zhuoran Duan,Haoran Liu,Xueyan Liu,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoSolver 是一个面向遥感推理的框架，通过构建大规模过程监督数据集 Geo-PRM-2M（采用熵引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索和视觉幻觉注入），训练令牌级过程奖励模型 GeoPRM，并结合 Process-Aware Tree-GRPO 强化学习算法，实现了可验证的逐步推理。GeoSolver-9B 在多个遥感基准上达到 SOTA，且 GeoPRM 作为通用地理空间验证器，可有效扩展测试时计算并增强通用 VLM 的跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型（VLM）在遥感解译中取得显著进展，但其在复杂分步推理方面仍面临挑战。现有的链式思维（CoT）推理方法难以保证中间步骤的视觉忠实性（visual faithfulness），这是当前遥感推理的关键瓶颈。

Method: GeoSolver 提出三阶段方法：(1) 构建 Geo-PRM-2M 数据集，通过熵引导的 MCTS 和视觉幻觉注入生成令牌级过程监督数据；(2) 训练 GeoPRM，一个令牌级过程奖励模型，提供细粒度忠实性反馈；(3) 提出 Process-Aware Tree-GRPO 强化学习算法，结合树结构探索与忠实性加权奖励机制，实现中间步骤的精确信用分配。

Result: GeoSolver-9B 在多个遥感基准上取得最优性能。GeoPRM 实现了鲁棒的测试时缩放（TTS），作为通用地理空间验证器，不仅能缩放 GeoSolver-9B 的性能，还能直接增强通用 VLM，展现出显著的跨模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过过程监督强化学习转向可验证的遥感推理是可行的，令牌级过程奖励模型和忠实性加权的强化学习算法能有效解决视觉忠实性问题，且训练得到的验证器具有良好的跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly advanced remote sensing interpretation, enabling them to perform complex, step-by-step reasoning remains highly challenging. Recent efforts to introduce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to this domain have shown promise, yet ensuring the visual faithfulness of these intermediate steps remains a critical bottleneck. To address this, we introduce GeoSolver, a novel framework that transitions remote sensing reasoning toward verifiable, process-supervised reinforcement learning. We first construct Geo-PRM-2M, a large-scale, token-level process supervision dataset synthesized via entropy-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and targeted visual hallucination injection. Building upon this dataset, we train GeoPRM, a token-level process reward model (PRM) that provides granular faithfulness feedback. To effectively leverage these verification signals, we propose Process-Aware Tree-GRPO, a reinforcement learning algorithm that integrates tree-structured exploration with a faithfulness-weighted reward mechanism to precisely assign credit to intermediate steps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our resulting model, GeoSolver-9B, achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse remote sensing benchmarks. Crucially, GeoPRM unlocks robust Test-Time Scaling (TTS). Serving as a universal geospatial verifier, it seamlessly scales the performance of GeoSolver-9B and directly enhances general-purpose VLMs, highlighting its remarkable cross-model generalization.

</details>


### [113] [LR-SGS: Robust LiDAR-Reflectance-Guided Salient Gaussian Splatting for Self-Driving Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12647)
*ZY Chen,F Zhu,H Zhu,DY Kong,XK Kuang,YJ Zhang,CM Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LiDAR反射率引导的显著性高斯溅射方法(LR-SGS)，利用激光雷达的几何和反射率特征初始化显著性高斯表示，并引入反射率作为光照不变材质通道与RGB联合对齐，在Waymo数据集上以更少高斯和更短训练时间实现了更优的重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在自动驾驶场景中未充分利用点云中的丰富信息（如反射率）以及LiDAR与RGB的互补性，导致在高自运动、复杂光照等挑战场景下重建质量退化。

Method: 提出LR-SGS方法：1) 从LiDAR提取几何和反射率特征点，通过显著性变换和密度控制初始化结构感知的显著性高斯表示；2) 将LiDAR强度校准为反射率，作为光照不变的材质通道附加到每个高斯上，与RGB联合对齐以强制边界一致性。

Result: 在Waymo开放数据集上，LR-SGS以更少的高斯数和更短的训练时间实现了更优的重建性能，在复杂光照场景下PSNR超越OmniRe 1.18 dB。

Conclusion: 充分利用LiDAR反射率信息与RGB的互补性，通过显著性高斯表示和光照不变材质通道，有效提升了自动驾驶场景下3DGS的重建鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods have demonstrated the feasibility of self-driving scene reconstruction and novel view synthesis. However, most existing methods either rely solely on cameras or use LiDAR only for Gaussian initialization or depth supervision, while the rich scene information contained in point clouds, such as reflectance, and the complementarity between LiDAR and RGB have not been fully exploited, leading to degradation in challenging self-driving scenes, such as those with high ego-motion and complex lighting. To address these issues, we propose a robust and efficient LiDAR-reflectance-guided Salient Gaussian Splatting method (LR-SGS) for self-driving scenes, which introduces a structure-aware Salient Gaussian representation, initialized from geometric and reflectance feature points extracted from LiDAR and refined through a salient transform and improved density control to capture edge and planar structures. Furthermore, we calibrate LiDAR intensity into reflectance and attach it to each Gaussian as a lighting-invariant material channel, jointly aligned with RGB to enforce boundary consistency. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset demonstrate that LR-SGS achieves superior reconstruction performance with fewer Gaussians and shorter training time. In particular, on Complex Lighting scenes, our method surpasses OmniRe by 1.18 dB PSNR.

</details>


### [114] [Demystifying Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16870)
*Ruisi Wang,Zhongang Cai,Fanyi Pu,Junxiang Xu,Wanqi Yin,Maijunxian Wang,Ran Ji,Chenyang Gu,Bo Li,Ziqi Huang,Hokin Deng,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散视频模型的推理能力主要来源于去噪步骤（Chain-of-Steps），而非先前认为的帧序列（Chain-of-Frames），并识别出工作记忆、自我修正、感知先行等涌现行为，最后提出一种无需训练的策略来提升推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战先前将视频模型推理归因于Chain-of-Frames（帧间顺序推理）的主流观点，揭示其真正的推理机制。

Method: 通过定性分析和定向探针实验，研究扩散去噪过程中的推理行为；并在扩散步骤内分析DiT各层的功能专业化分工。

Result: 推理主要发生在扩散去噪步骤中（早期探索多个候选解，逐步收敛）；发现工作记忆、自我修正与增强、感知先行等涌现行为；扩散步骤内早期层编码感知结构，中间层执行推理，后期层整合潜在表征。

Conclusion: 提出一种无需训练的推理增强策略（集成不同随机种子的潜在轨迹），为利用视频模型固有的推理动力学作为新的智能基座提供系统性理解。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have revealed an unexpected phenomenon: diffusion-based video models exhibit non-trivial reasoning capabilities. Prior work attributes this to a Chain-of-Frames (CoF) mechanism, where reasoning is assumed to unfold sequentially across video frames. In this work, we challenge this assumption and uncover a fundamentally different mechanism. We show that reasoning in video models instead primarily emerges along the diffusion denoising steps. Through qualitative analysis and targeted probing experiments, we find that models explore multiple candidate solutions in early denoising steps and progressively converge to a final answer, a process we term Chain-of-Steps (CoS). Beyond this core mechanism, we identify several emergent reasoning behaviors critical to model performance: (1) working memory, enabling persistent reference; (2) self-correction and enhancement, allowing recovery from incorrect intermediate solutions; and (3) perception before action, where early steps establish semantic grounding and later steps perform structured manipulation. During a diffusion step, we further uncover self-evolved functional specialization within Diffusion Transformers, where early layers encode dense perceptual structure, middle layers execute reasoning, and later layers consolidate latent representations. Motivated by these insights, we present a simple training-free strategy as a proof-of-concept, demonstrating how reasoning can be improved by ensembling latent trajectories from identical models with different random seeds. Overall, our work provides a systematic understanding of how reasoning emerges in video generation models, offering a foundation to guide future research in better exploiting the inherent reasoning dynamics of video models as a new substrate for intelligence.

</details>


### [115] [To See or To Please: Uncovering Visual Sycophancy and Split Beliefs in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18373)
*Rui Hong,Shuxue Quan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三层诊断框架，发现72.9%的VLM样本存在"视觉谄媚"现象——内部视觉证据保留但输出幻觉回答，且在模型缩放中该问题加剧，而传统对齐训练未能解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型视觉语言模型(VLM)在正确回答时是否真正依赖视觉信息尚不明确，现有评估方法难以区分感知、依赖和对齐三种失败模式。

Method: 提出三层诊断框架(Tri-Layer Diagnostic Framework)，包含三个样本级指标：潜在异常检测(Latent Anomaly Detection)、视觉必要性分数(Visual Necessity Score)和竞争分数(Competition Score)，通过反事实盲化、噪声和冲突三种干预方式，在9个VLM和9000个模型-样本对上进行分析。

Result: 72.9%的样本表现出"视觉谄媚"(Visual Sycophancy)现象，即内部证据保存但解码出幻觉回答；零样本表现出"稳健拒绝"(Robust Refusal)；在Qwen-VL系列中，模型缩放单调减少语言捷径但加剧视觉谄媚；诊断分数可用于无训练的选择性预测策略，在50%覆盖率下准确率提升最高达+9.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 当前对齐训练仅消除了拒绝作为解码输出，但未能解决视觉谄媚这一接地问题；模型规模和更先进的后训练技术单独无法解决接地问题；提出的诊断框架可用于无需训练的预测改进。

Abstract: When VLMs answer correctly, do they genuinely rely on visual information? We introduce a Tri-Layer Diagnostic Framework with three per-sample metrics: Latent Anomaly Detection, Visual Necessity Score, and Competition Score, which disentangle perception, dependency, and alignment failures. Across 9 VLMs and 9,000 model-sample pairs under counterfactual blind, noise, and conflict interventions, 72.9% of samples exhibit Visual Sycophancy, a Split Beliefs pattern in which internal evidence is preserved yet a hallucinated answer is decoded, while zero samples show Robust Refusal, indicating that current alignment training has eliminated refusal as a decoding outcome. Scaling within the Qwen-VL family, both within- and across-generation, monotonically reduces Language Shortcuts but amplifies Visual Sycophancy, showing that scale and newer post-training alone cannot resolve the grounding problem. Diagnostic scores further enable a training-free selective-prediction strategy yielding up to +9.5 percentage points accuracy at 50% coverage.

</details>


### [116] [DirectFisheye-GS: Enabling Native Fisheye Input in Gaussian Splatting with Cross-View Joint Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00648)
*Zhengxian Yang,Fei Xie,Xutao Xue,Rui Zhang,Taicheng Huang,Yang Liu,Mengqi Ji,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DirectFisheye-GS，将鱼眼相机模型直接集成到3DGS框架中，无需图像去畸变预处理，并引入特征重叠驱动的跨视图联合优化策略，解决边缘浮点伪影问题，在公开数据集上达到或超越SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对鱼眼图像先进行去畸变预处理，导致黑边信息丢失和重采样引起的细节密度稀释，进而使3DGS过拟合低频区域产生模糊和浮点伪影。本文旨在消除预处理步骤，实现鱼眼图像原生输入训练。

Method: 1) 将鱼眼相机模型直接集成到3DGS框架中，实现原生输入；2) 提出特征重叠驱动的跨视图联合优化策略，建立高斯在不同视图间一致的几何与光度约束，解决边缘畸变导致的形状极端化问题。

Result: 在公开数据集上匹配或超越了现有最先进方法的性能，有效消除了边缘浮点伪影，提升了重建质量。

Conclusion: 直接集成鱼眼模型优于预处理去畸变方案，跨视图联合优化策略不仅适用于鱼眼场景，也可推广至现有针孔相机管道。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has enabled efficient 3D scene reconstruction from everyday images with real-time, high-fidelity rendering, greatly advancing VR/AR applications. Fisheye cameras, with their wider field of view (FOV), promise high-quality reconstructions from fewer inputs and have recently attracted much attention. However, since 3DGS relies on rasterization, most subsequent works involving fisheye camera inputs first undistort images before training, which introduces two problems: 1) Black borders at image edges cause information loss and negate the fisheye's large FOV advantage; 2) Undistortion's stretch-and-interpolate resampling spreads each pixel's value over a larger area, diluting detail density -- causes 3DGS overfitting these low-frequency zones, producing blur and floating artifacts. In this work, we integrate fisheye camera model into the original 3DGS framework, enabling native fisheye image input for training without preprocessing. Despite correct modeling, we observed that the reconstructed scenes still exhibit floaters at image edges: Distortion increases toward the periphery, and 3DGS's original per-iteration random-selecting-view optimization ignores the cross-view correlations of a Gaussian, leading to extreme shapes (e.g., oversized or elongated) that degrade reconstruction quality. To address this, we introduce a feature-overlap-driven cross-view joint optimization strategy that establishes consistent geometric and photometric constraints across views-a technique equally applicable to existing pinhole-camera-based pipelines. Our DirectFisheye-GS matches or surpasses state-of-the-art performance on public datasets. Project Page: https://yzxqh.github.io/DirectFisheye-GS/ .

</details>


### [117] [SenBen: Sensitive Scene Graphs for Explainable Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08819)
*Fatih Cagatay Akyon,Alptekin Temizel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SenBen，首个面向敏感内容的大规模场景图基准（含13,999帧电影画面、场景图标注和16个敏感标签），并蒸馏了一个241M参数的紧凑学生模型，通过多任务训练策略（后缀对象标识、VAR损失、解耦的Q2L标签头）在敏感内容场景图生成任务上显著超越现有模型，同时推理速度提升7.6倍、显存需求降低16倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核系统仅将图像分类为安全/不安全，缺乏空间定位和可解释性，无法解释检测到何种敏感行为、涉及谁或发生在何处。因此需要构建一个带有空间语义理解的敏感内容基准和高效模型。

Method: 1) 构建SenBen基准：从157部电影中提取13,999帧，用Visual Genome风格场景图（25个对象类、28个属性、14个谓词）和16个敏感标签（5大类）进行标注；2) 将前沿VLM蒸馏为241M学生模型；3) 采用多任务训练策略：后缀对象身份标识解决词汇不平衡、Vocabulary-Aware Recall (VAR) Loss、解耦的Query2Label标签头+非对称损失。

Result: 学生模型在SenBen Recall上比标准交叉熵训练提升+6.4个百分点；在接地场景图指标上超越除Gemini外的所有VLM和所有商业安全API；在所有模型中取得最高目标检测和字幕生成分数；推理速度提升7.6倍，GPU显存需求降低16倍。

Conclusion: 通过场景图结构可以赋予内容审核系统空间可解释性，而紧凑的学生模型能够在保持高性能的同时大幅提升推理效率，为敏感内容审核的实际部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Content moderation systems classify images as safe or unsafe but lack spatial grounding and interpretability: they cannot explain what sensitive behavior was detected, who is involved, or where it occurs. We introduce the Sensitive Benchmark (SenBen), the first large-scale scene graph benchmark for sensitive content, comprising 13,999 frames from 157 movies annotated with Visual Genome-style scene graphs (25 object classes, 28 attributes including affective states such as pain, fear, aggression, and distress, 14 predicates) and 16 sensitivity tags across 5 categories. We distill a frontier VLM into a compact 241M student model using a multi-task recipe that addresses vocabulary imbalance in autoregressive scene graph generation through suffix-based object identity, Vocabulary-Aware Recall (VAR) Loss, and a decoupled Query2Label tag head with asymmetric loss, yielding a +6.4 percentage point improvement in SenBen Recall over standard cross-entropy training. On grounded scene graph metrics, our student model outperforms all evaluated VLMs except Gemini models and all commercial safety APIs, while achieving the highest object detection and captioning scores across all models, at $7.6\times$ faster inference and $16\times$ less GPU memory.

</details>


### [118] [Mining Attribute Subspaces for Efficient Fine-tuning of 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10095)
*Yu Jiang,Hanwen Jiang,Ahmed Abdelkader,Wen-Sheng Chu,Brandon Y. Feng,Zhangyang Wang,Qixing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方法，通过合成数据集生成受控变化的LoRA子空间，发现这些子空间近似解缠（正交），并整合成精简LoRA子空间，从而提升下游任务微调的效率和预测精度，且能泛化到真实数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究3D基础模型微调中LoRA子空间是否与纹理、几何、相机运动、光照等变化类型相关联，以及这些子空间是否解缠，并寻找有效计算方法。

Method: 通过合成数据集生成受控变化，分别微调LoRA适配器，提取每种变化类型的LoRA子空间，验证其近似解缠性，并整合为精简LoRA子空间。

Result: 各变化类型的LoRA子空间近似正交（解缠），整合后的精简子空间在合成数据和真实数据集上均能提升下游任务微调效率和预测精度。

Conclusion: LoRA子空间与不同变化类型相关联且近似解缠，基于合成数据提取的精简子空间可泛化至真实场景，为3D基础模型高效微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: With the emergence of 3D foundation models, there is growing interest in fine-tuning them for downstream tasks, where LoRA is the dominant fine-tuning paradigm. As 3D datasets exhibit distinct variations in texture, geometry, camera motion, and lighting, there are interesting fundamental questions: 1) Are there LoRA subspaces associated with each type of variation? 2) Are these subspaces disentangled (i.e., orthogonal to each other)? 3) How do we compute them effectively? This paper provides answers to all these questions. We introduce a robust approach that generates synthetic datasets with controlled variations, fine-tunes a LoRA adapter on each dataset, and extracts a LoRA sub-space associated with each type of variation. We show that these subspaces are approximately disentangled. Integrating them leads to a reduced LoRA subspace that enables efficient LoRA fine-tuning with improved prediction accuracy for downstream tasks. In particular, we show that such a reduced LoRA subspace, despite being derived entirely from synthetic data, generalizes to real datasets. An ablation study validates the effectiveness of the choices in our approach.

</details>


### [119] [Degradation-Consistent Paired Training for Robust AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10102)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou,Xiaokun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出退化一致性配对训练（DCPT），通过特征一致性损失和预测一致性损失显式增强AI生成图像检测器对JPEG压缩、高斯模糊等真实世界图像退化的鲁棒性，在Synthbuster基准上退化条件下平均准确率提升9.1个百分点，且不增加参数和推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在真实世界图像退化（如JPEG压缩、高斯模糊、分辨率降低）下性能显著下降，而SOTA方法（如B-Free）仅将退化鲁棒性视为数据增强的副产品，未将其作为显式训练目标。

Method: 提出退化一致性配对训练（DCPT）：对每张训练图像构造干净视图和退化视图，施加两种约束——特征一致性损失（最小化干净与退化表征的余弦距离）和预测一致性损失（基于对称KL散度对齐输出分布）。无需额外参数和推理开销。

Result: 在Synthbuster基准（9个生成器、8种退化条件）上，DCPT将退化条件下平均准确率提升9.1个百分点，干净准确率仅下降0.9%。JPEG压缩下提升最显著（+15.7%至+17.9%）。消融实验表明添加架构组件会导致过拟合。

Conclusion: 训练目标的改进比架构增强对退化鲁棒性更有效，DCPT以零额外成本实现了显著的鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors suffer significant performance degradation under real-world image corruptions such as JPEG compression, Gaussian blur, and resolution downsampling. We observe that state-of-the-art methods, including B-Free, treat degradation robustness as a byproduct of data augmentation rather than an explicit training objective. In this work, we propose Degradation-Consistent Paired Training (DCPT), a simple yet effective training strategy that explicitly enforces robustness through paired consistency constraints. For each training image, we construct a clean view and a degraded view, then impose two constraints: a feature consistency loss that minimizes the cosine distance between clean and degraded representations, and a prediction consistency loss based on symmetric KL divergence that aligns output distributions across views. DCPT adds zero additional parameters and zero inference overhead. Experiments on the Synthbuster benchmark (9 generators, 8 degradation conditions) demonstrate that DCPT improves the degraded-condition average accuracy by 9.1 percentage points compared to an identical baseline without paired training, while sacrificing only 0.9% clean accuracy. The improvement is most pronounced under JPEG compression (+15.7% to +17.9%). Ablation further reveals that adding architectural components leads to overfitting on limited training data, confirming that training objective improvement is more effective than architectural augmentation for degradation robustness.

</details>


### [120] [Radar-Camera BEV Multi-Task Learning with Cross-Task Attention Bridge for Joint 3D Detection and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12918)
*Ahmet İnanç,Özgür Erkent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CTAB是一个跨任务注意力桥接模块，通过在共享BEV空间中使用多尺度可变形注意力，实现检测和分割分支间的双向特征交换，从而提升自动驾驶中雷达-相机融合的3D感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-相机融合方法将检测和分割任务孤立处理，缺乏跨任务特征共享：检测中的目标级几何线索有助于分割，而分割中的密集道路布局上下文有助于检测。

Method: 提出CTAB模块，通过多尺度可变形注意力在共享BEV空间中双向交换检测和分割分支的特征；结合基于实例归一化的分割解码器和可学习BEV上采样以提供更精细的BEV表示。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，CTAB在7类分割任务上相比联合多任务基线有提升，且检测性能基本不变；在4类子集（可行驶区域、人行横道、人行道、车辆）上达到51.0 mIoU-4，同时保持有竞争力的3D检测性能。

Conclusion: 跨任务注意力桥接能够有效促进检测与分割间的特征共享，在提升分割精度的同时不损害检测性能，验证了多任务协同学习的价值。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations are the dominant paradigm for 3D perception in autonomous driving, providing a unified spatial canvas where detection and segmentation features are geometrically registered to the same physical coordinate system. However, existing radar-camera fusion methods treat these tasks in isolation, missing the opportunity for cross-task feature sharing: object-level geometric cues from detection can sharpen segmentation, while dense road-layout context from segmentation can anchor detection. We propose \textbf{CTAB} (Cross-Task Attention Bridge), a bidirectional module that exchanges features between detection and segmentation branches via multi-scale deformable attention in shared BEV space. CTAB is integrated into a multi-task framework with an Instance Normalization-based segmentation decoder and learnable BEV upsampling to provide a more detailed BEV representation. On nuScenes, CTAB improves segmentation on 7 classes over the joint multi-task baseline at essentially neutral detection. On a 4-class subset (drivable area, pedestrian crossing, walkway, vehicle), our joint multi-task model achieves 51.0 mIoU-4 while simultaneously providing competitive 3D detection.

</details>


### [121] [FiRe: Fine-grained Multimodal Reasoning for Enhanced Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13491)
*Yongjin Kim,Yoonjin Oh,Yerin Kim,Hyomin Kim,Jeeyoung Yun,Yujung Heo,Minjun Kim,Sungwoong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FiRe是一种基于多步细粒度推理的文本到图像生成方法，通过将提示分解为关键视觉需求、自判断生成图像的满足度并生成精确反馈进行局部优化，同时引入FiRe-GRPO强化学习方法实现步骤级奖励分配，显著提升了组合性文本到图像生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一MLLM虽然具备自我反思和精炼的推理能力，但在文本到图像生成中未被充分利用；而现有的多模态推理方法仅依赖于提示增强或整体图像-文本对齐判断，缺乏对详细提示属性的细粒度反思和精炼，导致细粒度控制能力有限。

Method: FiRe首先将提示分解为关键视觉需求，然后自判断生成图像中这些需求的满足程度，最后根据自生成的精确反馈进行局部优化。此外，提出FiRe-GRPO，将推理过程建模为步骤级决策问题，设计步骤特定奖励并计算步骤级优势进行细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在组合性文本到图像生成基准上，FiRe持续优于包括现有基于推理的方法在内的竞争性基线，取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: FiRe通过细粒度多步推理与步骤级强化学习相结合，有效提升了MLLM在文本到图像生成中的细粒度控制能力，验证了多步自我反思和精炼在图像生成中的有效性。

Abstract: With the rapid progress of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), unified MLLMs that jointly perform image understanding and generation have advanced significantly. However, despite the inherent reasoning capabilities of unified MLLMs for self-reflection and self-refinement, their use in text-to-image generation remains largely underexplored. Meanwhile, existing multimodal reasoning-based image generation methods mostly rely on prompt augmentation or holistic image-text alignment judgments, without fine-grained reflection and refinement of detailed prompt attributes, leading to limited fine-grained control. To address this limitation, we propose FiRe, a Fine-grained Multimodal Reasoning method for enhanced image generation by MLLM. In specific, FiRe performs a fine-grained multi-step reasoning by first decomposing the prompt into key visual requirements and then self-judging their satisfaction in the generated image, followed by localized refinement according to self-generated precise feedback. In addition, to further strengthen the MLLM's multimodal reasoning ability, we introduce FiRe-GRPO, a reinforcement learning method tailored to FiRe. Since standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) suffers from sparse, outcome-based rewards in multi-step reasoning, we formulate our reasoning process as a step-level decision-making problem, design step-specific rewards, and compute step-level advantages for granular credit assignment within GRPO. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FiRe consistently outperforms competitive text-to-image baselines, including existing reasoning-based methods, with particularly substantial gains on compositional text-to-image benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [DETR-ViP: Detection Transformer with Robust Discriminative Visual Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14684)
*Bo Qian,Dahu Shi,Xing Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DETR-ViP框架，通过全局提示集成和视觉-文本提示关系蒸馏解决视觉提示检测中缺乏全局判别性的问题，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉提示检测性能不佳的根本原因是视觉提示缺乏全局判别性，且该方向长期被忽视，仅作为文本提示检测器的副产品存在。

Method: 在基础图像-文本对比学习上，提出DETR-ViP框架，包含：1) 全局提示集成；2) 视觉-文本提示关系蒸馏；3) 选择性融合策略。

Result: 在COCO、LVIS、ODinW和Roboflow100数据集上，DETR-ViP在视觉提示检测任务中显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过消融实验和分析验证了各改进模块的有效性，揭示了视觉提示检测能力增强的潜在原因。

Abstract: Visual prompted object detection enables interactive and flexible definition of target categories, thereby facilitating open-vocabulary detection. Since visual prompts are derived directly from image features, they often outperform text prompts in recognizing rare categories. Nevertheless, research on visual prompted detection has been largely overlooked, and it is typically treated as a byproduct of training text prompted detectors, which hinders its development. To fully unlock the potential of visual-prompted detection, we investigate the reasons why its performance is suboptimal and reveal that the underlying issue lies in the absence of global discriminability in visual prompts. Motivated by these observations, we propose DETR-ViP, a robust object detection framework that yields class-distinguishable visual prompts. On top of basic image-text contrastive learning, DETR-ViP incorporates global prompt integration and visual-textual prompt relation distillation to learn more discriminative prompt representations. In addition, DETR-ViP employs a selective fusion strategy that ensures stable and robust detection. Extensive experiments on COCO, LVIS, ODinW, and Roboflow100 demonstrate that DETR-ViP achieves substantially higher performance in visual prompt detection compared to other state-of-the-art counterparts. A series of ablation studies and analyses further validate the effectiveness of the proposed improvements and shed light on the underlying reasons for the enhanced detection capability of visual prompts.

</details>


### [123] [World-R1: Reinforcing 3D Constraints for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24764)
*Weijie Wang,Xiaoxuan He,Youping Gu,Yifan Yang,Zeyu Zhang,Yefei He,Yanbo Ding,Xirui Hu,Donny Y. Chen,Zhiyuan He,Yuqing Yang,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World-R1提出通过强化学习(Flow-GRPO)将视频生成模型与3D约束对齐，在不修改底层架构的前提下，利用预训练3D基础模型和视觉语言模型的反馈增强几何一致性，并采用周期性解耦训练策略平衡刚体几何一致性与动态场景流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型虽能生成高质量视觉内容，但常出现几何不一致问题；已有方法通过修改架构注入3D先验，但计算成本高且可扩展性受限。因此，本文旨在以轻量级方式提升视频生成的3D几何一致性。

Method: 1) 引入专用于世界模拟的纯文本数据集；2) 采用Flow-GRPO（基于流的强化学习优化算法）；3) 利用预训练3D基础模型和视觉语言模型的反馈信号进行优化；4) 提出周期性解耦训练策略，在刚体几何一致性与动态场景流畅性之间取得平衡。

Result: 该方法显著提升了3D一致性，同时保持了原始基础模型的视觉质量，有效弥合了视频生成与可扩展世界模拟之间的差距。

Conclusion: World-R1通过强化学习方式实现了视频生成与3D约束的对齐，无需修改底层架构，为构建具有几何一致性的可扩展世界模拟器提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Recent video foundation models demonstrate impressive visual synthesis but frequently suffer from geometric inconsistencies. While existing methods attempt to inject 3D priors via architectural modifications, they often incur high computational costs and limit scalability. We propose World-R1, a framework that aligns video generation with 3D constraints through reinforcement learning. To facilitate this alignment, we introduce a specialized pure text dataset tailored for world simulation. Utilizing Flow-GRPO, we optimize the model using feedback from pre-trained 3D foundation models and vision-language models to enforce structural coherence without altering the underlying architecture. We further employ a periodic decoupled training strategy to balance rigid geometric consistency with dynamic scene fluidity. Extensive evaluations reveal that our approach significantly enhances 3D consistency while preserving the original visual quality of the foundation model, effectively bridging the gap between video generation and scalable world simulation.

</details>


### [124] [Decoding Scientific Experimental Images: The SPUR Benchmark for Perception, Understanding, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27604)
*Junpeng Ding,Zichen Tang,Haihong E,Mengyuan Ji,Yang Liu,Haolin Tian,Haiyang Sun,Pengqi Sun,Yang Xu,Yichen Liu,Haocheng Gao,Zijie Xi,Ruomeng Jiang,Peizhi Zhao,Rongjin Li,Yuanze Li,Jiacheng Liu,Zhongjun Yang,Jintong Chen,Siying Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPUR是一个包含4,264个QA对、来自1,084张专家筛选图像的科学实验图像基准，从面板级细粒度感知、跨面板关系理解和专家级推理三个层面评估多模态大模型，发现当前模型远未达到科学图像解读的专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验图像解读需要专家级的能力，而现有基准缺乏对MLLMs在细粒度视觉感知、跨面板关系理解和专家级推理方面的全面评估，亟需一个能够对标专家水平的基准来揭示AI for Science研究中的关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建SPUR基准，包含1,084张专家筛选图像和4,264个QA对；设计三个创新维度：面板级细粒度感知（数值、形态、信息定位六种细粒度面板类型）、跨面板关系理解（平均每样本14.3个面板）、专家级推理（涵盖五种实验范式的定性与定量推理）；对20种MLLM和四种多模态思维链方法进行综合评估。

Result: 当前所有20种MLLM和四种MCoT方法在SPUR基准上表现显著不足，远未达到科学图像解读的专家水平要求。

Conclusion: 科学实验图像理解是AI for Science研究中的关键瓶颈，现有MLLMs在该任务上能力严重不足，需要进一步的研究突破。

Abstract: We introduce SPUR, a comprehensive benchmark for scientific experimental image perception, understanding, and reasoning, comprising 4,264 question-answering (QA) pairs derived from 1,084 expert-curated images. SPUR features three key innovations: (1) Panel-Level Fine-Grained Perception: evaluating the visual perception of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) across three dimensions (numerical, morphological, and information localization) on six fine-grained panel types; (2) Cross-Panel Relation Understanding: utilizing complex images with an average of 14.3 panels per sample to evaluate MLLMs' ability to decipher intricate cross-panel relations; (3) Expert-Level Reasoning: assessment of qualitative and quantitative reasoning across five experimental paradigms to determine if models can infer conclusions from evidence as human experts do. Comprehensive evaluation of 20 MLLMs and four multimodal Chain-of-Thought (MCoT) methods reveals that current models fall significantly short of the expert-level requirements for scientific image interpretation, underscoring a critical bottleneck in AI for Science (AI4S) research.

</details>


### [125] [MultiSense-Pneumo: A Multimodal Learning Framework for Pneumonia Screening in Resource-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02207)
*Dineth Jayakody,Pasindu Thenahandi,Chameli Dommanige*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSense-Pneumo，一个用于肺炎筛查和分诊的多模态研究原型，整合结构化症状描述、咳嗽音频、语音和胸部X光片，通过可解释的后期融合算子生成统一筛查估计，但未作为临床验证系统部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺炎仍是全球发病率和死亡率的主要原因，尤其在资源匮乏地区，临床评估依赖异质性证据（症状、呼吸模式、语言描述、影像），而现有计算方法多为单模态且主要关注X光片，缺乏多模态整合方案。

Method: 系统包括确定性症状分诊、LightGBM声学分类、基于ResNet-18的域对抗X光片分析、Transformer语音识别，以及可解释的后期融合算子。每个模态转化为标准化关注信号，通过人工指定的启发式融合权重聚合为统一筛查估计。

Result: X光通路在合成域偏移下表现出较强的组件级性能；咳嗽声学的异常类别召回率存在显著局限性；缺乏配对的端到端多模态患者评估。

Conclusion: MultiSense-Pneumo作为筛查和分诊研究的框架和组件级原型，尚未达到临床验证或部署标准，但展示了多模态整合的可行性。

Abstract: Pneumonia remains a leading global cause of morbidity and mortality, particularly in low-resource settings where access to imaging, laboratory testing, and specialist care is limited. Clinical assessment relies on heterogeneous evidence, including symptoms, respiratory patterns, spoken descriptions, and chest imaging, making frontline screening inherently multimodal. However, many existing computational approaches remain unimodal and focus primarily on radiographs. In this work, we present MultiSense-Pneumo, a multimodal research prototype for pneumonia-oriented screening and triage support that integrates structured symptom descriptors, cough audio, spoken language, and chest radiographs. The system combines deterministic symptom triage, LightGBM-based acoustic classification, domain-adversarial radiograph analysis using ResNet-18, transformer-based speech recognition, and an interpretable late-fusion operator. Each modality is transformed into a normalized concern signal and aggregated into a unified screening estimate. The fusion weights are hand-specified and are treated as heuristic, interpretable parameters rather than learned or clinically optimized values. MultiSense-Pneumo is implemented with offline execution in mind on standard laptop-class hardware, but it is not presented as a deployment-validated or clinically validated diagnostic system. Experimental results demonstrate strong component-level performance of the radiograph pathway under synthetic domain shifts, while also highlighting important limitations, especially reduced abnormal-class recall for cough acoustics and the absence of paired end-to-end multimodal patient evaluation. MultiSense-Pneumo is therefore intended as a framework and component-level prototype for screening and triage research.

</details>


### [126] [UniPCB: A Generation-Assisted Detection Framework for PCB Defect Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04635)
*Huan Zhang,Lianghong Tan,Yichu Xu,Zishan Su,Jiangzhong Cao,Huanqi Wu,Linwei Zhu,Xu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对工业物联网中PCB缺陷检测面临的数据稀缺、样本不平衡及复杂背景下特征表示不足的挑战，提出UniPCB框架，通过多模态条件扩散生成模型合成缺陷样本以增强数据集，并设计倒置残差移位注意力与跨级互补融合模块提升检测性能，在DsPCBSD+上检测mAP@0.5达98.0%，生成FID达129.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网PCB缺陷检测中存在两大难题：1) 缺陷样本稀缺且类别不平衡，限制模型训练；2) 复杂电路背景下特征表示不足。现有生成方法仅依赖单模态条件且结构控制粗糙，检测方法虽改进架构但未解决数据瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出UniPCB框架，包含生成与检测双分支：1) 生成端：多模态条件生成器并行提取边缘、深度和文本条件，ScaleEncoder将条件嵌入扩散U-Net的四层分辨率，Condition Modulation采用FiLM风格的空间自适应调制实现结构对齐的缺陷样本合成；2) 检测端：倒置残差移位注意力（结合自注意力与移位卷积）捕捉全局上下文与局部纹理，跨级互补融合模块通过像素级门控实现选择性跨层特征融合。合成样本直接扩充检测训练集，实现生成与检测的协同增益。

Result: 在DsPCBSD+数据集上，检测任务mAP@0.5达98.0%，mAP@0.5:0.95达61.8%，超越所有对比方法；生成任务FID为129.61，SSIM为0.619，优于现有条件生成方法。

Conclusion: UniPCB通过生成-检测协同框架有效解决了IIoT场景下PCB缺陷检测的数据瓶颈与特征表示问题，生成质量的提升直接转化为检测性能的改善，验证了生成辅助检测范式的有效性。

Abstract: In the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT), enabling intelligent, real-time Printed Circuit Board (PCB) defect inspection is critical for ensuring product reliability. However, existing IIoT-based visual inspection systems face two compounding challenges: scarce and imbalanced defect samples that limit model training, and insufficient feature representation under complex circuit backgrounds. Existing generation methods rely on single-modality conditions with coarse structural control, while detection methods improve architectures without addressing the data bottleneck. To resolve both challenges jointly, we propose a generation-assisted PCB defect inspection framework that integrates controlled defect synthesis with task-specific defect detection within an IIoT-enabled pipeline. On the generation side, a Multi-modal Condition Generator extracts complementary edge, depth, and text conditions in parallel. A ScaleEncoder then embeds these conditions into the diffusion U-Net at four resolutions, and a Condition Modulation applies FiLM-style spatially-adaptive modulation at each scale, enabling structurally aligned and defect-aware sample synthesis to augment the scarce IIoT dataset. On the detection side, an Inverted Residual Shift Attention couples self-attention with shift-wise convolution to jointly capture global context and local texture, and a Cross-level Complementary Fusion Block generates pixel-level gates for selective cross-level feature fusion. The synthesized samples directly enrich the detection training set, so that improvements in generation compound with improvements in detection. Extensive experiments on DsPCBSD+ demonstrate that UniPCB achieves mAP@0.5 of 98.0% and mAP@0.5:0.95 of 61.8% on defect detection, surpassing all compared methods, while the generation branch attains an FID of 129.61 and SSIM of 0.619, outperforming existing conditional generation approaches.

</details>


### [127] [ScriptHOI: Learning Scripted State Transitions for Open-Vocabulary Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05057)
*Minh Anh Nguyen,Quang Huy Tran,Bao Ngoc Le,SuiYang Guang,Tuan Kiet Pham,Linh Chi Vo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScriptHOI提出一种结构化脚本框架，将交互短语分解为身体角色、接触、几何、 affordance、运动和物体状态等多个槽位，通过视觉状态分词器、槽位匹配器、区间部分标签学习和反事实脚本对比损失，解决开放词汇HOI检测中物体 affordance 主导和短语共现导致的错误预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇HOI检测器在预测时往往被物体 affordance 和短语级共现主导，导致模型仅根据刀和蛋糕的存在就预测"切蛋糕"，而未验证手、工具、目标、接触模式和物体状态是否共同支持该动作。需要一种能够分解交互短语并验证各要素一致性的方法。

Method: 1) 将交互短语分解为6个语义槽位（身体角色、接触、几何、affordance、运动、物体状态）；2) 视觉状态分词器将检测到的人-物对解析为对应的状态标记；3) 槽位匹配器估计脚本覆盖度和脚本冲突度以校准HOI logits；4) 区间部分标签学习为未标注候选提供基于脚本的上下概率边界约束；5) 反事实脚本对比损失通过交换单个脚本槽位来抑制仅依赖物体的捷径。

Result: 在HICO-DET、V-COCO和开放词汇HOI分割上，ScriptHOI提升了罕见和未见交互类别的识别性能，同时显著减少了 affordance 冲突的假阳性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化脚本分解和多重约束机制，ScriptHOI有效解决了开放词汇HOI检测中物体 affordance 主导问题，验证了细粒度语义分解和部分标签学习在HOI任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary human-object interaction (HOI) detection requires recognizing interaction phrases that may not appear as annotated categories during training. Recent vision-language HOI detectors improve semantic transfer by matching human-object features with text embeddings, but their predictions are often dominated by object affordance and phrase-level co-occurrence. As a result, a model may predict \textit{cut cake} from the presence of a knife and a cake without verifying whether the hand, tool, target, contact pattern, and object state jointly support the action. We propose \textbf{ScriptHOI}, a structured framework that represents each interaction phrase as a soft scripted state transition. Rather than treating a phrase as a single class token, ScriptHOI decomposes it into body-role, contact, geometry, affordance, motion, and object-state slots. A visual state tokenizer parses each detected human-object pair into corresponding state tokens, and a slot-wise matcher estimates both script coverage and script conflict. These two quantities calibrate HOI logits, expose missing visual evidence, and provide training constraints for incomplete annotations. To avoid suppressing valid but unannotated interactions, we further introduce interval partial-label learning, which constrains unannotated candidates with script-derived lower and upper probability bounds instead of assigning closed-world negatives. A counterfactual script contrast loss swaps individual script slots to discourage object-only shortcuts. Experiments on HICO-DET, V-COCO, and open-vocabulary HOI splits show that ScriptHOI improves rare and unseen interaction recognition while substantially reducing affordance-conflict false positives.

</details>


### [128] [D-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continuously Tuning Step-Distilled Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05204)
*Dengyang Jiang,Xin Jin,Dongyang Liu,Zanyi Wang,Mingzhe Zheng,Ruoyi Du,Xiangpeng Yang,Qilong Wu,Zhen Li,Peng Gao,Harry Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D-OPSD训练范式，通过上下文自蒸馏实现少步扩散模型的有策略监督微调，在不损失原始少步推理能力的前提下学习新概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高性能图像生成模型正从多步模型转向少步模型（如Z-Image-Turbo和FLUX.2-klein），但这些模型难以直接进行连续监督微调，因为常用微调技术会破坏其固有的少步推理能力。

Method: 提出D-OPSD范式：利用现代扩散模型中LLM/VLM编码器的上下文学习能力，将有策略学习建模为自蒸馏过程。训练时模型同时充当教师和学生（不同上下文），学生仅以文本特征为条件，教师以文本提示和目标图像的多模态特征为条件，在学生自身采样轨迹上最小化两个预测分布的差异。

Result: D-OPSD使模型能够在自身轨迹上优化并在自身监督下学习新概念、风格等，且不牺牲原始的少步生成能力。

Conclusion: D-OPSD为少步扩散模型提供了一种有效的有策略监督微调方法，解决了现有技术无法在微调中保持少步推理能力的核心问题。

Abstract: The landscape of high-performance image generation models is currently shifting from the inefficient multi-step ones to the efficient few-step counterparts (e.g, Z-Image-Turbo and FLUX.2-klein). However, these models present significant challenges for direct continuous supervised fine-tuning. For example, applying the commonly used fine-tuning technique would compromise their inherent few-step inference capability. To address this, we propose D-OPSD, a novel training paradigm for step-distilled diffusion models that enables on-policy learning during supervised fine-tuning. We first find that the modern diffusion models, where the LLM/VLM serves as the encoder, can inherit its encoder's in-context capabilities. This enables us to formulate the training as an on-policy self-distillation process. Specifically, during training, we make the model act as both the teacher and the student with different contexts, where the student is conditioned only on the text feature, while the teacher is conditioned on the multimodal feature of both the text prompt and the target image. Training minimizes the two predicted distributions over the student's own roll-outs. By optimizing on the model's own trajectory and under its own supervision, D-OPSD enables the model to learn new concepts, styles, etc., without sacrificing the original few-step capacity.

</details>


### [129] [VT-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for Visual-Tabular Multi-Modal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08146)
*Zi-Yi Jia,Zi-Jian Cheng,Xin-Yue Zhang,Kun-Yang Yu,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VT-Bench是首个统一的多模态视觉-表格数据基准，涵盖14个数据集、9个领域及75.6万样本，评估了23个代表性模型，旨在推动视觉-表格基础模型研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-表格数据在医疗、工业等高风险领域至关重要，但现有研究不足，缺乏统一基准来标准化该领域的评估，以推动模型发展。

Method: 构建了VT-Bench基准，聚合14个数据集（覆盖医疗、宠物、媒体、交通等9个领域，超过75.6万样本），包含判别性预测和生成性推理任务；评估了23个模型（包括单模态专家、专用视觉-表格模型、通用视觉语言模型及工具增强方法）。

Result: 实验揭示了现有模型在视觉-表格学习任务上面临显著挑战，各类代表性模型均存在不足。

Conclusion: VT-Bench为社区提供了统一的评估标准，有望推动更强大的多模态视觉-表格基础模型的发展。

Abstract: Multi-model learning has attracted great attention in visual-text tasks. However, visual-tabular data, which plays a pivotal role in high-stakes domains like healthcare and industry, remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce \textit{VT-Bench}, the first unified benchmark for standardizing vision-tabular discriminative prediction and generative reasoning tasks. VT-Bench aggregates 14 datasets across 9 domains (medical-centric, while covering pets, media, and transportation) with over 756K samples. We evaluate 23 representative models, including unimodal experts, specialized visual-tabular models, general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs), and tool-augmented methods, highlighting substantial challenges of visual-tabular learning. We believe VT-Bench will stimulate the community to build more powerful multi-modal vision-tabular foundation models.
  Benchmark: https://github.com/Ziyi-Jia990/VT-Bench

</details>


### [130] [Hide to See: Reasoning-prefix Masking for Visual-anchored Thinking in VLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11651)
*Seonghoon Yu,Dongjun Nam,Byung-Kwan Lee,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的"思考-回答"蒸馏框架，通过对学生模型显著推理前缀进行掩码，迫使模型在蒸馏过程中更多依赖视觉证据，从而缓解长推理轨迹中的视觉遗忘问题，在多项多模态推理基准上优于现有开源VLM和蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型VLM的思考-回答推理虽能提升性能，但计算成本高昂。将这种能力蒸馏到轻量级VLM时，面临长推理轨迹中模型逐渐遗忘视觉信息的问题，因此需要一种能增强学生模型在推理过程中视觉证据利用能力的蒸馏方法。

Method: 1) 令牌级显著推理前缀掩码：针对每个下一个令牌预测任务，选择性掩码高影响力的推理前缀；2) 自步进掩码预算调度：根据教师-学生分布差异（蒸馏难度）逐步增加掩码规模。在蒸馏阶段，用显著推理前缀掩码替代标准因果掩码，同时阻断未来令牌和显著推理线索。

Result: 在多模态推理基准上，该方法优于近期开源VLM、VLM蒸馏和自蒸馏方法，进一步分析证实了学生模型在思考过程中视觉利用能力的增强。

Conclusion: 通过掩码显著推理前缀迫使模型依赖视觉证据，是一种有效的VLM蒸馏策略，能显著提升紧凑型思考-回答VLM的推理性能并缓解视觉遗忘问题。

Abstract: Recent think-answer approaches in VLMs, such as Qwen3-VL-Thinking, boost reasoning performance by leveraging intermediate thinking steps before the final answer, but their computational cost becomes substantial, especially for larger VLMs. To distill such capabilities into compact think-answer VLMs, a primary objective is to improve the student's ability to utilize visual evidence throughout its reasoning trace, as long think-answer traces suffer from visual forgetting issues. To this end, we introduce a novel think-answer distillation framework that encourages the student to anchor its thinking on visual information by masking the student's salient reasoning prefixes. To compensate for such masked textual cues, the student is encouraged to rely more on visual evidence as an alternative source of information during distillation. Our masking strategies include: 1) token-wise salient reasoning-prefix masking, which masks high-influence reasoning prefixes selectively for each next-token prediction, and 2) self-paced masking budget scheduling, which gradually increases the masking scale according to distillation difficulty, measured by the discrepancy between teacher--student distributions. In the distillation phase, the student is guided by our salient reasoning-prefix mask, which blocks both future tokens and salient reasoning cues, in place of the standard causal mask used for auto-regressive language modeling. Experimental results show that our approach outperforms recent open-source VLMs, VLM distillation, and self-distillation methods on multimodal reasoning benchmarks, while further analyzes confirm enhanced visual utilization along the student thinking process.

</details>


### [131] [When Brains Disagree: Biological Ambiguity Underlies the Challenge of Amyloid PET Synthesis from Structural MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11867)
*Louise E. G. Baron,Ross Callaghan,David M. Cash,Philip S. J. Weston,Hojjat Azadbakht,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验证明，MRI到淀粉样蛋白PET合成任务性能受限的根本原因在于生物学上的模糊性（MRI反映神经退行性变，PET测量淀粉样蛋白病理，两者在时间上脱耦），而非模型架构复杂度不足；引入血浆生物标志物等多模态信息可有效解决该模糊性，提升合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI-to-淀粉样蛋白PET合成模型在不同研究中表现差异大，且增加模型复杂度未能带来一致性提升，研究者怀疑这是由于MRI与PET测量不同的病理过程（神经退行性变 vs. 淀粉样蛋白病理）之间存在时间脱耦，导致相似的MRI模式可能对应不同的淀粉样蛋白状态，形成内在的一对多模糊映射。本研究旨在通过科学实验验证这一假设。

Method: 设计两项对照实验：(1) 通过按淀粉样蛋白和神经退行性变状态对配对的MRI-PET数据进行分层，控制训练分布。在受控设计下使用两种标准合成模型，比较明确映射与模糊映射下的性能差异；(2) 引入血浆生物标志物作为正交生物学信息，检验多模态输入能否解决模糊性。

Result: 生物上明确的映射在隔离条件下是可学习的，但引入数据模糊性后性能显著下降，证实模糊性而非架构容量是性能受限的原因；引入血浆生物标志物等多模态信息后，性能提升且稳定性恢复。

Conclusion: MRI-to-淀粉样蛋白PET合成中有限且不一致的性能是由内在生物学模糊性导致的，实现稳定且有意义的进展需要多模态信息整合，而非增加模型架构复杂度。

Abstract: Structural MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis has been proposed as a non-invasive alternative for amyloid assessment in Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, reported performance of identical models varies widely across studies, and increasingly complex architectures have not led to consistent gains. This inconsistency is thought to be caused by a fundamental biological ambiguity: MRI captures neurodegeneration, while PET measures amyloid pathology - two processes that are often temporally decoupled in AD. As a result, similar MRI patterns may correspond to different amyloid states, creating ambiguous one-to-many mappings. MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis may therefore be intrinsically ill-posed; however, this idea has yet to be tested scientifically. The aim of this work is to test this hypothesis through two controlled experiments. We first control the training distribution by stratifying paired MRI-PET data by amyloid and neurodegeneration status. Using two standard synthesis models under a controlled design, we show that biologically unambiguous mappings are learnable in isolation, but performance collapses when data ambiguity is introduced. This demonstrates that ambiguity in the data distribution, rather than architectural capacity, constrains performance. Second, we show that introducing orthogonal biological information in the form of plasma biomarkers resolves this ambiguity. When multimodal inputs are incorporated, performance improves and stability is restored. Together, these findings suggest that limited and inconsistent performance in MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis is explained by intrinsic biological ambiguity, and that stable, meaningful progress requires multimodal integration rather than architectural complexity.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Text Prompts: Visual-to-Visual Generation as A Unified Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12271)
*Yaofang Liu,Kangning Cui,Meng Chu,Zhaoqing Li,Suiyun Zhang,Jean-Michel Morel,Xiaodong Cun,Haoxuan Che,Rui Liu,Raymond H. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉到视觉（V2V）生成范式，通过V2V-Zero框架利用已有VLM的隐藏状态替代文本条件，实现无需训练的视觉条件图像生成，在GenEval上达到0.85，并构建Simple-V2V Bench评估七个视觉条件任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成器依赖文本提示作为输入，但人类常通过视觉制品（排版、草图、参考图等）进行创作，文本序列化过程会压缩空间结构、精确外观和字形等信号，形成表达瓶颈。

Method: V2V-Zero：训练无关框架，通过提取冻结VLM的最终层隐藏状态（来自视觉页面）替换纯文本条件，利用VLM已将文本和图像映射到同一条件空间的性质，实现视觉到视觉的条件生成。

Result: 在GenEval上V2V-Zero达到0.85（Qwen-Image骨干），匹配优化后的文生图性能；Simple-V2V Bench上得32.7/100，优于开源图像基线；HunyuanVideo-1.5视频扩展得20.2/100；机制分析显示95.0%条件token注意力集中在视觉页面隐藏状态。

Conclusion: V2V范式有效突破了文本条件瓶颈，属性绑定能力强，但内容生成不可靠且结构控制仍困难；视觉路由主导了推理路径，该接口可迁移至视频生成。

Abstract: Humans often specify and create through visual artifacts: typography sheets, sketches, reference images, and annotated scenes. Yet modern visual generators still ask users to serialize this intent into text, a bottleneck that compresses signals like spatial structure, exact appearance, and glyph shape. We propose \textbf{\emph{visual-to-visual} (V2V)} generation, in which the user conditions a generative model with a visual specification page rather than a text prompt. The page is not an edit target, but a visual document that specifies the desired output. We introduce \textbf{V2V-Zero}, a training-free framework that exposes this interface in existing vision-language model (VLM) conditioned generators by replacing text-only conditioning with final-layer hidden states extracted from visual pages, exploiting the fact that the frozen VLM already maps both text and images into the generator's conditioning space.
  On GenEval, V2V-Zero reaches 0.85 with a frozen Qwen-Image backbone, closely matching its optimized text-to-image performance without fine-tuning. To evaluate the broader V2V space, we introduce \textbf{Simple-V2V Bench}, spanning seven visual-conditioning tasks and seven models, including GPT Image 2, Nano Banana 2, Seedream 5.0 Lite, open-weight baselines, and a video extension. V2V-Zero scores 32.7/100, outperforming evaluated open-weight image baselines and revealing a clear capability hierarchy: attribute binding is strong, content generation is unreliable, and structural control remains hard even for commercial systems. A HunyuanVideo-1.5 extension scores 20.2/100, showing the interface transfers beyond images. Mechanistic analysis shows the default reasoning path is primarily visually routed, with 95.0\% of conditioning-token attention mass on visual-page hidden states.

</details>


### [133] [Bayesian In Vivo Tracking of Synapses using Joint Poisson Deconvolution and Diffeomorphic Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13455)
*Shashwat Kumar,Dominic M. Padova,Binish Narang,Gabrielle I. Coste,Austin R. Graves,Richard L. Huganir,Adam S. Charles,Michael I. Miller,Anuj Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模板的贝叶斯框架，将突触建模为在非线性组织变形下运动的变亮度点源，同时实现突触检测、图像去噪去卷积、荧光强度推断、组织运动配准和参数置信区间估计，并在2D+t模拟数据和两周内小鼠3D+t活体显微数据上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活体双光子显微镜成像存在低信噪比、高散粒噪声、非线性组织运动、荧光非平稳波动以及点扩散函数引起的模糊等问题，使得在高突触密度区域检测和跟踪突触非常困难，亟需一种能够同时处理这些挑战的统一框架。

Method: 采用统一贝叶斯方法，构建了一个包含微分同胚映射（用于域扭曲）、高斯点扩散函数（用于成像过程）和泊松观测模型（用于原始光子计数）的后验概率模型，将突触建模为在非线性组织变形下运动的变亮度点源，实现五项任务的联合求解。

Result: 在2D+t模拟数据集和两周内小鼠3D+t活体荧光突触显微数据集上验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯模板框架能够同时解决低SNR活体显微成像中突触检测与跟踪的多重挑战，为研究学习记忆过程中的大规模突触动态以及相关神经系统疾病中的异常提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Synapses are densely packed submicron structures that dynamically reorganize during learning and memory formation. Longitudinal \textit{in vivo} imaging of fluorescently tagged synaptic receptors offers a promising opportunity to study large-scale synaptic dynamics and how these processes are disrupted in neurological disease. However, in vivo imaging with 2-photon microscopy uses low laser power and therefore suffers from low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and high shot noise, nonlinear tissue motion between days, nonstationary fluctuations in synaptic fluorescence, and significant blur induced by the microscope point spread function (PSF). Together, these factors make it challenging to detect and track synapses, especially in regions with high synaptic density. This paper presents a novel template-based framework for modeling synapses as varying luminance point sources that move under a nonlinear tissue deformation. Taking a unified Bayesian approach, we apply this model to microscopy data by deriving a posterior that incorporates a diffeomorphic mapping for domain warping, a Gaussian point spread function for the imaging process, and a Poisson observation model for raw photon counts. The Bayesian solution simultaneously: (1) Constructs a probabilistic template of synapse locations, (2) denoises and deconvolves the image data, (3) infers fluorescence intensities, (4) performs diffeomorphic image registration to correct for tissue motion, and (5) provides confidence regions for these parameter estimates. We demonstrate the framework on both a 2D+t simulated dataset and a 3D+t longitudinal \textit{in vivo} microscopy dataset of fluorescent synapses imaged in a mouse over two weeks.

</details>


### [134] [MiVE: Multiscale Vision-language features for reference-guided video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14664)
*Tong Wang,Meng Zou,Chengjing Wu,Xiaochao Qu,Luoqi Liu,Xiaolin Hu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MiVE通过利用VLM的多尺度分层特征（浅层空间细节+深层语义理解），统一集成到自注意力扩散Transformer中，解决了参考引导视频编辑中跨模态对齐和细节丢失问题，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考引导视频编辑方法存在两种范式局限：解耦编码器存在模态间隙，统一视觉-语言编码器依赖最终层表示而丢失细粒度空间细节。作者旨在利用VLM分层编码特性来克服这些问题。

Method: 提出MiVE框架，将VLM（Qwen3-VL）重新用作多尺度特征提取器，提取从浅层空间细节到深层全局语义的分层特征，并集成到统一的自注意力扩散Transformer中，替代传统交叉注意力设计以消除模态不匹配。

Result: 在人类偏好评估中排名最高，优于学术方法和商业系统，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 利用VLM多尺度层次化特征可以有效弥合模态间隙并保留空间细节，为参考引导视频编辑提供更优方案。

Abstract: Reference-guided video editing takes a source video, a text instruction, and a reference image as inputs, requiring the model to faithfully apply the instructed edits while preserving original motion and unedited content. Existing methods fall into two paradigms, each with inherent limitations: decoupled encoders suffer from modality gaps when processing instructions and visual content independently, while unified vision-language encoders lose fine-grained spatial details by relying solely on final-layer representations. We observe that VLM layers encode complementary information hierarchically -- early layers capture localized spatial details essential for precise editing, while deeper layers encode global semantics for instruction comprehension. Building on this insight, we present MiVE (Multiscale Vision-language features for reference-guided video Editing), a framework that repurposes VLMs as multiscale feature extractors. MiVE extracts hierarchical features from Qwen3-VL and integrates them into a unified self-attention Diffusion Transformer, eliminating the modality mismatch inherent in cross-attention designs. Experiments demonstrate that MiVE achieves state-of-the-art performance by ranking highest in human preference, outperforming both academic methods and commercial systems.

</details>


### [135] [EgoExo-WM: Unlocking Exo Video for Ego World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15477)
*Danny Tran,Roberto Martín-Martín,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过从外心视频中提取结构化身体姿态并利用人类运动学先验将其转换为自我中心视频，从而利用野外外心数据训练自我中心世界模型，显著提升了预测质量和下游规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心世界模型受限于自我中心训练数据的稀缺性及其对人体物理动作的部分可观测性，而外心视频虽丰富且能清晰呈现身体姿态，但缺乏与智能体动作空间的直接对齐且非自我中心视角，因此需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 从外心视频中提取结构化身体姿态作为动作表示，基于人类运动学先验将外心视频转换为自我中心视频，从而将野外外心数据整合到自我中心世界模型的训练中。

Result: 使用转换后的数据训练全身动作条件化的自我中心世界模型，显著提升了预测质量和下游规划性能，能够推断出实现视觉目标状态所需的身体姿态序列。

Conclusion: 该方法为利用任意野外视频构建强大的自我中心世界模型铺平了道路，可进一步应用于机器人规划和增强现实导航等领域。

Abstract: Egocentric world models present a promising direction for enabling agents to predict and plan, but their performance is constrained by the limited availability of egocentric training data and its inherent partial observability of humans' physical actions. In contrast, exocentric video is abundant and reveals body poses well, but lacks direct alignment with an agent's action space -- and is not egocentric. We propose a method to bridge this gap by extracting structured body pose from exocentric video as a representation of action and transforming the exocentric video to egocentric video, informed by a human kinematics prior. This process unlocks the integration of in-the-wild exocentric data for egocentric world model training. We show that training whole-body action-conditioned egocentric world models with our converted data significantly improves both prediction quality and downstream planning performance, where we infer the sequence of body poses needed to achieve a visual goal state. Our approach paves the way to enlist arbitrary in-the-wild videos for building powerful egocentric world models, furthering applications in robot planning and augmented-reality guidance.

</details>


### [136] [Xiaomi Auto World Model: A Joint World Model Integrating Reconstruction and Generation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18137)
*Lijun Zhou,Hongcheng Luo,Zhenxin Zhu,Cheng Chi,Mingfei Tu,Kaixin Xiong,Lei Gong,Zhanqian Wu,Zehan Zhang,Fangzhen Li,Hao Li,Yingying Shen,Jiale He,Haohui Zhu,Shan Zhao,Kai Wang,Zhiwei Zhan,Yuechuan Pu,Kaiyuan Tan,Ruiling Yang,Xianqi Wang,Tianyi Yan,Jiawei Zhou,Lei Zhang,Jingyang Zhao,Xi Zhou,Chitian Sun,Chenming Wu,Jiong Deng,Hongwei Xie,Ming Lu,Kun Ma,Long Chen,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Bing Wang,Haiyang Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一技术系统JWM，包含WorldRec（基于稀疏场景查询的前馈重建架构用于世界表示）和WorldGen（双向预训练+因果微调的三阶段框架用于世界生成），两者深度融合实现自动驾驶闭环仿真与数据合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶世界模型需要同时具备世界表示和世界生成两大核心能力，现有方法分别处理两者导致不一致性和性能瓶颈，亟需统一框架实现协同优化。

Method: WorldRec：在3D空间中初始化结构化查询，通过跨视图、跨时间特征聚合实现空间一致性，生成紧凑高保真3D高斯场景表示。WorldGen：两阶段训练框架（双向预训练+三阶段因果微调：Teacher Forcing、ODE蒸馏、DMD），仅需4步去噪即可实现高质量在线因果视频生成。JWM：深度融合WorldRec和WorldGen实现协同增益。

Result: JWM在生成稳定性、跨帧一致性和视觉保真度上实现协同增益，支持仅4步去噪的高质量在线因果视频生成，为闭环仿真、数据合成和端到端训练提供基础。

Conclusion: 提出的统一技术系统有效解决了自动驾驶世界模型的两大核心能力，通过深度集成表示与生成模块实现了超越单独模块的性能提升，为自动驾驶仿真和训练奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: This report presents a unified technical system addressing the two core capabilities of world models for autonomous driving: world representation and world generation. For world representation, we propose WorldRec, a feed-forward reconstruction architecture driven by sparse scene queries. WorldRec initializes structured queries in 3D space, leveraging them to aggregate cross-view, cross-temporal features, thereby naturally enforcing spatial consistency across frames and yielding compact yet high-fidelity 3D Gaussian scene representations. For world generation, we propose WorldGen, a two-stage training framework of bidirectional pretraining followed by causal fine-tuning through three progressive stages (Teacher Forcing, ODE distillation, and DMD), enabling high-quality online causal video generation in as few as 4 denoising steps. Building on both modules, we further introduce the JWM, which deeply integrates WorldRec and WorldGen to achieve synergistic gains in generation stability, cross-frame consistency, and visual fidelity, providing a solid foundation for closed-loop simulation, data synthesis, and end-to-end training in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [137] [Mind Your Margin and Boundary: Are Your Distilled Datasets Truly Robust?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20606)
*Muquan Li,Yingyi Ma,Yihong Huang,Hang Gou,Ke Qin,Ming Li,Yuan-Fang Li,Tao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C²R 是一个结合攻击感知课程学习与对比鲁棒性目标的框架，通过鲁棒边际视角推导扰动分数并采用类平衡对比损失，在多个数据集和六种攻击下实现了最优鲁棒精度，平均超越先前鲁棒DD方法2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法仅优化干净准确率而忽略鲁棒性；已有的鲁棒DD方法对所有对抗扰动统一处理，未区分近零鲁棒边际的主导影响，且未显式增强决策边界的类间分离性，导致精度-鲁棒性权衡不佳。

Method: 从鲁棒边际视角推导扰动分数近似每个样本的鲁棒铰链损失，据此设计攻击感知课程，优先训练最小边际的对抗样本；同时引入类平衡对比鲁棒性损失，在强制对抗不变性的同时显式扩大类间边界分离。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、Tiny-ImageNet及多个ImageNet-1K子集上，针对六种攻击的评测中，C²R取得最优鲁棒准确率，平均超越先前鲁棒DD方法2.8%。

Conclusion: C²R通过精细化处理鲁棒边际的样本差异并增强类间分离，有效改善了数据集蒸馏中的精度-鲁棒性权衡，为鲁棒蒸馏提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) compresses a large training set into a small synthetic set for efficient training, but most DD methods optimize only clean accuracy and leave robustness uncontrolled. Recent robust DD methods improve robustness, yet they often suffer from a poor accuracy-robustness trade-off because they (i) treat all adversarially perturbed examples uniformly, despite robust risk being dominated by near-zero robust margins, and (ii) do not explicitly increase inter-class separation in the decision boundary where attacks concentrate. We present Contrastive Curriculum for Robust Dataset Distillation (C$^2$R), a framework that couples an attack-aware curriculum with a contrastive robustness objective. From a robust-margin perspective, we derive a perturbation score that approximates each sample's robust hinge, enabling a curriculum that prioritizes the smallest-margin adversaries that most directly drive robust error. In parallel, a class-balanced contrastive robustness loss enforces adversarial invariance while explicitly widening boundary separation across classes. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100, Tiny-ImageNet, and multiple ImageNet-1K subsets under six attacks show that C$^2$R achieves the best robust accuracy, outperforming prior robust DD by $2.8$% on average.

</details>


### [138] [Broken Memories: Detecting and Mitigating Memorization in Diffusion Models with Degraded Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22050)
*Yuanmin Huang,Mi Zhang,Chen Chen,Feifei Li,Geng Hong,Xiaoyu You,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次发现扩散模型中的记忆化会导致内部数值不稳定性（表现为"破碎"伪影），借鉴数值方法中的稳定性分析提出基于潜变量更新范数的经验稳定区域，并构建了逐步骤检测与自适应缓解框架，在不改变提示或引导条件下有效抑制记忆化，在Stable Diffusion 1.4上实现AUC>0.999的检测性能和0.0%的记忆率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成高质量图像时存在记忆训练数据的问题，带来隐私和版权风险。现有方法缺乏对记忆化内在机理的理解，亟需一种不牺牲生成质量且不依赖提示修改的检测与缓解方案。

Method: (1) 发现记忆化导致生成过程中的数值不稳定性，表现为视觉"破碎"伪影；(2) 借鉴数值方法中的稳定性分析，引入基于潜变量更新范数的经验稳定区域来量化稳定行为；(3) 提出逐步骤检测与自适应缓解的即插即用框架，在不修改提示或引导条件下进行干预。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion 1.4上，检测AUC>0.999，缓解后记忆率为0.0%，额外开销极小（每张图像约0.01秒）。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效检测并缓解扩散模型的记忆化问题，同时保持语义保真度和图像质量，为隐私保护和版权合规提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel at generating high-quality images, their tendency to memorize training data poses significant privacy and copyright risks. In this work, we for the first time identify that memorization induces internal numerical instability, often manifesting as visually ``broken'' artifacts. Inspired by stability analysis in numerical methods, we introduce empirical stability regions based on latent update norms to quantitatively characterize stable behavior during generation. Leveraging this, we propose a principled, on-the-fly framework for step-wise detection and adaptive mitigation. Our approach suppresses memorization without altering prompts or guidance, thereby preserving semantic fidelity and image quality. Extensive experiments on Stable Diffusion 1.4 demonstrate that our method achieves an AUC $>0.999$ detection performance and a $0.0\%$ memorization rate after mitigation with negligible overhead ($\approx0.01$s per image).

</details>


### [139] [The Neglected Baseline in Model Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22417)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有模型解释方法普遍忽略基线(baseline)的问题，重新阐述了模型解释任务和解释原则，统一了梯度法、积分梯度法和泰勒展开并明确各方法的基线，分析了IG、LayerCAM、ODAM、Difference Map等方法的缺陷，提出通过归因误差替代有缺陷的评估方法，并开发了一种具有清晰合理基线的模型解释方法，支持基于任意层特征的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型解释方法普遍忽略基线的重要性，导致解释结果不精确甚至错误。作者旨在通过重新审视基线的作用，统一相关方法并纠正现有方法的缺陷。

Method: 1) 重新阐述模型解释任务和解释原则，论证基线的重要性；2) 统一梯度法、IG方法和泰勒展开，明确各方法的基线；3) 分析IG、LayerCAM、ODAM、Difference Map等方法的缺陷；4) 提出基于归因误差的评估方法替代基于边际效应或完美模型假设的评估方式；5) 修正IG方法，开发具有清晰合理基线的新解释方法，支持任意层特征解释。

Result: 提出的方法具有明确合理的基线，在归因准确性上优于现有方法，且支持基于不同层的特征进行解释，不同层的结果反映了不同特征提取阶段的特征提取程度差异。

Conclusion: 基线在模型解释中至关重要，忽略基线会导致解释偏差。基于归因误差的评估方法更精确可靠。所提方法在不同层级上的解释结果均合理，且能反映特征提取的阶段性差异。

Abstract: We observe that existing model interpretation methods generally ignore the baseline, and such neglect often results in imprecise or even incorrect interpretation. In this paper, we reformulate the task of model interpretation and the interpretation principles for model interpretation results to demonstrate the importance of the baseline. We further unify gradient-based methods, Integrated Gradients (IG) methods, and Taylor expansion, clarifying the connections among them and explicitly identifying the baseline for each method. On this basis, we analyze the flaws and errors in related model interpretation methods (IG, LayerCAM, ODAM, Difference Map). We advocate evaluating the quality of model interpretation results precisely through the attribution error between the attribution result and the attribution target, rather than adopting flawed evaluation methods, such as those based on marginal-effect or the assumption of perfect model performance. We revise IG and develope a model interpretation method with a clear and reasonable baseline, achieving better results. Our method supports model interpretation based on features from any layer. Interpretation based on features from different layers are all reasonable, and the differences among these results reflect varying degrees of feature extraction at different feature extraction stages.

</details>


### [140] [Suicide Risk Assessment from AI-powered Video Surveillance: An Interpretable Framework for Prevention in Metro Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22904)
*Safwen Naimi,Wassim Bouachir,Guillaume-Alexandre Bilodeau,Brian Mishara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了地铁站自杀风险评估（SRA）任务，并提出了一个可解释的框架，通过整合行人追踪、活动识别、站台语义分割和轨迹驱动的风险热图建模，在真实监控数据上实现了83.2%的ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地铁站中理解和监控人类行为对支持自杀预防工作至关重要，早期识别高风险情境可促进及时干预。然而，利用监控视频进行评估具有挑战性，需要准确感知人体运动、理解站台几何结构以及聚合异质行为线索。

Method: 提出可解释框架，整合行人追踪、活动识别、站台语义分割和轨迹驱动的风险热图建模，通过累积证据而非直接推断意图来评估自杀风险。

Result: 在真实监控数据上达到83.2%的ROC-AUC。

Conclusion: 通过将SRA形式化为独立任务并建立完整操作流程的基准，突显了自杀风险评估的复杂性，为面向社会公益的可解释AI系统研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Understanding and monitoring human behavior in metro stations play an important role in supporting suicide prevention efforts, where early identification of high-risk situations can enable timely intervention. This requires assessing suicide risk from a surveillance video by jointly reasoning about the behavior of each passenger, his/her spatial context, and temporal dynamics. However, this assessment using videos captured by surveillance cameras is challenging, as it demands accurate perception of human motion, understanding of platform geometry, and aggregation of heterogeneous behavioral cues over time. In this work, we formalize the task of Suicide Risk Assessment (SRA) in metro stations and introduce the first interpretable framework that addresses this challenge. Unlike approaches that focus on isolated subtasks or attempt to infer intent directly, our formulation assesses suicide risk from accumulated evidence by incorporating person tracking, activity recognition, semantic segmentation of the platform, and trajectory-driven risk heatmap modeling. By formalizing SRA as a distinct task and benchmarking a complete operational pipeline achieving 83.2% ROC-AUC on real surveillance data, this work highlights the complexity of suicide risk assessment and opens new directions for research on interpretable AI systems for social good.

</details>


### [141] [GFSR: Geometric Fidelity and Spatial Refinement for Reliable Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23327)
*Tiancheng Wang,Zhaolu Ding,Richeng Xu,Tianhui Zheng,Hui Liu,Hanyu Xuan,Zhiliang Wu,Guanghui Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GFSR框架，包含LaneIoU引导的置信度校准(LCC)和自适应门控位置细化(AGLR)，解决车道检测中分类置信度与几何质量弱相关、回归模块弱化采样点关联的问题，在CULane和CurveLanes上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车道检测方法存在两个局限：1) 分类置信度仅表征车道先验的类别存在性，与几何质量弱相关，基于此进行阈值过滤和NMS会保留高置信度但几何质量差的先验，剔除低置信度但几何质量好的先验；2) 回归模块弱化采样点间的相关性，阻碍了对远距离、高曲率、复杂拓扑车道的细粒度优化，导致欠拟合。

Method: 提出GFSR框架，包含两个核心模块：1) LCC (LaneIoU-guided Confidence Calibration)：以LaneIoU作为软监督显式估计车道先验的几何保真度，与分类置信度融合构建协同可靠性指数(CRI)指导先验过滤；2) AGLR (Adaptive Gated Location Refinement)：在每个细化阶段与回归头协作，预测采样点横向偏移并通过门控机制自适应调节校正幅度，增强点间相关性。

Result: 在CULane上F1_50和F1_75分别为81.46%和65.01%，在CurveLanes上F1_50达87.35%，均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: GFSR有效解决了分类置信度与几何质量脱节以及采样点关联弱化的问题，通过几何保真度感知的置信度校准和自适应门控位置细化，显著提升了复杂场景下车道检测的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Lane detection stands as a crucial perception task in autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. However, existing methods still degrade in complex real scenarios due to two major limitations. First, classification confidence only characterizes the categorical existence of lane priors and has no strong correlation with geometric quality. If threshold filtering and NMS are conducted merely based on this confidence, the model tends to retain lane priors with high confidence while eliminating those with lower confidence but superior geometric representation. Secondly, the regression modules in existing methods weaken correlations among sampling points, hindering fine-grained optimization of distant, high-curvature and complex-topology lanes and causing underfitting. To address these issues, we propose Geometric Fidelity and Spatial Refinement (GFSR), a framework consisting of LaneIoU-guided Confidence Calibration (LCC) and Adaptive Gated Location Refinement (AGLR). Specifically, LCC adopts LaneIoU as soft supervision to explicitly estimate the geometric fidelity of lane priors, which is further fused with classification confidence to construct the Collaborative Reliability Index (CRI). This index guides lane prior filtering, effectively retaining those with high classification confidence and favorable geometric quality. Meanwhile, cooperating with regression heads in each refinement stage, AGLR predicts sampling point lateral offsets and adopts a gating mechanism to adaptively regulate correction magnitude, strengthen inter-point correlations and boost model adaptability as well as robustness toward complex lane scenarios. Extensive experiments on CULane and CurveLanes demonstrate that our GFSR achieves state-of-the-art performance on CULane, with F1_50 and F1_75 scores of 81.46% and 65.01%, and reaches 87.35% F1_50 on CurveLanes.

</details>


### [142] [EgoProx: Evaluating MLLMs on Egocentric 3D Proximity Reasoning Across a Cognitive Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24456)
*Jinzhao Li,Yinuo Chen,Dongxu Piao,Panwang Pan,Yifan Yu,Dong Wang,Honglei Yan,Liang Yue,Shaofei Wang,Yixin Chen,Siyuan Huang,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoProx基准，用于评估多模态大模型在自我中心3D邻近性推理方面的能力，涵盖意图、探索、利用和行动链推理等认知环节。研究发现当前MLLMs虽具备一定空间知识，但在空间推理VQA上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在日常生活中不断进行3D邻近性推理（身体与周围物体的空间关系），但目前尚不清楚多模态大模型（MLLMs）是否具备这种具身3D推理能力。

Method: 设计EgoProx基准，构建认知链任务（意图、探索、利用、行动链推理）；开发基于智能体的数据引擎以生成多样化且一致的QA对；在多种主流MLLMs上进行评估，并进行数据集特定和任务特定的指令微调分析。

Result: 观察到较大的跨领域增益，表明当前MLLMs包含一定的空间知识；但模型仍然难以有效利用这些知识进行空间推理VQA。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs具备部分空间知识，但在自我中心3D邻近性推理任务中，从知识到推理的有效转化仍是关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Humans constantly reason about 3D proximity, the relations between their body and surrounding objects, to guide perception and action in daily life. Whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can perform such embodied 3D reasoning remains unclear. To this end, we introduce EgoProx, a benchmark for egocentric 3D proximity reasoning. We organize our tasks along a cognitive chain, covering intention, exploration, exploitation, and chain-of-actions reasoning. We also design an agent based data engine that produces diverse and consistent QA pairs at scale. We benchmark prevailing MLLMs on EgoProx and conduct additional analyses with dataset specific and task specific instruction tuning. We observe large cross-domain gains, indicating that current MLLMs contain some spatial knowledge; however, they still struggle to effectively leverage it for spatial reasoning VQA.

</details>


### [143] [Resolving Ambiguity in Composed Image Retrieval via Calibrated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24634)
*Amsisan Tran,Baogh Le,Tuan Kiet Pham,Sui Yang Guang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有组合图像检索（CIR）任务假设查询映射到单一目标，忽视了查询的模糊性（如"更正式一点"对应的是语料库中的一个区域）。作者将CIR重新定义为"不确定性下的校准意图解析"，引入共形预测层提供有覆盖保证的候选集，并通过期望信息增益策略提出最有效的澄清问题。在AmbiCIR基准上，该方法在单轮任务中达到SOTA，同时显著减少了交互次数，并首次报告了有效的覆盖率和校准指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIR方法都假设一个查询对应单一目标（用Recall@K评估），但实际中如"make it more formal"这类查询本质上是欠定的（underdetermined），导致了著名的假阴性问题（false-negative problem）。模型无法区分精确查询和模糊查询。

Method: 将CIR重新定义为"校准的意图解析"：1）检索器外包裹共形预测层，返回有覆盖保证的候选集，集合大小作为模糊度的量化指标；2）当集合较大时，基于期望信息增益策略从可解释的模糊轴中提出最有效的澄清问题；3）引入AmbiCIR基准（包含人类验证的用户模拟器，利用CIRR的辅助/对话标注和CIRCO的多正例设置）。

Result: 在开放域和时尚基准上，单轮性能匹配SOTA（确认对精确查询无代价）；在交互轮次上显著少于朴素对话基线；首次在CIR任务上报告有效的覆盖率和校准指标。

Conclusion: 该工作从根本上修正了CIR任务的定义——从单目标匹配转为不确定性下的意图解析，通过共形预测实现了可校准、可交互的检索范式，为处理查询歧义性提供了理论保证和实用方案。

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) searches a corpus with a reference image and a text describing how to modify it. Despite rapid progress from triplet-trained compositors to zero-shot and generative methods, essentially all systems share one assumption: that a query maps to a single target, scored by Recall@K against one annotation. We argue this is fundamentally at odds with the task. A query such as make it more formal does not name an image but a region of the corpus, and which member the user intends is genuinely underdetermined. This underspecification is the root of the well-known false-negative problem and leaves current models unable to tell a precise query from an ambiguous one. We reframe CIR as calibrated intent resolution under uncertainty: a retriever is wrapped in a conformal prediction layer that returns a candidate set with a coverage guarantee and whose size is a principled measure of ambiguity; when the set is large, an expected-information-gain policy asks the single most useful clarifying question, drawn from interpretable ambiguity axes, and the set contracts. We introduce AmbiCIR, a benchmark and human-validated user simulator that revive the dormant auxiliary and dialogue annotations of CIRR and extend the multiple-positive setting of CIRCO. Across open-domain and fashion benchmarks our method matches single-turn state of the art, confirming calibrated resolution is cost-free on precise queries, while reaching the intended target in a fraction of the interaction budget required by naive conversational baselines, and it is the first to report valid coverage and calibration for the task.

</details>


### [144] [Where Detectors Fail: Probing Generative Space for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24906)
*Zijie Cao,Weijie Tu,Yao Xiao,Weijian Deng,Liang Lin,Pengxu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE框架，通过主动探索生成过程中的困难区域（利用检测器作为评判器引导生成器产生难以分类的样本），从而提升AI生成图像检测器的泛化能力，实验表明该方法能有效泛化到未见过的生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在生成设置变化时性能显著下降，表明仅靠扩大训练数据规模不足以解决泛化问题，关键在于训练过程中对生成变化的覆盖不足。生成模型编辑研究表明，内部表示的微小改变就能产生多样且有意义的变化，这些变化在标准采样下未被充分探索。

Method: PROBE框架将检测器作为评判器，通过流形级修改主动引导生成器产生难以分类的真实样本，暴露标准采样策略下罕见的失败案例，并利用这些困难样本来优化检测器。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验结果表明，PROBE增强了对未见过的生成器的泛化能力，提升了AI生成图像检测的整体性能。

Conclusion: 主动探索生成过程中的困难区域而非被动依赖固定数据源，是提升AIGI检测器泛化性的有效途径；代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated images (AIGI) remains challenging because detectors often fail to generalize to unseen generators. Although existing methods are trained on large datasets, their performance still degrades when generation settings change, indicating that data scale alone is insufficient and that limited coverage of generative variations during training is a key factor. Studies on generative model editing show that small changes in internal representations can produce diverse and meaningful image variations, many of which are not explored under standard sampling. Leveraging this insight, we propose PROBE (Probing Robustness via Boundary Exploration), a framework that improves detector generalization by actively exploring challenging regions of the generative process. Instead of treating the generator as a fixed data source, PROBE uses the detector as a critic to steer the generator through manifold-level modifications, producing realistic samples that are difficult to classify. These samples expose failure cases that are uncommon under standard data sampling strategies and are used to refine the detector. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks indicate that PROBE enhances generalization to unseen generators, resulting in more generalizable AIGI detection performance. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Amamiya-C/PROBE-AIGI-Detection

</details>


### [145] [Tetris: Tile-level Sampling for Efficient and High-Fidelity Video Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25538)
*Chanwut Kittivorawong,Alena Chao,Charlie Si,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tetris是一个面向固定视频的轨道提取系统，通过基于瓦片的Polyomino数据模型实现细粒度时空剪枝，在保持5%精度损失前提下，相比先前系统实现最高17.4倍吞吐量提升，相比全帧参考管线提升68.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通过时间帧采样降低成本，但会丢失帧间运动信息，导致细粒度跟踪效果下降。固定视频中大量帧区域不含感兴趣目标，且不同区域可容忍不同采样率，现有方法未能充分利用这一特性进行高效剪枝。

Method: Tetris将视频分解为基于瓦片的Polyomino数据模型，运行三个上游算子：分类器识别相关瓦片并分组为Polyomino；整数线性规划(ILP)在用户指定精度约束下剪枝冗余Polyomino；打包器将剩余Polyomino组装为画布以最小化检测器调用次数。

Result: 在7个固定视频数据集上，Tetris在5%跟踪精度损失范围内均达标，而先前系统在3个数据集上超出该界限。在此界限下，Tetris吞吐量比先前系统高17.4倍，比全帧参考管线高68.8倍。

Conclusion: Tetris通过细粒度时空剪枝策略，在保持高保真度的同时大幅降低检测器开销，证明了针对固定视频场景的专用轨道提取系统具有显著优势。

Abstract: Track materialization converts raw video into reusable object tracks that downstream queries can run against without rerunning tracking, but extracting those tracks efficiently and with high fidelity remains expensive. Prior systems reduce cost through temporal frame sampling, erasing the inter-frame motion that fine-grained tracking requires. In stationary video, however, large portions of each frame contain no objects of interest, and the remaining regions tolerate different sampling rates. We present Tetris, a track-extraction system that decomposes videos into a tile-based polyomino data model, enabling fine-grained spatiotemporal pruning that reduces detector calls with minimal fidelity loss.
