Tetris runs three operators upstream of the user-provided detector: a classifier identifies relevant tiles and groups them into polyominoes, an integer linear program (ILP) prunes redundant polyominoes under a user-specified accuracy constraint, and a packer assembles the survivors into canvases that minimize detector calls. Across 7 stationary-video datasets, Tetris stays within a 5% tracking accuracy loss of a full-frame, every-frame reference pipeline, whereas prior systems exceed this bound on 3 of the 7 datasets. At this 5% bound, Tetris achieves up to 17.4x higher throughput than prior systems and up to 68.8x higher than the reference pipeline. The project page is at https://tetris-db.github.io .

</details>


### [146] [ControlLight: Towards Controllable, Consistent, and Generalizable Low-Light Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25569)
*Yufeng Yang,Jianzhuang Liu,Jisheng Chu,Yuqi Peng,Xianfang Zeng,Jiancheng Huang,Shifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ControlLight提出了一种可控、一致且泛化的低光照增强框架，通过构建大规模连续光照强度监督数据集和引入错位感知加权流匹配损失，实现了用户可灵活控制增强强度、保持视觉一致性和真实感的效果，在多个任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的低光照增强方法通常在有限数据集上训练，且仅针对单一增强目标，导致在实际应用中的泛化能力和可控性受限。

Method: 1) 构建了包含连续光照强度监督的大规模真实退化图像数据集；2) 提出错位感知加权流匹配损失（misalignment-aware weighted flow matching loss），在不同控制强度下保持图像结构一致性；3) 允许用户通过灵活控制增强强度来编辑真实低光照图像，同时保持视觉一致性和真实感。

Result: 在多种低光照增强任务上达到SOTA性能，展现出强连续可控性和对真实场景的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: ControlLight通过可控、一致且泛化的框架有效解决了现有低光照增强方法的局限，为实际应用提供了更灵活和可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing deep learning-based low-light enhancement methods are typically trained on limited datasets with single enhancement targets, which restricts their generalization ability and controllability in real-world applications. To overcome these limitations, we propose ControlLight, a controllable, consistent, and generalizable framework for low-light enhancement. We first construct a large-scale dataset of real-world degraded images with continuous illumination-strength supervision. To further ensure consistent outputs under different control strengths, we introduce a misalignment-aware weighted flow matching loss that preserves image structure across continuous enhancement strengths. ControlLight allows users to edit real-world degraded low-light images toward satisfactory enhancement results by flexibly controlling the strength while preserving visual consistency and realism. Extensive experiments show that ControlLight achieves state-of-the-art performance against existing low-light enhancement approaches while demonstrating strong continuous controllability and generalization to real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [From Contrast to Consistency: Rethinking Event-based Continuous-Time Optical Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25570)
*Rui Hu,Song Wu,Wen Yang,Jinjian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于时空结构一致性（STSC）的混合监督框架，用于事件相机的连续时光流估计，通过双向互补多尺度架构和课程式混合训练策略，在多个基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级延迟和高动态范围，能够异步捕捉亮度变化，为精细时间精度的运动建模提供了独特机会。然而，时间密集的真实标注稀缺限制了监督学习的效果，而对比度最大化框架忽略了时间连续性和结构一致性，导致复杂运动下的轨迹失真。

Method: 1) 提出时空结构一致性（STSC）范式，联合约束局部结构稳定性和轨迹连续性；2) 设计双向互补多尺度架构增强表示和鲁棒性；3) 采用课程式混合训练策略，实现从监督点约束到自监督流形正则化的平滑过渡。

Result: 在多个基准上的连续时间和标准光流估计任务中均取得了最优性能。

Conclusion: 证明了所提出的混合监督学习范式在事件相机光流估计中的有效性，能够实现物理上一致的运动估计。

Abstract: Estimating continuous optical flow is a fundamental yet challenging problem in dynamic visual perception. Event-based cameras, with microsecond latency and high dynamic range, capture brightness changes asynchronously, offering a unique opportunity to model motion with fine temporal precision. However, the scarcity of temporally dense ground-truth annotations limits the effectiveness of supervised learning, while contrast maximization (CM) frameworks, focused on sharpening the Image of Warped Events (IWE), often neglect temporal continuity and structural coherence, leading to distorted trajectories under complex motion. To overcome these challenges, we propose a hybrid-supervised framework for continuous-time optical flow estimation, grounded in the principle of Spatio-temporal Structural Consistency (STSC). This paradigm jointly enforces local structural stability and trajectory continuity, ensuring physically coherent motion across time. To further enhance representation and robustness, we design a bidirectionally complementary multi-scale architecture and employ a curriculum-guided hybrid training strategy, enabling a smooth transition from supervised point constraints to self-supervised manifold regularization. Comprehensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both continuous-time and standard optical flow estimation, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed learning paradigm.

</details>


### [148] [AI-T2I: Aggregating-and-Isolating Cross-Attention to Diffusion Models for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25763)
*Shipeng Cao,Biao Qian,Haipeng Liu,Yang Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型在文本到图像生成中跨注意力图对齐不精确的问题，本文提出AI-T2I方法，通过聚合损失解决同主体内token激活分散问题，并通过隔离损失推远不同主体间token激活，实现了精确的文本-图像对齐，在多个基准上超越SOTA，并展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在文本-图像对齐方面存在不足，已有工作主要关注不同主体token之间激活重叠问题（inter-subject-token），而忽略了对同一主体内部token激活分散（intra-subject-token scattering）的问题。

Method: 提出AI-T2I方法：(1) 设计聚合损失（aggregation loss），识别并整合分散的token内激活，同时隐式缓解潜在的跨token重叠问题；(2) 引入隔离损失（isolation loss），推远不同token之间的激活，实现精确的文本-图像对齐。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，AI-T2I在文本到图像合成任务中超越了现有最先进方法，并且在可控布局生成和个性化生成等下游任务中展现出优秀的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的聚合-隔离交叉注意力机制有效解决了扩散模型中文本-图像对齐的两类问题（激活分散和激活重叠），为文本到图像生成提供了更精确的对齐方案。

Abstract: Text-to-image synthesis has made significant progress, benefiting from the strong generative capabilities of diffusion models. However, these models struggle to achieve precise text-to-image alignment within cross-attention maps during the denoising process. Existing works primarily focus on inter-subject-token activations (i.e., cross-attention scores) overlap for different subjects, overlooking the intra-subject-token activations scattering issue for identical subjects. In this paper, we propose an Aggregating-and-Isolating cross-attention approach to diffusion models for Text-to-Image synthesis, dubbed AI-T2I. Technically, to address the scattering issue, we devise an aggregation loss to identify and consolidate the scattered intra-token activations, which implicitly helps mitigate the potential overlap issue. Upon that, an isolation loss is further introduced to push the inter-token activations apart, thus fulfilling precise text-to-image alignment. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of AI-T2I over the state-of-the-art works for text-to-image synthesis. Furthermore, our AI-T2I exhibits excellent generalization across other tasks, e.g., controllable layout generation and personalized generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Hatter77/AI-T2I.

</details>


### [149] [Global Structure-from-Motion Meets Feedforward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26103)
*Linfei Pan,Johannes Schönberger,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合经典SfM方法与前馈式3D重建方法优势的新型SfM流水线，在多种场景下取得了最先进的结果，并开源了实现代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SfM方法在低纹理、低重叠、对称等场景下存在鲁棒性不足的问题，而前馈式方法虽在这些困难场景表现优异，但在可扩展性、精度和鲁棒性方面存在局限，且通常不如经典方法在标准重建场景中的表现。本文旨在结合两者优势，弥补各自不足。

Method: 系统分析了经典SfM与前馈式方法的局限性，设计了一种新型SfM流水线，通过融合两类方法的各自优势来实现鲁棒且精确的相机位姿估计与3D场景结构重建。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在广泛场景下达到了最先进的重建效果。

Conclusion: 融合经典与前馈方法的SfM流水线能够有效提升多场景下的重建性能，开源实现有利于社区进一步研究和应用。

Abstract: Structure-from-Motion -- the process of simultaneously estimating camera poses and 3D scene structure from a collection of images -- remains a central challenge in computer vision, with many open problems yet to be solved. Recent advances in feedforward 3D reconstruction have made significant strides in overcoming persistent failure cases of classical SfM methods, particularly in scenarios characterized by low texture, limited overlap, and symmetries. However, while feedforward approaches excel in these challenging conditions, they often face limitations regarding scalability, accuracy, or robustness, and typically fall short of classical methods in standard reconstruction settings. In this work, we systematically analyze these limitations and propose a new Structure-from-Motion pipeline by combining the respective strengths of classical and feedforward methods. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets show the benefits of our approach, achieving state-of-the-art results across a wide range of scenarios. We share our system as an open-source implementation at https://github.com/colmap/gluemap.

</details>


### [150] [TailedCore: Few-Shot Sampling for Unsupervised Long-Tail Noisy Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02775)
*Yoon Gyo Jung,Jaewoo Park,Jaeho Yoon,Kuan-Chuan Peng,Wonchul Kim,Andrew Beng Jin Teoh,Octavia Camps*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对无监督异常检测中正常数据集存在污染（噪声）和长尾分布的问题，提出TailSampler估计类别数量并采样尾部样本，在此基础上构建记忆库模型TailedCore，实现尾部信息捕获与噪声鲁棒性的平衡，在多数设置下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督异常检测模型在正常数据集同时包含污染噪声和未知长尾类别分布时，面临"尾部类与噪声的权衡"问题：鲁棒于像素噪声的模型在尾部类样本上性能下降，反之亦然。现有方法未能有效独立处理这两个挑战。

Method: 1) 提出TailSampler，基于嵌入相似度的类别分布对称性假设来估计样本类别数量，从而识别并采样尾部类样本；2) 构建TailedCore记忆库模型，将尾部类样本和噪声样本独立处理，使记忆库既能捕获尾部类信息又具有噪声鲁棒性。

Result: 在无监督长尾噪声异常检测设定下，TailedCore在大多数评估设置中优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦尾部类和噪声的独立处理，可以有效缓解尾部类与噪声之间的权衡问题，提升无监督异常检测在复杂真实环境下的性能。

Abstract: We aim to solve unsupervised anomaly detection in a practical challenging environment where the normal dataset is both contaminated with defective regions and its product class distribution is tailed but unknown. We observe that existing models suffer from tail-versus-noise trade-off where if a model is robust against pixel noise, then its performance deteriorates on tail class samples, and vice versa. To mitigate the issue, we handle the tail class and noise samples independently. To this end, we propose TailSampler, a novel class size predictor that estimates the class cardinality of samples based on a symmetric assumption on the class-wise distribution of embedding similarities. TailSampler can be utilized to sample the tail class samples exclusively, allowing to handle them separately. Based on these facets, we build a memory-based anomaly detection model TailedCore, whose memory both well captures tail class information and is noise-robust. We extensively validate the effectiveness of TailedCore on the unsupervised long-tail noisy anomaly detection setting, and show that TailedCore outperforms the state-of-the-art in most settings.

</details>


### [151] [Efficient All-Pairs Correlation Volume Sampling for Optical Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16942)
*Karlis Martins Briedis,Markus Gross,Christopher Schroers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的全对偶相关体采样的算法，在保持数学等价于RAFT算子的前提下，显著降低了内存和计算复杂度。相比默认实现可降低高达99%内存使用，相比按需采样提升最高92%速度，并在8K超高清数据集上达到了SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流估计方法使用全对偶相关体进行局部代价采样，导致二次计算和内存复杂度。虽然存在按需计算的替代方案，但实际速度较慢，因此许多方法只能在降采样分辨率上处理图像，丢失了细粒度细节。

Method: 提出一种兼顾内存和计算效率的全对偶相关体采样算法，在数学上精确匹配RAFT定义的算子，同时避免二次复杂度的冗余计算和存储。

Result: 相比按需采样提速最高92%，内存占用相当；相比默认实现内存降低高达99%，端到端推理在高分辨率输入下可节省最高63%的计算时间。在8K超高清数据集上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 本文提出的高效相关体采样方法有效解决了光流估计中高分辨率输入下的内存和计算瓶颈，使得在保持精度的同时能够处理8K级别超高清图像，并在高分辨率场景下取得了最优的精度与速度平衡。

Abstract: Recent optical flow estimation methods often employ local cost sampling from a dense all-pairs correlation volume. This results in quadratic computational and memory complexity in the number of pixels. Although an alternative memory-efficient implementation with on-demand cost computation exists, this is significantly slower in practice and therefore many prior methods process images at downsampled resolutions, missing fine-grained details. To address this, we propose an algorithm for both memory and compute-efficient implementation of the all-pairs correlation volume sampling, still matching the exact mathematical operator as defined by RAFT. Our approach outperforms on-demand sampling by up to 92% while maintaining equally low memory usage, and performs at least on par with the default implementation with up to 99% lower memory usage. As cost sampling makes up a significant portion of the overall runtime, this can translate to up to 63% savings for the total end-to-end model inference on high-resolution inputs. Our evaluation of existing methods includes an 8K ultra-high-resolution dataset and an inference-time extension of the SEA-RAFT method. With this, we achieve state-of-the-art results at high resolutions both in accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [152] [Lifting Data-Tracing Machine Unlearning to Knowledge-Tracing for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11253)
*Yuwen Tan,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于数据追踪的机器遗忘范式升级为基于知识追踪的机器遗忘范式，以更好地支持基础模型的遗忘需求。作者从实践需求和认知研究两个角度论证了这一转变的必要性，并通过多模态基础模型的案例展示了该范式的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数据追踪型机器遗忘无法满足基础模型多样化的遗忘需求（如来自监管机构、企业用户、产品团队的请求），因为这些利益相关方通常无法访问模型的完整训练数据。相比之下，基于知识或能力层面的遗忘请求更为便捷和实用。

Method: 本文是一项立场论文（position paper），从实践需求和认知科学两个维度论证从数据追踪到知识追踪的范式转换。实践上分析了基础模型场景下多利益相关方无法访问训练数据的现实困境；认知上论证了知识追踪遗忘更接近人脑的遗忘机制。最后通过视觉-语言基础模型案例展示了该范式的可能实现路径。

Result: 提出并论证了知识追踪机器遗忘范式，识别了该范式的非平凡挑战，并通过一个视觉-语言基础模型的案例研究展示了该范式的具体实例化方案。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘应从数据追踪转向知识追踪范式，以更好地适应基础模型的应用场景。这一转变既有实践必要性，也有认知科学依据，但面临诸多技术挑战有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes certain training data points and their influence from AI models (e.g., when a data owner revokes their consent to allow models to learn from the data). In this position paper, we propose to lift data-tracing machine unlearning to knowledge-tracing for foundation models (FMs). We support this position based on practical needs and insights from cognitive studies. Practically, tracing data cannot meet the diverse unlearning requests for FMs, which may be from regulators, enterprise users, product teams, etc., who have no access to FMs' massive training data. Instead, it is convenient for these parties to issue an unlearning request about the knowledge or capability FMs (should not) possess. Cognitively, knowledge-tracing unlearning aligns with how the human brain forgets more closely than tracing individual training data points does. We further discuss the nontrivial challenges in the knowledge-tracing machine unlearning paradigm. Finally, we provide a concrete case study about a vision-language FM to illustrate how an unlearner might instantiate the knowledge-tracing machine unlearning paradigm. Code is available at: https://1yuwen.github.io/Knowledge-Tracing-MU-Page.

</details>


### [153] [Inference-Time Search Using Side Information for Diffusion-Based Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03352)
*Mahdi Farahbakhsh,Vishnu Teja Kunde,Dileep Kalathil,Krishna Narayanan,Jean-Francois Chamberland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DISS框架，通过推理时搜索将侧信息以即插即用、无需训练的方式融入扩散模型逆问题求解器，在多种逆问题（修复、超分、去模糊）和多种求解器（DPS、DAPS、MPGD）上一致提升重建质量，支持参考图像、文本描述、MRI扫描等多种侧信息形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的逆问题求解方法通常忽略了能显著提升重建质量的侧信息，尤其在严重病态设定下。

Method: 提出DISS框架，通过推理时搜索（inference-time search）将侧信息以即插即用、无需训练的方式集成到现有扩散逆问题求解器中。

Result: 在修复、超分辨率、多种去模糊任务上，结合DISS框架的DPS、DAPS、MPGD求解器相较原始方法一致提升重建质量。

Conclusion: DISS框架能有效利用多种侧信息（参考图像、文本描述、解剖MRI扫描）提升扩散逆问题求解性能，具有通用性和即插即用的优势。

Abstract: Diffusion models have been used as priors for solving inverse problems. However, existing approaches typically overlook side information that could significantly improve reconstruction quality, especially in severely ill-posed settings. In this work, we propose a novel framework that incorporates side information into existing diffusion-based inverse problem solvers via inference-time search, in a plug-and-play, training-free manner. Through extensive experiments across a range of inverse problems, including inpainting, super-resolution, and several deblurring tasks, and across multiple diffusion-based inverse problem solvers (DPS, DAPS, and MPGD), we show that augmenting each solver with our framework consistently improves the quality of the reconstructions over the corresponding original method. To demonstrate the generality of our approach, we consider diverse forms of side information, including reference images, textual descriptions, and anatomical MRI scans. The code is available at this \href{https://github.com/mahdi-farahbakhsh/DISS}{repository}\footnote{https://github.com/mahdi-farahbakhsh/DISS}.

</details>


### [154] [Explainable Cross-Disease Reasoning for Cardiovascular Risk Assessment from Low-Dose Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06625)
*Yifei Zhang,Jiashuo Zhang,Mojtaba Safari,Xiaofeng Yang,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释的跨疾病推理框架，从低剂量胸部CT中联合提取肺部发现和心脏特征，基于临床知识路径进行心血管风险评估，在NLST队列中AUC达0.919（CVD筛查）和0.838（死亡率预测），优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量胸部CT可同时捕获肺部和心脏结构，现有方法通常独立建模这两个领域，未能显式表示其生理交互作用，缺乏跨器官的因果推理机制。

Method: 提出可解释跨疾病推理框架，包含四个组件：冻结的肺风险先验、肺感知模块、智能体推理模块、心脏子体积特征提取器。遵循约束的临床信息路径：提取肺部发现→基于医学知识建立跨器官机制→生成心血管预测及自然语言推理依据。

Result: 在NLST队列中，CVD筛查AUC达0.919，CVD死亡率预测AUC达0.838，优于心脏专用模型、单疾病模型和基础模型基线。消融实验表明性能提升不能归因于额外的胸腔视觉特征、固定规则传播或单一推理后端。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可审计的跨疾病心血管风险评估方法，通过显式建模肺-心生理交互实现了更好的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Low-dose chest computed tomography (LDCT) captures pulmonary and cardiac structures in a single scan, enabling joint assessment of lung and cardiovascular health. Existing approaches typically model these domains independently and do not explicitly represent their physiological interactions. We propose an Explainable Cross-Disease Reasoning Framework for cardiovascular risk assessment from LDCT. The framework follows a constrained clinical-information pathway: it extracts pulmonary findings, grounds cross-organ mechanisms in medical knowledge, and produces a cardiovascular prediction with a natural-language rationale. It combines four components: a frozen lung-risk prior, a pulmonary perception module, an agentic reasoning module, and a cardiac subvolume feature extractor. Their outputs are fused to integrate localized cardiac evidence with mechanism-level pulmonary context. On the National Lung Screening Trial cohort, the framework achieves an AUC of 0.919 for CVD screening and up to 0.838 for CVD mortality prediction, outperforming cardiac-specific, single-disease, and foundation-model baselines. Targeted controls indicate that the gains are not explained by additional thoracic visual features alone, fixed rule propagation, or a single reasoning backend. The proposed framework thus provides an auditable approach to cross-disease cardiovascular risk assessment from LDCT.

</details>


### [155] [Kandinsky 5.0: A Family of Foundation Models for Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14993)
*Vladimir Arkhipkin,Vladimir Korviakov,Nikolai Gerasimenko,Denis Parkhomenko,Viacheslav Vasilev,Alexey Letunovskiy,Nikolai Vaulin,Maria Kovaleva,Ivan Kirillov,Lev Novitskiy,Denis Koposov,Nikita Kiselev,Alexander Varlamov,Dmitrii Mikhailov,Vladimir Polovnikov,Andrey Shutkin,Julia Agafonova,Ilya Vasiliev,Anastasiia Kargapoltseva,Anna Dmitrienko,Anastasia Maltseva,Anna Averchenkova,Olga Kim,Tatiana Nikulina,Denis Dimitrov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kandinsky 5.0 是一个包含图像和视频生成模型的基础框架，涵盖 6B 参数的图像模型、2B 参数的轻量级视频模型和 19B 参数的高质量视频模型，通过多阶段训练、数据策展、SFT 和 RL 后训练等技术实现高性能生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个大规模、公开可用、支持高分辨率图像和视频生成的基础框架，推动高质量生成模型的研究与可及性。

Method: 提出了三个核心模型系列（Image Lite 6B、Video Lite 2B、Video Pro 19B）；采用多阶段训练流程，包括大规模预训练、数据策展（收集、处理、过滤、聚类），以及自监督微调（SFT）和基于强化学习（RL）的后训练；在架构、训练和推理方面进行了优化以提升生成速度和质量。

Result: 在人类评估中，Kandinsky 5.0 在多种任务上实现了最先进的性能和高速生成能力。

Conclusion: Kandinsky 5.0 作为一个大规模生成框架，有望通过开源代码和训练检查点推动高质量生成模型在学术社区的发展。

Abstract: This report introduces Kandinsky 5.0, a family of state-of-the-art foundation models for high-resolution image and 10-second video synthesis. The framework comprises three core line-up of models: Kandinsky 5.0 Image Lite - a line-up of 6B parameter image generation models, Kandinsky 5.0 Video Lite - a fast and lightweight 2B parameter text-to-video and image-to-video models, and Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro - 19B parameter models that achieves superior video generation quality. We provide a comprehensive review of the data curation lifecycle - including collection, processing, filtering and clustering - for the multi-stage training pipeline that involves extensive pre-training and incorporates quality-enhancement techniques such as self-supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based post-training. We also present novel architectural, training, and inference optimizations that enable Kandinsky 5.0 to achieve high generation speeds and state-of-the-art performance across various tasks, as demonstrated by human evaluation. As a large-scale, publicly available generative framework, Kandinsky 5.0 leverages the full potential of its pre-training and subsequent stages to be adapted for a wide range of generative applications. We hope that this report, together with the release of our open-source code and training checkpoints, will substantially advance the development and accessibility of high-quality generative models for the research community.

</details>


### [156] [Align & Invert: Solving Inverse Problems with Diffusion and Flow-based Models via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16870)
*Loukas Sfountouris,Giannis Daras,Paris Giampouras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将表示对齐（REPA）技术应用于逆问题求解，通过在推理时将扩散/流模型与DINOv2视觉编码器的内部表示对齐，显著提升了图像重建质量和感知真实性，同时减少了所需离散化步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，生成模型内部表示与自监督编码器表示对齐能作为有效归纳偏置改善收敛和样本质量。本文将其扩展至逆问题领域，旨在利用预训练生成模型作为先验时，通过表示对齐引导推理过程的重建。

Method: 在逆问题推理过程中，对扩散/流模型与DINOv2视觉编码器施加表示对齐（REPA）正则化，将模型关于近似目标特征的表示进行对齐，引导潜变量向干净图像状态演化。

Result: 在超分辨率、框内修复、高斯去模糊和运动去模糊等多个任务上的实验表明，REPA一致提升了重建质量，并带来效率提升——减少了所需离散化步数。

Conclusion: REPA正则化可视为在DINOv2嵌入空间中最小化某种散度测度的变分方法；在特定正则性假设下，REPA更新可引导潜扩散状态向干净图像状态靠拢。该方法具有通用性，可集成到多种最先进的逆问题求解器中。

Abstract: Enforcing alignment between the internal representations of diffusion or flow-based generative models and those of pretrained self-supervised encoders has recently been shown to provide a powerful inductive bias, improving both convergence and sample quality. In this work, we extend this idea to inverse problems, where pretrained generative models are employed as priors. We propose applying representation alignment (REPA) between diffusion or flow-based models and a DINOv2 visual encoder, to guide the reconstruction process at inference time. Although ground-truth signals are unavailable in inverse problems, we empirically show that aligning model representations of approximate target features can substantially enhance reconstruction quality and perceptual realism. We provide theoretical results showing (a) that REPA regularization can be viewed as a variational approach for minimizing a divergence measure in the DINOv2 embedding space, and (b) how under certain regularity assumptions REPA updates steer the latent diffusion states toward those of the clean image. These results offer insights into the role of REPA in improving perceptual fidelity. Finally, we demonstrate the generality of our approach by We integrate REPA into multiple state-of-the-art inverse problem solvers, and provide extensive experiments on super-resolution, box inpainting, Gaussian deblurring, and motion deblurring confirming that our method consistently improves reconstruction quality, while also providing efficiency gains reducing the number of required discretization steps.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EarthMind-SR（文中也称AirCast-SR），一个将全球AI天气预报从0.25度（~28公里）降尺度到1公里、每小时分辨率的大气超分辨率基础模型。该模型采用条件化Latent Consistency Model扩散框架的三维U-Net架构，在美国本土训练，实现了近零偏差，并在印度和德国展示了零样本全球迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公里尺度的业务天气预报对于传统数值天气预报模型而言计算成本过高，限制了能源、农业和灾害管理等领域对高时空分辨率预报的获取。

Method: 采用Latent Consistency Model扩散框架下的条件化三维U-Net架构，以GraphCast预报为输入、NOAA的AORC数据为目标，在CONUS区域进行基于分块样本的训练，实现从0.25度到1公里分辨率、每小时时间分辨率的降尺度。

Result: 在所有变量和预报时效上实现近零偏差；径向功率谱密度分析表明在10-100公里波长范围内保留了精细尺度大气结构；在CONUS跨冬、夏、春季案例验证，并在印度和德国展示了无需微调的零样本全局迁移能力。

Conclusion: 作为开放权重的基础模型，EarthMind-SR建立了公里尺度AI天气预报的新范式，为区域微调、蒸馏以及在气候服务和灾害预报中的下游应用提供了平台。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [158] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SilIF 是一种对孤立森林的增强方法，通过添加基于轮廓系数的评分层，在树表示空间中对路径长度指纹进行聚类并计算轮廓分数，与原始 IF 分数加权结合。在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测基准上 AUC-PR 提升 0.0080，但在合成数据集上无改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测在标签稀缺的交易欺诈检测中广泛应用，孤立森林因其可扩展性和易部署性而受欢迎，但存在改进空间。作者希望通过引入结构分组信息来增强 IF 的检测能力。

Method: SilIF 为每个样本提取各棵树的路径长度向量作为"指纹"，对这些指纹进行聚类得到结构分组，然后计算轮廓系数衡量样本与其所属分组及最近邻分组的匹配程度，最后通过超参数 alpha 将轮廓分数与原始 IF 分数加权组合。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测数据集（约59万笔交易，3.5%欺诈率）上，SilIF（alpha=1.0）在5个随机种子下平均 AUC-PR 提升0.0080，所有种子均优于基线（配对t检验 p=0.046）。在合成信用卡数据集 Sparkov 上无改进。

Conclusion: SilIF 是一种可调参、易部署的孤立森林增强方法。论文诚实地报告了其在真实数据上有效但在合成数据上无效的情况，并分析了两种结果的条件差异。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [159] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PushCen-ADFL提出了一种通信高效的异步去中心化联邦学习框架，通过质心表示空间中的通信-聚合-稳定闭环设计，在非对称通信和延迟客户端参与下实现稳定训练，相比基线方法在异构数据下准确率提升最高6%，同时每轮推送通信成本降低80%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步去中心化联邦学习面临点对点通信频繁、有向拓扑上的异步更新以及非独立同分布数据共同导致的通信开销过大、聚合偏差严重和模型漂移等问题，亟需一种通信高效且训练稳定的解决方案。

Method: 提出PushCen-ADFL框架，核心思想是将通信、聚合和局部稳定化耦合在共享的质心表示空间中：1) 客户端交换质心形式的消息；2) 采用保平均的push-sum混合校正聚合偏差；3) 在相同质心空间中引入轻量级质心正则化以缓解异构性和陈旧性导致的漂移；4) 设计有界、发送端去重的缓冲区以增强对不规则异步到达的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉数据集上的实验表明，PushCen-ADFL在数据异构场景下准确率提升最高6%，同时每轮推送通信成本降低超过80%，实现了良好的准确率-通信权衡。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL通过质心空间的耦合设计有效解决了ADFL中通信效率与训练稳定性之间的矛盾，为大规模异构系统中的去中心化联邦学习提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [160] [When Rule Violations Are Rare: Chimera Training for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26171)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种神经规则评估器，通过将逻辑约束编译为有向无环图、使用子树MLP门和嵌合体训练（特征级反事实构造），在无真实异常样本条件下检测语义约束违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际异常并非简单稀有输入，而是语义约束的违反（如对象共现、动作前提、时序关系）。现有方法依赖独立事件假设或同图像语义训练，难以处理约束违规的稀疏性和组合性。

Method: 1) 将逻辑约束编译为DAG，内部逻辑算子使用特征感知的子树MLP门；2) 通过真实概念标签的布尔传播获得中间监督；3) 提出嵌合体训练：在特征级拼接不同样本的子树特征，用继承的硬标签通过逻辑算子计算目标，提供反事实训练样本。

Result: 在CLEVRER、OpenImages、VidOR上，规则级异常AUROC优于独立事件和同图像语义训练基线，尤其在组合规则和关系规则上提升显著。

Conclusion: 该方法无需真实异常图像即可学习语义约束违规检测，可输出标量异常分数和规则级归因。

Abstract: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs, but violations of semantic constraints: objects co-occur in structured ways, actions imply preconditions, and events satisfy temporal or relational regularities. We study anomaly detection in this setting, where constraints are given as logical rules over learned visual concepts, but real rule violations are rare or absent during training. We propose a neural rule evaluator that compiles each constraint into a directed acyclic graph and learns feature-aware subtree MLP gates for its internal logical operators. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to a parent representation and a rule-satisfaction probability, with intermediate supervision obtained from exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. The key difficulty is that same-image training data often provide insufficient coverage of informative truth configurations and also allow shortcut solutions. To address this, we introduce chimera training: an operand-level counterfactual construction at the feature level. Instead of mixing input images, we concatenate subtree features from different samples; each operand keeps the hard truth label of the sample it came from, and the chimera target is obtained by applying the node's logical operator to those inherited labels. This supplies supervised logical counterexamples without requiring real anomalous images. Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR, the resulting evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules. The method yields both scalar anomaly scores and rule-level attributions.

</details>


### [161] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoQuant 是一种无需训练的量化后处理方法，通过峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）将激活分布塑造成更易于量化的形式，在 W4A4KV4 设置下平均保留 97% 的浮点精度，并将 LLaMA-2 13B 的性能差距缩小 42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活量化是高效大语言模型部署的主要瓶颈，现有 PTQ 方法虽能抑制异常值或平衡通道，但未明确什么样的激活分布才易于离散化，导致量化误差仍然较大。

Method: 从信息论角度分析量化误差，提出量化友好的激活应同时具有较小的数值范围和充分的分散度；据此设计 Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) 来重塑分布，并引入自适应异常值 token 选择以提升优化鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个 LLM 家族中，InfoQuant 在 W4A4KV4 设置下平均保留 97% 的浮点精度，LLaMA-2 13B 的性能差距相比之前最先进方法缩小 42%。

Conclusion: InfoQuant 是一种有效的无需训练的量化后处理方法，通过主动塑造激活分布而非被动抑制异常值，显著提升了低比特量化性能。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [162] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAC，一种噪声感知的自适应混合训练权重控制器，通过在线估计梯度方差和信号不一致性动态调整监督微调与强化学习的混合比例，在数学、代码、科学、逻辑基准上持续优于固定和规则基线，开销低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合后训练中固定的混合调度无法适应监督微调与强化学习两种信号相对噪声随时间变化的问题。

Method: GAC：基于梯度方差和两种训练信号之间不一致性的在线估计，推导自适应混合权重；引入平滑、先验引导和有界更新；复用现有训练张量。

Result: 在数学、代码、科学、逻辑基准上，GAC一致优于强固定基线和基于规则的基线；更大模型规模下收益更显著；训练开销低于1%。

Conclusion: 噪声感知的自适应混合策略能有效提升混合后训练性能，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>


### [163] [Max-Window Scale Estimation for Near-Lossless HiF8 W8A8 Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26189)
*Yingying Cheng,Jinquan Shi,Li Zhou,Zhiyang He,Zhaoyi Sun,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了HiF8 W8A8低比特浮点量化感知训练（QAT）在OpenPangu-Embedded-1B模型中的应用，识别并分离了两种正交的失败模式：amax饱和（延迟缩放估计导致前向裁剪破坏知识敏感表征）和灾难性遗忘（激进学习率覆盖预训练常识知识）。通过保守的64步历史窗口最大算法DTS策略解决amax饱和，通过500步BF16预热+lr=10^{-5}的QAT缓解遗忘，最终方案在MMLU、HellaSwag、ARC-Challenge上分别仅下降0.43%、0.58%、0.22%，训练损失APE仅0.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特浮点量化感知训练在LLM部署中虽能提升效率，但会引入标准训练指标无法检测的细微失败模式，需要系统研究并识别这些隐藏的失败模式及其成因。

Method: 通过8组控制实验系统研究HiF8 W8A8 QAT，利用延迟张量缩放（DTS）分析视角，分离出amax饱和与灾难性遗忘两种失效模式。采用保守的64步历史窗口最大算法DTS策略解决amax饱和，使用500步BF16预热后以lr=10^{-5}进行QAT缓解遗忘。

Result: 最终配置在MMLU下降0.43%，HellaSwag下降0.58%，ARC-Challenge下降0.22%，训练损失绝对百分比误差（APE）仅0.11%（10,000步内），两种修复方案必要且充分。

Conclusion: 低比特浮点QAT中存在两种标准指标不可检测的正交失败模式，通过针对性修复可实现与BF16基线几乎无差别的部署性能，揭示了量化训练中延迟缩放估计与学习率调谐的关键作用。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) with low-bit floating-point formats enables efficient LLM deployment, yet introduces subtle failure modes invisible to standard training metrics. We present a systematic study of HiF8 W8A8 QAT for OpenPangu-Embedded-1B through the lens of Delayed Tensor Scaling (DTS). Across eight controlled experiments, we identify and disentangle two orthogonal failure modes: (i)amax saturation, where delayed scale estimates silently corrupt knowledge-sensitive representations via forward-pass clipping, and (ii)catastrophic forgetting, where an aggressive learning rate overwrites pretrained commonsense knowledge independently of quantization. Neither is detectable from training loss alone. We address amax saturation with a conservative max-algorithm DTS strategy over a 64-step history window, and mitigate forgetting via a 500-step BF16 warmup followed by QAT at lr=10^{-5}. Both fixes are necessary and sufficient: our final configuration achieves 0.43% MMLU drop, 0.58% HellaSwag drop, and 0.22% ARC-Challenge drop versus a matched BF16 baseline, with a training loss APE of only 0.11% over 10,000 steps.

</details>


### [164] [Modeling Dynamic Mixtures of Time-Delay Systems from Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26191)
*Ren Fujiwara,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DelayMix，一种将流式时间序列视为动态混合时滞系统的在线建模框架，通过构建系统概要张量（基于马尔可夫参数序列）固定长度表示过去状态，在保证鲁棒性的同时减少内存占用，并利用张量分解选择最佳匹配模型，实现对环境变化和时滞变化的快速适应。真实数据实验表明该方法在预测精度和适应速度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据流中系统快速变化（如环境因素或输入延迟变化导致的 regime shifts）会降低模型性能，且使用多个小模型时面临精度、鲁棒性和内存使用之间的权衡问题。

Method: 将流式时间序列视为动态混合时滞系统的在线框架；通过构建基于系统马尔可夫参数序列的概要系统张量来捕获系统动态和时滞特征；必要时使用张量分解算法提取相关历史模型并选择最适合当前状态的系统。

Result: 在真实数据集上，DelayMix在预测精度和对时滞变化的适应速度上持续优于其他方法，尤其是在高度非平稳数据上表现突出。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对环境变化的快速适应，计算高效，并能有效平衡精度、鲁棒性和内存使用之间的权衡。

Abstract: This research addresses the problem of adaptive modeling in time-series data streams with clear input-output relationships. This problem is challenging because rapid system changes (regime shifts) caused by environmental factors or input delay changes degrade model performance, and the trade-off among accuracy, robustness, and memory usage arises when using multiple small models for each time-series pattern. To address these issues, this paper presents an online framework/method that treats streaming time series as dynamic mixtures of time-delay systems. This framework maintains robustness of model tracking and reduces memory usage by summarizing past regimes using a fixed-length representation that captures both the system dynamics and input-output delays. Concretely, this approach constructs a summary system tensor using the system's Markov parameter series, capturing both dynamic behavior and delay characteristics. If necessary, a tensor decomposition algorithm extracts relevant past models from the tensor and helps select the system that best fits the current regime. This method enables rapid adaptation to environmental changes and is computationally efficient. Tests on real datasets show that DelayMix consistently outperforms other methods, achieving superior forecast accuracy and faster adaptation to delays, especially for highly non-stationary data.

</details>


### [165] [Co-folding model guided by structural proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26192)
*Alon Shtrikman,Nitzan Simchi,Michal Ran Shchory,Sagie Brodsky,Eran Seger,Kirill Pevzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIMS-Fold是一个推理时引导扩散框架，通过整合XL-MS和HDX-MS结构蛋白质组学数据，引导生成采样轨迹，在诱导接近靶标上实现比纯计算模型更高的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构生成模型能预测静态结构，但无法捕捉蛋白质复合物的正确构象状态，这对抗体和PROTAC等诱导接近药物设计至关重要。结构蛋白质组学数据（XL-MS、HDX-MS）虽提供空间和动态信息，但整合这些稀疏异质测量数据仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出AIMS-Fold框架，采用推理时引导扩散方法，利用从XL-MS空间约束和HDX-MS溶剂可及性分布导出的可微物理势，主动引导生成采样轨迹。

Result: 单独使用XL-MS或HDX-MS方法均可提高预测精度，二者整合产生协同提升。在挑战性的诱导接近靶标上，AIMS-Fold优于Boltz-2等纯计算无引导的最先进模型。

Conclusion: AIMS-Fold建立了整合结构蛋白质组学数据与扩散模型生物物理先验的计算框架，为诱导接近药物的基于结构的药物设计提供了强大方法。

Abstract: Protein structure generative models excel at predicting single protein static structures from sequence, but routinely fail to capture the correct conformational state of protein complexes, critical for protein design and induced proximity modalities such as antibodies and PROTACs. While structural proteomics techniques like Cross-Linking Mass Spectrometry (XL-MS) and Hydrogen-Deuterium Exchange (HDX-MS) offer valuable spatial and dynamic insights, integrating these sparse, heterogeneous measurements into these models remains an open challenge. Here, we bridge this gap by combining structural proteomics data with the rich biophysical priors learned by pretrained diffusion models. We introduce AIMS-Fold, an inference-time guided-diffusion framework that actively steers the generative sampling trajectory using differentiable physical potentials derived from XL-MS spatial restraints and HDX-MS solvent accessibility profiles. We demonstrate that these structural methods individually enhance predictive accuracy, and their integration yields synergistic improvement. Crucially, by leveraging these experimental restraints, AIMS-Fold achieves higher accuracy on challenging induced proximity targets than purely computational, unguided state-of-the-art models like Boltz-2. This establishes our framework as a powerful, integrative computational approach for the structure based drug design of induced proximity drugs. Evaluation code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [166] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAD框架，通过统一分类（Outlier Exposure）和重构（Masked Autoencoder）两种范式，利用分类模块生成概率软掩码指导重构模块，同时重构模块缓解分类模块的泛化问题，从而有效检测细微和复杂的时间序列异常。实验表明CoAD在高质量基准上显著优于现有方法，且轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测方法中，基于Outlier Exposure的分类方法泛化能力差，基于Masked Autoencoder的重构方法存在掩码对齐问题，且现有深度学习方法难以检测细微和持续异常。作者希望统一两种范式以互补优势、弥补各自缺陷。

Method: 提出CoAD框架，包含分类模块和重构模块的协同设计：分类模块生成概率软掩码指导重构模块的掩码策略，重构模块缓解分类模块的泛化问题。分类模块还针对分类粒度不当和频率信息忽略问题进行了专门设计。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集上，在严格评估协议下，CoAD显著优于现有SOTA深度学习和传统数据挖掘方法。同时CoAD轻量化，速度远快于现有方法。

Conclusion: CoAD验证了深度学习在时间序列异常检测中的潜力，其协同设计有效解决了细微和复杂异常检测难题，并具备大规模实时应用的实用价值。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [167] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathoFM提出了一种编码器中心的多目标预训练框架（局部补全+时间连续性+无监督上下文动态），用于病理步态分析，在脊髓损伤的少量、异质临床时间序列上实现了跨任务类型（分类与回归）的鲁棒迁移。实验表明，动态中心的目标组合（而非纯分组或纯重建）能在判别边界与波形保真度之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习受限于小样本、异质性队列和设备漂移，且下游任务涵盖分类和回归。基础模型预训练虽有前景，但关键问题是：预训练目标应施加何种归纳偏置，才能使表征跨任务类型和受试者迁移？

Method: 提出PathoFM，一种编码器中心Transformer，在多元步态窗口上使用三个互补目标预训练：1）局部补全（重建连续掩码跨度，强化局部结构）；2）时间连续性（从观测前缀预测掩码中期延续，强制执行平滑性和因果一致性）；3）无监督上下文动力学（通过注意力机制，基于受试者示例窗口进行支持-查询重建）。

Result: 动力学中心的目标组合产生最均衡的迁移效果：分组目标有利于判别边界但会降低连续目标所需的幅度保真度；纯重建目标保留波形结构但分类性能可能不足。局部重建+时间连续性+上下文条件（在示例可访问时）的组合产生鲁棒的泛化表征。

Conclusion: 在临床时间序列预训练中，动力学中心的目标混合（而非纯对比学习或纯生成式）是实现跨任务类型迁移的最佳选择，为病理步态分析及其他临床时间序列场景提供了方法论指导。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [168] [The Bridge-Garden Dilemma in LLM Distillation: Why Mixing Hard and Soft Labels Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26246)
*Guanghui Wang,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Jin-Wen Wu,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bridge-Garden分解理论，将生成步骤分为需精确匹配的"桥梁"和可灵活选择的"花园"，据此设计混合硬/软标签的知识蒸馏策略，在降低训练成本9.7倍的同时，在推理和编码任务上优于多种KD基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏中，软标签理论上信息更丰富，但实践中混合硬标签和软标签总能获得更好结果，这一现象的原因尚不清楚。作者试图解释这一现象并设计更优的蒸馏策略。

Method: 提出Bridge-Garden分解理论：将生成步骤分为Bridge（需精确匹配token）和Garden（可灵活选择）。基于此设计混合监督方法，自适应平衡硬标签和软标签的权重。

Result: 在7组教师-学生模型对（Qwen、Llama、Gemma、DeepSeek）上，推理和编码基准中优于基于散度和on-policy的KD基线，训练成本降低9.7倍。

Conclusion: 混合硬/软标签的好处并非来自更接近教师模型，而是来自降低暴露偏差（训练与推理分布不匹配）。Bridge-Garden理论为理解这一现象提供了理论基础，并指导了高效蒸馏策略的设计。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

</details>


### [169] [Quantized Keys Steal Attention: Bias Correction for KV-Cache Compression in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26266)
*Tuna Tuncer,Felix Becker,Thomas Pfeil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对自回归视频扩散模型中的KV缓存量化导致的质量下降问题，论文提出了一种基于Jensen偏差的逐注意力分数修正方法，通过在线计算查询范数与量化步长来消除量化噪声引起的注意力偏差。该方法几乎不增加计算开销和内存，在INT2量化下恢复至接近BF16质量，比INT4量化内存减半且效果更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成中KV缓存成为内存瓶颈，量化到低比特可减少内存压力但会严重降低视频质量，需要找到量化导致质量下降的根本原因并提出有效修正方案。

Method: 发现量化噪声导致注意力权重系统性偏差（Jensen偏差）：因softmax中指数函数的凸性，量化键的贡献被膨胀，从当前未量化块中窃取注意力。推导出基于查询范数和缓存键量化步长的逐注意力分数修正项，使用二阶泰勒展开实现近似计算，开销可忽略。

Result: 在MAGI-1、SkyReels-V2和HY-WorldPlay上，INT2量化下该方法恢复大部分质量损失，达到接近BF16的视频质量，且比INT4量化节省50%内存。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析发现并修正了KV缓存量化中的Jensen偏差，可以在极低比特量化下保持高质量视频生成，显著降低内存需求。

Abstract: Chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion models rely on a KV cache of previously generated chunks to avoid redundant computation, but this cache quickly becomes a memory bottleneck as videos grow longer. Methods that quantize the KV cache to low bitwidths reduce memory pressure but degrade video quality. We show that a key driver of this degradation is a systematic bias in attention weights: due to the convexity of the exponential in softmax attention, quantization noise inflates the contribution of cached keys, a phenomenon we call the Jensen bias. This effect causes quantized keys to steal attention mass from the unquantized current chunk. We derive a per-attention-score correction that removes this bias in expectation, computed on the fly from the quantization step sizes of the cached keys and the query norm. Using a second-order Taylor approximation, the additional computational overhead is negligible, and no additional memory is needed alongside the cache. Evaluated on MAGI-1, SkyReels-V2, and HY-WorldPlay at INT2 quantization, our correction recovers most of the quality lost to aggressive quantization, reaching near-BF16 video quality, and can outperform INT4 quantization while using 50% less memory.

</details>


### [170] [Scaling World-Model Reinforcement Learning Through Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26282)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Zhancun Mu,Yuanzhao Zhang,Yiming Yang,Hai Wang,Zhuo Sun,Che Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MBDPO框架，通过扩散策略表示统一世界模型中的搜索与策略优化，解决了搜索与价值学习之间的结构错位问题，在多种设置下展现出良好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的强化学习方法受限于模型偏差和误差累积问题，但更关键的是搜索与价值学习之间存在结构错位：策略改进依赖于非搜索策略诱导的值函数，导致训练不一致和次优学习。

Method: 提出MBDPO框架，将策略优化重铸为潜在世界模型中搜索轨迹上的扩散过程，从数据集中提取隐式能量函数来锚定策略，通过优化得分场来实现策略提升并缓解错位问题。

Result: 在多任务离线预训练、在线学习和离线到在线微调等多种设置下进行评测，离线大规模数据集预训练中观察到随模型容量增加的一致单调性能提升。

Conclusion: MBDPO通过统一搜索与策略优化，有效解锁了世界模型在可扩展策略学习中的潜力，为解决结构错位问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) can be effectively supported at scale through the use of world models. However, in practice, scaling such approaches remains fundamentally limited. A commonly recognized challenge is model bias and error compounding, which degrade long-horizon predictions. Beyond these issues, we identify a more critical yet underexplored bottleneck: a structural misalignment between search and value learning in existing world model approaches. In particular, policy improvement often relies on value functions induced by a separate, non-search policy, resulting in training inconsistency and ultimately suboptimal learning. To address this limitation, we propose Model-Based Diffusion Policy Optimization (MBDPO) in world models, a framework that unifies search and policy optimization through diffusion policy representations, thereby unlocking the potential of world models for scalable policy learning. Instead of constructing an explicit planner over a learned world model, we reformulate policy optimization as a diffusion process over searched trajectories in latent world models. In this view, we extract an implicit energy function from the collected dataset that anchors the policy, enabling MBDPO to refine the score field for policy optimization while mitigating misalignment. We evaluate MBDPO across a wide range of settings, including multi-task offline pretraining, online learning, and offline-to-online fine-tuning. In the offline regime, we further investigate its scaling behavior by pretraining on large-scale datasets, observing consistent and monotonic performance gains with increasing model capacity.

</details>


### [171] [DeepInterestGR: Mining Deep Multi-Interest Using Multi-Modal LLMs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18907)
*Yangchen Zeng,Zhenyu Yu,Zhiyuan Hu,Wenxin Zhang,Jinze Wang,Rongfeng Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DeepInterestGR框架，通过整合深度兴趣挖掘到生成式推荐流程中，解决了现有方法依赖浅层文本特征的问题。采用多LLM兴趣挖掘、奖励标记深度兴趣、兴趣增强项目离散化等技术创新，在三个Amazon数据集上相比14个基线方法取得5.8%-9.9%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法存在"浅层兴趣"问题——仅依赖表面文本特征，无法捕捉用户潜在动机，限制了推荐个性化和可解释性。

Method: 提出了DeepInterestGR框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 多LLM兴趣挖掘(MLIM)通过结构化推理提示挖掘深层兴趣；2) 奖励标记深度兴趣(RLDI)进行质量控制；3) 基于RQ-VAE的兴趣增强项目离散化(IEID)。结合两阶段SFT-GRPO训练流程，并由兴趣感知奖励引导。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Sports、Instruments三个数据集上，HR@10相对提升5.8%-8.3%，NDCG@10相对提升7.7%-9.9%，跨域泛化增益达+24.8%。

Conclusion: 融合深层语义兴趣能够有效改进基于SID的生成式推荐。

Abstract: We introduce DeepInterestGR, a novel framework that integrates deep interest mining into the generative recommendation pipeline. This addresses the "Shallow Interest" problem - existing generative methods rely on surface-level textual features and fail to capture latent user motivations, limiting personalization depth and recommendation interpretability. Our approach leverages Multi-LLM Interest Mining (MLIM) via structured reasoning prompting, Reward-Labeled Deep Interest (RLDI) for quality control, and Interest-Enhanced Item Discretization (IEID) via RQ-VAE, combined with a two-stage SFT-GRPO training pipeline guided by an Interest-Aware Reward. We validate DeepInterestGR on three Amazon Review benchmarks (Beauty, Sports, Instruments), comparing against 14 state-of-the-art baselines including SASRec, BERT4Rec, TIGER, LC-Rec, and S-DPO. Our method achieves 5.8%-8.3% relative improvements on HR@10 and 7.7%-9.9% on NDCG@10 over the strongest baseline, with cross-domain generalization gains of +24.8%. These results provide evidence that incorporating deep semantic interests can effectively improve SID-based generative recommendation.

</details>


### [172] [Two-Parameter Flows for Learning Population Dynamics of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26285)
*Paul Schwerdtner,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需轨迹数据、仅利用无标签样本学习高维概率密度随时间演化动力学的方法，通过两参数流学习基础分布到各边际分布的采样时间传输，再回归合成轨迹提取物理时间速度，证明了解的唯一性和正则性，可扩展到高维并允许非梯度动力学（如旋转/循环现象）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密度动力学学习方法通常需要轨迹信息或昂贵的逐步骤最优传输耦合，难以处理高维数据且无法建模旋转/循环等非梯度动力学。本文旨在设计一种无需轨迹数据、可扩展至高维且能处理非梯度动力学的方法。

Method: 引入两参数流框架：首先利用条件流匹配技术学习从基础分布到每个时间边际分布的采样时间传输（采样时间流），然后通过回归合成轨迹上的速度场提取物理时间动力学速度，证明解的唯—性和正则继承性。

Result: 理论证明物理时间动力学解唯一且继承采样时间传输的正则性；方法可扩展到高维，避免逐步骤最优传输耦合，同时支持非梯度动力学以解释旋转或循环物理现象。

Conclusion: 该方法通过解耦采样时间与物理时间，利用成熟的条件流匹配技术，实现了高维概率密度动力学的有效学习，为处理复杂物理系统中的非梯度动力学提供了一种通用且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning the dynamics of high-dimensional probability densities over time using unlabeled samples, without assuming access to trajectory information. We introduce two-parameter flows that learn only sampling-time transports from a base distribution to each marginal and then extract a physics-time velocity by regressing on coupled synthetic trajectories. We prove that the resulting physics-time dynamics are unique and inherit regularity from the sampling-time transports. Because we can build on standard, well-developed conditional flow matching techniques for learning the base-to-marginal transports, our approach scales to high dimensions and avoids per-step optimal-transport couplings, while allowing admissible non-gradient dynamics that can naturally explain rotational or circulating physics phenomena.

</details>


### [173] [Stateful Inference for Low-Latency Multi-Agent Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26289)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种有状态推理架构，通过持久化KV缓存和增量处理，将传统多轮工具调用中每轮的O(n_t)计算成本降低为O(Δ_t)的增量成本，相比vLLM和SGLang在6轮工作流中每轮加速2.1倍，35轮工作流中中位数轮次加速4.2倍，端到端耗时减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM推理框架将每次工具调用视为独立请求，即使前一轮85-95%的提示内容未发生变化，也需要从头重新处理整个对话，导致大量冗余计算。

Method: 提出有状态推理架构：(1) 持久化KV缓存跨轮次存活，仅接收新token进行增量更新；(2) 基数前缀缓存将这种增量复用扩展到交织的多智能体流量场景；(3) 提示查找投机解码器加速结构化输出生成。

Result: 在新型全生成工作负载上，相比vLLM和SGLang，6轮智能体工作流每轮加速2.1倍，35轮工作流中位数轮次加速4.2倍，端到端耗时减半。

Conclusion: 优势来源于有状态复用和投机解码，而非传统缓存机制，证明了增量推理架构在多智能体工具调用场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent tool calling is becoming the dominant interaction pattern for LLM-based systems, yet existing inference frameworks treat each tool call as an independent request, re-processing the entire conversation from scratch even though 85-95% of the prompt is unchanged from the previous turn.
  We present a stateful inference architecture that converts the $O(n_t)$ per-turn cost of conventional serving into an $O(Δ_t)$ delta-only cost: a persistent KV cache lives across turns and advances by ingesting only the new tokens, while a radix prefix cache extends this across interleaved multi-agent traffic and a prompt-lookup speculative decoder accelerates structured output. Against vLLM and SGLang on novel, fully-generated workloads, the reference implementation is $2.1\times$ faster per turn on a 6-turn agentic workflow and $4.2\times$ on the median turn of a 35-turn one, halving end-to-end wall time. The advantage comes from stateful reuse and speculation, not caching.

</details>


### [174] [Curriculum Learning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26315)
*Sandeep Kumar,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Staged-Competence，一个基于课程学习的框架，通过按难度组织偏好数据、能力采样和渐进更新参考模型，提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性和OOD泛化能力，在三个模型家族上平均降低16%有害响应率和20%越狱攻击成功率，同时保持通用能力且几乎不增加过度拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO在安全对齐中广泛使用但存在脆弱性，尤其是在OOD泛化方面表现不佳，现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出Staged-Competence框架：(1) 按难度组织偏好数据；(2) 采用基于能力的采样策略；(3) 训练过程中渐进更新参考模型。该框架与策略优化损失无关，可扩展到其他DPO变体。

Result: 在三个模型家族上平均OOD有害响应率降低16%，越狱攻击成功率降低20%，通用能力保持良好，过度拒答率接近零。仅用75%训练数据即可达到基线安全水平，且能更好分离安全与不安全响应。

Conclusion: Staged-Competence有效提升了DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性，是一种与损失函数无关、可迁移的通用框架。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) is widely used for safety alignment in large language models. However, prior work shows it is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation. In this paper, we investigate whether Curriculum Learning can improve the robustness of DPO-based safety alignment. We propose Staged-Competence, a curriculum-based framework that organises preference data by difficulty, employs competence-based sampling, and progressively updates the reference model during training. Averaged across three model families, Staged-Competence reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, while preserving general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal. We further show that Staged-Competence (1) matches baseline safety with only 75% of the training data and (2) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses. Staged-Competence is agnostic to the policy optimisation loss and can extend to other DPO variants and alignment domains. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety.

</details>


### [175] [Semigroup Consistency as a Diagnostic for Learned Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26324)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化半群误差（Normalized Semigroup Error），作为评估学习型物理模拟器在长时间滚动中时序组合能力的后验诊断指标，并通过一维热方程和Burgers动力学的实验验证其与滚动退化的正相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型物理模拟器通常仅通过单步或短时预测误差评估，无法捕捉时序组合与长滚动中的失败模式。为检测这些失败，需要一种能够反映长时间演化一致性的诊断指标。

Method: 利用自治、状态完备系统的半群性质（直接演化s+t步与先演化s步再演化t步应一致），定义归一化半群误差作为模型无关的后验诊断指标。在1D热方程和Burgers方程上，对时间条件ConvNet和FNO基线模型进行评估。

Result: 半群误差与滚动退化呈正相关，轨迹级Spearman相关系数ρ=0.635，95%置信区间[0.621, 0.649]。半群正则化的效果不一致，表明其主要作为评估诊断指标而非普适有效的训练目标。

Conclusion: 归一化半群误差是有价值的长期滚动评估诊断工具，但其作为训练正则项的效果有限，应优先用于模型评估而非训练。

Abstract: Learned physics simulators are often evaluated by one-step or short-horizon prediction error, but these metrics can miss failures in temporal composition and long-horizon rollout. For autonomous, state-complete systems, exact solution maps satisfy a semigroup law: direct evolution over $s+t$ should agree with evolution over $s$ followed by $t$. We propose normalized semigroup error as a post hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic comparing these direct and composed learned predictions. On one-dimensional heat and Burgers dynamics with time-conditioned ConvNet and FNO baselines, semigroup error is positively associated with rollout degradation, with trajectory-level Spearman correlation $ρ= 0.635$ and $95%$ CI $[0.621, 0.649]$. Semigroup regularization has mixed effects, supporting semigroup consistency primarily as an evaluation diagnostic rather than a universally beneficial training objective.

</details>


### [176] [Reparametrizing Shampoo and SOAP for Subspace Basis Updates and BFloat16 Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26327)
*Alan Milligan,Zikun Xu,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Felix Dangel,Wu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对Shampoo类方法（如KL-Shampoo、SOAP、KL-SOAP）中预条件子的重参数化方法，通过子空间QR分解只更新部分基向量，支持BFP16存储，在降低计算和内存开销的同时缓解了低精度存储带来的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shampoo类方法依赖QR分解，需使用FP32精度且计算昂贵，导致大预条件矩阵时时间和内存开销大；使用BFP16存储虽可减少内存但会损害性能。

Method: 提出预条件子的重参数化方法，将更新的基向量与未变化的基向量结合形成完整基，通过子空间中的QR分解仅更新部分基向量，支持BFP16存储。

Result: 该方法适用于KL-Shampoo、SOAP、KL-SOAP等使用QR分解的Shampoo类方法；在BFP16存储下提升了SOAP和KL-SOAP的性能，使KL-SOAP能够达到或超越KL-Shampoo；整体上使Shampoo类方法更节省内存和时间。

Conclusion: 该重参数化方法有效解决了Shampoo类方法中QR分解计算昂贵与低精度存储性能下降的问题，在保持性能的同时提升了内存和时间效率。

Abstract: Shampoo-based methods, such as KL-Shampoo and SOAP, have demonstrated strong performance in training neural networks and rely on QR decomposition. Because existing QR implementations require single-precision (FP32) arithmetic and remain computationally expensive, these methods become time- and memory-intensive when their preconditioning matrices are large. Moreover, using BFloat16 (BFP16) storage to reduce memory usage can degrade the performance of Shampoo-based methods. We propose a reparametrization of the preconditioner that supports BFP16 storage and forms a complete basis by combining updated basis vectors with unchanged ones. By updating only part of the basis through QR decomposition in a subspace, our approach reduces computational overhead while mitigating the performance degradation caused by BFP16 storage. Our approach applies broadly to Shampoo-based methods that employ QR decomposition, including KL-Shampoo, SOAP, and KL-SOAP. In particular, it improves the performance of SOAP and KL-SOAP under BFP16 storage, enabling KL-SOAP to match or exceed KL-Shampoo. Overall, our approach makes Shampoo-based methods more memory- and time-efficient.

</details>


### [177] [QAM-W: Joint 2D Codebook Quantization for LLM Weights via Hadamard Rotation and Activation-Aware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26339)
*Preetam Sharma,Kacper Dobek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QAM-W编解码器，通过L2归一化、块Hadamard旋转、2D坐标配对和Lloyd-Max量化恢复权重行内成对坐标结构，在~5.5 bpw下与SmoothQuant W8A8质量相当但节省32%权重比特，跨5个LLM验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量后训练量化器丢弃了权重行内的成对坐标结构，QAM-W旨在恢复该结构以提升量化效率。

Method: 每行进行L2归一化、块Hadamard旋转、配对为2D坐标，使用针对单位圆高斯训练的单Lloyd-Max码本进行量化，并引入激活感知的每通道缩放。

Result: ~5.5 bpw下BF16 WikiText-2困惑度偏差在±0.4%以内，匹配SmoothQuant W8A8质量但减少32%权重比特；2D联合编码优于极坐标编码2-15pp ΔPPL；KL与ΔPPL%的Spearman ρ=0.99。

Conclusion: QAM-W在5-6 bpw高质量区域有效，3.5 bpw在量化容忍架构上有竞争力，但严格4 bpw下不如QTIP。

Abstract: Scalar post-training quantizers discard pairwise coordinate structure within weight rows. We introduce QAM-W (Quadrature Amplitude Modulation for Weights), a codec that recovers this structure: each row is L2-normalized, block-Hadamard rotated, paired into 2D coordinates, and quantized against a single Lloyd-Max codebook trained on the unit circular Gaussian, with activation-aware per-channel scaling. In a cross-model study spanning five LLMs from four families (1.1B--13B parameters) and eight quantized configurations, the activation-aware variant at $\approx 5.5$ bpw stays within $\pm 0.4\%$ of BF16 WikiText-2 perplexity on every model, matching the SmoothQuant W8A8 quality envelope at $32\%$ fewer weight bits. Joint 2D coding outperforms polar (amplitude $\times$ phase) coding by 2--15~pp $Δ$PPL at equal bitrate, and paired KL against BF16 tracks $Δ$PPL\% at Spearman $ρ= 0.99$ across 37 (method, model) rows, consistent with a monotone composite bound from codec distortion to KL divergence. A 3.5~bpw variant is competitive on quantization-tolerant architectures. At strict 4~bpw, the rotated-codebook frontier method QTIP outperforms QAM-W; the contribution is the quality-preserving 5--6~bpw band.

</details>


### [178] [Trust Region Q Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27079)
*Yonghoon Dong,Kyungmin Lee,Changyeon Kim,Jaehyuk Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRQAM，一种稳定的离策略强化学习微调算法，通过投影对偶下降自适应控制预训练流策略的路径空间KL散度，在50个OGBench任务上以68%的成功率显著超越基线（46%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略强化学习微调预训练流策略因多步采样导致优化不稳定；QAM虽通过重构成无记忆SOC问题缓解此问题，但其批评引导改进存在固有脆弱性——批评器条件不良时微小误差被放大，常导致模型崩塌。

Method: 提出TRQAM算法：通过投影对偶下降优化SOC动力学中的置信域参数λ，理论证明路径空间KL可表示为λ的闭式函数，从而精确控制与预训练流策略的偏差，实现稳定的离策略强化学习。

Result: 在50个OGBench任务上，TRQAM在离线RL和离线到在线RL设置中均持续优于先前方法；离线RL成功率达68%，大幅超越最强基线（46%）。

Conclusion: TRQAM通过自适应置信域机制有效解决了离策略微调中流策略的稳定性问题，验证了精确控制策略偏差对于稳定离策略RL的关键作用。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning of pretrained flow policies remains challenging due to the instability of optimization arising from the multi-step sampling process. Recently, Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) addressed this issue by reformulating into a memoryless stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem with a learned critic. However, QAM inherits a fundamental fragility of critic-guided improvement: small critic errors are amplified when critics are ill-conditioned, often leading to model collapse. This paper introduces Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM), a stable off-policy fine-tuning algorithm that adaptively controls the path-space KL with pretrained flow policies through projected dual descent. Specifically, we optimize the trust-region parameter $λ$ in SOC dynamics, and theoretically show that the path-space KL can be represented by a closed-form function of $λ$. As a result, our method can precisely control the exact deviation from pretrained flow policies, achieving stable off-policy RL. Through experiments on 50 OGBench tasks, TRQAM consistently outperforms prior arts in both offline RL and offline-to-online RL. In particular, TRQAM achieves an overall success rate of 68% in offline RL, substantially improves the strongest baseline at 46%.

</details>


### [179] [A PAC-Bayesian View of Generalisation for Physics-Informed Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26341)
*Thien V. Nguyen,Amaury Habrard,Benjamin Guedj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个PAC-Bayesian框架来分析物理信息机器学习（PIML）在无界损失下的泛化特性，推导出与输入梯度范数相关的泛化界，并提出了自界感知学习算法，实验证明所得界非平凡且优于联合界基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIML在回归任务中表现出色，但其统计泛化性质尚不明确，现有分析依赖近似或稳定性论证，未能充分揭示物理结构如何影响有限数据下的泛化。

Method: 采用多任务视角统一处理数据保真项、PDE残差、初始和边界条件，利用PAC-Bayesian框架推导出复杂度与输入梯度范数相关的泛化界，并在Sobolev和Poincaré假设下实例化，提出自界感知学习算法。

Result: 在标准PDE基准上验证，所得泛化界非平凡、显著优于联合界基线，且可在训练过程中有效最小化。

Conclusion: 为PIML的泛化提供了原则性的统计基础，揭示了物理正则性与泛化之间的直接联系。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) integrates mechanistic knowledge, typically in the form of partial differential equations (PDE), into data-driven models. Despite strong empirical performance, its statistical generalisation properties remain poorly understood, particularly in the regression setting with unbounded losses. Existing analyses rely on approximation or stability arguments and do not fully capture how physical structure influences generalisation from finite data. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for PIML that provides high-probability generalisation guarantees in the presence of unbounded losses. We adopt a multi-task perspective that jointly treats data fidelity, PDE residuals, initial and boundary conditions, avoiding the looseness induced by standard union-bound approaches. Our analysis leverages the structure of physics-informed objectives to derive novel bounds where the complexity scales with input-gradient norms of the losses, revealing a direct link between physical regularity and generalisation. We instantiate this framework under Sobolev and Poincaré-type assumptions, yielding two classes of bounds that trade off statistical complexity and smoothness in different regimes. Building on these results, we propose a self-bounding-aware learning algorithm that directly optimises tractable surrogates of the derived bounds, along with a practical procedure to estimate the associated constants in realistic settings. Empirical evaluations on standard PDE benchmarks demonstrate that our bounds are non-vacuous, significantly tighter than union-bound baselines, and can be effectively minimised during training. Overall, our results provide a principled statistical foundation for the generalisation of physics-informed models.

</details>


### [180] [MechRL: Reinforcement Learning Agents Perform Circuit Discovery for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26343)
*Barsat Khadka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将电路发现重新表述为强化学习问题，训练一个PPO智能体在GPT-2 small的144个注意力头上进行零消融操作，通过对比奖励同时优化目标任务损害和通用预测损害。智能体在两个训练任务（induction和IOI）上达到每集最优表现，并在未见过的第三个任务（docstring completion）上通过best-of-five规划恢复了96%的oracle上限。其识别出的关键头与经典文献中因果非冗余的头高度一致，冗余头则被正确降权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机制可解释性方法在识别实现特定行为的注意力头电路时，每个新任务都需要定制化的分析流程，缺乏通用性和可迁移性。作者希望提出一种无需针对每个任务重新设计分析流程的自动化电路发现方法。

Method: 将电路发现建模为强化学习问题：智能体将GPT-2 small的144个注意力头作为离散动作空间，每个动作触发一次零消融（zero-ablation）并计算对比奖励——从目标任务的损害中减去对通用下一词预测的损害。使用PPO算法在向量化的多任务环境中同时训练induction和IOI两个任务。

Result: 单个PPO策略在两个训练任务上均达到每集最优（per-episode oracle）；在未见过的第三个任务（docstring completion）上，best-of-five规划恢复96%的oracle上限。智能体选中的头与经典文献中在单头消融下因果非冗余的头高度吻合，文献标记为冗余的类别则被正确降权。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合因果干预是识别机制电路单头瓶颈的可行且可迁移的方法，与现有的路径修补（path-patching）方法互补。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has identified small sets of attention heads that implement specific behaviours in transformer language models, but recovering these circuits typically requires a bespoke analytical pipeline for each new task. We recast circuit discovery as a reinforcement-learning problem. An agent operates over the 144 attention heads of GPT-2 small as a discrete action space; each action triggers a zero-ablation and a contrastive reward that subtracts the ablation's damage to general next-token prediction from its damage to the target task. A single PPO policy, trained on two tasks (induction and IOI) in a vectorised multi-task environment, attains the per-episode oracle on both training tasks and on a held-out third task (docstring completion). Its preferred heads coincide with the canonical heads of established literature on precisely the axes those papers identify as causally non-redundant under single-head ablation; the categories they identify as redundant are correctly de-prioritised by the agent. On the held-out task, best-of-five planning recovers 96\% of the oracle ceiling with no task signal supplied at evaluation. These results indicate that reinforcement learning over causal interventions is a viable, transferable substrate for identifying the single-head bottlenecks of mechanistic circuits, complementary to existing path-patching approaches.

</details>


### [181] [When Correct Demonstrations Hurt: Rethinking the Role of Exemplars in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26350)
*Chenghao Qiu,Chunli Peng,Yufeng Yang,Kuan-Hao Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了上下文学习中的一个反直觉现象：示范的正确性并不保证其效用，某些正确示范甚至可能降低ICL准确率。作者提出任务保持扰动框架，将这种失效模式形式化为上下文证据偏移，并通过实验证明扰动示范会显著降低ICL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL研究默认认为正确的输入-输出示范有助于模型学习，但直觉上正确的示范未必有用。作者希望通过揭示正确性-效用差距，推动对ICL机制的更深入理解。

Method: 提出任务保持扰动（task-preserving perturbations）框架：仅改变示范输入，同时保持该示范仍为同一任务的正确实例。包括标签更新扰动（任务相关语义变化，重新计算目标）和目标保持扰动（原始目标仍然有效）。将失效模式形式化为上下文证据偏移（contextual evidence shift）。

Result: 在情感分类、逻辑推理和数学应用题三个任务上，任务保持扰动示范会显著降低ICL性能，尤其对小模型、困难任务和高扰动比例的影响更为明显。

Conclusion: 稳健的ICL不仅需要评估示范的正确性，还需要评估示范如何影响上下文推理。这为理解和改进ICL提供了新方向。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is often motivated by the intuition that demonstrations help because they provide correct input-output examples. However, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: correctness does not guarantee exemplar utility, and some correct demonstrations can even reduce ICL accuracy. To study this correctness-utility gap, we introduce task-preserving perturbations, where only the exemplar input is changed, while the example remains a correct instance of the same task. Concretely, each perturbed exemplar is assigned the target induced by the task mapping. This framework covers both label-updating perturbations, where task-relevant semantics change and targets are recomputed, and stricter target-preserving perturbations, where the original target remains valid. We formalize the resulting failure mode as contextual evidence shift: task-preserving perturbations can change the effective mixture of evidence used by the model for contextual inference, thereby separating exemplar correctness from exemplar utility. Across sentiment classification, logical reasoning, and math word problems, we find that task-preserving perturbed demonstrations can substantially degrade ICL performance, especially for smaller models, harder tasks, and higher perturbation ratios. Our results show that robust ICL requires evaluating not only whether demonstrations are correct, but also how they influence contextual inference. Code is available at https://github.com/Chenghao-Qiu/Task-Preserving-ICL.

</details>


### [182] [Energy-Gated Attention and Wavelet Positional Encoding: Complementary Inductive Biases for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26355)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出能量门控注意力（EGA）和莫雷特位置编码（MoPE）两种互补组件，分别建模token的能量显著性（选择关注什么）和尺度选择性局部性（指定在什么位置范围关注），两者组合在字符级语言建模上表现出超加性效果（0.119验证损失提升，大于各自之和0.092+(-0.032)），验证了显著性和局部性作为互补归纳偏置的核心发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力机制将所有token视为等显著性、所有位置视为等局部性，缺乏对输入信息结构的感知；作者识别出两种标准注意力缺失的互补归纳偏置：能量显著性（哪些token集中了信息能量）和尺度选择性局部性（不同频率下位置影响的范围）。

Method: 1) 能量门控注意力（EGA）：通过单个线性投影学习key token嵌入的能量估计，对值聚合进行门控，实现选择性关注；2) 莫雷特位置编码（MoPE）：用可学习的高斯加窗小波替换固定正弦编码，自适应语料联合位置-频率定位，指定各尺度下注意力操作的位置范围。

Result: 在TinyShakespeare上（≤6M参数）：EGA单独提升0.092验证损失，MoPE单独下降0.032（低于基线），但两者组合提升0.119，呈现超加性；消融实验表明结构化谱先验（固定小波门控、尺度初始化头、固定正弦PE）始终不如其无约束可学习对应物。

Conclusion: 显著性和局部性是互补的归纳偏置，两者结合产生的超加性效果是核心实证发现——每个偏置解决另一个无法单独填补的空白；未来最重要方向是更大规模的多种子验证。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention computes pairwise token similarity but treats all tokens as equally salient and all positions as equally local, regardless of the informational structure of the input. We identify two complementary inductive biases that standard attention lacks: energy salience (which tokens concentrate informational energy, learned end-to-end without explicit frequency decomposition) and scale-selective locality (how far positional influence extends at each frequency, implemented via Morlet wavelet encoding). We address both with two simple components. Energy-Gated Attention (EGA) gates value aggregation by a learned energy estimate of key token embeddings, computed via a single linear projection; it selects what to attend to. Morlet Positional Encoding (MoPE) replaces fixed sinusoidal encodings with learned Gaussian-windowed wavelets that adapt the joint position-frequency localization to the corpus; it specifies where attention operates at each scale. On TinyShakespeare, EGA alone achieves +0.092 validation loss improvement over standard attention (+0.103 over Phase 1-3 baseline); MoPE alone is -0.032 (below baseline as a standalone encoding); but their combination achieves +0.119 -- more than the sum of parts. This superadditivity, observed across two independent training runs, is the central empirical finding: salience and locality are complementary inductive biases, each addressing a gap the other cannot fill alone. Ablations confirm that structured spectral priors (Morlet wavelet gates, scale-initialized heads, fixed sinusoidal PE) consistently underperform their unconstrained learned counterparts, while complementary learned components interact superadditively. All experiments are at small scale (<=6M parameters, character-level benchmarks, single seed); larger-scale multi-seed validation is the most important direction for future work.

</details>


### [183] [Balancing Plasticity and Stability with Fast and Slow Successor Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26357)
*Raymond Chua,Doina Precup,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG
