  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR研究在报告性能提升时，常将策略改进与预算不匹配、尝试膨胀/校准漂移、基准数据污染三个混淆因素混为一谈，导致对RLVR实际效果的测量被夸大，缺乏足够的验证可靠性。

Method: 采用预算匹配的复现实验（确保RLVR和基线使用相同计算预算）、部分提示污染探测方法（将受污染数据作为记忆探针而非推理证据）来量化三个混淆因素的影响。并提出了包含预算匹配饱和曲线（含方差）、校准与弃权追踪、评判器鲁棒性压力测试、显式污染筛查的评估标准。

Result: 许多广泛引用的RLVR性能差距在预算匹配、提示和数据集版本对齐、污染集处理后显著缩小或消失。当前测量方法系统性地夸大了RLVR的能力增益，同时掩盖了可靠性代价。

Conclusion: RLVR在可验证领域仍然是有效且可部署的，但推理能力增益在没有上述控制措施的情况下应被视为暂时的、未经充分验证的。建议采用所提出的最低评估标准来保证RLVR效果报告的可信度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a practical, scalable way to improve large language models on math, code, and other structured tasks. However, we argue that many headline RLVR gains are not yet well validated because reports often conflate policy improvement with three confounds: (i) budget mismatch between RLVR and baseline evaluations, (ii) attempt inflation and calibration drift that convert abstentions into confident answers, and (iii) benchmark data contamination. Using budget-matched reproductions and partial-prompt contamination probes, we find that several widely cited gaps shrink substantially or disappear once budgets, prompts, and dataset versions are matched and contaminated sets are treated as memorization probes rather than evidence of reasoning. This does not mean that RLVR is ineffective, but it implies that current measurements often overstate capability gains and obscure reliability costs. We therefore propose a compact, tax-aware minimum standard for RLVR training and evaluation: budget-matched saturation curves with variance, calibration, and abstention tracking, a judge-robustness stress test when LLM judges are used, and an explicit contamination screen. With these controls, RLVR remains effective and deployable in verifiable domains, but reasoning gains should be treated as provisional without them.

</details>


### [257] [DiVeQ: Differentiable Vector Quantization Using the Reparameterization Trick](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26469)
*Mohammad Hassan Vali,Tom Bäckström,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiVeQ和SF-DiVeQ两种向量量化方法，通过将量化视为添加模拟量化失真的误差向量来保持硬前向传播同时允许梯度流动，无需辅助损失或温度调度，在图像压缩、图像生成和语音编码任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型中常用的向量量化存在硬赋值导致梯度阻断、无法端到端训练的问题，需要设计一种既能保持硬量化的前向特性又能让梯度正常传播的量化方法。

Method: DiVeQ将量化过程视为添加一个模拟量化失真的误差向量，在保持前向硬赋值的同时允许梯度流动；SF-DiVeQ将输入分配到由码字间连线构成的曲线上，减少量化误差并实现码本完全利用。

Result: 在VQ-VAE图像压缩、VQGAN图像生成和DAC语音编码任务中，所提方法在重建质量和样本质量上均优于替代量化方法。

Conclusion: DiVeQ和SF-DiVeQ实现了端到端训练，无需辅助损失或温度调度，是一种有效的向量量化改进方案。

Abstract: Vector quantization is common in deep models, yet its hard assignments block gradients and hinder end-to-end training. We propose DiVeQ, which treats quantization as adding an error vector that mimics the quantization distortion, keeping the forward pass hard while letting gradients flow. We also present a space-filling variant (SF-DiVeQ) that assigns input to a curve constructed by the lines connecting codewords, resulting in less quantization error and full codebook usage. Both methods train end-to-end without requiring auxiliary losses or temperature schedules. In VQ-VAE image compression, VQGAN image generation, and DAC speech coding tasks across various data sets, our proposed methods improve reconstruction and sample quality over alternative quantization approaches.

</details>


### [258] [Monte Carlo Permutation Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06381)
*Tristan Cazenave*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCPS算法，通过改进GRAVE的探索项统计方式（纳入路径上所有模拟的统计信息），在多种游戏中超越GRAVE，并给出无偏置超参数的数学推导公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度强化学习不可用或计算资源受限的场景下（如通用游戏玩法），需要一种更高效的MCTS算法来替代现有的GRAVE算法。

Method: 在MCTS节点的探索项中，纳入从根节点到当前节点路径上所有模拟（playout）的统计信息；并基于数学推导改进了GRAVE的加权公式，消除了其偏置超参数。

Result: 在Hex、Go、AtariGo、NoGo和Wargame等多种游戏中，MCPS几乎始终优于GRAVE算法。

Conclusion: MCPS作为一种通用MCTS算法，在计算资源有限时比GRAVE更高效，且新加权公式无需人工设定偏置超参数。

Abstract: We propose Monte Carlo Permutation Search (MCPS), a general-purpose Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) algorithm that improves upon the GRAVE algorithm. MCPS is relevant when deep reinforcement learning is not an option or when the computing power available before play is not substantial, such as in General Game Playing. The principle of MCPS is to include in the exploration term of a node the statistics on all the playouts that contain all the moves on the path from the root to the node. We test MCPS on a variety of games: Hex, Go, AtariGo, NoGo and a Wargame. MCPS almost always outperforms GRAVE. We also provide a mathematical derivation of the formulas used for weighting the three sources of statistics. These formulas are an improvement on the GRAVE formula since they no longer use the bias hyperparameter of GRAVE.

</details>


### [259] [MATT-CTR: Unleashing a Model-Agnostic Test-Time Paradigm for CTR Prediction with Confidence-Guided Inference Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08932)
*Moyu Zhang,Yun Chen,Yujun Jin,Jinxin Hu,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MATT，一种模型无关的测试时范式，通过层级概率哈希估计特征组合置信度，并基于置信度采样生成多条推理路径进行聚合预测，以缓解低置信度特征组合对CTR模型推理阶段的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTR模型优化主要集中在训练阶段（模型架构和训练目标），忽视了推理阶段的优化机会。特别是低频特征组合会导致预测性能下降，产生不可靠或低置信度的输出。

Method: 1) 提出层级概率哈希方法估计不同阶特征组合的出现频率作为置信度分数；2) 以置信度为采样概率，通过迭代采样生成多条实例特定的推理路径；3) 聚合多条路径的预测分数进行鲁棒预测。

Result: 大量离线实验和在线A/B测试验证了MATT在现有CTR模型上的兼容性和有效性。

Conclusion: MATT作为一种模型无关的测试时范式，能够有效解锁已训练CTR模型的预测潜力，提升模型在推理阶段对低置信度特征组合的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recently, a growing body of research has focused on either optimizing CTR model architectures to better model feature interactions or refining training objectives to aid parameter learning, thereby achieving better predictive performance. However, previous efforts have primarily focused on the training phase, largely neglecting opportunities for optimization during the inference phase. Infrequently occurring feature combinations, in particular, can degrade prediction performance, leading to unreliable or low-confidence outputs. To unlock the predictive potential of trained CTR models, we propose a Model-Agnostic Test-Time paradigm (MATT), which leverages the confidence scores of feature combinations to guide the generation of multiple inference paths, thereby mitigating the influence of low-confidence features on the final prediction. Specifically, to quantify the confidence of feature combinations, we introduce a hierarchical probabilistic hashing method to estimate the occurrence frequencies of feature combinations at various orders, which serve as their corresponding confidence scores. Then, using the confidence scores as sampling probabilities, we generate multiple instance-specific inference paths through iterative sampling and subsequently aggregate the prediction scores from multiple paths to conduct robust predictions. Finally, extensive offline experiments and online A/B tests strongly validate the compatibility and effectiveness of MATT across existing CTR models.

</details>


### [260] [Cross-Receiver Generalization for RF Fingerprint Identification via Feature Disentanglement and Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09405)
*Yuhao Pan,Xiucheng Wang,Fushuo Huo,Nan Cheng,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种跨接收机鲁棒的射频指纹识别框架，通过解耦发射机和接收机特征，有效解决了实际部署中因接收机更换导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中，RF信号天然耦合了发射机特有特征与接收机相关失真，导致模型在训练和评估使用同一设备时能取得良好性能，但更换接收机会造成显著性能下降。现有方法未有效解决这一跨接收机泛化问题。

Method: 提出跨接收机鲁棒RFFI框架，包括：(1)对抗域对齐抑制发射机特征中的残余接收机信息；(2)接收机感知正则化增强接收机特定表征的域内一致性；(3)特征分离约束解耦潜在空间中两类特征成分。

Result: 在多接收机WiFi数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在跨接收机评估中持续优于现有最先进基线，显著提升了接收机更换时的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦发射机与接收机特征，该框架有效增强了RFFI系统在实际部署场景中的泛化能力，为解决接收机引入的可变性难题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Radio frequency fingerprint identification (RFFI) is a key technique for wireless network security, leveraging intrinsic hardware imperfections to enable transmitter identification. Although deep neural networks are effective at extracting discriminative RF features, their performance is significantly affected by receiver-induced variability in practical deployments. In real-world scenarios, RF signals inherently entangle transmitter-specific characteristics with receiver-dependent distortions, leading models to capture receiver-related patterns when training and evaluation are conducted on the same device. Consequently, replacing the receiver during deployment often results in notable performance degradation. To address this issue, we propose a cross-receiver robust RFFI framework that explicitly disentangles transmitter-specific and receiver-specific representations. The proposed method integrates adversarial domain alignment with receiver-aware regularization to suppress residual receiver information in transmitter features while enforcing intra-receiver consistency in receiver-specific representations. A feature separation constraint is further introduced to decouple the two components in the latent space. Extensive experiments on multi-receiver WiFi datasets demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under cross-receiver evaluation and significantly improves robustness to receiver replacement.

</details>


### [261] [Learning to Reason Efficiently with Discounted Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23486)
*Alex Ayoub,Kavosh Asadi,Dale Schuurmans,Csaba Szepesvári,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过折扣强化学习惩罚推理token的方法，在保持准确性的同时缩短思维链长度，将LRM推理建模为随机最短路径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型常消耗过多token，增加计算成本和延迟。作者挑战"更长响应提升准确性"的假设，希望在目标导向的顺序决策问题中快速达成目标。

Method: 采用折扣强化学习框架，通过惩罚推理token（可解释为小token成本），结合受限策略类中的Blackwell最优性分析，将问题建模为随机最短路径问题。

Result: 实验验证了理论结果：该方法在保持准确性的前提下有效缩短了思维链长度。

Conclusion: 更长的推理响应并非必要，通过适当的token成本惩罚可以在不牺牲准确率的情况下实现更简洁的推理。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) often consume excessive tokens, inflating computational cost and latency. More broadly, in goal reaching sequential decision problems we often want to reach the goal quickly, and LRM reasoning can be viewed through this lens. We challenge the assumption that longer responses improve accuracy. By penalizing reasoning tokens using a discounted reinforcement learning setup (interpretable as a small token cost) and analyzing Blackwell optimality in restricted policy classes, we encourage concise yet accurate reasoning, analogous to preferring shorter successful trajectories in a stochastic shortest path problem. Experiments confirm our theoretical results that this approach shortens chains of thought while preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [262] [An End-to-End Learning Approach for Solving Capacitated Location-Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02525)
*Changhao Miao,Yuntian Zhang,Tongyu Wu,Fang Deng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于深度强化学习（DRL）的端到端方法DRLHQ，用于求解容量约束选址路径问题（CLRP）及其开放变体（OCLRP），通过异构查询注意力机制有效处理选址与路径决策之间的相互依赖关系，在合成和基准数据集上均优于传统方法和DRL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLRP是组合优化中的经典难题，选址与路径决策之间存在复杂的约束和相互依赖关系，现有DRL方法主要针对车辆路径问题（VRP）及其变体，针对CLRP的研究仍十分匮乏。

Method: 提出DRLHQ方法，采用编码器-解码器结构，首次将CLRP建模为端到端学习问题；将CLRP重新表述为适用于多种决策的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），该框架可适配其他DRL方法；引入异构查询注意力机制，动态适应不同决策阶段，以更好地处理选址与路径决策间的相互依赖。

Result: 在合成数据集和基准数据集上的实验结果表明，DRLHQ在求解CLRP和OCLRP时，解决方案质量和泛化性能均优于代表性传统方法和DRL基线。

Conclusion: 提出的DRLHQ方法有效解决了CLRP及OCLRP问题，其端到端学习框架和异构查询注意力机制为处理选址-路径联合优化问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: The capacitated location-routing problems (CLRPs) are classical problems in combinatorial optimization, which require simultaneously making location and routing decisions. In CLRPs, the complex constraints and the intricate relationships between various decisions make the problem challenging to solve. With the emergence of deep reinforcement learning (DRL), it has been extensively applied to address the vehicle routing problem and its variants, while the research related to CLRPs still needs to be explored. In this paper, we propose the DRL with heterogeneous query (DRLHQ) to solve CLRP and open CLRP (OCLRP), respectively. We are the first to propose an end-to-end learning approach for CLRPs, following the encoder-decoder structure. In particular, we reformulate the CLRPs as a markov decision process tailored to various decisions, a general modeling framework that can be adapted to other DRL-based methods. To better handle the interdependency across location and routing decisions, we also introduce a novel heterogeneous querying attention mechanism designed to adapt dynamically to various decision-making stages. Experimental results on both synthetic and benchmark datasets demonstrate superior solution quality and better generalization performance of our proposed approach over representative traditional and DRL-based baselines in solving both CLRP and OCLRP.

</details>


### [263] [Transformers with RL or SFT Provably Learn Sparse Boolean Functions, But Differently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17852)
*Bochen Lyu,Yiyang Jia,Xiaohao Cai,Zhanxing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一分析了强化学习（RL）与监督微调（SFT）在一层Transformer中学习k-稀疏布尔函数的Chain-of-Thought能力，证明了k-PARITY、k-AND、k-OR三种函数的可学习性，并揭示了RL同时学习整个CoT链而SFT逐步学习CoT链的不同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强化学习和监督微调在触发Transformer的Chain-of-Thought推理能力方面的机制差异，特别是在中间推理步骤的学习动态上。

Method: 通过统一的数学框架分析过程奖励RL和SFT的学习动态，以k-稀疏布尔函数（可递归分解为固定2-稀疏布尔函数）为测试基准，推导可学习的充分条件，并在k-PARITY、k-AND、k-OR三个基本例子上验证。

Result: RL同时学习整个CoT链的所有步骤，而SFT则逐步、按顺序学习CoT链；两种方法均能成功学习三种k-稀疏布尔函数，但学习路径存在本质差异。

Conclusion: RL与SFT触发CoT能力的机制不同，比较二者需考虑奖励设计和教师强制（teacher forcing）的使用方式。

Abstract: Transformers can acquire Chain-of-Thought (CoT) capabilities to solve complex reasoning tasks through fine-tuning. Reinforcement learning (RL) and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) are two primary approaches to this end. In this work, we specifically examine RL with process rewards and SFT for learning $k$-sparse Boolean functions with a one-layer transformer through intermediate reasoning steps akin to CoT. In particular, we consider $k$-sparse Boolean functions that can be recursively decomposed into fixed 2-sparse Boolean functions. We first analyze the learning dynamics of RL fine-tuning with process reward and SFT in a unified way. This allows us to identify sufficient conditions under which the transformer provably learns these sparse Boolean functions. We then verify that these conditions hold for three basic examples, including $k$-PARITY, $k$-AND, and $k$-OR, thus demonstrating their learnability via both RL and SFT. Notably, we reveal that RL and SFT exhibit distinct learning behaviors: RL learns the whole CoT chain simultaneously, whereas SFT naturally learns the CoT chain step by step. Overall, our findings provide insights on the mechanisms underlying RL and SFT and how they differ in triggering the CoT capabilities of transformers, and suggest that the comparison between RL and SFT may need to consider the reward design and the use of teacher forcing.

</details>


### [264] [Normalizing Flows on Quotient Manifolds via Boundary Quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22882)
*William Ghanem,Benjamin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出边界商框架，用于学习商流形（如 N/G）上的密度，并通过构造正规化流实现对称感知学习，在透镜空间等商流形上取得高效且优质的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密度估计方法难以直接在具有非平凡拓扑或对称性的商流形（如 N/G）上高效建模，需要一种能够利用底层简单域结构进行学习的框架。

Method: 提出边界商（boundary quotients）概念，通过将商流形 N/G 视为更简单域 N 的边界商来构造密度；当离散群 G 作用在 N 上时，利用该框架构造正规化流（normalizing flows），并在亏格为 g 的曲面 Σ_g 上实例化。对于有限群 G，展示其在对称感知学习中的应用，以三维球面的循环商为例。

Result: 在透镜空间（lens spaces）上的实验表明，使用简单的商前（pre-quotient）RealNVP 模型能够取得强性能，同时显著降低评估成本。

Conclusion: 边界商框架提供了一种高效且通用的方法，用于在商流形上学习密度，尤其适用于具有对称性的场景，可在降低计算代价的同时保持良好性能。

Abstract: We introduce boundary quotients and present a framework for learning densities on manifolds that arise as boundary quotients of simpler domains. We show that this framework can be used to construct normalizing flows on quotient manifolds $N/G$, where a discrete group $G$ acts on $N$. We instantiate this construction for genus-$g$ surfaces $Σ_g$. When $G$ is finite, we show applicability to symmetry aware learning; we demonstrate this on cyclic quotients of the 3-sphere. Experiments on lens spaces show that simple pre-quotient RealNVP models can achieve strong results while being substantially cheaper to evaluate.

</details>


### [265] [Reconstructing Multi-Scale Physical Fields from Extremely Sparse Measurements with an Autoencoder-Diffusion Cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01572)
*Letian Yi,Tingpeng Zhang,Mingyuan Zhou,Guannan Wang,Quanke Su,Zhilu Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对极端传感器稀疏下的全场重建问题，提出Cascaded Sensing（Cas-Sensing）层级框架：先通过神经算子的粗尺度估计器确定全局结构锚点，再用条件扩散模型学习残差分布，结合掩码级联训练和流形约束引导，实现稳定且多模态的后验推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端传感器稀疏导致全场重建问题严重病态，后验分布严重欠约束且本质多模态，现有方法（确定性映射坍缩不确定性、直接条件学习无法覆盖解空间、似然引导采样对噪声和传感器配置敏感）均产生不稳定的后验估计，亟需一种结构化的不确定性建模方法。

Method: 提出Cas-Sensing层级框架：(1) 粗尺度确定性估计器：基于神经算子的功能自编码器，使用掩码输入训练，从稀疏观测映射到粗尺度结构场，充当最大后验估计器确定主导全局配置；(2) 细尺度条件扩散模型：仅学习残差分布，将采样约束在稳定邻域内，抑制观测一致模态间的竞争；(3) 掩码级联训练：通过多样化稀疏观测模式增强鲁棒性；(4) 流形约束引导：在推理时将观测一致性作为细化机制而非全局模态选择过程。

Result: 通过层级分解将病态后验推断转化为良态的残差推断任务，粗尺度锚定固定主要自由度，细尺度扩散模型在稳定邻域内采样，掩码级联训练提升对不同传感配置的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Cas-Sensing通过结构化层级分解有效解决了极端稀疏传感下的不确定性建模问题，为物理场重建提供了稳定且多模态的后验估计框架。

Abstract: Extreme sensor sparsity makes full-field reconstruction a fundamentally ill-posed problem in scientific sensing,where the goal is to infer physical fields from sparse measurements.In this regime,the posterior is severely underconstrained and inherently multimodal,making its approximation highly ill-conditioned.Specifically,deterministic mappings collapse uncertainty,direct conditional learning cannot cover the space of possible observation-conditioned solutions,and likelihood-guided sampling becomes highly sensitive to noise and sensor configurations.These limitations result in unstable posterior estimates and highlight the need for modeling uncertainty in a structural manner.To this end,we propose Cascaded Sensing,a hierarchical framework that restructures posterior inference across scales.Rather than modeling the full-field posterior directly,Cas-Sensing first resolves global structural ambiguity through a deterministic coarse-stage estimator.A neural-operator-based functional autoencoder,trained with masked inputs,maps sparse observations to a coarse-scale structural field,acting analogously to a maximum a posteriori estimator that selects the dominant global configuration.This structural anchor fixes the principal degrees of freedom of the posterior and transforms the problem into a better-conditioned residual inference task.A conditional diffusion model then learns only the refined-scale residual distribution,confining sampling to a stable neighborhood of plausible solutions and suppressing competition among observation-consistent modes.To enhance robustness under varying sensing conditions,we introduce mask-cascade training,which exposes the model to diverse sparse observation patterns through intermediate coarse reconstructions.During inference,manifold-constrained guidance enforces observation consistency as a refinement mechanism rather than a global mode-selection process.

</details>


### [266] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Antibody Language Models Using SAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05794)
*Rebonto Haque,Oliver M. Turnbull,Anisha Parsan,Nithin Parsan,John J. Yang,Anna L. Beukenhorst,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了TopK和Ordered稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在抗体语言模型可解释性中的应用，发现TopK SAEs擅长揭示生物有意义的潜在特征但因果控制能力不足，而Ordered SAEs通过层级结构实现可靠的生成控制但牺牲了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索稀疏自编码器在抗体语言模型（领域特定蛋白质语言模型）中的机械可解释性能力，并评估不同SAE变体在特征发现与生成控制方面的优劣。

Method: 在抗体语言模型上分别应用TopK SAEs和Ordered SAEs，分析其潜在特征与生物学概念的对应关系，并通过生成任务测试特征的因果控制能力。

Result: TopK SAEs能揭示生物学有意义的潜在特征，但高特征-概念相关性并不保证因果生成控制；Ordered SAEs能可靠识别可控制特征，但激活模式更复杂、可解释性更低。

Conclusion: 特征映射到概念时TopK SAEs已足够，但需要精确生成控制时Ordered SAEs更优。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are a mechanistic interpretability technique that have been used to provide insight into learned concepts within large protein language models. Here, we employ TopK and Ordered SAEs to investigate autoregressive antibody language models, and steer their generation. We show that TopK SAEs can reveal biologically meaningful latent features, but high feature-concept correlation does not guarantee causal control over generation. In contrast, Ordered SAEs impose a hierarchical structure that reliably identifies steerable features, but at the expense of more complex and less interpretable activation patterns. These findings advance the mechanistic interpretability of domain-specific protein language models and suggest that, while TopK SAEs suffice for mapping latent features to concepts, Ordered SAEs are preferable when precise generative steering is required.

</details>


### [267] [Training-Free Vector Quantization via Gaussian VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.06609)
*Tongda Xu,Wendi Zheng,Jiajun He,Jose Miguel Hernandez-Lobato,Yan Wang,Ya-Qin Zhang,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaussian Quant (GQ)方法，通过先训练带约束的高斯VAE再无需额外训练地转换为VQ-VAE，利用随机高斯噪声作为码本，在理论和实践上均优于现有VQ-VAE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ-VAE作为离散自编码器在压缩图像为离散token方面表现出色，但由于离散化过程导致训练困难，现有方法训练复杂且不稳定。

Method: GQ首先在目标散度约束（TDC）下训练高斯VAE，然后生成随机高斯噪声作为码本，通过寻找与后验均值最近的噪声向量完成向VQ-VAE的零额外训练转换。理论上证明了当码本大小的对数超过高斯VAE的bits-back编码率时，可保证较小的量化误差。

Result: 在UNet和ViT架构上，GQ均优于VQGAN、FSQ、LFQ、BSQ等先前VQ-VAE方法；TDC也改进了TokenBridge等先前高斯VAE离散化方法。

Conclusion: GQ提供了一种简单有效的VQ-VAE训练新范式，通过理论保证和实践启发式约束，实现了高质量离散图像压缩，且无需额外训练步骤。

Abstract: Vector-quantized variational autoencoders (VQ-VAEs) are discrete autoencoders that compress images into discrete tokens. However, they are difficult to train due to discretization. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective technique dubbed Gaussian Quant (GQ), which first trains a Gaussian VAE under certain constraints and then converts it into a VQ-VAE without additional training. For conversion, GQ generates random Gaussian noise as a codebook and finds the closest noise vector to the posterior mean. Theoretically, we prove that when the logarithm of the codebook size exceeds the bits-back coding rate of the Gaussian VAE, a small quantization error is guaranteed. Practically, we propose a heuristic to train Gaussian VAEs for effective conversion, named the target divergence constraint (TDC). Empirically, we show that GQ outperforms previous VQ-VAEs, such as VQGAN, FSQ, LFQ, and BSQ, on both UNet and ViT architectures. Furthermore, TDC also improves previous Gaussian VAE discretization methods, such as TokenBridge. The source code is provided in https://github.com/tongdaxu/VQ-VAE-from-Gaussian-VAE.

</details>


### [268] [A Multivariate Bernoulli-Based Sampling Method for Multi-Label Data with Application to Meta-Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08371)
*Simon Chung,Colby J. Vorland,Donna L. Maney,Andrew W. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑标签依赖性的多元伯努利分布加权采样算法，用于多标签数据集中平衡少数类标签的表示，在Web of Science等数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签数据集中标签频率差异大且存在依赖关系，现有采样方法难以在已知偏差下获取包含足够稀缺标签的样本，需要一种能考虑标签依赖性的采样算法。

Method: 以多元伯努利分布建模多标签问题，利用观测标签频率估计分布参数，为每个标签组合计算权重，通过加权采样获得目标分布特征并兼顾标签依赖性。

Result: 在包含64个生物医学类别的Web of Science文献等多数据集上，该方法保持了类别频率顺序，缩小了最常与最罕见类别间的频率差异，生成了更均衡的子样本，增强了少数类别的表示。

Conclusion: 该采样算法有效处理了多标签数据中的标签频率不均衡和依赖性问题，提升了少数类别的代表性。

Abstract: Datasets may contain observations with multiple labels. If the labels are not mutually exclusive, and if the labels vary greatly in frequency, obtaining a sample that includes sufficient observations with scarcer labels to make inferences about those labels, and which deviates from the population frequencies in a known manner, creates challenges. In this paper, we consider a multivariate Bernoulli distribution as our underlying distribution of a multi-label problem. We present a novel sampling algorithm that takes label dependencies into account. It uses observed label frequencies to estimate multivariate Bernoulli distribution parameters and calculates weights for each label combination. This approach ensures the weighted sampling acquires target distribution characteristics while accounting for label dependencies. We applied this approach to a variety of datasets, including a sample of research articles from Web of Science labeled with 64 biomedical topic categories. We aimed to preserve category frequency order, reduce frequency differences between most and least common categories, and account for category dependencies. This approach produced a more balanced sub-sample, enhancing the representation of minority categories.

</details>


### [269] [BUILD with Precision: Bottom-Up Inference of Linear DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16111)
*Hamed Ajorlou,Samuel Rey,Gonzalo Mateos,Geert Leus,Antonio G. Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BUILD是一种确定性逐步算法，利用线性高斯SEM等噪声方差下观测数据的集成精度矩阵的独特结构，通过识别叶节点及其父节点并迭代剪枝，从真实精度矩阵精确重构DAG；针对有限数据估计精度矩阵时的病态问题，提出周期性重估计策略提升鲁棒性，在合成基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中学习有向无环图(DAG)的结构是因果发现、统计信号处理和机器学习中的核心问题，现有方法在可识别性和计算复杂度上存在挑战。

Method: 利用线性高斯结构方程模型(SEM)等噪声方差条件下观测数据集成精度矩阵的独特结构，提出BUILD算法——一种确定性逐步算法，通过识别叶节点及其父节点，然后移除关联边来剪枝叶节点，逐步进行直至完全重构DAG。针对有限数据估计精度矩阵的病态问题，提出周期性重估计策略以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的合成基准上，BUILD与最先进的DAG学习算法相比表现出优越性能，同时提供了显式的复杂度控制。

Conclusion: BUILD算法利用精度矩阵的结构特性，通过自底向上的迭代剪枝策略实现DAG的精确重构，并引入周期性重估计机制缓解有限样本下的误差累积问题，在计算效率和恢复精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Learning the structure of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) from observational data is a central problem in causal discovery, statistical signal processing, and machine learning. Under a linear Gaussian structural equation model (SEM) with equal noise variances, the problem is identifiable and we show that the ensemble precision matrix of the observations exhibits a distinctive structure that facilitates DAG recovery. Exploiting this property, we propose BUILD (Bottom-Up Inference of Linear DAGs), a deterministic stepwise algorithm that identifies leaf nodes and their parents, then prunes the leaves by removing incident edges to proceed to the next step, exactly reconstructing the DAG from the true precision matrix. In practice, precision matrices must be estimated from finite data, and ill-conditioning may lead to error accumulation across BUILD steps. As a mitigation strategy, we periodically re-estimate the precision matrix (with less variables as leaves are pruned), trading off runtime for enhanced robustness. Reproducible results on challenging synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that BUILD compares favorably to state-of-the-art DAG learning algorithms, while offering an explicit handle on complexity.

</details>


### [270] [A Logical View of GNN-Style Computation and the Role of Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19332)
*Pablo Barceló,Floris Geerts,Matthias Lanzinger,Klara Pakhomenko,Jan Van den Bussche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了MPLang（一种通过线性消息传递和激活函数捕获GNN计算的语言）的数值和布尔表达能力。首先分析了无激活函数的A-MPLang片段，给出了其基于游走求和特征的表达能力刻画。对于有界激活函数，证明了（温和条件下）所有最终常数激活函数产生相同的表达力，并涵盖了先前为具有最终常数激活函数但无线性层的GNN建立的逻辑。最后，证明了线性层存在下无界和有界激活之间的首个表达能力分离：使用ReLU的MPLang在数值查询上严格强于使用最终常数激活函数（如截断ReLU）的MPLang。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN的表达能力是当前研究热点，但现有工作主要聚焦于布尔查询（如图同构测试），对数值查询的表达能力理解不足。特别是线性层与不同激活函数的组合如何影响GNN的表达力尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 通过形式语言MPLang对GNN的计算过程进行抽象，将其分解为线性消息传递和激活函数两部分，分别研究无激活函数(A-MPLang)、有界激活函数(最终常数激活)和无界激活函数(ReLU)片段的表达能力，并基于游走求和特征给出理论刻画。

Result: 1) 给出了A-MPLang基于游走求和特征的完整表达能力刻画；2) 证明了所有最终常数激活函数在数值和布尔查询上表达力等价，且统一了先前建立的相关逻辑；3) 首次证明了在线性层存在下，ReLU在数值查询上严格强于最终常数激活函数。

Conclusion: 线性层与无界激活函数（如ReLU）的组合能赋予GNN更强的数值表达能力，这一发现对理解GNN的设计选择（尤其是激活函数的选择）具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We study the numerical and Boolean expressiveness of MPLang, a declarative language that captures the computation of graph neural networks (GNNs) through linear message passing and activation functions. We begin with A-MPLang, the fragment without activation functions, and give a characterization of its expressive power in terms of walk-summed features. For bounded activation functions, we show that (under mild conditions) all eventually constant activations yield the same expressive power - numerical and Boolean - and that it subsumes previously established logics for GNNs with eventually constant activation functions but without linear layers. Finally, we prove the first expressive separation between unbounded and bounded activations in the presence of linear layers: MPLang with ReLU is strictly more powerful for numerical queries than MPLang with eventually constant activation functions, e.g., truncated ReLU. This hinges on subtle interactions between linear aggregation and eventually constant non-linearities, and it establishes that GNNs using ReLU are more expressive than those restricted to eventually constant activations and linear layers.

</details>


### [271] [An Empirical Study of Machine Learning Robustness and Scalability for Imbalanced Tabular Clinical Data in Emergency and Critical Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21602)
*Yusuf Brima,Marcellin Atemkeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在MIMIC-IV-ED和eICU两个临床数据库上，系统比较了六类模型（决策树、随机森林、XGBoost、TabNet、TabICL、TabPFN v2.6）在不平衡表格数据上的性能。结果显示，TabPFN v2.6和TabICL在MIMIC-IV-ED上表现最佳，XGBoost在eICU上最优；基于树的模型扩展性最好，而表格基础模型在性能和效率间提供了有竞争力的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科和ICU中临床数据高度不平衡，传统机器学习模型偏向多数类，导致对罕见但关键的临床结局（如病情恶化、急诊分诊）预测性能下降。现有研究缺乏对多种模型家族在不平衡临床表格数据上的系统比较。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV-ED和eICU两个数据库上，对决策树、随机森林、XGBoost、TabNet、TabICL和TabPFN v2.6六类模型进行评估。可训练模型使用贝叶斯超参数优化，基础模型在预训练推理模式下评估（无任务特定重加权）。使用Macro F1分数、对递增不平衡的鲁棒性和计算可扩展性作为评估指标，涵盖7项临床预测任务。

Result: MIMIC-IV-ED上TabPFN v2.6和TabICL的Macro F1平均排名最高，XGBoost具有竞争力；eICU上XGBoost表现最佳，基础模型表现中等。TabNet在不平衡加剧时性能退化最严重且计算成本最高。树方法随数据规模扩展性最好，基础模型任务适配成本低。

Conclusion: 无单一模型在所有临床场景中占优。表格基础模型正缩小与强基线模型的性能差距，同时提供独特的效率-性能权衡，可能有利于资源受限的临床环境。

Abstract: Every year, millions of patients pass through emergency departments and intensive care units, where clinicians must make high-stakes decisions under time pressure and uncertainty. Machine learning could support prediction of deterioration, triage, and rare critical outcomes, but clinical data are often severely imbalanced, biasing models toward majority classes and reducing predictive performance. Developing robust and efficient models for imbalanced clinical tabular data therefore remains an important challenge.
  We evaluated six model families on imbalanced tabular data from the MIMIC-IV-ED and eICU databases: Decision Tree, Random Forest, XGBoost, TabNet, TabICL, and TabPFN v2.6. Trainable models were optimized using Bayesian hyperparameter tuning, while foundation models were evaluated in their pretrained inference regime without task-specific reweighting. Models were assessed using Macro F1-score, robustness to increasing imbalance, and computational scalability across seven clinical prediction tasks.
  Results differed across datasets. On MIMIC-IV-ED, TabPFN v2.6 and TabICL achieved the strongest average Macro F1 ranks, with XGBoost remaining competitive. On eICU, XGBoost consistently performed best, followed by other tree-based methods, while foundation models achieved intermediate performance. Across both datasets, TabNet showed the largest degradation under increasing imbalance and the highest computational cost. Training-time analysis showed that tree-based methods scaled most favorably with dataset size, while foundation models offered low per-task adaptation cost.
  These findings suggest that no single model family dominates across all clinical settings. However, tabular foundation models are narrowing the performance gap with strong classical baselines while offering a distinct efficiency-performance trade-off that may benefit resource-constrained clinical environments.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Binary: Turning Partial Success into Dense Verifiable Rewards for Reinforcement Learning in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03525)
*Longwen Wang,Yirui Liu,Xuan'er Wu,Xiaohui Hu,Yuankai Fan,Kaidong Yu,Qizhen Weng,Wei Xi,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeRPO是一个将代码生成中测试用例的部分成功转化为可靠密集奖励的强化学习框架，通过校准基数偏差和融合全局结果，在几乎零额外开销下显著提升了代码生成的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成强化学习中，测试集级别的结果奖励虽然保证功能性正确但奖励稀疏，而外部奖励模型提供密集监督却存在对齐问题和额外开销。由于代码评估天然产生多个测试用例结果，部分成功（通过部分测试用例）提供了内在、可验证的密集监督来源，但如何系统性地利用这一信号是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出VeRPO框架：(1) 将部分成功奖励建模为加权和形式，理论分析发现存在基数偏差(cardinality bias)，导致策略更新过度偏向容易测试的成功而非前沿测试的进展；(2) 引入动态密度校准的局部奖励(density-calibrated local reward)，显式纠正基数偏差；(3) 将局部密集奖励与全局执行结果融合，增强与端到端功能正确性的一致性。

Result: 在多个基准和设置上的实验表明，VeRPO在pass@1上比基于结果奖励和奖励模型的基线方法提升高达+8.83%，时间开销可忽略（<0.02%），且零GPU内存额外开销。

Conclusion: VeRPO通过系统性地利用代码评估中天然存在的部分成功信号，提供了一种无需额外模型、零GPU内存开销的密集奖励方案，有效解决了奖励稀疏性和对齐问题，在代码生成RL中取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Effective reward design is a central challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) for code generation. Mainstream test-suite-level outcome rewards enforce functional correctness but induce sparsity, while external Reward Models (RMs) provide dense supervision at the cost of misalignment and additional overhead. Since code evaluation naturally yields multiple test-case-level outcomes, partial success, i.e., passing a subset of test cases, offers an intrinsic, verifiable source of dense supervision. In this paper, we propose VeRPO (Verifiable Dense Reward Policy Optimization), an RL framework that systematically turns verifiable partial success into reliable dense rewards. We analyze partial-success rewards using a weighted sum formulation, theoretically identifying a critical cardinality bias that causes policy updates to disproportionately favor gains from easy-test successes over progress on frontier tests. Based on this, VeRPO introduces a dynamic, density-calibrated local reward that explicitly corrects this bias and provides robust dense supervision from partial success. To enhance alignment with end-to-end functional correctness, VeRPO further integrates the local dense reward with global execution outcomes. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and settings demonstrate that VeRPO outperforms outcome-driven and RM-based baselines, achieving up to +8.83 pass@1 gain with negligible time cost (< 0.02%) and zero GPU memory overhead.

</details>


### [273] [Approximate Equivariance via Projection-based Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05028)
*Torben Berndt,Jan Stühmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于投影的正则化方法，通过将线性层分解为等变和非等变分量，在算子级别（而非逐点）惩罚非等变性，相较于基于样本的正则化器，在SO(3)群上显著提高了效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似等变方法通常采用基于样本的正则化器，依赖训练时的数据增强，样本复杂度高，特别是在SO(3)等连续群上。需要一种更高效的方法来在尊重对称性和拟合数据分布之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出基于投影的正则化器，利用线性层在等变和非等变分量上的正交分解，在算子级别（即完整群轨道上）而非逐点惩罚非等变性，并在空间域和谱域中精确高效地计算惩罚项。

Result: 在实验中，该方法在模型性能和效率上 consistently 优于先前的近似等变方法，相较于基于样本的正则化器实现了显著的运行时间增益。

Conclusion: 基于投影的算子级正则化是处理近似等变性的有效途径，兼具理论优雅性和实际计算优势。

Abstract: Equivariance is a powerful inductive bias in neural networks, improving generalisation and physical consistency. Recently, however, non-equivariant models have regained attention, due to their better runtime performance and imperfect symmetries that might arise in real-world applications. This has motivated the development of approximately equivariant models that strike a middle ground between respecting symmetries and fitting the data distribution. Existing approaches in this field usually apply sample-based regularisers which depend on data augmentation at training time, incurring a high sample complexity, in particular for continuous groups such as $SO(3)$. This work instead approaches approximate equivariance via a projection-based regulariser which leverages the orthogonal decomposition of linear layers into equivariant and non-equivariant components. In contrast to existing methods, this penalises non-equivariance at an operator level across the full group orbit, rather than point-wise. We present a mathematical framework for computing the non-equivariance penalty exactly and efficiently in both the spatial and spectral domain. In our experiments, our method consistently outperforms prior approximate equivariance approaches in both model performance and efficiency, achieving substantial runtime gains over sample-based regularisers.

</details>


### [274] [ORLoopBench: Solver-in-the-Loop Benchmarks for Self-Correction and Behavioral Rationality in Operations Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21008)
*Ruicheng Ao,David Simchi-Levi,Xinshang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ORLoopBench基准，将不可行模型修复形式化为求解器在环的马尔可夫决策过程，通过RLVR训练使8B模型在LP修复上超越前沿API（95.3% vs 92.4% RR@5），并揭示了代码再生中的语义漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准将运筹学视为从问题描述到求解器代码的一次性翻译，忽略了实践中关键的调试循环（识别IIS、冲突定位、修复），导致模型缺乏诊断和修复不可行模型的能力。

Method: 将不可行模型修复形式化为求解器在环的MDP，每次动作触发求解器重新执行和IIS重新计算；构建OR-Debug-Bench（5,362个LP/MILP修复实例）和OR-Bias-Bench（库存决策合理性评估）；采用求解器验证的RLVR（Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards）训练。

Result: 8B模型在LP修复上RR@5达95.3%，超越前沿API的92.4%；改进了诊断行为并迁移到MILP修复；发现可运行的再生MILP代码存在语义漂移（求解了错误的问题）。

Conclusion: 基于求解器预言机的过程级评估能够实现可靠的运筹学自我修正训练，暴露了传统一次性代码生成评估的局限性。

Abstract: Operations Research practitioners debug infeasible models through an iterative process: inspecting Irreducible Infeasible Subsystems ( IIS), identifying constraint conflicts, and repairing formulations until feasibility is restored. Existing LLM benchmarks mostly treat OR as one-shot translation from problem descriptions to solver code, omitting this diagnostic loop. We formalize infeasible-model repair as a solver-in-the-loop Markov Decision Process in which each action triggers solver re-execution and IIS recomputation, yielding deterministic, verifiable feedback. We introduce ORLoopBench, a benchmark suite with two components: OR-Debug-Bench releases 5,362 LP/MILP repair instances, while OR-Bias-Bench evaluates closed-form operational decision rationality across inventory settings. Solver-verified RLVR training enables an 8B model to surpass frontier APIs on LP repair (95.3% vs 92.4% RR @5), improves diagnostic behavior, and transfers to MILP repair. The same evaluation exposes semantic drift in whole-model code regeneration: feasible regenerated MILPs can solve the wrong problem. Process-level evaluation with solver oracles enables targeted training for reliable OR self-correction.

</details>


### [275] [ECSEL: Explainable Classification via Signomial Equation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21789)
*Adia Lumadjeng,Ilker Birbil,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECSEL，一种通过学习符号回归形式的符号方程（signomial equations）实现可解释分类的方法，既作为分类器也作为解释器，在标准符号回归基准上比现有方法恢复更多目标方程且计算量更少，分类精度媲美传统机器学习模型，并在真实场景中支持反事实推理和偏差检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多符号回归基准数据集本身具有简洁的符号方程结构，但现有可解释分类方法要么牺牲精度，要么解释性不足；作者希望构建一种既能保持高分类精度、又能提供结构化闭式表达式作为解释的分类方法。

Method: ECSEL直接学习符号方程（signomial equations）形式的闭式表达式，该表达式同时充当分类器和解释器；通过符号回归技术高效构造结构化表达式，并满足全局特征行为、决策边界分析和局部特征归因等可解释性性质。

Result: 在标准符号回归基准上，ECSEL恢复目标方程的比例高于现有最先进方法，且计算量显著更低；分类精度与成熟机器学习模型（如随机森林、XGBoost等）相当；在电子商务和欺诈检测两个真实案例中，学习到的方程能够揭示数据集偏差、支持反事实推理并提供可操作的洞察。

Conclusion: ECSEL成功地在可解释性与分类性能之间取得了平衡，通过符号方程形式同时实现了高精度分类和结构化的全局/局部解释，尤其适用于需要透明决策和因果推理的场景。

Abstract: We introduce ECSEL, an explainable classification method that learns formal expressions in the form of signomial equations, motivated by the observation that many symbolic regression benchmarks admit compact signomial structure. ECSEL directly constructs a structural, closed-form expression that serves as both a classifier and an explanation. On standard symbolic regression benchmarks, our method recovers a larger fraction of target equations than competing state-of-the-art approaches while requiring substantially less computation. Leveraging this efficiency, ECSEL achieves classification accuracy competitive with established machine learning models without sacrificing interpretability. Further, we show that ECSEL satisfies some desirable properties regarding global feature behavior, decision-boundary analysis, and local feature attributions. Experiments on benchmark datasets and two real-world case studies i.e., e-commerce and fraud detection, demonstrate that the learned equations expose dataset biases, support counterfactual reasoning, and yield actionable insights.

</details>


### [276] [Constrained Meta Reinforcement Learning with Provable Test-Time Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21845)
*Tingting Ni,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种带安全约束的元强化学习算法，在测试任务上具有可证明的安全性和样本复杂度保证，并给出了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元强化学习虽然在测试任务上提升了样本效率，但许多实际应用（如机器人、医疗）要求测试时满足安全约束，如何在保证安全的同时降低样本复杂度是一个未解决问题。

Method: 提出一种算法，在训练阶段学习的基础上对策略进行精炼，使测试阶段能快速学习近优策略，同时提供可证明的安全性和样本复杂度保证，并推导了匹配的下界以证明样本复杂度 tight。

Result: 算法在测试任务上具有可证明的安全性和最优的样本复杂度（匹配下界）。

Conclusion: 该方法填补了约束元强化学习中安全性与样本效率双重保证的空白，理论结果表明样本复杂度是最优的。

Abstract: Meta reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to leverage experience across a distribution of tasks on which the agent can train at will, enabling faster learning of optimal policies on new test tasks. Despite its success in improving sample complexity on test tasks, many real-world applications, such as robotics and healthcare, impose safety constraints during testing. Constrained meta RL provides a promising framework for integrating safety into meta RL. An open question in constrained meta RL is how to ensure safety of the policy on the real-world test task, while reducing the sample complexity and thus, enabling faster learning of optimal policies. To address this gap, we propose an algorithm that refines policies learned during training, with provable safety and sample complexity guarantees for learning a near optimal policy on the test tasks. We further derive a matching lower bound, showing that this sample complexity is tight.

</details>


### [277] [Graph is a Substrate Across Data Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22384)
*Ziming Li,Xiaoming Wu,Zehong Wang,Jiazheng Li,Yijun Tian,Jinhe Bi,Yunpu Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出G-Substrate，一个以共享图结构为中心的学习框架，通过统一结构模式和交错角色训练策略，使图表示能够跨模态和任务持久化积累，实验证明其优于任务隔离和朴素多任务学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图结构学习通常是模态和任务隔离的，图表示在各自任务上下文中构建后被丢弃，导致跨模态和任务的 structural regularities 被重复构建而非在中间图表示层面积累，因此需要研究如何组织图结构以使其能够跨异构模态和任务持久化积累。

Method: 提出G-Substrate框架，包含两个互补机制：1）统一结构模式（unified structural schema），确保跨异构模态和任务的图表示兼容性；2）交错角色训练策略（interleaved role-based training strategy），使同一图结构在学习过程中暴露于多种功能角色。

Result: 在多个领域、模态和任务上的实验表明，G-Substrate 优于任务隔离方法和朴素多任务学习方法。

Conclusion: 图结构可以作为持久化跨学习上下文的结构基质，通过统一结构模式和交错角色训练，能够有效积累跨模态和任务的图表示知识，提升多任务学习效果。

Abstract: Graphs provide a natural representation of relational structure that arises across diverse domains. Despite this ubiquity, graph structure is typically learned in a modality- and task-isolated manner, where graph representations are constructed within individual task contexts and discarded thereafter. As a result, structural regularities across modalities and tasks are repeatedly reconstructed rather than accumulated at the level of intermediate graph representations. This motivates a representation-learning question: how should graph structure be organized so that it can persist and accumulate across heterogeneous modalities and tasks? We adopt a representation-centric perspective in which graph structure is treated as a structural substrate that persists across learning contexts. To instantiate this perspective, we propose G-Substrate, a graph substrate framework that organizes learning around shared graph structures. G-Substrate comprises two complementary mechanisms: a unified structural schema that ensures compatibility among graph representations across heterogeneous modalities and tasks, and an interleaved role-based training strategy that exposes the same graph structure to multiple functional roles during learning. Experiments across multiple domains, modalities, and tasks show that G-Substrate outperforms task-isolated and naive multi-task learning methods. The codebase, model, and datasets are available at https://github.com/zmli6/G-Substrate.

</details>


### [278] [Probing the Knowledge Boundary: An Interactive Agentic Framework for Deep Knowledge Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00959)
*Yuheng Yang,Siqi Zhu,Tao Feng,Ge Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个交互式智能体框架，通过四种自适应探索策略和三级知识处理流水线，系统性地提取和量化LLM中的参数化知识。实验发现递归分类法最有效，存在知识缩放定律，以及通用模型与专业模型在知识提取上的不同表现特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多静态且对系统性知识探测支持有限，LLM作为压缩知识库时其真正包含的知识及知识边界尚不明确，因此需要一种有效的框架来系统提取和量化LLM的知识。

Method: 提出交互式智能体框架，包含四种自适应探索策略（在不同粒度上探测知识）；引入三级知识处理流水线：基于向量的去重过滤、基于LLM裁决的语义重叠消解、领域相关性审核。通过这组方法确保提取知识的质量。

Result: 递归分类法是最有效的探索策略；存在知识缩放定律（更大模型恢复更多知识）；发现Pass@1与Pass@k的权衡关系：领域专用模型初始准确率高但退化快，通用模型在长时间提取中保持稳定性能；不同模型家族因训练数据差异而呈现可测量的不同知识轮廓。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一套系统量化LLM参数化知识的方法，揭示了知识缩放定律、模型类型间的权衡特性以及预训练数据对模型知识轮廓的决定性影响。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can be seen as compressed knowledge bases, but it remains unclear what knowledge they truly contain and how far their knowledge boundary extends. Existing benchmarks are mostly static and provide limited support for systematic knowledge probing. In this paper, we propose an interactive agentic framework to systematically extract and quantify the knowledge of LLMs. Our method includes four adaptive exploration policies to probe knowledge at different granularity. To ensure the quality of extracted knowledge, we introduce a three-stage knowledge processing pipeline that combines vector-based filtering to remove strict duplicates, LLM-based adjudication to resolve ambiguous semantic overlap, and domain relevance auditing to retain valid knowledge units. Through extensive experiments, we find that Recursive Taxonomy is the most effective exploration strategy. We also observe a clear knowledge scaling law, where larger models consistently recover more knowledge. In addition, we identify a Pass@1 versus Pass@k trade-off: domain-specialized models achieve higher initial accuracy but experience rapid degradation, while general-purpose models maintain stable performance over extended extraction. Finally, our results show that differences in training data composition lead to distinct and measurable knowledge profiles across model families, reflecting how pretraining shapes each model's parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [279] [ECHO-2: A Large-Scale Distributed Rollout Framework for Cost-Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02192)
*Jingwei Song,Meng Chen,Jie Xiao,Qingnan Ren,Jiaqi Huang,Yangshen Deng,Chris Tong,Wanyi Chen,Suli Wang,Zhisheng Chen,Ziqian Bi,Shuo Lu,Yiqun Duan,Xu Wang,Rymon Yu,Lynn Ai,Eric Yang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ECHO-2是一个分布式强化学习框架，通过结合集中式学习与分布式推理、可控策略过时机制以及重叠流水线设计，在广域网环境下实现LLM后训练的成本效率提升，同时保持与强基线相当的RL奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式部署rollout执行可以利用更具成本效益的推理资源，但广域网环境下的协调和策略传播带来挑战。现有方法未充分考虑非可忽略的传播延迟对训练效率的影响。

Method: 1) 集中式学习与分布式rollout结合，将策略过时性作为用户可控参数；2) 提出基于重叠的容量模型，关联训练时间、传播延迟和rollout吞吐量；3) 采用对等辅助的流水线广播（peer-assisted pipelined broadcast）缓解传播瓶颈；4) 成本感知的异构工作节点激活策略。

Result: 在4B到32B参数的LLM上进行GRPO后训练实验，在真实广域网带宽条件下，ECHO-2显著提高了成本效率，同时保持了与强基线相当的RL奖励。

Conclusion: ECHO-2有效解决了分布式RL后训练中广域网传播延迟带来的挑战，证明了通过重叠设计和成本感知调度可以在不牺牲训练质量的前提下大幅降低部署成本。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a critical stage in post-training large language models (LLMs), involving repeated interaction between rollout generation, reward evaluation, and centralized learning. Distributing rollout execution offers opportunities to leverage more cost-efficient inference resources, but introduces challenges in wide-area coordination and policy dissemination. We present ECHO-2, a distributed RL framework for post-training with remote inference workers and non-negligible dissemination latency. ECHO-2 combines centralized learning with distributed rollouts and treats bounded policy staleness as a user-controlled parameter, enabling rollout generation, dissemination, and training to overlap. We introduce an overlap-based capacity model that relates training time, dissemination latency, and rollout throughput, yielding a practical provisioning rule for sustaining learner utilization. To mitigate dissemination bottlenecks and lower cost, ECHO-2 employs peer-assisted pipelined broadcast and cost-aware activation of heterogeneous workers. Experiments on GRPO post-training of LLMs ranging from 4B to 32B parameters under real wide-area bandwidth regimes show that ECHO-2 significantly improves cost efficiency while preserving RL reward comparable to strong baselines.

</details>


### [280] [Rank-Learner: Orthogonal Ranking of Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03517)
*Henri Arno,Dennis Frauen,Emil Javurek,Thomas Demeester,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rank-Learner，一种直接学习治疗效果排序的两阶段学习器，无需精确估计CATE，具有Neyman正交性，理论保证强，实验优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多决策问题只需对个体按治疗效果排序而非精确估计效果大小，但现有文献大多聚焦因果效应估计，直接学习治疗效果排序的问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Rank-Learner两阶段学习器：第一阶段估计 nuisance 函数；第二阶段通过成对学习目标直接优化治疗效果排序，无需显式CATE估计。该学习器具有Neyman正交性，对nuisance函数估计误差鲁棒，且支持任意机器学习模型。

Result: 大量实验表明Rank-Learner在排序任务上 consistently 优于标准CATE估计器和非正交排序方法。

Conclusion: 为 practitioners 提供了一种新的、正交的两阶段学习器，用于按治疗效果对个体进行排序，具有较强的理论保证和实证效果。

Abstract: Many decision-making problems require ranking individuals by their treatment effects rather than estimating the exact effect magnitudes. Examples include prioritizing patients for preventive care interventions, or ranking customers by the expected incremental impact of an advertisement. Surprisingly, while causal effect estimation has received substantial attention in the literature, the problem of directly learning rankings of treatment effects has largely remained unexplored. In this paper, we introduce Rank-Learner, a novel two-stage learner that directly learns the ranking of treatment effects from observational data. We first show that naive approaches based on precise treatment effect estimation solve a harder problem than necessary for ranking, while our Rank-Learner optimizes a pairwise learning objective that recovers the true treatment effect ordering, without explicit CATE estimation. We further show that our Rank-Learner is Neyman-orthogonal and thus comes with strong theoretical guarantees, including robustness to estimation errors in the nuisance functions. In addition, our Rank-Learner is model-agnostic, and can be instantiated with arbitrary machine learning models (e.g., neural networks). We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method through extensive experiments where Rank-Learner consistently outperforms standard CATE estimators and non-orthogonal ranking methods. Overall, we provide practitioners with a new, orthogonal two-stage learner for ranking individuals by their treatment effects.

</details>


### [281] [Stochastic Decision Horizons for Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04599)
*Nikola Milosevic,Leonard Franz,Daniel Haeufle,Georg Martius,Nico Scherf,Pavel Kolev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机决策视野（SDH）框架，用于解决带每步约束满足的强化学习问题。通过状态-动作持续概率在违反约束时有效缩短决策视野，开发了首个离策略且正则化的瞬时约束RL算法，并在90肌肉人形机器人控制和安全导航任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中，约束强化学习需要在每一步都满足约束（瞬时约束），而非仅在累积层面满足。现有CMDP方法难以保证每步约束满足，缺乏同时支持离策略学习和正则化的理论框架。

Method: 1) 提出SDH框架，违反约束时通过状态-动作持续概率缩短有效决策视野；2) 基于Control as Inference推导两种语义：吸收态语义（AS-SAC，仅幸存决策支付熵成本）和虚拟终止语义（VT-MPO，停止奖励累积但保持决策过程）；3) 引入违反深度分布（violation-depth profile）连接SDH与CMDP，证明SDH以违反总指数权重加权轨迹。

Result: 在90肌肉人形机器人H2190上，VT-MPO以4倍更少环境步数达到与SOTA相当的步态真实感；在Safety Gymnasium上，违反深度分布正确识别SDH表现优异的区域。

Conclusion: SDH为瞬时约束RL提供了理论严谨且实践有效的框架，揭示了违反深度分布是理解SDH与CMDP差异的关键：当违规事件发生在单一特征尺度时两者等价，当罕见深度违规与频繁浅层违规混合时则不等价。

Abstract: We propose stochastic decision horizons (SDH), a theoretically grounded framework for solving constrained RL problems with every-step constraint satisfaction, a desirable property in many real-world applications. In SDH, a constraint violation yields an effective shortening of horizon via a state-action continuation probability. Using Control as Inference, we develop the first off-policy and regularized algorithms for RL with instantaneous constraints. We identify two principled semantics for what counts as a decision after a violation. Absorbing-state semantics end the decision process, so only surviving decisions pay entropy cost, yielding max-entropy AS-SAC. Virtual-termination keeps the decision process alive while stopping reward credit, yielding KL-regularized VT-MPO. To connect SDH with CMDPs, we track how violations accumulate along trajectories (their violation-depth profile). SDH effectively weights each trajectory by the exponential of its total violation; this matches an additive CMDP budget exactly when violations occur at a single characteristic scale, and we pinpoint where it cannot: when rare, deep violations mix with frequent, shallow ones. Experiments validate the theory. On the 90-muscle H2190 humanoid (Hyfydy), VT-MPO matches state-of-the-art gait realism with $4\times$ fewer environment steps and substantially more stable training. On Safety Gymnasium, violation-depth profiles correctly identify the regimes in which SDH delivers strong reward-violation trade-offs. Experiments validate the theory. On the 90-muscle H2190 humanoid (Hyfydy), VT-MPO matches state-of-the-art gait realism with 4x fewer environment steps and substantially more stable training. On Safety Gymnasium, violation-depth profiles correctly identify the regimes in which SDH delivers strong reward-violation trade-offs.

</details>


### [282] [Rethinking the Trust Region in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04879)
*Penghui Qi,Xiangxin Zhou,Zichen Liu,Tianyu Pang,Chao Du,Min Lin,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DPPO，用直接策略散度估计替代PPO的比率裁剪机制，解决LLM微调中PPO对低概率token过度惩罚、高概率token约束不足的问题，并通过Binary和Top-K近似实现高效计算，在训练稳定性和效率上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO的比率裁剪机制基于采样token的单一蒙特卡洛估计，在LLM大词表场景下导致次优学习动态：低概率token被过度惩罚，高概率token的灾难性偏移却约束不足，造成训练低效和不稳定。

Method: 提出DPPO，用直接估计的策略散度（如TV或KL散度）替代启发式裁剪；为降低显存开销，引入Binary和Top-K两种高效近似方法，以极低开销捕获关键散度信息。

Result: 大量实验表明DPPO在训练稳定性和效率上优于现有方法，为基于RL的LLM微调提供了更稳健的基础。

Conclusion: DPPO通过更 principled 的散度约束解决了PPO在LLM场景下的结构性缺陷，是更稳健的RL微调方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a cornerstone for fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs), with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) serving as the de facto standard algorithm. Despite its ubiquity, we argue that the core ratio clipping mechanism in PPO is structurally ill-suited for the large vocabularies inherent to LLMs. PPO constrains policy updates based on the probability ratio of sampled tokens, which serves as a noisy single-sample Monte Carlo estimate of the true policy divergence. This creates a sub-optimal learning dynamic: updates to low-probability tokens are aggressively over-penalized, while potentially catastrophic shifts in high-probability tokens are under-constrained, leading to training inefficiency and instability. To address this, we propose Divergence Proximal Policy Optimization (DPPO), which substitutes heuristic clipping with a more principled constraint based on a direct estimate of policy divergence (e.g., Total Variation or KL). To avoid huge memory footprint, we introduce the efficient Binary and Top-K approximations to capture the essential divergence with negligible overhead. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that DPPO achieves superior training stability and efficiency compared to existing methods, offering a more robust foundation for RL-based LLM fine-tuning. Our code is available at https://github.com/sail-sg/Stable-RL.

</details>


### [283] [Position: Machine Learning for Heart Transplant Allocation Policy Optimization Should Account for Incentives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04990)
*Ioannis Anagnostides,Itai Zilberstein,Zachary W. Sollie,Arman Kilic,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出器官分配不仅是优化问题，更是涉及多方参与者的复杂博弈，当前基于机器学习的分配方法忽略了激励机制这一根本性障碍。以美国成人心脏移植为例，揭示了决策流程中的激励错配及其负面后果，呼吁下一代分配政策应具备激励意识，并提出了整合机制设计、策略分类、因果推断和社会选择的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前器官分配领域正从基于规则的固定系统转向机器学习和数据驱动优化，但这些方法忽略了参与方（器官获取组织、移植中心、临床医生、患者、监管机构）之间的激励错配问题，导致实际执行中出现负面后果。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），通过分析美国成人心脏移植分配流程，识别各决策环节中的激励错配，并基于数据展示其当前造成的负面影响，进而提出研究议程。

Result: 识别出器官分配决策管线中存在显著的激励错配，且这些错配在当前已造成不良后果。现有机器学习方法因忽视激励因素而无法保证分配系统的鲁棒性、效率、公平性和可信度。

Conclusion: 下一代器官分配政策应当具备激励意识（incentive-aware），机器学习社区需要整合机制设计、策略分类、因果推断和社会选择等工具，以应对各参与方的策略性行为。

Abstract: The allocation of scarce donor organs constitutes one of the most consequential algorithmic challenges in healthcare. While the field is rapidly transitioning from rigid, rule-based systems to machine learning and data-driven optimization, we argue that current approaches often overlook a fundamental barrier: incentives. In this position paper, we highlight that organ allocation is not merely an optimization problem, but rather a complex game involving organ procurement organizations, transplant centers, clinicians, patients, and regulators. Focusing on US adult heart transplant allocation, we identify critical incentive misalignments across the decision-making pipeline, and present data showing that they are having adverse consequences today. Our main position is that the next generation of allocation policies should be incentive aware. We outline a research agenda for the machine learning community, calling for the integration of mechanism design, strategic classification, causal inference, and social choice to ensure robustness, efficiency, fairness, and trust in the face of strategic behavior from the various constituent groups.

</details>


### [284] [Constructing Industrial-Scale Optimization Modeling Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10450)
*Zhong Li,Hongliang Lu,Tao Wei,Yuxuan Chen,Wenyu Liu,Yuan Lan,Fan Zhang,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIPLIB-NL基准，通过结构感知的反向构建方法从真实MIP实例生成自然语言到优化公式的映射，揭示了现有系统在工业规模问题上的严重性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将自然语言需求转化为优化公式和求解器代码仍高度依赖人工，LLM虽被探索用于此任务，但现有评估仅基于玩具级或合成基准，无法反映工业规模问题（变量和约束达10³–10⁶）的真实难度，缺乏与真实优化模型对应的自然语言-参考公式/代码对齐的基准。

Method: 采用结构感知的反向构建方法论：（i）从扁平求解器公式中恢复紧凑、可复用的模型结构；（ii）基于统一模型-数据分离格式，反向生成与恢复结构显式绑定的自然语言规范；（iii）通过专家评审、人-LLM交互及独立重建检查执行迭代语义验证。

Result: 构建了223个一对一重建实例，保留原始MIP实例的数学内容，实现现实的NL-to-optimization评估。实验表明，在现有基准上表现优异的系统在MIPLIB-NL上性能显著下降，暴露了玩具规模下不可见的失败模式。

Conclusion: MIPLIB-NL填补了真实工业级自然语言到优化公式评估基准的空白，揭示了当前LLM方法在处理规模化、结构化优化问题时的根本性局限，为后续研究提供了更具挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: Optimization modeling underpins decision-making in logistics, manufacturing, energy, and finance, yet translating natural-language requirements into correct optimization formulations and solver-executable code remains labor-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) have been explored for this task, evaluation is still dominated by toy-sized or synthetic benchmarks, masking the difficulty of industrial problems with $10^{3}$--$10^{6}$ (or more) variables and constraints. A key bottleneck is the lack of benchmarks that align natural-language specifications with reference formulations/solver code grounded in real optimization models. To fill in this gap, we introduce MIPLIB-NL, built via a structure-aware reverse construction methodology from real mixed-integer linear programs in MIPLIB~2017. Our pipeline (i) recovers compact, reusable model structure from flat solver formulations, (ii) reverse-generates natural-language specifications explicitly tied to this recovered structure under a unified model--data separation format, and (iii) performs iterative semantic validation through expert review and human--LLM interaction with independent reconstruction checks. This yields 223 one-to-one reconstructions that preserve the mathematical content of the original instances while enabling realistic natural-language-to-optimization evaluation. Experiments show substantial performance degradation on MIPLIB-NL for systems that perform strongly on existing benchmarks, exposing failure modes invisible at toy scale.

</details>


### [285] [Vital Trace: Protocol-Constrained Patient-State Reasoning for Longitudinal Clinical Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12833)
*Zhan Qu,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vital Trace是一个基于协议约束的多智能体框架，通过紧凑的持久化患者状态记忆和四个协调智能体（路由器、推理者、审计者、管理者）进行阶段性推理，结合全局协议中的生理状态转换规则，实现长程ICU轨迹上的临床风险预测。在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上，相比自由形式的多智能体基线，该方法在时间一致性、通信稳定性、校准性和可解释性方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的临床推理系统在处理长程电子健康记录时，由于反复序列化患者历史或无限制的文本智能体消息交换，导致上下文漂移、推理不稳定以及推理成本不断增长。

Method: Vital Trace采用协议约束的多智能体框架，包含：(1) 紧凑的持久化患者状态记忆代替无界文本历史；(2) 四个协调智能体（路由器、推理者、审计者、管理者）执行阶段性推理；(3) 人工整理的全局协议，包含生理状态转换规则和动态患者状态表示，追踪血流动力学、呼吸、肾脏、代谢和炎症不稳定性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上，针对未来血管升压支持、呼吸支持、肾脏支持和病情恶化预测任务，结构化协议约束推理在时间一致性、通信稳定性、校准性和可解释性方面优于自由形式多智能体基线，同时在长程ICU轨迹上保持强预测性能。

Conclusion: 协议约束的结构化推理能够有效改善长程临床推理中的上下文漂移和通信不稳定问题，为ICU临床风险预测提供更可靠、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical reasoning over electronic health records requires tracking evolving physiological measurements, laboratory results, and interventions across extended patient trajectories. Existing LLM-based clinical reasoning systems often rely on repeatedly serializing patient histories or exchanging unconstrained textual agent messages, leading to context drift, unstable reasoning, and growing inference cost over long horizons. We present Vital Trace, a protocol-constrained multi-agent framework for future clinical risk prediction over evolving ICU trajectories. Instead of maintaining unbounded textual histories, Vital Trace uses a compact persistent patient-state memory together with staged reasoning performed by four coordinated agents: a Router, Reasoner, Auditor, and Steward. To support temporally coherent reasoning, we introduce a manually curated Global Protocol containing physiological state-transition rules and a dynamic patient-state representation that tracks hemodynamic, respiratory, renal, metabolic, and inflammatory instability over time. We evaluate Vital Trace on MIMIC-IV and eICU using future vasopressor-support, respiratory-support, renal-support, and deterioration prediction tasks. Results show that structured protocol-constrained reasoning improves temporal consistency, communication stability, calibration, and interpretability compared with free-form multi-agent baselines while achieving strong predictive performance across long ICU trajectories.

</details>


### [286] [Benchmark Leakage Trap: Can We Trust LLM-based Recommendation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13626)
*Mingqiao Zhang,Qiyao Peng,Yinghui Wang,Hongtao Liu,Yumeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现并验证了大语言模型（LLM）在推荐系统中存在基准数据泄露问题——LLM在预训练或微调阶段可能记忆了评测基准数据，导致性能指标虚高。通过模拟多种泄露场景（域内/跨域数据混合持续预训练），实验表明：域相关泄露会带来虚假的性能提升，域无关泄露则通常降低推荐准确率，揭示了数据泄露作为LLM推荐中一个关键但此前被忽视的影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推荐系统中广泛应用，但评估可靠性面临挑战。此前未被关注的问题是：LLM可能在预训练或微调阶段接触并记忆了评测基准数据集，导致性能指标被人为抬高，无法反映真实模型能力。

Method: 通过设计多种数据泄露场景模拟实验，对基础模型进行持续预训练，使用策略性混合语料（包含域内和跨域的用户-物品交互数据），分别在域相关和域无关泄露条件下评估推荐性能。

Result: 数据泄露具有双重效应：域相关泄露导致显著但虚假的性能提升，夸大模型真实能力；域无关泄露则通常降低推荐准确率。这表明数据泄露的影响具有复杂性和条件依赖性。

Conclusion: 数据泄露是LLM推荐中一个关键但此前未被充分考虑的污染因素，会影响模型真实性能的评估。研究揭示了评估LLM推荐系统时需要考虑数据泄露问题的必要性。

Abstract: The expanding integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into recommender systems poses critical challenges to evaluation reliability. This paper identifies and investigates a previously overlooked issue: benchmark data leakage in LLM-based recommendation. This phenomenon occurs when LLMs are exposed to and potentially memorize benchmark datasets during pre-training or fine-tuning, leading to artificially inflated performance metrics that fail to reflect true model performance. To validate this phenomenon, we simulate diverse data leakage scenarios by conducting continued pre-training of foundation models on strategically blended corpora, which include user-item interactions from both in-domain and out-of-domain sources. Our experiments reveal a dual-effect of data leakage: when the leaked data is domain-relevant, it induces substantial but spurious performance gains, misleadingly exaggerating the model's capability. In contrast, domain-irrelevant leakage typically degrades recommendation accuracy, highlighting the complex and contingent nature of this contamination. Our findings reveal that data leakage acts as a critical, previously unaccounted-for factor in LLM-based recommendation, which could impact the true model performance. We release our code at https://github.com/yusba1/LLMRec-Data-Leakage.

</details>


### [287] [GICDM: Mitigating Hubness for Reliable Distance-Based Generative Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16449)
*Nicolas Salvy,Hugues Talbot,Bertrand Thirion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生成模型评估中使用的嵌入空间存在枢纽性现象（hubness），这会扭曲最近邻关系并导致距离度量产生偏差。为此，提出生成式迭代上下文差异性度量（GICDM），结合多尺度扩展，有效纠正了枢纽性引起的度量失效，在合成和真实基准上恢复可靠评估，并与人类判断更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型评估通常依赖高维嵌入空间中的距离度量，但这些空间存在枢纽性现象（hubness），导致最近邻关系失真、距离度量产生偏差，从而影响评估可靠性。

Method: 基于经典迭代上下文差异性度量（ICDM），提出生成式ICDM（GICDM），用于同时校正真实数据和生成数据的邻域估计，并引入多尺度扩展以改善实际表现。

Result: 在合成数据和真实基准上的大量实验表明，GICDM能够解决由枢纽性引起的评估失效，恢复可靠的度量行为，并提升与人类评估结果的一致性。

Conclusion: GICDM有效缓解了嵌入空间中的枢纽性现象对生成模型评估造成的偏差，是一种更可靠、更符合人类判断的评估方法。

Abstract: Generative model evaluation commonly relies on high-dimensional embedding spaces to compute distances between samples. We show that dataset representations in these spaces are affected by the hubness phenomenon, which distorts nearest-neighbor relationships and biases distance-based metrics. Building on the classical Iterative Contextual Dissimilarity Measure (ICDM), we introduce Generative ICDM (GICDM), a method to correct neighborhood estimation for both real and generated data. We introduce a multi-scale extension to improve empirical behavior. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real benchmarks demonstrate that GICDM resolves hubness-induced failures, restores reliable metric behavior, and improves alignment with human assessment.

</details>


### [288] [GUI-Libra: Training Native GUI Agents to Reason and Act with Action-aware Supervision and Partially Verifiable RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22190)
*Rui Yang,Qianhui Wu,Zhaoyang Wang,Hanyang Chen,Ke Yang,Hao Cheng,Huaxiu Yao,Baolin Peng,Huan Zhang,Jianfeng Gao,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUI-Libra提出了一套针对GUI智能体的定制化后训练方案，通过动作感知的SFT、基于KL正则化的RLVR训练以及81K推理数据集，有效弥合了开源与闭源GUI智能体在长程导航任务上的差距，显著提升了步骤准确率和任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源GUI智能体在长程导航任务上落后于闭源系统，根源在于：（1）缺乏高质量、与动作对齐的推理数据；（2）直接套用通用后训练流程，忽视了GUI智能体的独特挑战——标准CoT推理的SFT会损害grounding能力，以及逐步RLVR训练面临部分可验证性问题（多个动作可能正确但仅有一个示范动作被用于验证）。

Method: (1) 构建并筛选了81K GUI推理数据集，缓解动作对齐推理数据稀缺问题；(2) 提出动作感知SFT（Action-aware SFT），混合推理-然后-动作与直接动作数据，并通过token重加权强调动作和grounding；(3) 引入KL正则化作为RLVR训练的信任区域，改善离线到在线的可预测性，并提出成功自适应缩放（success-adaptive scaling）以降低不可靠负梯度的影响。

Result: 在多个Web和Mobile基准测试中，GUI-Libra一致提升了步骤准确率和端到端任务完成率，无需昂贵的在线数据收集即可实现更强的任务求解能力。

Conclusion: 精心设计的后训练方案和数据筛选可以显著解锁GUI智能体的任务求解能力。作者开源了数据集、代码和模型，以促进面向推理能力的GUI智能体数据高效后训练研究。

Abstract: Open-source native GUI agents still lag behind closed-source systems on long-horizon navigation tasks. This gap stems from two limitations: a shortage of high-quality, action-aligned reasoning data, and the direct adoption of generic post-training pipelines that overlook the unique challenges of GUI agents. We identify two fundamental issues in these pipelines: (i) standard SFT with CoT reasoning often hurts grounding, and (ii) step-wise RLVR-tyle training faces partial verifiability, where multiple actions can be correct but only a single demonstrated action is used for verification. This makes offline step-wise metrics weak predictors of online task success. In this work, we present GUI-Libra, a tailored training recipe that addresses these challenges. First, to mitigate the scarcity of action-aligned reasoning data, we introduce a data construction and filtering pipeline and release a curated 81K GUI reasoning dataset. Second, to reconcile reasoning with grounding, we propose action-aware SFT that mixes reasoning-then-action and direct-action data and reweights tokens to emphasize action and grounding. Third, to stabilize RL under partial verifiability, we identify the overlooked importance of KL regularization in RLVR and show that a KL trust region is critical for improving offline-to-online predictability; we further introduce success-adaptive scaling to downweight unreliable negative gradients. Across diverse web and mobile benchmarks, GUI-Libra consistently improves both step-wise accuracy and end-to-end task completion. Our results suggest that carefully designed post-training and data curation can unlock significantly stronger task-solving capabilities without costly online data collection. We release our dataset, code, and models to facilitate further research on data-efficient post-training for reasoning-capable GUI agents.

</details>


### [289] [Phase-Type Variational Autoencoders for Heavy-Tailed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01800)
*Abdelhakim Ziani,András Horváth,Paolo Ballarini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Phase-Type变分自编码器（PH-VAE），通过将解码器分布定义为连续时间马尔可夫链的吸收时间（即Phase-Type分布），实现对重尾数据的灵活建模，在合成和真实基准测试中优于高斯、Student-t和极值分布基线的VAE解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE使用简单解码器分布（如高斯分布）无法捕捉真实世界数据中普遍存在的重尾行为，而现有的重尾感知扩展局限于预定义的参数化族，其尾部行为固定先验，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出PH-VAE，其解码器分布为潜在条件化的Phase-Type（PH）分布，即连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）的吸收时间，通过组合多个指数时间尺度实现灵活且解析可解的解码器，能够直接从观测数据自适应学习有限范围内的尾部行为。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中，PH-VAE准确逼近多种重尾分布，在建模观测尾部行为和极端分位数上显著优于高斯、Student-t和基于极值分布的VAE解码器；在多变量设置中，通过共享潜在表示捕捉真实的跨维尾部依赖。

Conclusion: 这是首项将Phase-Type分布集成到深度生成建模中的工作，桥接了应用概率论与表示学习，为重尾数据建模提供了一种灵活且解析可解的新范式。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed distributions are ubiquitous in real-world data, where rare but extreme events dominate risk and variability. However, standard Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) employ simple decoder distributions, such as Gaussian distributions, that fail to capture heavy-tailed behavior, while existing heavy-tail-aware extensions remain restricted to predefined parametric families whose tail behavior is fixed a priori. We propose the Phase-Type Variational Autoencoder (PH-VAE), whose decoder distribution is a latent-conditioned Phase-Type (PH) distribution, defined as the absorption time of a continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC). This formulation composes multiple exponential time scales, yielding a flexible and analytically tractable decoder that adapts its finite-range tail behavior directly from the observed data. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that PH-VAE accurately approximates diverse heavy-tailed distributions, significantly outperforming Gaussian, Student-t, and extreme-value-based VAE decoders in modeling observed tail behavior and extreme quantiles. In multivariate settings, PH-VAE captures realistic cross-dimensional tail dependence through its shared latent representation.
  To our knowledge, this is the first work to integrate Phase-Type distributions into deep generative modeling, bridging applied probability and representation learning.

</details>


### [290] [Disentangled Representation Learning through Unsupervised Symmetry Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11790)
*Barthélémy Dang-Nhu,Louis Annabi,Sylvain Argentieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需强先验知识或限制性子群假设的对称性解耦表征学习方法，让具身智能体通过与环境的无监督交互自主发现动作空间的群结构，并证明了在最小假设下真实对称群分解的可辨识性，在两个算法（群分解发现与线性对称解耦表征学习）上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对称性的解耦表征学习需要强先验知识（如对称群的具体结构）或对子群性质施加限制性假设，这限制了方法的普适性和自动化程度。本文旨在移除这些约束，让智能体通过自主交互发现环境中的对称群结构。

Method: 1) 理论证明：在最小假设下证明了真实对称群分解的可辨识性；2) 算法一：从交互数据中发现群分解；3) 算法二：学习线性对称解耦表征（LSBD），无需假设具体的子群性质。

Result: 在三个具有不同群分解的环境中，该方法在所有场景下均优于现有的LSBD方法。

Conclusion: 通过让智能体自主发现动作空间的群结构，可以在无需强先验知识的条件下实现有效的对称性解耦表征学习，为具身智能体的无监督解耦学习提供了更通用的框架。

Abstract: Symmetry-based disentangled representation learning leverages the group structure of environment transformations to uncover the latent factors of variation. Prior approaches to symmetry-based disentanglement have required strong prior knowledge of the symmetry group's structure, or restrictive assumptions about the subgroup properties. In this work, we remove these constraints by proposing a method whereby an embodied agent autonomously discovers the group structure of its action space through unsupervised interaction with the environment. We prove the identifiability of the true symmetry group decomposition under minimal assumptions, and derive two algorithms: one for discovering the group decomposition from interaction data, and another for learning Linear Symmetry-Based Disentangled (LSBD) representations without assuming specific subgroup properties. Our method is validated on three environments exhibiting different group decompositions, where it outperforms existing LSBD approaches.

</details>


### [291] [FedTreeLoRA: Reconciling Statistical and Functional Heterogeneity in Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13282)
*Jieming Bian,Lei Wang,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTreeLoRA 提出了一种树状聚合结构，在联邦微调大语言模型时，动态地为不同层构建不同的聚合层级，解决统计异质性与功能异质性耦合的问题，实现泛化与个性化的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA个性化方法仅处理客户端间的统计异质性（水平维度），却忽略了LLM不同层之间的功能异质性（垂直维度），这两者正交但相互耦合，导致最优参数共享深度依赖于客户端相似度，现有方法无法灵活应对。

Method: 提出 FedTreeLoRA 框架，采用树状结构聚合，根据客户端相似度动态构建聚合层级：浅层（trunks）进行广泛共享以捕捉共识，深层（branches）逐步特化以保留个性化。

Result: 在 NLU 和 NLG 基准测试上显著优于现有最先进方法，有效调和了泛化能力与个性化需求之间的矛盾。

Conclusion: 首次将统计异质性与功能异质性两个维度统一建模，证明了层粒度对齐的重要性，为联邦LLM微调的个性化设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a standard for privacy-preserving LLM fine-tuning. However, existing personalized methods predominantly operated under a restrictive Flat-Model Assumption: they addressed client-side \textit{statistical heterogeneity} but treated the model as a monolithic block, ignoring the \textit{functional heterogeneity} across LLM layers. We argue that these two statistical (horizontal) and functional (vertical) dimensions, are \textit{orthogonal in source yet coupled in interaction}, implying that the optimal depth of parameter sharing is functionally dependent on client similarity. To address this, we propose \textbf{FedTreeLoRA}, a framework employing tree-structured aggregation for fine-grained, layer-wise alignment. By dynamically constructing an aggregation hierarchy, FedTreeLoRA allows clients to share broad consensus on shallow `trunks' while progressively specializing on deep `branches'. Experiments on NLU and NLG benchmarks demonstrate that FedTreeLoRA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods by effectively reconciling generalization and personalization.

</details>


### [292] [Beyond Linearity in Attention Projections: The Case for Nonlinear Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13381)
*Marko Karbevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在Transformer的Query投影中用非线性残差结构（$Q(X)=X+f_\theta(X)$）替代原始的线性投影$W_Q$，其中$f_\theta$是瓶颈MLP。这一设计基于此前发现$W_Q$可被设为恒等映射而不影响性能的理论基础。实验表明，在GPT-3 small风格模型上，该方法相比基线模型验证集log-loss降低2.40%，困惑度降低6.81%，甚至优于参数增加12.5%的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论分析表明Transformer中的Query投影矩阵$W_Q$可被设为恒等映射而不影响性能，这表明$W_Q$的线性变换可能并非必要。作者希望利用这一发现，用更灵活的非线性结构替代$W_Q$，在保持或提升性能的同时不显著增加模型参数量。

Method: 将标准Transformer中的Query线性投影$W_Q \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d}$替换为非线性残差结构$Q(X) = X + f_\theta(X)$，其中$f_\theta$是瓶颈MLP（参数量为$d^2 + O(d)$）。恒等项$X$起到锚定作用，使非线性变换基于已知的良好先验进行微调。

Result: 在GPT-3 small风格模型上，该方法相比基线模型取得了一致改进：验证集log-loss降低2.40%，困惑度降低6.81%。性能甚至优于参数量增加12.5%的更大模型。

Conclusion: 非线性Query残差结构在较小规模上优于标准线性投影，值得在大规模模型和跨模态场景中进一步探索。

Abstract: Recent algebraic analysis shows that in decoder-only and encoder-only transformers, the Query projection $W_Q$ may be set to identity without noticeable performance deterioration. This is possible because attention depends on $X$ only through the products $XW_Q, XW_K, XW_V$, allowing basis transformations to be absorbed by adjacent layers and propagated through the network. We replace $W_Q \in \R^{d \times d}$ with a nonlinear residual of the form $Q(X) = X + f_θ(X)$, where $f_θ$ is a bottleneck MLP with $d^2 + O(d)$ parameters. The identity term anchors the nonlinearity to a known-good prior. Experiments on GPT-3 small style models show consistent improvement over the baseline ($2.40\%$ lower validation log-loss, $6.81\%$ lower perplexity), comfortably outperforming a model with 12.5\% more non-embedding parameters. These results motivate investigation at larger scales and across modalities.

</details>


### [293] [Flow Matching Policy Optimization with Mirror Descent and Entropy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17685)
*Ting Gao,Stavros Orfanoudakis,Nan Lin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Hoogendoorn,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMER框架，利用基于ODE的流匹配解决SDE扩散策略在熵计算和策略梯度方面的局限性，通过优势加权条件流匹配损失实现无仿真策略优化，并引入解析熵目标校正tanh变换导致的密度失真，在稀疏奖励任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习中平衡策略表达力与探索-利用权衡是核心挑战。现有SDE扩散策略存在两个关键问题：随机逆过程导致熵不可解（需启发式探索），以及通过长去噪链计算策略梯度昂贵且不稳定。

Method: 1) 理论论证优势加权条件流匹配损失可作为策略镜像下降的无仿真替代；2) 推导解析熵目标校正tanh变换导致的密度失真；3) 基于有效样本量动态调整镜像下降温度以构建鲁棒信任区域。

Result: 在稀疏奖励FrankaKitchen环境中实现优越性能，在标准密集奖励MuJoCo基准测试中保持竞争性结果。

Conclusion: 基于ODE的流匹配天然解决了SDE扩散策略的熵可解性和策略梯度计算问题，FMER框架通过无仿真优化、解析熵校正和动态温度调节实现了更优的强化学习性能。

Abstract: Balancing policy expressiveness with the exploration-exploitation trade-off is a core challenge in online Reinforcement Learning (RL). While Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE)-based diffusion policies can represent complex, multimodal action distributions, they suffer from two critical limitations: their stochastic reverse processes render entropy intractable (necessitating heuristic exploration), and computing policy gradients through long denoising chains is expensive and unstable. In this work, we show that ODE-based flow matching inherently resolves these issues by enabling both simulation-free policy optimization and tractable entropy computation. Building on this, we introduce Flow Matching Policy Optimization with Mirror Descent and Entropy Constraints (FMER). Our framework exploits this insight in three ways. First, we theoretically establish that minimizing an advantage-weighted conditional flow matching loss acts as a simulation-free surrogate for policy mirror descent. This steers the velocity field toward high-value regions while entirely avoiding backpropagation through the ODE solver. Second, we derive an analytic entropy objective that corrects for the density distortion caused by the $\tanh$ transformation (mapping an unbounded latent space to bounded actions), thereby facilitating principled maximum-entropy optimization. Finally, we dynamically tune the mirror descent temperature based on the effective sample size to enforce a robust trust region during training. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FMER achieves superior performance on the challenging sparse-reward FrankaKitchen environment, while maintaining competitive results across standard dense-reward MuJoCo benchmarks.

</details>


### [294] [Diet Your LLM: Dimension-wise Global Pruning of LLMs via Merging Task-specific Importance Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23985)
*Jimyung Hong,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIET是一种无需训练的、结合维度级粒度与任务感知选择的LLM结构化剪枝方法，通过跨任务激活幅度分析并采用多数投票构建全局掩码，在7个零样本基准上相比现有方法显著提升精度（Gemma-2 2B模型20%稀疏度下平均精度提升近10%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法面临关键权衡：任务无关方法无法适应特定任务需求，而任务感知方法需要昂贵训练来学习任务适应性。需要一种既具备任务感知能力又无需额外训练成本的结构化剪枝方法。

Method: DIET方法包含三个步骤：(1) 使用每任务仅100个样本分析激活幅度；(2) 计算维度级任务重要性分数；(3) 通过多数投票机制合并各任务重要性分数，构建单一全局剪枝掩码。整个过程无需预计算或训练。

Result: 在Gemma-2 2B和9B模型上的7个零样本基准测试中，DIET显著优于现有方法。例如在Gemma-2 2B模型20%稀疏度下，平均精度提升近10%。该优势在不同稀疏度和模型规模下均保持一致。

Conclusion: DIET定位为一种实用且鲁棒的结构化LLM剪枝选择，它在维度级粒度与任务感知之间取得了有效平衡，同时避免了昂贵的训练成本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, but their massive scale poses significant challenges for practical deployment. Structured pruning offers a promising solution by removing entire dimensions or layers, yet existing methods face critical trade-offs: task-agnostic approaches cannot adapt to task-specific requirements, while task-aware methods require costly training to learn task adaptability.
