We propose DIET (Dimension-wise global pruning of LLMs via merging Task-wise importance scores), a training-free structured pruning method that combines dimension-level granularity with task-aware selection. DIET profiles activation magnitudes across tasks using only 100 samples per task, then applies majority voting to construct a single global mask. DIET does not require large costs from pre-computation or training. Experiments on seven zero-shot benchmarks using Gemma-2 2B and 9B models demonstrate the effectiveness of DIET; for example, at 20% sparsity on Gemma-2 2B, DIET achieves near 10% average accuracy improvement, compared to previous state-of-the-art structured pruning methods. This advantage persists across various sparsity levels and model scales, positioning DIET as a practical and robust choice for structured LLM pruning.

</details>


### [295] [OCR-Reasoning Benchmark: Unveiling the True Capabilities of MLLMs in Complex Text-Rich Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17163)
*Mingxin Huang,Yongxin Shi,Dezhi Peng,Songxuan Lai,Zecheng Xie,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OCR-Reasoning基准，专门评估多模态大模型在文本丰富图像中的推理能力，包含1,069个人工标注样本，涵盖6种核心推理能力和18项实际推理任务，并提供答案和逐步推理过程的双重标注。评估表明最先进模型在该基准上准确率均低于50%，显示文本丰富图像推理仍是一个亟待解决的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态慢思考系统在视觉推理任务中表现优异，但在文本丰富图像推理任务上的能力缺乏专门的系统化基准评估。

Method: 构建OCR-Reasoning基准，包含1,069个人工标注样本，覆盖6种核心推理能力和18种实际推理任务，提供最终答案和逐步推理过程的双重标注。

Result: 最先进的多模态大模型在文本丰富图像推理任务中准确率均低于50%，表现困难显著。

Conclusion: 文本丰富图像推理仍是一个亟待解决的重要挑战，现有MLLMs在该任务上能力严重不足。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal slow-thinking systems have demonstrated remarkable performance across various visual reasoning tasks. However, their capabilities in text-rich image reasoning tasks remain understudied due to the absence of a dedicated and systematic benchmark. To address this gap, we propose OCR-Reasoning, a novel benchmark designed to systematically assess Multimodal Large Language Models on text-rich image reasoning tasks. Specifically, OCR-Reasoning comprises 1,069 human-annotated examples spanning 6 core reasoning abilities and 18 practical reasoning tasks in text-rich visual scenarios. Unlike existing text-rich image understanding benchmarks that only provide a final answer, this benchmark additionally provides a detailed step-by-step reasoning process. This dual annotation enables the evaluation of both the models' final answers and their reasoning processes, thereby offering a holistic assessment of text-rich reasoning capabilities. By leveraging this benchmark, we conducted a comprehensive evaluation of the latest MLLMs. Our results demonstrate that even the most advanced MLLMs exhibit substantial difficulties in text-rich image reasoning tasks, with none achieving an accuracy above 50\% on our benchmark, indicating that the challenges of text-rich image reasoning are an urgent issue to be addressed. The benchmark and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/SCUT-DLVCLab/OCR-Reasoning.

</details>


### [296] [Understanding the Challenges in Iterative Generative Optimization with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23994)
*Allen Nie,Xavier Daull,Zhiyi Kuang,Abhinav Akkiraju,Anish Chaudhuri,Max Piasevoli,Ryan Rong,YuCheng Yuan,Prerit Choudhary,Shannon Xiao,Rasool Fakoor,Adith Swaminathan,Ching-An Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式优化利用LLM迭代改进工件，但实践中仅9%的智能体采用自动化优化，原因在于设置学习循环时存在隐藏设计选择（起始工件、信用分配、批处理策略），这些选择在不同领域缺乏通用设置方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式优化（使用LLM迭代改进代码、工作流或提示）是构建自我改进智能体的有前途方法，但在实践中仍然脆弱，仅有9%的智能体使用任何自动化优化。研究者希望揭示这种脆弱性的根源。

Method: 通过MLAgentBench、Atari和BigBench Extra Hard三个案例研究，系统调查了三个关键因素：起始工件（确定可达解空间）、执行轨迹的信用分配范围（截断轨迹仍可改进Atari智能体）、以及将试错分批为学习证据的策略（更大批次并不单调提升BBEH泛化性能）。

Result: 发现这些设计决策可以决定生成式优化能否成功，但它们在先前工作中很少被明确讨论。不同的起始工件决定了MLAgentBench中哪些解可达，截断轨迹仍能改进Atari智能体，更大批次并不单调提升BBEH上的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 缺乏一种简单、通用的跨领域学习循环设置方式是生产化和采用的主要障碍。研究提供了这些选择的实用指导。

Abstract: Generative optimization uses large language models (LLMs) to iteratively improve artifacts (such as code, workflows or prompts) using execution feedback. It is a promising approach to building self-improving agents, yet in practice remains brittle: despite active research, only 9% of surveyed agents used any automated optimization. We argue that this brittleness arises because, to set up a learning loop, an engineer must make ``hidden'' design choices: What can the optimizer edit and what is the "right" learning evidence to provide at each update? We investigate three factors that affect most applications: the starting artifact, the credit horizon for execution traces, and batching trials and errors into learning evidence. Through case studies in MLAgentBench, Atari, and BigBench Extra Hard, we find that these design decisions can determine whether generative optimization succeeds, yet they are rarely made explicit in prior work. Different starting artifacts determine which solutions are reachable in MLAgentBench, truncated traces can still improve Atari agents, and larger minibatches do not monotonically improve generalization on BBEH. We conclude that the lack of a simple, universal way to set up learning loops across domains is a major hurdle for productionization and adoption. We provide practical guidance for making these choices.

</details>


### [297] [Where Hindsight Credit Can Reside: A Signed-Capacity View of Token Updates in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11056)
*Yuhang He,Haodong Wu,Siyi Liu,Hongyu Ge,Hange Zhou,Keyi Wu,Zhuo Zheng,Qihong Lin,Zixin Zhong,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HAPO方法，通过四象限分解将token熵与奖励极性结合进行信用分配，在数学推理任务上取得竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏结果奖励导致token级信用分配困难，需要更好地利用token熵信息来指导策略更新。

Method: 1. 将token级信用定义为从行为策略到事后后验的奖励条件偏移；2. 通过条件互信息(CMI)证明token熵上界决定可能的信用；3. 提出四象限分解，按奖励极性和token熵将更新分开；4. 提出HAPO，对GRPO进行符号保持的修改，实现容量引导的优势重新分配。

Result: HAPO在数学推理基准上，在两种模型设置下均达到具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 高熵象限中的符号化更新是持续推理能力提升的关键，低熵更新则快速饱和。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning ability of Large Language Models (LLMs), but sparse outcome rewards make token-level credit assignment difficult.
  We study token-level credit as a reward-conditioned shift from the behavior policy to a hindsight posterior.
  In autoregressive RLVR, this shift can be expressed through Conditional Mutual Information (CMI), which shows that token entropy upper-bounds possible hindsight credit.
  Entropy, however, indicates capacity rather than update direction, so we introduce the Four Quadrant Decomposition to separate updates by reward polarity and token entropy.
  Controlled interventions show that these two factors jointly shape token updates.
  Sustained reasoning gains concentrate in signed high-entropy quadrants, whereas low-entropy updates saturate quickly.
  Based on this analysis, we propose Hindsight-Aware Policy Optimization (HAPO), a sign-preserving modification to GRPO that performs capacity-guided advantage reallocation.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks in two model settings show that HAPO achieves competitive performance among entropy-aware baselines.

</details>


### [298] [CUDABeaver: Benchmarking LLM-Based Automated CUDA Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08455)
*Shiyang Li,Haoyang Chen,Mattia Fazzini,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CUDABEAVER基准测试和pass@k(M,C,A)指标，用于评估LLM修复CUDA程序缺陷的真实能力，发现传统评估忽略性能退化问题，修复器在严格性能约束下成功率可骤降40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUDA程序调试因硬件行为、编译器决策、内存层级和异步执行等复杂交互而具有挑战性。现有LLM的CUDA编程评估存在漏洞：模型可以通过"退化性修复"（将代码简化为更慢但能通过测试的版本）来通过正确性测试，掩盖了真实修复能力。

Method: 构建CUDABEAVER基准测试（213个任务），每个任务包含损坏的候选代码、原生构建/测试命令、原始错误证据和一个可编辑文件。提出pass@k(M,C,A)协议条件指标，将修复器M、语料库C和协议轴A（包括性能损失容忍度）显式纳入评估。

Result: 在7个前沿LLM上的实验表明：协议感知评估能更真实反映CUDA调试能力；高性能损失容忍度下修复器表现强，但严格性能要求会使成功率骤降，分数变化高达40个百分点。

Conclusion: 现有LLM修复CUDA程序的评估因忽视性能退化而高估了模型能力，CUDABEAVER和pass@k(M,C,A)指标提供了更严谨的评估框架，揭示了协议条件对评测结果的显著影响。

Abstract: Debugging CUDA programs has long been challenging because failures often arise from subtle interactions among hardware behavior, compiler decisions, memory hierarchy, and asynchronous execution. More importantly, with the rapid expansion of GPU usage across scientific computing, machine learning, graphics, and systems workloads, CUDA debugging has become more challenging than ever. Current evaluations of LLM-based CUDA programming largely miss this setting: a model can pass correctness tests with repair by degeneration, simplifying the CUDA code into a safer but slower program that abandons the original optimization structure. We introduce CUDABEAVER, a benchmark for CUDA debugging from real failing workspaces produced during LLM-based CUDA generation. Each task provides the broken candidate, native build/test commands, raw error evidence, and a single editable file. CUDABEAVER evaluates whether a fixer truly repairs the failing CUDA code or merely finds a slower test-passing replacement, reporting results by failure category, debugging trajectory, stagnation mode, and performance preservation. We further propose pass@k(M,C,A), a protocol-conditional CUDA debugging metric by making the fixer M, corpus C, and protocol axes Aexplicit. Using this metric across 213 tasks and seven frontier LLMs, we show that protocol-aware evaluation gives a more faithful view of CUDA debugging ability: when performance-loss tolerance is high, fixers appear much stronger, but even a minor stricter performance requirement can sharply reduce measured success, shifting scores by up to 40 percentage points.

</details>


### [299] [Test-Time Compute for Dense Retrieval: Agentic Program Generation with Frozen Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11374)
*Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现测试时计算（test-time compute）不仅对大型推理模型有益，也能提升小型嵌入模型的检索质量。通过一个无参数、无外部模型的智能体程序搜索循环，在冻结编码器API上探索144个候选程序，发现了12个帕累托最优程序，这些程序通过增加推理计算来提升检索质量。该搜索独立重新发现了Rocchio伪相关反馈、ColBERT风格句子级MaxSim、倒数排序融合和Fisher线性判别等方法。所有前沿程序在全部14个搜索任务上均提升了nDCG@10。一个从发现前沿中选出的单一固定程序，在三个未见过的编码器家族和19个未见过的检索任务中，在61%的模型-任务组合上提升了nDCG@10，无需任何逐任务选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索测试时计算是否能够提升小型嵌入模型的检索性能，而不需要重新训练模型或依赖外部参数，从而验证冻结编码器也能从额外推理计算中获益。

Method: 采用智能体程序搜索循环（agentic program-search loop），在冻结编码器API上探索144个候选程序，通过测试时计算生成12个帕累托最优程序，这些程序在不引入可训练参数或外部模型的前提下，实现额外推理计算与检索质量的权衡。

Result: 所有前沿程序在14个搜索任务上均提升了nDCG@10指标；一个从发现前沿选出的单一固定程序，在三个未见编码器家族和19个未见任务上，对61%的模型-任务对实现了nDCG@10提升。

Conclusion: 测试时计算不仅可以提升大型推理模型，也能有效提升小型嵌入模型的检索质量，且搜索发现的方法与多种经典检索技术（如Rocchio反馈、ColBERT MaxSim、RRF、Fisher判别）具有内在一致性，表明存在通用的、无需重新训练的测试时计算策略。

Abstract: Test-time compute is widely believed to benefit only large reasoning models. We show it also helps small embedding models. Since modern embedding models are distilled from LLM backbones, a frozen encoder should benefit from extra inference compute without retraining. An agentic program-search loop explores 144 candidate programs over a frozen encoder API and produces twelve Pareto-optimal programs that trade extra inference compute for retrieval quality. The search independently rediscovers Rocchio pseudo-relevance feedback, ColBERT-style MaxSim at sentence granularity, reciprocal rank fusion, and the Fisher linear discriminant, all without trainable parameters or external models. Every frontier program improves nDCG@10 over the frozen baseline on all 14 tasks used during program search. Generalization is validated separately: a single fixed program, selected from the discovery frontier before any held-out evaluation, improves nDCG@10 on 61% of model-task pairs across three unseen encoder families and nineteen held-out retrieval tasks, without any per-task selection.

</details>


### [300] [MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13779)
*Mind Lab,:,Song Cao,Vic Cao,Andrew Chen,Kaijie Chen,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Kaixuan Fan,Hera Feng,Huan Feng,Arthur Fu,Jun Gao,Hongquan Gu,Aaron Guan,Nolan Ho,Mutian Hong,Hailee Hou,Peixuan Hua,Charles Huang,Miles Jiang,Nora Jiang,Yuyi Jiang,Qiuyu Jin,Fancy Kong,Andrew Lei,Kyrie Lei,Alexy Li,Lucian Li,Ray Li,Theo Li,Zhihui Li,Jiayi Lin,Kairus Liu,Kieran Liu,Logan Liu,Xiang Liu,Irvine Lu,Maeve Luo,Runze Lv,Pony Ma,Verity Niu,Anson Qiu,Vincent Wang,Rio Yang,Maxwell Yao,Carrie Ye,Regis Ye,Wenlin Ye,Josh Ying,Danney Zeng,Yuhan Zhan,Anya Zhang,Di Zhang,Ruijia Zhang,Sueky Zhang,Ya Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ada Zhou,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Xinyue Zhu,Murphy Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MinT是一个用于LoRA后训练和在线服务的托管基础设施系统，支持在共享基础模型上管理百万级LoRA策略目录，通过Scale Up/Scale Down/Scale Out三个维度优化训练和服务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA策略管理需要为每个策略物化为完整检查点，资源开销大；缺乏统一的系统抽象来管理LoRA适配器的训练、评估、服务和回滚生命周期。

Method: MinT提出三层扩展：(1) Scale Up：支持前沿规模密集和MoE架构的LoRA强化学习；(2) Scale Down：仅导出LoRA适配器（可小于基础模型1%），通过适配器交接减少步骤延迟；(3) Scale Out：分离策略可寻址性与CPU/GPU工作集，支持百万级策略目录和千级适配器活跃波。

Result: 适配器交接在4B密集模型上减少18.3x步骤延迟，30B MoE上减少2.85x；多策略GRPO缩短1.77x和1.45x壁钟时间；打包MoE LoRA张量提升在线引擎加载8.5-8.7x；支持单引擎遍历100K策略和集群级千适配器活跃波。

Conclusion: MinT通过系统化抽象实现了在共享1T级基础模型上高效管理、训练和服务百万级LoRA策略目录，大幅降低了资源开销和操作复杂度。

Abstract: We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.

</details>


### [301] [AMARIS: A Memory-Augmented Rubric Improvement System for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18592)
*Peilin Wu,Xinlu Zhang,Kun Wan,Wentian Zhao,Gang Wu,Xinya Du,Zhiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMARIS 是一个基于记忆增强的评分标准改进系统，通过持久化存储训练过程中的滚动分析、步骤级摘要和评分标准更新记录，利用最近和语义相关的历史信息来迭代优化评分标准，从而在 RL 微调 LLM 中提供更稳定、可解释且有效的奖励信号，在 GPQA-Diamond 和 IFBench 等基准上相比强基线分别提升 +2.8 和 +2.2 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应评分标准方法通常仅基于当前批次或实例级别的局部证据更新标准，丢弃了训练过程中产生的诊断信息，导致难以追踪重复失败模式、评估先前评分标准编辑的效果，或在早期标准饱和后提升标准。

Method: AMARIS 引入持久化评估记忆，存储滚动分析、步骤级摘要和评分标准更新记录，检索近期和语义相关的历史来修订评分标准，并支持异步运行以降低阻塞延迟。

Result: 在科学、医学、指令遵循和创意写作等任务上，AMARIS 在全局和实例级评分标准设置下均优于静态、局部自适应和消融记忆的基线，例如在 GPQA-Diamond 上提升 +2.8 点，在 IFBench 上提升 +2.2 点；记忆减少了振荡性评分标准编辑，并支持从早期失败纠正到后期课程推进的进展。

Conclusion: AMARIS 通过记忆增强的评分标准更新机制有效提升了 RL 微调 LLM 的性能，减少了评分标准更新的振荡，实现了渐进式的课程推进，并且异步设计降低了同步更新带来的阻塞延迟。

Abstract: Rubric-based reward shaping provides interpretable and editable reward signals for fine-tuning LLMs via reinforcement learning (RL), but existing adaptive rubric methods typically update criteria from local evidence such as the current batch or instance-level comparisons. This local view discards diagnostic information produced during training, making it difficult to track recurring failures, evaluate previous rubric edits, or raise standards once earlier criteria become saturated. We introduce AMARIS, A Memory-Augmented Rubric Improvement System that grounds rubric updates in longitudinal training evidence. AMARIS stores rollout analyses, step-level summaries, and rubric update records in a persistent evaluation memory, then retrieves recent and semantically relevant history to revise rubrics. We evaluate AMARIS across science, medicine, instruction following, and creative writing under both global and instance-specific rubric settings. AMARIS improves over static, local-adaptive, and memory-ablated baselines, such as +2.8 points on GPQA-Diamond and +2.2 points on IFBench over the strongest baselines, while analysis shows that memory reduces oscillatory rubric edits and supports a progression from early failure correction to later curriculum advancement. AMARIS runs asynchronously alongside the normal RL loop, reducing blocking latency relative to synchronous rubric updates.

</details>


### [302] [SPHERICAL KV: Angle-Domain Attention and Rate-Distortion Retention for Efficient Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18856)
*Anay Chauhan,Gurucharan Marthi Krishna Kumar,Arion Das,Amit Dhanda,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spherical KV 提出了一种基于注意力几何的长上下文推理方法，通过角度域注意力（ADA）和率失真保留（RDR）两个组件，在减少KV缓存占用的同时保持解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存的内存占用随上下文长度增长，且解码过程受限于HBM带宽而非计算能力。现有方法（如逐出、窗口化、量化、卸载）虽能减少缓存占用，但压缩后的状态在解码时仍需重建为稠密向量，导致关键路径瓶颈未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出Spherical KV方法，包含两个核心组件：(i) 角度域注意力（ADA），将键以球形参数化表示（标量半径+紧凑角度编码），直接基于编码计算注意力logits，避免稠密键重建；(ii) 率失真保留（RDR），在固定预算下联合选择每个token和头的保留/丢弃决策及精度层级，生成层级同质化的页面，支持轻量元数据和合并读取。

Result: ADA保留了分页、局部块、适合融合的解码路径，直接针对实际服务场景中的HBM流量进行优化。RDR在固定预算下实现了KV驻留的减少，同时通过层级同质化页面和合并读取保持了解码效率。

Conclusion: ADA和RDR共同提供了一种面向部署的机制，在减少KV缓存占用的同时保持解码效率，将KV分配视为基于注意力几何的率失真问题来解决。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly constrained by the KV cache: resident memory grows with context length, and decoding becomes limited by repeated High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) streaming rather than arithmetic. Existing methods such as eviction, windowing, quantization, and offloading reduce footprint, but often leave the critical-path bottleneck only partially addressed, especially when compressed states must still be reconstructed into dense vectors during decoding.
  We present Spherical KV, a long-context inference method that treats KV allocation as a rate-distortion problem grounded in attention geometry for efficient decoding. The method is built on two ideas: (i) represent directional information cheaply in the decode hot loop, and (ii) allocate retention and precision according to estimated future utility. Its first component, Angle-Domain Attention (ADA), stores keys in a spherical parameterization consisting of a scalar radius and compact angle codes, and computes attention logits directly from these codes without reconstructing dense keys. This preserves a paged, block-local, fusion-friendly decode path and directly targets HBM traffic in realistic serving settings. Its second component, Rate-Distortion Retention (RDR), jointly chooses keep/drop decisions and precision tiers per token and head under a fixed budget, producing tier-homogeneous pages with lightweight metadata and coalesced reads. Together, ADA and RDR provide a deployment-oriented mechanism for reducing KV residency while preserving decode efficiency.

</details>


### [303] [FLUIDSPLAT: Reconstructing Physical Fields from Sparse Sensors via Gaussian Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18866)
*Huaxi Huang,Meng Li,Zhengqing Gao,Xi Zhou,Xiaoshui Huang,Xiao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLUIDSPLAT是一种受3D高斯泼溅启发的传感器条件模型，将稀疏传感器读数转化为K个各向异性高斯基元形成的分区统一支架，作为流场的空间可解释中间表示。理论上证明了对于Sobolev光滑场，逼近率为O(K^{-s/d})，含噪声观测下平方风险分解为偏差O(K^{-2s/d})和方差O(σ²K/N)，平衡后得最优基元数K*∼(N/σ²)^{d/(2s+d)}，揭示稀疏传感下的方差瓶颈，进而引入状态条件残差解码器。在2D/3D四个基准上相比强基线实现11-28%误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从稀疏表面传感器重建连续流场的神经方法通常将传感器读数编码为隐式潜码，缺乏空间可解释性，且对于表示能力应如何随观测数量扩展缺乏形式化指导。

Method: 受3D高斯泼溅启发，FLUIDSPLAT预测K个各向异性高斯基元形成分区统一支架作为可解释中间表示；理论推导了逼近率、平方风险分解及最优基元数；结合状态条件残差解码器缓解方差瓶颈。

Result: 在圆柱绕流、AirfRANS、FlowBench LDC-3D、PhySense-Car 3D四个基准上实现11-28%误差降低。

Conclusion: 稀疏传感下基元数不能任意增长，存在方差瓶颈，需结合残差解码器；空间显式、可解释的中间表示优于隐式潜码方法。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous flow fields from sparse surface-mounted sensors is central to aerodynamic design, flow control, and digital-twin instrumentation. Existing neural methods for this task typically encode sensor readings into implicit latent codes with little spatial interpretability and limited formal guidance on how representational capacity should scale with observation count. Inspired by 3D Gaussian Splatting, we introduce FLUIDSPLAT, a sensor-conditioned model that predicts K anisotropic Gaussian primitives forming a partition-of-unity scaffold, a spatially explicit and interpretable intermediate representation of the flow. For an idealized Gaussian primitive estimator, we prove an $O(K^{-s/d})$ approximation rate for fields with Sobolev smoothness $s$; incorporating $N$ noisy observations yields a squared-risk decomposition with bias $O(K^{-2s/d})$ and variance $O(σ^{2}K/N)$.Balancing the two yields $K^{*}\!\sim\!(N/σ^{2})^{d/(2s+d)}$: primitive count cannot grow freely under sparse sensing, revealing a variance bottleneck that motivates complementing the scaffold with a state-conditioned residual decoder. Across four benchmarks spanning 2D and 3D, FLUIDSPLAT achieves 11-28% error reduction over several strong baselines on cylinder flow, AirfRANS, FlowBench LDC-3D, and PhySense-Car 3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [304] [Your Neighbors Know: Leveraging Local Neighborhoods for Backdoor Detection in Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19969)
*Sayan Biswas,Antoine Boutet,Davide Frey,Romaric Gaudel,Rachid Guerraoui,Maxime Jacovella,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Dimitri Lerévérend,François Taïani,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Argus是一种面向去中心化学习的后门攻击检测框架，通过节点间协作共享触发器模式并基于结构相似性度量区分真实后门与假阳性，首次提供了理论收敛保证，实验表明在保持模型效用的同时可将攻击成功率降低高达90个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习（DL）中后门攻击研究不足，现有防御方法往往忽视DL的约束条件（如无中心服务器），亟需一种原生支持去中心化环境、无需中心协调器且无需先验触发器知识的后门检测机制。

Method: Argus框架包含两个阶段：(1) 本地阶段：诚实节点在本地分析接收到的模型更新以识别潜在后门触发器；(2) 协作阶段：节点与邻居共享触发器信息，利用结构相似性度量进行一致性检验——假阳性触发器的模式在不同参与者间不一致，而真阳性触发器呈现一致模式。未通过协作测试的模型更新被拒绝，持续作恶的发送者最终被驱逐。

Result: 在三个标准数据集上对比三个基线方法，Argus将攻击成功率降低高达90个百分点（相比无防御），模型效用与全知oracle的差距在5个百分点以内。随着数据异构性增加，Argus相比基线方法的优势更加明显。

Conclusion: Argus首次为去中心化学习场景下的后门检测机制提供了理论收敛保证，证明以高概率过滤可疑模型更新能够维持与标准DL可比的收敛速率，是一种有效且实用的去中心化后门防御方案。

Abstract: Decentralized learning (DL) is an emerging machine learning paradigm where nodes collaboratively train models without a central server. However, the collaborative nature of DL makes it vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a model is taught to behave normally on standard inputs while executing hidden, malicious actions when encountering data with specific triggers. Backdoor attacks in DL remain understudied and existing defenses often overlook DL constraints. We introduce Argus, a novel backdoor detection framework native to DL that requires neither a central coordinator nor prior knowledge of the trigger. In Argus, honest nodes locally analyze received model updates to identify potential backdoor triggers. Nodes then collectively share their triggers with their neighbors and use a structural similarity metric to separate true backdoors from false alarms induced by data heterogeneity. A key insight is that false positive triggers exhibit inconsistencies across participants while true positive ones show consistent patterns. Model updates that fail this collaborative test are rejected, and persistently malicious senders are eventually evicted. We provide the first theoretical convergence guarantees for a DL-specific backdoor detection mechanism, showing that filtering out suspicious model updates with high probability preserves a convergence rate comparable to standard DL. We implement and evaluate Argus on three standard datasets and against three state-of-the-art baselines. Across settings, Argus reduces attack success rates by up to 90 points compared to no defense, while preserving model utility within 5 percentage points of an omniscient oracle. Furthermore, the effectiveness of Argus compared to baselines improves as data heterogeneity increases.

</details>


### [305] [Weasel: Out-of-Domain Generalization for Web Agents via Importance-Diversity Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20291)
*Fatemeh Pesaran Zadeh,Seyeon Choi,Xing Han Lù,Siva Reddy,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Weasel是一种面向Web智能体离线训练的高效轨迹选择方法，通过平衡单步重要性与多样性的子集选择、目标中心AXTree剪枝以及风格一致推理路径生成，在多个基准上实现了约9.7-12.5倍的训练加速，同时提升了跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体在特定轨迹和领域上微调后难以泛化到域外场景，且离线训练因噪声、冗余轨迹和长AXTree状态导致计算效率低下。

Method: 提出Weasel方法：(1) 基于固定预算的轨迹子集选择，优化单步重要性与状态、网站、交互模式多样性的平衡目标，使用贪心算法高效求解；(2) 目标中心AXTree剪枝，仅保留真实动作目标周围的内容；(3) 用模型生成、风格一致的推理路径替代专家轨迹，缓解推理原生模型的风格不匹配问题。

Result: 在AgentTrek和NNetNav训练数据集上，使用Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma3-4B和Qwen3-8B模型，在WebArena、WorkArena和MiniWob基准上评估，Weasel提升了域外性能，同时减少了训练成本，实现了约9.7-12.5倍的训练加速。

Conclusion: Weasel通过数据选择与预处理策略有效解决了Web智能体训练中泛化性和效率的双重挑战，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled web agents that follow natural language goals through multi-step browser interactions. However, agents fine-tuned on specific trajectories and domain often struggle to generalize out of domain, and offline training can be compute-inefficient due to noisy, redundant trajectories and long accessibility-tree (AXTree) states. To address both issues, we propose Weasel, a trajectory selection method for offline training of web agents. Weasel selects a fixed-budget subset of trajectory steps by optimizing an objective that balances unary importance with pairwise diversity over states, websites, and interaction patterns, solving efficiently with a greedy algorithm. We further improve efficiency with target-centered AXTree pruning that keeps only content around the ground-truth action target, and we mitigate style mismatch for reasoning-native models by replacing expert traces with model-generated, style-consistent rationales. Across AgentTrek and NNetNav training datasets, evaluations in WebArena, WorkArena, and MiniWob, and experiments with Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma3-4B, and Qwen3-8B, Weasel improves out-of-domain performance while reducing training cost, producing roughly 9.7-12.5$\times$ training speedups over standard fine-tuning. We make the code available at https://github.com/fatemehpesaran310/weasel.

</details>


### [306] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Xinyue Xu,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM) 是一个统一架构，通过嵌套层次化概念组织，使单一模型支持自适应概念粒度推理，将干预成本从线性降至对数级别 O(log K)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在实际部署中面临高昂的测试时干预成本：要么使用单一静态概念集导致专家需穷举标注，要么训练多个不同概念预算的模型造成大量计算和维护开销。需要一种既能动态调整概念粒度又能保持单一模型的方法。

Method: 受Matryoshka表示学习启发，将概念按最大相关性和最小冗余度组织成嵌套层次结构，使其能在无需重新训练的情况下以多种概念粒度进行推理，实现单一模型内的自适应概念利用。

Result: 理论证明干预成本从线性降至对数级别 O(log K)，并保证单调性能提升；实验表明MCBM性能与独立训练的多个模型相当，同时支持动态高效的专家交互。

Conclusion: MCBM通过嵌套概念层次结构有效解决了CBM部署中的干预成本瓶颈，在保持模型性能的同时实现了可扩展的专家交互，为可解释深度学习提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

</details>


### [307] [A Sharper Picture of Generalization in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20988)
*Paul Lintilhac,Sair Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从傅里叶谱视角研究Transformer在布尔域上的泛化行为，通过PAC-Bayes理论证明稀疏低阶谱可实现平坦极小值及非真空泛化界，解释思维链提升高阶函数泛化的原因，并通过实验和机制可解释性研究验证理论构造的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（Edelman et al., 2022; Trauger & Tosh, 2024）仅从Rademacher复杂度推导泛化界，本文旨在探索通过PAC-Bayes理论获得Transformer泛化界的可行性，提供更细致的泛化分析框架。

Method: 1. 证明存在实现任意稀疏性不超过上下文长度的布尔函数的平坦极小值；2. 对理想化低锐度学习器应用PAC-Bayes界，得到非真空泛化界；3. 用该理论解释思维链为何提升高阶目标函数的泛化；4. 通过性质测试高效估计复杂度参数；5. 进行机制可解释性研究验证理论构造在真实Transformer中的现实性。

Result: 1. 稀疏低阶傅里叶谱可实现低锐度构造并获得良好泛化性质；2. 得到非真空的PAC-Bayes泛化界；3. 为思维链提升高阶函数泛化提供形式化解释；4. 复杂度参数可通过性质测试高效估计；5. 实验和机制可解释性研究支持理论构造在真实Transformer中的现实性。

Conclusion: PAC-Bayes理论为Transformer泛化分析提供了比Rademacher复杂度更细致的工具；稀疏低阶谱是关键的结构性先验；思维链通过分解高复杂度函数提升泛化。

Abstract: We study transformers' generalization behavior on boolean domains from the perspective of the Fourier spectra of their target functions. In contrast to prior work (Edelman et al., 2022; Trauger & Tosh, 2024), which derived generalization bounds from Rademacher complexity, we investigate the feasibility of obtaining generalization bounds via PAC-Bayes theory. We show that sparse spectra concentrated on low-degree components enable low-sharpness constructions with good generalization properties. Our idea is to show the existence of flat minima implementing any boolean function of sparsity no greater than the context length, and then apply a PAC-Bayes bound to an idealized low-sharpness learner, resulting in a non-vacuous generalization bound. We use this to give a formal account of why chain-of-thought improves generalization for high-degree target functions, and show that the complexity parameters in our bound can be efficiently estimated via property testing. We evaluate predictions empirically and conduct a mechanistic interpretability study to support the realism of our theoretical construction in real transformers.

</details>


### [308] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，利用自定义模拟器生成合成数据进行训练，从Hi-C相互作用图中准确推断着丝粒位置，能处理实体数量和大小的变异性，并跨多种物种验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将着丝粒识别等相互作用图推理问题形式化为通用的逆问题，并解决现有方法在处理实体数量和大小变异性方面的不足。

Method: 采用Transformer架构来处理可变数量和尺寸的实体，并设计自定义模拟器生成丰富的合成数据进行训练。

Result: 该方法在着丝粒定位任务上，能够在多种基因组大小的物种中准确恢复其基因组位置。

Conclusion: 数据驱动方法结合Transformer架构与合成数据训练，可有效解决相互作用图的通用逆问题，并具备跨物种泛化能力。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [309] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层间学习率（LLR）方法，基于重尾自正则化（HT-SR）理论为Transformer不同层自适应分配学习率：弱重尾性层用大学习率加速训练，强重尾性层用小学习率提升泛化。在LLaMA、GPT-nano等架构、AdamW/Muon优化器、60M-3B参数规模上，LLR实现最高1.5倍训练加速，并将1B模型零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，3B模型从48.58%提升至50.61%，且调参开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习中所有层使用统一学习率的做法忽略了Transformer各层的结构异质性，限制了LLM的性能潜力。

Method: 基于HT-SR理论，通过分析权重相关矩阵的经验谱密度（ESD）量化每层的重尾性，弱重尾性层分配较大学习率以加速训练，强重尾性层分配较小学习率以促进收敛和泛化。

Result: LLR实现最高1.5倍训练加速；1B模型零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，3B模型从48.58%提升至50.61%；学习率设置可从统一基线直接迁移，调参开销低。

Conclusion: LLR通过层间差异化学习率策略有效提升了Transformer训练效率与模型泛化能力，且易于部署。

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes more balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures ranging from LLaMA to GPT-nano, optimizers including AdamW and Muon, and model scales from 60M to 3B parameters with up to 100B training tokens demonstrate the effectiveness of LLR. LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and consistently outperforms uniform-learning-rate baselines. In particular, it improves the average zero-shot accuracy of 1B models from 47.09% to 49.02%, and that of 3B models from 48.58% to 50.61%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it can transfer nearly optimal learning-rate settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [310] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CogAdapt框架，通过LeadBridge适配器将3导联可穿戴信号转换为12导联表示，并结合ProFine渐进微调策略，将临床ECG基础模型适应到可穿戴认知负荷评估任务，在两个数据集上分别达到0.626和0.768的macro-F1分数，显著优于从头训练的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对于自适应人机交互至关重要，但面临标注数据有限和跨主体泛化能力差的挑战。虽然临床ECG基础模型预训练数据丰富，但由于传感器配置不匹配和任务差异，无法直接应用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架，包含两个核心组件：1) LeadBridge——可学习适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为解剖学一致的12导联表示；2) ProFine——渐进微调策略，逐步解冻编码器层并防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive两个公开数据集上采用留一受试者交叉验证，CogAdapt显著优于从头训练的基线模型，macro-F1分数分别达到0.626和0.768。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基础模型适配策略对基于可穿戴传感器的跨主体认知负荷评估具有可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [311] [Iterative Refinement Neural Operators are Learned Fixed-Point Solvers: A Principled Approach to Spectral Bias Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24041)
*Xiaotian Liu,Shuyuan Shang,Xiaopeng Wang,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代精炼神经算子（IRNO），通过固定点迭代的逐步残差修正机制增强预训练神经算子，并配合渐进频谱损失函数，有效解决了神经算子在高频细节建模中的频谱偏差问题，在湍流和活性物质等物理系统中取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子作为科学建模的数据驱动替代模型，采用单次前向推理，难以捕捉高频细节（即频谱偏差问题），亟需一种能有效提升高频分辨率的方法。

Method: 提出迭代精炼神经算子（IRNO），在预训练算子基础上添加可学习的精炼模块，通过固定点迭代逐步施加残差修正；提出渐进频谱损失函数，在训练过程中逐步增加对高频分量的惩罚权重。

Result: 在湍流模拟中误差最多降低56.05%；在活性物质系统中，相对于基础算子，归一化误差在低频降至27.72-36.10%，中频降至5.07-6.68%，高频降至1.48-2.04%，且性能在超出训练迭代次数后保持稳定。

Conclusion: IRNO通过迭代精炼范式有效克服了神经算子的频谱偏差，理论保证了算子的压缩收敛性，实验验证了其在多尺度物理建模中的优越性能。

Abstract: Neural operators serve as fast, data-driven surrogates for scientific modeling but typically rely on a monolithic, single-pass inference procedure that struggles to resolve high-frequency details, a limitation known as spectral bias. We introduce the Iterative Refinement Neural Operator (IRNO), which augments pre-trained operators with a learned refinement module iteratively applied via fixed-point iteration. IRNO decomposes the prediction into a coarse initialization followed by successive residual corrections, paralleling classical numerical solvers. Under local assumptions, we establish contraction of the induced operator, ensuring convergence to a unique fixed point. To explicitly target high-frequency errors, we propose a progressive spectral loss that adaptively increases penalty on high-frequency components over refinement steps during training. Across physical systems, IRNO consistently lowers error, with up to 56.05% improvement on turbulent flow. On Active Matter, spectral analysis reveals that, relative to base operator, the normalized error ratios decrease to 27.72-36.10% in low-, 5.07-6.68% in mid-, and 1.48-2.04% in high-frequencies, remaining stable beyond the trained iteration count. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaotianliu-dartmouth/Iterative_Refinement_Neural_Operator

</details>


### [312] [Hidden-State Privacy Has an Empty Middle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24042)
*Alexander Okezue Bell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对单层隐藏状态隐私的高斯发布机制，1536种协方差中没有一个能同时实现中等效用和中等隐私。本文证明了费雪球下界，发现对角逆费雪发布Σ*_diag是唯一在32层模型网格上最坏攻击者top-1≤0.001的机制，但位于隐私/效用边缘。广义特征机制在欧几里得检索下达到13倍帕累托改进，但在自适应马氏攻击下崩溃至100% top-1。全文轨迹序列逆变器在Σ_diag下恢复0%的GPT-2前缀。从头训练的分裂记忆Transformer在90M参数时达到G_Mah∈[20,33]，相比同预算GPT基线保持6-24倍优势。这些结果将隐藏状态发布从高斯类内的机制设计重新定位为架构或发布联合设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现现有高斯发布机制在隐藏状态隐私保护中无法兼顾效用与隐私（"空的中间"现象），促使作者从理论上证明这种不可能性，并探索超越高斯机制的架构级解决方案。

Method: 采用理论证明（费雪球下界）与实验验证相结合的方法：系统测试1536种高斯发布协方差；提出对角逆费雪发布机制；设计广义特征机制；开发全轨迹序列逆变器攻击；训练分裂记忆Transformer架构。

Result: (1) 所有高斯发布均无法同时实现中等效用与隐私；(2) Σ*_diag是最优对角机制但位于边缘；(3) 广义特征机制在自适应马氏攻击下完全失效；(4) 序列逆变器在Σ_diag下恢复率0%；(5) 分裂记忆Transformer在90M参数达到G_Mah∈[20,33]，相比GPT基线优势6-24倍。

Conclusion: 高斯协方差类内的机制设计无法实现隐藏状态的安全发布，必须转向架构或发布联合设计范式，分裂记忆Transformer展示了这一方向的有效性。

Abstract: Of $1{,}536$ Gaussian release covariances we tested for single-layer hidden-state privacy, zero achieve both moderate utility and moderate privacy against an adaptive retrieval attacker. We prove a complementary Fisher-ball lower bound: every full-rank Gaussian release at $O(1)$ Fisher utility admits a direction whose Mahalanobis signal grows linearly in hidden width, ruling out uniform Gaussian safety in the class and matching the empirical empty middle. The diagonal inverse-Fisher release $Σ^\star_{\mathrm{diag}}(\mathcal{K}) = (2\mathcal{K}/d)\,\mathrm{diag}(1/F_{ii})$ is the unique minimax-optimal diagonal mechanism at first-order KL budget $\mathcal{K}$ and the only release with worst-attacker top-1 $\le 0.001$ at every point of a 32 model-layer grid, but it sits on a privacy/utility edge rather than filling the middle. A generalized-eigen mechanism reaching $13\times$ Pareto reduction under Euclidean retrieval collapses to $100\%$ top-1 under the adaptive Mahalanobis attacker, and a full-trajectory sequence inverter recovers $94\%$ of clean GPT-2 prefixes but $0\%$ under $Σ_{\mathrm{diag}}$. A split-memory transformer trained from scratch reaches $G_{\mathrm{Mah}} \in [20, 33]$ at 90M and maintains a $6$--$24\times$ advantage over same-budget GPT baselines from 30M to 1B at a fixed-token language-modeling loss penalty; pretrained models top out at 9.3. These results reframe hidden-state release from mechanism-design within the Gaussian class to architecture or release co-design.

</details>


### [313] [Rethinking Weak Supervision in Anomaly Detection: A Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26068)
*Xu Yao,Siyuan Zhou,Zhenbo Wu,Chaochuan Hou,Shuang Liang,Shiping Wang,Hailiang Huang,Songqiao Han,Minqi Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WSADBench是首个统一评估弱监督异常检测三大场景（不完全、不精确、不准确监督）的基准，通过70万+实验揭示了四种关键发现：弱监督场景间存在强相关性、专用WSAD算法仅在极端标签稀缺时有效、无标签数据效用有限、模型对标签噪声具有非对称敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督异常检测的三个研究方向（不完全、不精确、不准确监督）长期相互孤立，缺乏统一框架来评估它们是否解决独特挑战还是共享基础机制。

Method: 构建统一基准WSADBench，系统变化标签数量、粒度和质量，在4种模态上评估36种算法（从专用WSAD方法到表格基础模型），基于70万+实验进行标准化协议评估。

Result: (i) 弱监督场景间存在强相关性；(ii) 专用WSAD算法仅在极端标签稀缺时表现优异，随监督增强或OOD场景下被表格基础模型和通用分类方法超越；(iii) 无标签数据效用不一致且提升有限；(iv) 模型对不同类型标签噪声具有非对称敏感性。

Conclusion: WSADBench作为统一评估基准揭示了弱监督异常检测各方向的内在联系和性能边界，开源代码和数据集以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Weakly supervised anomaly detection (WSAD) has developed in three primary directions: incomplete, inexact, and inaccurate supervision. However, these directions remain isolated, lacking a unified framework to assess whether they address unique challenges or share fundamental mechanics. This paper introduces WSADBench, the first benchmark that unifies evaluation across distinct weakly supervised scenarios, benchmarking diverse approaches from specialized WSAD methods to advanced tabular foundation models. WSADBench establishes standardized protocols to evaluate 36 algorithms across 4 modalities by systematically varying label quantity, granularity, and quality, revealing the performance boundaries of various methods. Based on over 700K experiments, WSADBench reveals four critical insights: (i) Strong intrinsic correlations exist between these weak supervision scenarios, challenging the isolation of current research directions. (ii) Specialized WSAD algorithms excel only in extreme label-scarcity regimes but are quickly dominated by tabular foundation models and general classification methods as supervision increases or in OOD scenarios. (iii) Unlabeled data shows inconsistent utility across settings, with marginal gains compared to label refinement. (iv) Models exhibit asymmetric sensitivity to different types of label noise. We release WSADBench as an open-source benchmark with code and datasets to facilitate future WSAD research: https://github.com/SUFE-AILAB/WSADBench.

</details>


### [314] [Global Convergence of Wasserstein Policy Gradient for Entropy-Regularized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26078)
*Zhaoyu Zhu,Rui Gao,Shuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Wasserstein策略梯度（WPG）方法在熵正则化强化学习中的全局收敛性建立了理论基础，通过利用Bellman结构（包括软Bellman残差的KL表示、Bellman压缩和Bellman预解恒等式）结合对数Sobolev不等式，推导出分布层面的Polyak–Łojasiewicz条件，并证明了WPG以几何速率收敛至离散化偏差范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WPG在连续控制问题中表现优异，但其全局收敛性质一直缺乏理论理解。标准Langevin分析不适用，因为RL目标通过Bellman递归依赖于策略而非静态凸泛函，且Langevin漂移由软Q函数决定，其正则性需在策略迭代中加以控制。

Method: 利用熵正则化RL的Bellman结构，证明：软Bellman残差相对于Gibbs策略具有逐状态的KL表示；Bellman压缩将残差与全局最优性差距联系起来；Bellman预解恒等式将价值改进与相对Fisher信息关联。结合均匀对数Sobolev不等式，推导出分布Polyak–Łojasiewicz条件，并建立正则性和均匀界以控制离散化误差。

Result: 获得了WPG的几何收敛速率，收敛至离散化偏差范围内。理论上揭示了熵正则化RL虽非传统凸优化问题，但Bellman递归诱导出有利于全局收敛的PL几何结构。

Conclusion: 本文首次为WPG提供了完整的全局收敛理论，展示了Bellman结构如何替代凸性成为收敛分析的核心工具，对理解策略梯度方法的几何性质有重要理论意义。

Abstract: Wasserstein policy gradient (WPG) is a policy optimization method for reinforcement learning (RL) that exploits the optimal-transport geometry of action distributions. For the entropy-regularized RL objective, WPG evolves each state-conditional policy by transporting it along the action gradient of the soft Q-function together with a Langevin-type diffusion. Despite its appeal for continuous-control problems, its global convergence properties remain poorly understood. Standard Langevin analyses do not directly apply, because the RL objective depends on the policy through the Bellman recursion rather than through a static convex functional, and the Langevin drift is determined by the soft Q-function, whose regularity must be controlled along the policy iterates.
  In this paper, we develop a global convergence theory for WPG by exploiting the Bellman structure of entropy-regularized RL. We show that the role usually played by convexity can be replaced by a Bellman-based argument: the soft Bellman residual admits a statewise KL representation with respect to a Gibbs policy; Bellman contraction relates this residual to the global optimality gap; and a Bellman resolvent identity connects value improvement to relative Fisher information. Combined with a uniform log-Sobolev inequality (LSI) for the evolving Gibbs family, these ingredients yield a distributional Polyak--Łojasiewicz condition. We further establish the regularity and uniform bounds needed to control the discretization error, thereby obtaining geometric contraction up to a discretization bias. Conceptually, our analysis shows that although entropy-regularized RL is not convex in the usual flat sense, the Bellman recursion induces a favorable Polyak--Lojasiewicz-type (PL) geometry that supports global convergence of WPG.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [315] [When Does Adaptive Guidance Help? Belief-Aware Privileged Distillation for Autonomous Driving Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26155)
*Mehmet Haklidir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Belief-Aware GSAC (BA-GSAC)，通过集成不确定性动态调节知识蒸馏系数 λ，并在 Highway-Env 上系统比较五种策略。研究发现自适应系数在严重部分可观测条件下会崩溃为 λ_min，原因是集成模型仅基于部分观测预测导致"可观测性盲区"。线性衰减调度反而取得最佳性能，表明调度效应而非集成不确定性驱动了稳定收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GSAC 使用固定的蒸馏系数 λ，无法根据智能体的不确定性进行自适应调节。本文旨在探究自适应引导机制何时真正有效，并揭示其失效原因。

Method: 提出 BA-GSAC，利用集成模型的输出不一致性（ensemble disagreement）动态调节蒸馏系数 λ。在三种 POMDP 难度级别下系统比较五种策略：固定 λ∈{0.01, 0.1}、自适应、线性衰减和 vanilla SAC。

Result: 自适应系数在严重遮挡条件下约 3K 步内坍缩至 λ_min；线性衰减调度在严重 POMDP 下取得最佳性能（均值 116.5，CV=8.9%）；自适应策略的 warmup 阶段提供了可测量的稳定性（CV=13.3% vs 29.8%）。

Conclusion: 调度效应而非集成不确定性驱动了稳定性收益；指出了集成预测目标是一个重要的设计选择——训练集成模型使用指导智能体的特权全状态预测而非部分观测，可能是解决"可观测性盲区"的关键架构修复方向。

Abstract: Guided Soft Actor-Critic (GSAC) distills knowledge from a privileged full-state teacher to a partial-observation student for autonomous driving, but uses a fixed distillation coefficient lambda regardless of the agent's uncertainty. We present Belief-Aware GSAC (BA-GSAC), which modulates lambda via ensemble disagreement, and use it as a testbed for a systematic empirical study asking: when does adaptive guidance actually help? Evaluating five strategies (fixed lambda in {0.01, 0.1}, adaptive, linear decay, and vanilla SAC) across three POMDP difficulty levels on Highway-Env, we find that preliminary single-seed runs suggest benefits under mild and moderate partial observability, but under severe occlusion (evaluated with 3 seeds for all methods) the adaptive coefficient collapses to lambda_min within about 3K steps. We trace this to an observability blindness phenomenon: because the ensemble predicts partial observations, it achieves low disagreement even under heavy occlusion, modeling what is visible but unable to detect what is missing. We diagnose the root cause and propose an architectural fix (training the ensemble on full-state predictions using the guiding actor's privileged access); while not validated here, we show that even with current limitations, the warmup phase provides measurable stabilization (CV=13.3% vs. 29.8% for constant lambda=0.01). In fact, a simple deterministic linear decay schedule achieves the best severe-POMDP performance across all metrics (mean 116.5, CV=8.9%), suggesting that the scheduling effect, not the ensemble, drives the stability benefit. These findings provide practical guidance for designing uncertainty-aware teacher-student frameworks and highlight ensemble prediction targets as an important design choice.

</details>


### [316] [PhyPush: One Push is All You Need for Sensorless Physical Property Estimation with Physics-Guided Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26284)
*Koyo Fujii,Luis Figueredo,Praminda Caleb-Solly,Ivan Boschi,Edoardo Ida',Marco Carricato,Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhyPush是一个利用单次推挤的末端执行器速度数据，通过物理引导的Transformer框架估计物体质量和摩擦系数的系统，无需专用传感器，在仿真和真实实验中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体属性估计方法依赖力/力矩传感器、触觉阵列或多相机运动捕捉系统等专用硬件，限制了可扩展性和实际部署。作者希望仅利用标准机械臂上可用的运动学数据实现质量和摩擦系数的精准估计。

Method: 提出PhyPush框架，核心是一个物理引导的Transformer模型。输入仅为单次推挤中导出的末端执行器速度（运动学数据），通过结合牛顿第二定律和库仑摩擦模型的物理引导损失函数进行训练，增强物理一致性和泛化能力。

Result: 在仿真中，PhyPush相比有权访问完整力信息的基线方法误差降低超过10%；在实际实验中，优于纯数据驱动的损失方法。在多种仿真和真实场景下的域外条件下均取得更准确的质量和摩擦估计。

Conclusion: 物理引导学习能够仅依靠单次推挤和现成的运动学数据实现低成本、传感器高效的物理属性估计，为可扩展的机器人操作提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Accurately estimating object mass and friction is fundamental to achieving reliable and adaptive robotic manipulation. Although interactive perception provides a powerful mechanism for inferring such properties, most existing approaches depend on specialized hardware such as force/torque sensors, tactile arrays, or multi-camera motion-capture systems, limiting scalability and deployment. This paper presents PhyPush, a physics-guided Transformer framework that estimates an object's mass and friction coefficient using only kinematically derived end-effector velocity from a single push. This typically requires data available on standard robotic arms. The model incorporates constraints from Newton's second law and the Coulomb friction model through a physics-guided loss, improving physical consistency and generalization to unseen objects and surfaces. Across diverse simulation and real-world setups, PhyPush consistently achieves more accurate mass and friction estimation in challenging out-of-domain conditions. In simulation, it reduces error by over 10% compared with a baseline that has privileged access to full force information, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms a data-driven loss approach. Overall, the results demonstrate that physics-guided learning can enable low-cost, sensor-efficient estimation of physical properties, relying solely on a single push and readily available kinematic data.

</details>


### [317] [Collaborative Navigation and Exploration with $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26304)
*Evangelos Psomiadis,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于β-稀疏高斯过程的异构机器人协作导航框架，使移动传感器机器人在带宽受限条件下联合选择传输的地图点和导航动作，同时预测未知环境区域。在火星和地球地图上的仿真表明，该框架相比无通信减少18%路径成本，相比原始数据传输减少76%信息传输量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构机器人在未知环境中协作导航时面临的感知、通信和计算限制问题，特别是带宽约束下如何高效传输环境信息以辅助领航机器人导航。

Method: 提出β-稀疏高斯过程模型用于任务感知的诱导点选择；开发了平衡任务相关性与探索的动作选择策略；使传感器机器人能联合优化传输的地图点和导航动作，同时预测未探索区域。

Result: 在火星和地球地图仿真中，路径成本降低18%（相比无通信），传输信息量减少76%（相比原始数据传输基线）。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效在带宽受限条件下实现高效的环境信息传输，显著降低通信开销同时提升导航性能，验证了稀疏高斯过程在机器人协作导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative navigation of heterogeneous robots in unknown environments poses significant challenges due to sensing, communication, and computational limitations. In this work, a lead robot navigates toward a target while a mobile sensor robot (e.g., a drone) assists by transmitting information about its locally observed environment under bandwidth constraints. We propose a framework that enables the sensor to jointly select its transmitted map points and navigation actions online, while also predicting unexplored regions of the environment. To this end, we present $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes, a novel and robust variational sparse Gaussian Process model for task-aware inducing point selection. Furthermore, we develop an action-selection strategy that balances task relevance with exploration. Simulations on Mars and Earth maps show that the framework can reduce path cost by 18% relative to no communication and decrease transmitted information by 76% compared to raw-data transmission baselines.

</details>


### [318] [RCSP: Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning for Safe Dynamic Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26348)
*Zhengye Han,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出风险敏感推测场景规划(RCSP)层，通过预测性风险评估解决移动机器人在动态瓶颈环境中的近失承诺问题。RCSP维护轻量级局部运动推测信念，采样未来交互并惩罚高风险尾部，通过局部安全检查执行。在MuJoCo瓶颈任务中，RCSP实现无碰撞导航并提升安全性和路径质量；在ROS2/Gazebo中，添加该层可减少动态近失故障；在DynaBARN/Jackal测试中，DWA和TEB仍保持更优的基准成功率，揭示了方法边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态环境中面临"近失承诺问题"：当前安全的运动速度可能使机器人承诺进入一条即将被移动障碍物关闭的通道。现有方法缺乏对这种预测性风险的充分考虑，导致机器人在动态瓶颈区域容易发生碰撞。

Method: 提出Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning (RCSP)规划层：1) 维护轻量级局部运动推测信念；2) 采样短时域未来交互场景；3) 对高风险尾部进行风险敏感惩罚；4) 通过局部安全检查执行最终控制指令。

Result: MuJoCo瓶颈任务：RCSP实现无碰撞且安全性、路径质量优于非自适应预测器，但增加了延迟。ROS2/Gazebo：在标准Nav2栈中添加局部安全层减少了动态近失故障。DynaBARN/Jackal迁移测试：DWA和TEB在严格基准成功率上更强，揭示了RCSP的适用边界。

Conclusion: RCSP可作为预测性风险模块，补充现有导航栈在动态瓶颈环境中的能力，但其性能边界存在于与传统规划器（如DWA、TEB）的对比中。

Abstract: Mobile robots can fail before they collide: a velocity that is safe now may commit the robot to a passage that moving obstacles will soon close. We study this predictive near-miss commitment problem and propose Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning (RCSP), a planning layer that evaluates candidate commands against plausible short-horizon obstacle futures. RCSP maintains a lightweight belief over local motion conjectures, samples future interactions, penalizes high-risk tails, and executes through a local safety check. In controlled MuJoCo bottleneck tasks, the RCSP planner reaches the goal without collisions and yields higher secondary safety and path-quality point estimates than a non-adaptive predictor, with additional latency. In ROS2/Gazebo, adding the local safety layer to a standard Nav2 stack reduces dynamic near-miss failures. On official DynaBARN/Jackal transfer, tuned DWA and TEB remain stronger on strict benchmark success, revealing the boundary of the approach. These simulation results position RCSP as a predictive-risk module that complements existing navigation stacks in dynamic bottleneck regimes.

</details>


### [319] [Closing the Loop in Teleoperation: Episode-Level Data Quality Assessment and Feedback for High-Quality Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26349)
*Gokul Narayanan,Yash Shahapurkar,Melih Erdogan,Brian Zhu,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据质量评估与反馈（DQAF）框架，通过即时后回合反馈（基于语义任务进展和机器人遥测数据）帮助新手操作员提升遥操作演示质量，实验表明接收反馈的操作员能更快产生高质量演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动化正从刚性自动化向灵活自适应系统转型，需要大规模真实机器人演示数据。然而新手操作员采集的遥操作演示虽任务成功但常因运动低效、重复修正、接近关节限位等问题而次优，现有二元成功/失败反馈无法指出具体改进方向。

Method: 构建DQAF框架，提取子任务进展、运动平滑度、停顿、运动学限位等质量相关信号，将其转化为结构化质量评估和可操作的自然语言反馈，在每次演示后立即提供给操作员。

Result: 诊断验证研究中，系统与人类评审员在数据集筛选中的拒绝原因和可操作反馈上表现一致；三项用户研究中，接收即时反馈的操作员比未接收者更快提升演示质量。

Conclusion: DQAF框架能有效弥补二元成功/失败反馈的不足，通过解释次优原因并指出具体待改进行为，加速新手操作员的学习曲线，从而提升遥操作数据采集效率和质量。

Abstract: Industrial automation is at a pivotal moment, as Physical AI is driving a transition from rigid, hand-engineered automation systems toward more flexible and adaptive systems. This shift has created a growing demand for large-scale, real-world robot demonstration data, making teleoperation an increasingly important mechanism for data collection. However, high-quality teleoperated demonstrations remain difficult to obtain in practice, as novice operators often produce episodes that are task-successful but suboptimal for downstream use due to inefficient motion, repeated corrections, or operation near robot joint limits. We present a Data Quality Assessment and Feedback (DQAF) framework that closes the loop in teleoperation by providing immediate post-episode feedback grounded in semantic task progress and robot telemetry. The framework extracts quality relevant signals such as sub-task progress, motion smoothness, stalls, kinematic limits and converts them into structured quality assessments and actionable natural-language feedback. Unlike binary success or failure feedback, the proposed system explains why an episode is suboptimal and highlights specific behaviors to correct in the next trial. We evaluate the framework through a diagnostic validation study and a pilot user study. In the validation study, the system is compared with a human reviewer during dataset curation, producing rejection reasons and actionable feedback for improvement. In the pilot study with three novice operators across two manipulation tasks, the operator who received the systems immediate, automated post-episode feedback improved faster than those who did not, producing higher-quality demonstrations sooner.

</details>


### [320] [Multi-Robot Box Transport over Different Surfaces with Decentralized Role-based Proportional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26430)
*Aditya Bhatt,Himavarshini Yarragangu,Urvish Shah,Venkata Sai Yaswanth Mohan Thota,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为R2P2的异步分散式任务与运动规划方法，用于多机器人协作推运矩形箱体。该方法通过角色分配（推、支撑、防止）和规则/比例控制，在不同倾斜度和摩擦力的地形上实现协作运输，在六机器人仿真和四台TurtleBot实物实验中验证了其泛化能力和优于虚拟领航-跟随方法的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作推运物体在建筑、仓库和灾后 debris 清理等领域有广泛应用，但不同倾斜度和摩擦力的表面给协作运输带来了独特挑战。现有方法往往依赖集中式控制或频繁通信，存在单点故障、同步和共识需求高等问题。

Method: 提出R2P2（Roles with Rules and Proportional-control Primitive）方法。首先根据操纵模式（箱体旋转vs平移）和地形信息，通过规则为机器人分配角色（推、支撑、防止）；然后根据角色采用基于规则的控制或比例速度控制。每个机器人只需观测自身和箱体的位置及朝向即可执行任务。

Result: 在六机器人NVIDIA IsaacSim仿真中，R2P2在不同表面摩擦/倾斜和箱体质量场景下均表现出泛化能力，成功率优于标准虚拟领航-跟随方法。在四台TurtleBot的实物实验中成功完成了1.2kg箱体的协作运输。

Conclusion: R2P2作为一种异步分散式方法，有效降低了通信、同步和共识需求，避免了单点故障，同时在不同地形和载荷条件下展现了良好的鲁棒性和可泛化性。

Abstract: Collaborative transport of objects via pushing by multiple robots has many applications, ranging from construction and warehouse environments to post disaster debris clean-up. Achieving collaborative transport over surfaces with different inclination and friction properties however poses unique challenges. To address these challenges, this paper presents an asynchronous decentralized task and motion planning approach for transporting rectangular boxes of varying mass over flat, uphill and downhill terrain. Such a decentralized approach alleviates communication, synchronization and consensus needs and mitigates single point of failure issues. Our approach, called R2P2 or Roles with Rules and Proportional-control Primitive, assigns roles (e.g., push, support and prevent) to robots based on rules cognizant of the mode of manipulation needed (box rotation vs translation); this is followed by either rule-based control or proportional control of robot velocity based on the roles. Each robot is assumed to observe the location and heading of self and the box in executing the role and controls. R2P2 is evaluated with a six-robot team deployed in a simulator built using NVIDIA IsaacSim -- demonstrating generalizability across different surface friction/inclination and box mass scenarios, and better success rate compared to a standard virtual-leader-follower method. R2P2 is also successfully validated with a physical experiment, where it is executed onboard four turtlebots tasked with moving a 1.2 kg box.

</details>


### [321] [Robust Koopman Control Barrier Filters for Safe Actor-Critic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26452)
*Dhruv S. Kushwaha,Zoleikha A. Biron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust Koopman-CBF SAC框架，通过学习Koopman预测器构建提升空间中的线性CBF约束，并利用投影残差边际补偿逼近误差，在安全控制任务中实现零约束违反，同时揭示了高阶和多步扩展的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法中，控制屏障函数(CBF)虽能通过最小侵入式安全滤波器保证前向不变性，但其在无模型RL中的应用受限于精确动力学需求与手工设计的屏障证书，亟需一种数据驱动且鲁棒的CBF-RL融合框架。

Method: 提出Robust Koopman-CBF SAC框架：(1) 从数据中学习有限维Koopman预测器以获取提升空间线性动力学；(2) 在提升空间中构建仿射CBF约束，通过二次规划安全层执行；(3) 利用保留轨迹数据的投影残差边际收紧CBF条件以补偿逼近误差；(4) 评论家基于执行的安全动作训练，演员正则化朝向Koopman-CBF可行集以降低对滤波器的依赖。

Result: 在CartPole稳定与跟踪任务上实现零约束违反，回报匹敌或超越无约束SAC；在高维Safety Gymnasium locomotion任务中部分设置减少违规，但暴露出速度屏障与线性EDMD模型的局限性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒Koopman-CBF滤波器是连接无模型RL与可证明安全的有效桥梁，但需高阶及多步扩展以应对复杂场景，结果明确了此类滤波器保持有效性的结构条件。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic systems requires policies that improve task performance while satisfying state and input constraints during both training and deployment. Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a principled mechanism for enforcing forward invariance through minimally invasive safety filters, but their use in model-free RL is limited by the need for accurate dynamics and hand-designed barrier certificates. We propose Robust Koopman-CBF SAC, a safety-filtered actor--critic framework that learns a finite-dimensional Koopman predictor from data, constructs affine CBF constraints in the lifted space, and enforces them through a quadratic-program safety layer. To account for finite-dimensional Koopman approximation error, the CBF condition is tightened using a projected residual margin estimated from held-out rollout data. The critic is trained on the executed safe action, while the actor is regularized toward the Koopman-CBF feasible set, reducing dependence on the filter over training. Across safe-control benchmarks, the method achieves zero constraint violations on CartPole stabilization and tracking while matching or exceeding unconstrained SAC returns. On high-dimensional Safety Gymnasium locomotion tasks, the method reduces violations in some settings but also exposes important limitations of first-order velocity barriers and linear EDMD models, motivating high-order and multi-step Koopman-CBF extensions. These results suggest that robust Koopman-CBF filters are a promising bridge between model-free RL and certifiable safety, while clarifying the structural conditions under which such filters remain effective. All code is available at \href{https://github.com/DhruvKushwaha/Koopman-CBF-Soft-Actor-Critic}{Github Repository}.

</details>


### [322] [Efficient On-policy Visual-RL via Stochastic Decoupled Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26478)
*Haoxiang You,Yilang Liu,Davis Zong,Qian Wang,Teeratham Vitchutripop,Qi Wang,Daniel Rakita,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出随机解耦策略梯度（SDPG），一种轻量级视觉强化学习方法，可在单张RTX 4080 GPU上数小时内端到端训练多样化视觉运动控制策略，在视觉MuJoCo基准上优于基线方法，并支持仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉强化学习方法训练时间长、计算和内存开销大，需要大量批渲染环境，难以在有限算力下高效训练视觉运动控制策略。

Method: 通过轨迹滚动的随机扰动来估计策略梯度，大幅减少批渲染环境的数量，降低计算和内存开销。

Result: 在视觉MuJoCo基准上，SDPG在训练时间、内存使用和奖励方面持续优于基线方法；在灵巧操作、具有挑战性的运动任务以及真实物理硬件上的仿真到现实迁移中均表现出色。

Conclusion: SDPG是一种高效、轻量级的视觉强化学习方法，能够在有限计算资源下快速训练多样化视觉运动控制策略，并支持有效的仿真到现实迁移。

Abstract: We present the stochastic decoupled policy gradient (SDPG), a lightweight visual reinforcement learning (RL) method that trains diverse visuomotor control policies end-to-end within a few hours on a single NVIDIA RTX 4080 GPU. SDPG estimates policy gradients via random perturbations of trajectory rollouts, requiring orders of magnitude fewer batch-rendered environments and substantially reducing compute and memory overhead. On visual MuJoCo benchmarks, SDPG consistently outperforms baseline methods in training time, memory usage, and rewards. Finally, to support future research, we introduce a suite of realistic visual robotics benchmarks spanning dexterous manipulation, challenging locomotion, and demonstrate effective sim-to-real transfer on physical hardware.

</details>


### [323] [Provably Safe Motion Planning Under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26625)
*Ibon Gracia,Qi Heng Ho,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在未知分布随机扰动下，结合Wasserstein模糊管学习与采样规划的概率完备安全运动规划算法，通过降维模糊管和基于bandit的高效有效性检查提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样规划方法在处理未知分布随机扰动下的概率约束时，存在保守性过高或可扩展性差的问题，亟需一种既能保障安全又具备概率完备性的高效算法。

Method: 1) 利用轨迹数据学习Wasserstein模糊管（包含状态分布序列的模糊集）；2) 降维策略：用多个低维模糊管替代单个高维模糊管降低保守性；3) 基于bandit的有效性检查器提升经验性能而不牺牲概率完备性。

Result: 在杂乱环境中，算法能在严格安全阈值下找到有效规划，并优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 结合数据驱动模糊集学习与采样规划是处理未知分布随机扰动下安全约束的有效框架，降维和bandit策略在保障概率完备的同时显著提升了实用性能。

Abstract: We present a provably safe sampling-based motion planning algorithm for robotic systems affected by random disturbances of unknown distribution. We consider systems with linear or linearizable dynamics evolving in workspace with arbitrary-shaped obstacles subject to state and control constraints. Safety requirements are formulated as chance-constraints. Our approach leverages data from trajectories of the system to learn a Wasserstein ambiguity tube, i.e., a sequence of ambiguity sets, which contains the trajectory of the system's state distribution with high confidence. This ambiguity tube is then used in a probabilistically complete algorithm to grow a sampling-based motion planning tree that respects the constraints of the problem. We show that learning several lower-dimensional ambiguity tubes instead of a single high-dimensional one effectively reduces the conservatism and boosts scalability. Additionally, we design an efficient bandit-based validity checker that remarkably increases the empirical performance of our approach without sacrificing probabilistic completeness. Case studies show our algorithm finds valid plans in cluttered environments under strict safety thresholds, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [324] [Enabling Extensible Embodied Capabilities with Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26637)
*Xueyang Zhou,Zijia Wang,Qianjiang Li,Yibo Hu,Guiyao Tie,Li Wan,Yidan Liu,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出能力外部化方法，通过标准化协议ETP将具身智能中的异构能力解耦为独立工具，构建了包含100+工具的EmbodiedToolBench，实验表明该方法在认知和感知任务上提升显著（平均增益31%-36%），但在执行类能力上提升有限，且工具调用时机、选择和方式仍是挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能方法将感知、推理、规划和控制统一在单一参数化策略中，但这些能力本质上是层次化和异质的，难以在单个模型中可靠学习和模块化。

Method: 提出能力外部化方法，将异构能力解耦为独立优化的工具；引入具身工具协议ETP（标准化工具注册、发现、调用和执行）；构建100+验证工具的EmbodiedToolBench进行评估。

Result: 在仿真和真实平台实验中，能力外部化在EB-ALFRED和EB-Navigation上分别带来31%和36%的平均提升；认知和感知类能力提升显著，执行类能力提升有限。

Conclusion: 工具增强能一致提升具身性能，但存在能力边界；何时、选择何种以及如何调用工具仍是所有模型的持久挑战，具身工具能力是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Most existing embodied intelligence methods formulate perception, reasoning, planning, and control within a unified parameterized policy. Yet these capabilities are inherently hierarchical and heterogeneous, making them difficult to reliably learn and modularize within a single model. We propose a capability externalization approach that decouples heterogeneous capabilities into independently optimized tools, dynamically invoked at inference time. To this end, we introduce Embodied Tool Protocol (ETP), a standardized protocol for embodied tool registration, discovery, invocation, and execution, and curate 100+ validated tools spanning perception, cognition, reasoning, and execution as the tool base. Building on this, we construct EmbodiedToolBench to evaluate both whether tool augmentation improves embodied performance and how well current models use tools across tool-necessity recognition, tool selection, tool execution, and tool-chain composition. Experiments across simulation and real-world platforms confirm that capability externalization consistently improves embodied performance (avg. gain 31% on EB-ALFRED and 36% on EB-Navigation), yet reveal a clear boundary: gains are substantial for cognition and perception but are limited for execution-type capabilities. Moreover, our analysis reveals that knowing when, which, and how to invoke tools remains a persistent challenge across all models, thereby highlighting embodied tool competence as a critical direction for future research.

</details>


### [325] [HyperSim: A Holistic Sim-To-Real Framework For Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26638)
*Junyi Dong,Haotian Luo,Ziwei Xu,Shengwei Bian,Heng Zhang,Sitong Mao,Jingyi Guo,Yang Xu,Wenhao Chen,Qiuyu Feng,Yao Mu,Ping Luo,Shunbo Zhou,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperSim框架，通过高保真环境合成、对抗轨迹生成和仿真-现实联合训练三大支柱系统性地弥合仿真到现实的领域鸿沟，在400次真实世界任务执行中，ACT和π₀策略分别达到80%和95%的sim-to-real成功率，在物理扰动下完成率提升35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展数据规模与多样性对泛化具身智能至关重要，但物理数据采集成本高昂，而仿真到现实的领域鸿沟严重阻碍了策略迁移。现有方法在视觉保真度、数据覆盖和域不变表示方面存在不足，亟需一个系统性框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: HyperSim框架包含三大核心模块：(1) 高保真环境合成——增强视觉逼真度；(2) 对抗轨迹生成——扩展数据覆盖范围；(3) 仿真-现实联合训练——强制学习域不变表示。三者协同作用，系统性地解决仿真到现实迁移中的领域差异问题。

Result: 在400次真实世界任务执行的大规模实证研究中，完整流程在ACT上达到80%的sim-to-real成功率，在π₀上达到95%。采用对抗轨迹训练的策略在物理扰动下实现35%更高的完成率，显著增强了对动态不确定性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HyperSim框架通过系统性地弥合sim-to-real鸿沟，有效提升了机器人操纵策略从仿真到真实世界的迁移成功率与鲁棒性，为具身智能的规模化数据生成和策略泛化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling data volume and diversity is critical for generalizing embodied intelligence. While synthetic data generation offers a scalable alternative to expensive physical data acquisition, transferring robotic manipulation policies from simulation to the real world (sim-to-real) remains a formidable challenge due to the domain gap. This paper presents HyperSim, a holistic framework spanning from synthetic data generation to policy training and seamless real-world deployment. To systematically bridge the sim-to-real gap, HyperSim is realized through three core pillars: high-fidelity environment synthesis, adversarial trajectory generation, and sim-and-real co-training. Collectively, these modules address domain discrepancies by enhancing visual fidelity, expanding data coverage, and enforcing domain-invariant representations. We rigorously validate HyperSim through a large-scale empirical study involving 400 real-world task executions across two representative manipulation models. Assessed across three fine-grained metrics, our complete pipeline achieves remarkable sim-to-real success rates of 80% and 95% with ACT and π_{0}, respectively. Furthermore, policies trained on our adversarial trajectories exhibit significantly enhanced robustness against dynamic uncertainties, achieving a 35% higher completion rate under physical perturbations.

</details>


### [326] [L-Learning : A Lyapunov-Based Approach Leveraging Lagrangian Mechanics for Efficient and Stable Robot Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26648)
*Quan Quan,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出L-Learning，一种将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学相结合的数据驱动控制框架，通过学习系统能量函数实现轨迹跟踪的稳定性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制方法在动态不确定环境中性能退化，而数据驱动方法虽适应性更强但样本复杂度高且缺乏严格的稳定性保证，亟需一种兼具性能优化与稳定性保障的框架。

Method: L-Learning将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学融合到数据驱动控制框架中，从数据中显式学习系统的能量函数，以此优化控制性能并内在地保证闭环稳定性。

Result: 该方法具有优越的控制精度、理论稳定性保证和高样本效率。

Conclusion: L-Learning为实际机器人应用提供了一种有前景的解决方案，有效弥补了传统控制与数据驱动方法之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This paper presents L-Learning, a novel data-driven control framework for robotics that integrates Lyapunov stability theory with Lagrangian mechanics to enhance trajectory tracking performance. While traditional control methods often suffer from performance degradation in dynamic and uncertain environments, data-driven approaches, while more adaptable, are frequently limited by high sample complexity and a lack of rigorous stability guarantees. L-Learning mitigates these challenges by explicitly learning the system's energy function from data, thereby optimizing performance while ensuring closed-loop stability intrinsically. Characterized by superior control accuracy, theoretical stability guarantees, and high sample efficiency, L-Learning represents a promising solution for practical robotic applications.

</details>


### [327] [On the Generalization Capabilities, Design Choices and Limitations of Keypoint Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26649)
*Thomas Lips,Marco Moletta,Michael C. Welle,Danica Kragic,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于视觉基础模型的关键点模仿学习（KIL）在机器人操作中的泛化能力，通过2000+次真实世界实验，KIL在5个任务上达到75%成功率，显著优于RGB基线（47%），与S2-diffusion（73%）相当，但未超越其他表示方法并继承了基础模型的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB模仿学习需要大量 demonstrations 才能泛化到未见物体或场景，因此需要研究中间表示来提升机器人操作的泛化能力。视觉基础模型能够通过一次提取关键点来提供这种表示，但如何将其最优地集成到模仿学习中以及何时优于其他表示仍不清楚。

Method: 结合先前关键点模仿学习（KIL）工作的多种方法，研究若干设计选择以提供实践指南。通过2000+次真实世界 rollout 评估KIL对未见物体和场景变化的泛化能力，并探索基础模型用于关键点提取的局限性，将KIL扩展到多实例任务。

Result: KIL在5个任务上总体成功率为75%，显著优于RGB基线（47%），与S2-diffusion（73%）表现相当。KIL是一种数据高效的机器人学习方法，但并未超越其他表示方法，且继承了用于关键点提取的基础模型的局限性。

Conclusion: KIL证实了其作为数据高效的机器人学习方法的有效性，但相对于其他表示方法没有性能优势，且其表现受限于所使用的基础模型。需要进一步探索KIL的局限性及改进方向。

Abstract: RGB-based imitation learning requires many demonstrations to generalize to unseen objects or scenes, motivating research into intermediate representations to improve generalization for robotic manipulation. Visual foundation models enable one-shot extraction of keypoints to provide such representation. However, it remains unclear how to integrate them into imitation learning optimally and when they outperform alternative representations. We combine approaches from previous works on keypoint imitation learning (KIL) and investigate several design choices to provide practical guidelines. Using over 2000 real-world rollouts, we also assess the generalization capabilities of KIL to unseen objects and scene variations. KIL achieves a 75% overall success rate across five tasks, significantly outperforming the RGB baseline (47%) and performing on par with S2-diffusion (73%). Finally, we explore the limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction and extend KIL to tasks with multiple object instances. Our results confirm KIL as a data-efficient approach for robot learning, though it does not outperform alternative representations and inherits limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction. All rollout videos, demonstrations, and results are available at https://kil-manipulation.github.io/.

</details>


### [328] [SteelDS: A High-Resolution Video Dataset of E40 Steel Scrap for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26682)
*Melanie Neubauer,Christian Rauch,Gerald Koinig,Alexia Tischberger-Aldrian,Roland Pomberger,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提供了一个包含24,297帧标注视频的高分辨率数据集，展示了传送带上E40级钢和铜碎片的场景，支持机器学习模型在材料分类、目标检测和实例分割方面的开发，用于评估工业废钢分选中的铜杂质自动识别算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业磁选后阶段通常需要人工干预来去除铜污染物，现有数据集缺乏用于训练自动化分选算法的高质量标注数据，因此需要构建一个模拟真实工业分选条件的基准数据集。

Method: 在受控实验室环境中采集传送带上的E40级钢和铜碎屑的高分辨率视频数据，包含24,297帧标注图像，涵盖5个子集，涉及396个钢和101个铜对象（按尺寸分类），并变化对象间距和密度以模拟真实条件，提供像素级分割掩码和材料类别作为标注。

Result: 成功构建了包含24,297个标注帧、396个钢对象和101个铜对象的数据集，提供像素级分割掩码和材料类别标注，覆盖不同尺寸、间距和密度的工业分选场景。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为评估从复杂异质钢碎流中识别铜杂质的自动分选算法的基准，支持材料分类、目标检测和实例分割等机器学习模型的开发与评估。

Abstract: This dataset provides high-resolution, annotated video sequences of shredded E40-grade steel and copper scrap on a conveyor belt. Captured in a controlled laboratory environment, the data reflects the industrial post-magnetic sorting stage, where manual intervention is typically required to remove copper contaminants. The dataset comprises 24,297 labeled frames across five subsets, featuring 396 steel and 101 copper objects categorized by size. It supports the development of machine learning models for material classification, object detection, and instance segmentation. Variations in object spacing and density are included to simulate realistic industrial sorting conditions. Ground truth annotations include pixel-wise segmentation masks and material classes. This dataset serves as a benchmark for evaluating automated sorting algorithms aiming to identify copper impurities within complex, heterogeneous steel scrap streams.

</details>


### [329] [Look Further: Socially-Compliant Navigation System in Residential Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26710)
*Akira Shiba,Marina Obata,Nathan Kau,Zoltan Beck,Rishi Shah,Michael Sudano,Sabrina Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动变道（PLC）导航模式，将机器人的反应距离延长至8米以上，远超传统社交导航的交互距离。通过在直走廊场景中提前从中心移动到侧边，显著提升了人类对机器人运动的安全性、流畅性和礼貌性的感知；但在路口盲角场景中，各类运动模式的效果无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社交导航方法主要关注近距离（几米内）的人机交互冲突化解，如避免机器人侵入个人空间。然而，作者认为通过将反应距离扩展到远超出典型交互距离的8米以上，能够改善人类对机器人运动的感知，但目前缺乏对此类远距离主动避让模式的研究。

Method: 提出Proactive Lane-Changing（PLC）运动模式，使机器人在距迎面行人8米时即从走廊中心横向移动至一侧。构建了基于该模式的导航系统，并通过42名参与者的用户研究，在直走廊正面接近和路口盲角两种场景下，对比PLC与减速、停车、近距避碰等传统模式的效果。

Result: 在直走廊正面接近场景中，PLC在安全性、流畅性和礼貌性三个维度上均显著优于传统运动模式。但在路口盲角场景中，各类运动模式之间无显著差异，参与者偏好呈现多样化。

Conclusion: 延长机器人的反应距离至8米以上的主动变道策略，在直线走廊场景中能有效提升人机交互体验，但在盲角等可见性受限的场景中效果有限，需要结合其他策略或考虑用户个性化偏好。

Abstract: The distance at which a mobile robot reacts to a person strongly impacts various qualities of the human-robot interaction. In this paper, we focus on the navigation of a mobile delivery robot platform in a residential indoor hallway environment. Social navigation methods typically focus on avoiding uncomfortable human-robot interactions, such as when a robot encroaches on someone's personal space. Since personal space has been shown to be in the range of just a few meters, social navigation methods typically focus on deconflicting and resolving these short-range interactions. In this work, however, we demonstrate that by extending the reaction distance to over eight meters, far beyond the typical interaction distance, we can improve the human's perception of the robot's motion. We introduce the Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC) motion pattern and a navigation system that leverages it to react to people at an increased distance. This pattern consists of changing the robot's lateral position as it navigates down the hallway from the center to the side at an eight-meter distance from an oncoming person.
  We conducted a user study with 42 participants to assess their impressions of the delivery robot based on three service objectives: safety, smoothness, and politeness. In the straight hallway scenario (Frontal Approach), results showed significant improvement in each of these three objectives compared to typical motion patterns found in the literature: slowing down, stopping, and reactive collision avoidance in the proximity of a person. In contrast, in the intersection (Blind Corner) scenarios, none of the approaches performed significantly better than any other, with participants having a diverse range of preferences among robot motion patterns.

</details>


### [330] [Manipulating Tangible Virtual Object Dynamics to Promote Learning of Precision Force Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26782)
*Alberto Garzás-Villar,Alba Riera-Cardona,Alexis Derumigny,J. Micah Prendergast,Jane Murray Cramm,Laura Marchal-Crespo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出利用触觉虚拟物体的非线性动力学（AS-高斯弹簧模型）训练健康受试者的精确力控制能力，发现AS-高斯组在训练中力精度显著优于线性组，但长期保持效果无显著差异；受试者主要依赖学习的伸长量而非目标力，且人格特质（Free Spirit、Transform-of-Challenge）影响任务表现与探索行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉设备结合虚拟现实为训练精细力生成提供了新机遇，而精细力控制是中风后康复中被忽视但关键的组成部分。本研究旨在探索通过操控虚拟物体的渲染动力学来训练精确力控制，同时激活体感系统。

Method: 50名健康受试者执行冰壶模拟任务（拉伸虚拟弹簧至目标力释放），弹簧力-伸长关系在训练阶段被建模为线性、高斯或反对称高斯（AS-高斯）函数，其中AS-高斯和高斯函数在目标释放力处导数为零。通过力精度、人格特质（Free Spirit、Transform-of-Challenge）及转移任务（不同刚度、相同目标力）评估训练效果。
