Result: AS-高斯组在训练中始终比线性组力精度更高；高斯组仅在训练后期优于线性组。Free Spirit得分高者在高斯动力学下表现更差且探索行为减少；Transform-of-Challenge得分高者探索行为增加。长期保持无显著差异。转移任务表明受试者主要依赖学习的伸长量而非目标力。

Conclusion: 非线性动力学（特别是AS-高斯函数）有助于训练精细力控制，对体感神经康复具有潜力，但需进一步改进以减少对本体感觉线索的依赖，并在神经系统患者中验证。

Abstract: Robotic haptic devices combined with virtual reality offer novel opportunities to train fine force generation, an essential yet overlooked component of post-stroke rehabilitation. This study proposes that manipulating the rendered dynamics of tangible virtual objects can be leveraged to train precise force control while engaging the somatosensory system. We conducted an experiment with fifty healthy participants who performed a curling-inspired task in which they had to stretch a virtual spring to generate a target release force to propel the stone to a predefined location on the ice sheet. During training, the spring's force-elongation relationship was modeled as either a linear or non-linear function, i.e., a Gaussian or antisymmetric Gaussian (AS-Gaussian) function with zero derivative at the release target force. Results indicate that the AS-Gaussian group consistently achieved higher force accuracy during training than the linear group, while the Gaussian group only outperformed the linear group toward the end of training. Analysis of personality traits revealed that higher Free Spirit scores were associated with poorer performance and reduced task exploration under Gaussian dynamics, whereas higher Transform-of-Challenge scores correlated with increased exploration. Despite these training effects, no significant differences in long-term retention were found across spring types or personality traits. Participants primarily relied on learned target elongation rather than target force, as evidenced by performance in a transfer task with a different stiffness but the same target force. While promising for somatosensory neurorehabilitation, these methods require refinement to reduce reliance on proprioceptive cues before testing with neurological patients.

</details>


### [331] [Can VLA Models Learn from Real-World Data Continually without Forgetting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26820)
*Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,Xiaoquan Sun,Zetian Xu,Mingqi Yuan,Zhiyong Wang,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次在真实世界场景下研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的持续学习问题，构建了包含四种连续操作任务的数据集，发现VLA模型在异构真实世界演示中遭受严重灾难性遗忘，并系统评估了经验回放策略及其关键实现因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人领域前景广阔，但实际部署需要持续获取新技能的同时保留已学行为。此前研究仅在受限模拟环境中探索VLA的持续学习，缺乏真实条件下的研究。

Method: 构建了一个包含四个连续操作任务（刚体抓取放置、接触式按压、可变形物体折叠）的真实世界持续学习数据集，基于此进行综合实验，并系统评估经验回放策略的关键实现因素。

Result: VLA模型在持续学习异构真实世界演示时遭受严重的灾难性遗忘；经验回放策略的有效性受多种实现因素影响。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了真实世界持续VLA学习的首次实证研究，为部署长寿命机器人策略提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a promising foundation for general-purpose robotics. However, their successful deployment in real-world scenarios requires the ability to continually acquire new skills while retaining previously learned behaviors. While pioneering research has studied the continual learning of VLA models in narrowly simulated environments, this challenge remains largely unexplored under realistic conditions. To address this limitation, we construct a real-world continual learning dataset comprising four sequential manipulation tasks, spanning rigid-object pick-and-place, contact-rich pressing, and deformable-object folding. Using this dataset, we conduct comprehensive experiments and find that VLA models suffer significant catastrophic forgetting when continually learning from heterogeneous real-world demonstrations. We then systematically evaluate experience replay and uncover key implementation factors that govern its success. In summary, this work provides the first empirical study of real-world continual VLA learning and offers practical guidance for deploying long-lived robot policies.

</details>


### [332] [Learning Compositional Symbolic Task Rules from Demonstrations with Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26828)
*Oleh Borys,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将归纳逻辑编程（ILP）与任务分解相结合，从演示中学习可解释、可复用的符号化任务结构，并在合成积木组装任务中验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LfD方法只关注任务执行方式，忽略了高层任务结构。为使机器人更自主，需要学习可检查、可复用、人类可解释的任务表示。

Method: 采用归纳逻辑编程（ILP），将复杂任务分解为不同抽象（本体）层次上的多个简单学习目标，从演示和先验知识中推断符号规则，并在学习更高层任务结构时复用已学规则。

Result: 在合成积木组装场景中，学习到的抽象规则具有可解释性，并能泛化到包含未见物体的更难保留任务上。

Conclusion: 分解式ILP是实现任务级LfD的一种可行方法，其学得的符号化表示兼具可解释性与强泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration~(LfD) should capture not only how a task is executed, but also its high-level task structure that explains the demonstrated behavior. As robots become more autonomous, such task representations must be inspectable, reusable, and human-interpretable. To address this, we study how to represent and learn robotic tasks with inductive logic programming~(ILP) by decomposing a complex task into a series of simpler learning objectives at different abstraction (ontological) levels. The system infers symbolic rules from demonstrations and prior (domain) knowledge, and reuses learned rules when learning higher-level task structure. We evaluate the approach in a synthetic block-assembly scenario and show that the learned abstractions are interpretable and support strong generalization to harder, held-out tasks with unseen objects. These results provide preliminary evidence that decomposed ILP is a feasible approach to task-level LfD.

</details>


### [333] [A Bioinspired Underwater Robot with a Latch-Mediated Soft Bistable Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26936)
*Chongze Bi,Wenjie Wu,Zonghao Zuo,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受生物启发的软体双稳态驱动器，通过集成闩锁机制实现非对称能量输入与释放，并结合鳍结构实现了微型水下机器人的高效推进与机动控制，实验展示了稳定的周期性拍动、精准转向以及多种运动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电源能量密度低限制了微型水下机器人的发展，而自然界中螳螂虾、跳蚤等生物利用闩锁-弹簧驱动系统实现了快速运动，但如何在简单紧凑的结构中复现这种快速非对称驱动仍具挑战。

Method: 设计了一种受生物启发的软体双稳态驱动器，集成闩锁机构，通过单个电机实现非对称能量输入与释放，并与鳍结构耦合实现水下推进与机动。

Result: 实现了稳定的周期性拍动、精确转向，最大推力0.528 N、冲量0.147 Ns、垂直位移30 mm，通过调节鳍角可实现垂直上升、斜向前进和侧向平移等多种运动。

Conclusion: 本研究为紧凑型水下机器人提供了一种新颖、节能的运动控制方法，为探索、环境监测和检测等应用领域的先进仿生设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Underwater robotics has advanced significantly over recent decades. however, the development of miniaturized underwater robots remains limited by low energy densities of traditional power sources. Nature offers compelling solutions-organisms like mantis shrimps and fleas utilize latch-mediated spring actuation (LaMSA) systems that achieve rapid movements through a decoupled energy storage and release mechanism. Despite extensive studies of LaMSA, replicating such rapid, asymmetric actuation within simple, compact structures remains challenging. In this work, we introduce a bioinspired, soft bistable actuator with an integrated latch mechanism that enables asymmetric energy input and release using a single motor. Coupled with fin structures, this design facilitates efficient underwater propulsion and maneuverability. Experimental results demonstrate stable periodic flapping, precise steering, and a maximum thrust of 0.528 N, impulse of 0.147 Ns, and vertical displacement of 30 mm. By modulating fin angles, the robot achieves versatile motions, including vertical ascent, diagonal forward movement, and lateral translation. This study presents a novel, energy-efficient approach for controlling motion in compact underwater robots, paving the way for advanced biomimetic designs with potential applications in exploration, environmental monitoring, and inspection.

</details>


### [334] [Object Pose and Shape Estimation for Grasping: Does it Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26944)
*Pavan Karke,Kushal Shah,Gaurav Singh,Md Faizal Karim,K Madhava Krishna,Rajat Talak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了模块化方法（先估计物体姿态和形状，再通过反平行抓取采样生成抓取）与端到端抓取合成方法的性能对比。实验表明，模块化方法在所有测试中均优于端到端方法，尤其对小物体效果显著，但其性能依赖于姿态和形状估计的准确性，且在杂乱场景中会有所退化。此外，模块化方法还可结合视觉语言模型实现语言条件抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究现有物体姿态和形状估计方法是否足够成熟，以至于当与反平行抓取采样结合时，能够超越端到端的抓取合成方法。

Method: 将一种最先进的端到端抓取合成方法与三种模块化方法（先进行物体姿态和形状估计，再通过反平行采样生成抓取）进行对比实验。模块化方法中采用了两种不同的姿态和形状估计方式：一种基于编码器-解码器模型，另一种基于扩散模型。

Result: 模块化方法在所有实验中均优于端到端方法，能合成大量抓取姿态，即使对小物体也有效（端到端方法在此失败）；但模块化方法的有效性依赖于姿态和形状估计的准确性，在杂乱场景中性能会部分退化。

Conclusion: 模块化方法在平行爪抓取任务中优于端到端方法，且可结合视觉语言模型实现语言条件抓取，性能与SOTA基线LERF-TOGO相当。

Abstract: The problem of object pose and shape estimation has seen key advancements lately. Encoder-decoder (e.g., SAM3D, LRM, CRISP) and diffusion-based models (e.g., InstantMesh, Zero123, SceneComplete) have shown category-agnostic shape encoding capacity and open-set generalizability. In this work, we ask the question: Are the object pose and shape estimation methods mature enough, such that when used with antipodal grasp sampling, can outperform the end-to-end grasp synthesis methods? We explore this question in detail by scoping our study to parallel jaw grippers, 7-DoF grasps, and single-view RGB(-D) image as input. We implement and compare a state-of-the-art, end-to-end grasp synthesis method and three modular methods, which first estimate the object pose and shape for all objects in the scene, and generate grasps using antipodal sampling. We observe that the modular methods outperform the end-to-end method in all our experiments. The modular methods are able to synthesize plenty of grasps, even for small objects, where the end-to-end methods fail. The effectiveness of the modular methods is contingent on the accuracy of the pose and shape estimation, and suffers partial degradation in cluttered scenes - a limitation of the existing pose and shape estimation methods. We also analyze the failure modes and run-times for the three modular methods, which use two different ways of object pose and shape estimation: one based on an encoder-decoder model, while another a diffusion model. Finally, we demonstrate that the single-view object pose and shape estimation methods can be augmented with vision-language models to yield language-conditioned grasps from just single-view RGB-D image as input. We notice comparable performance to the state-of-the-art LERF-TOGO baseline.

</details>


### [335] [Trust, Geometry, and Rules: A Credibility-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safe USV Navigation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26974)
*Yuhang Zhang,Shuqi Chai,Yukang Zhang,Liusha Yang,Mingchuan Zhang,Wei Wang,Qingjiang Shi,Quanbo Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合可信度感知学习、几何安全屏蔽和连续规则感知嵌入的框架，使USV在动态海事环境中实现安全且符合COLREGs的自主导航，有效应对感知系统不确定性校准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的USV导航方法在面对感知系统校不准的不确定性时表现不佳：状态估计误差导致不可靠的信念状态误导价值函数，离散交通规则导致学习目标的非连续性。

Method: 1) 可信度加权值学习(CW-VL)：利用滤波器协方差与经验误差统计的差异构建动态信任因子，调节评论员的异方差损失；2) 协方差膨胀速度障碍(CI-VO)：将位置估计不确定性映射为集合角裕度，形成保守的几何安全屏蔽；3) 风险感知COLREGs义务嵌入：将二元会遇义务松弛为连续规则感知信号，提供平滑的扇区过渡信息。

Result: 仿真会遇实验表明：方法在感知不一致情况下训练鲁棒性提升，碰撞避免和COLREGs合规性均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过不确定性感知机制有效缓解了感知误差对RL训练的负面影响，在保持安全性的同时实现了更好的规则遵从。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) that is safe and compliant with the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs) remains a formidable challenge in dynamic maritime environments, particularly when perception systems exhibit miscalibrated uncertainty. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based methods often falter because state-estimation errors induce unreliable belief states that mislead the value function, while discrete traffic rules introduce discontinuity in the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a framework integrating credibility-aware learning, geometric safety shielding, and continuous rule-aware embedding. First, Credibility-Weighted Value Learning (CW-VL) introduces a dynamic trust factor derived from the discrepancy between filter-estimated covariance and empirical error statistics to modulate the critic's heteroscedastic loss, preventing policy overfitting to noisy samples. Second, the Covariance-Inflated Velocity Obstacle (CI-VO) maps position-estimation uncertainty into set-wise angular margins, forming a conservative geometric shield that overrides hazardous exploratory actions. Third, Risk-Aware COLREGs Duty Embedding relaxes binary encounter duties into continuous rule-aware signals, providing smooth sector-transition information and suppressing oscillation from sparse rule rewards. Simulated encounter studies demonstrate improved training robustness against perceptual inconsistency and superior collision avoidance and COLREGs compliance over baselines.

</details>


### [336] [Towards Shared Embodied Intelligence in Humanoid Robots through Optimization Development and Testing of the Human Aware ergoCub Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26991)
*Carlotta Sartore,Mohamed Elobaid,Lorenzo Rapetti,Giulio Romualdi,Stefano Dafarra,Nicola A. Piga,Ines Sorrentino,Paolo Maria Vicecone,Silvio Traversaro,Ugo Pattacini,Luca Fiorio,Francesco Draicchio,Giovanna Tranfo,Lorenzo Natale,Marco Maggiali,Daniele Pucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合共享智能与具身认知的架构，通过优化人形机器人的硬件和控制以匹配人类工效学指标，并具体实现了ergoCub机器人，旨在实现人机安全有效的物理协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过内部表征他人行为实现协作（共享智能），同时身体和认知能力随环境优化（具身认知）。现有机器人设计缺乏统一融合这两种原则，难以实现安全有效的人机物理协作。

Method: 提出共享具身智能架构：1）基于人类工效学指标优化机器人硬件参数；2）将人体模型嵌入机器人物理智能中；3）将人机交互建模为硬件配置的函数。具体实现为人形机器人ergoCub。

Result: 开发了ergoCub人形机器人，其形态和控制均针对人类协作任务进行了优化，验证了所提架构的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为设计以人类工效学优先的人形机器人提供了系统性方法，在工业与辅助机器人领域具有应用价值。

Abstract: Collaboration is central to human behavior, enabling tasks beyond individual capability. This ability arises from coordinating actions through internal representations of others, a concept known as shared intelligence. Additionally, humans are characterized by physical bodies and cognitive abilities that are optimized in response to their environment, a phenomenon referred to as embodied cognition. Designing humanoid robots that collaborate safely and effectively with people requires unifying these principles. Here we propose an architecture that integrates shared intelligence and embodied cognition to enable robots to physically collaborate with humans, where robot hardware and control are optimized for human metrics, using representations of the human body and motion intelligence. The ultimate goal is to achieve a form of shared embodied intelligence. Specifically, our architecture optimizes robot hardware and physical intelligence parameters with respect to human ergonomic metrics. This is accomplished by modeling human-robot interaction as a function of hardware configurations and embedding human models into the robot's physical intelligence. As a concrete implementation, we present the humanoid robot ergoCub, whose morphology and control have been optimized for collaborative tasks with humans. Our approach provides a framework for designing humanoid robots that prioritize human ergonomics at both the hardware and physical intelligence levels, with applications in industrial and assistive robotics.

</details>


### [337] [TPS-Drive: Task-Guided Representation Purification for VLM-based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27038)
*Jiaxiang Li,Yumao Liu,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TPS-Drive提出了一种任务引导的表示纯化框架，通过智能体中心分词器（Agent-Centric Tokenizer）将有限码本容量从静态背景重分配到关键动态智能体，结合解耦推理流水线和三阶段渐进训练范式，在空间状态预测和碰撞率降低方面取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM规划方法中，文本对齐策略将连续空间状态扁平化为符号，导致空间幻觉；密集视觉方法保留空间拓扑但背景纹理冗余引发表示干扰。亟需一种能同时保留关键空间结构并消除冗余的表示策略。

Method: 1) 智能体中心分词器：利用冻结的3D检测头监督任务引导的向量量化机制，将码本容量从静态背景重分配到动态智能体；2) 解耦推理流水线：依次进行场景理解、未来预测和动作生成；3) 三阶段渐进训练：最后通过奖励驱动优化超越纯模仿学习。

Result: 在开环nuScenes评估中实现精确的智能体空间状态预测并降低碰撞率；在严格的闭环NAVSIMv1和NAVSIMv2基准上创下新的安全记录。

Conclusion: TPS-Drive通过任务引导的表示纯化有效解决了VLM在自动驾驶规划中的空间幻觉和表示干扰问题，证明了将有限表示容量聚焦于关键动态要素可显著提升空间推理与安全性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation for autonomous driving planning, yet bridging semantic reasoning and precise 3D spatial forecasting remains a critical challenge. Existing representation strategies generally follow two paths: text-aligned methods flatten continuous spatial states into symbols, which compromises geometric structure and induces "spatial hallucinations"; dense visual methods preserve spatial topology but overwhelm standard tokenizers with redundant background textures, leading to "representation interference". To address these limitations, we introduce TPS-Drive, a novel framework centered on Task-Guided Representation Purification that empowers VLMs to Think in Purified Space. At its core, an Agent-Centric Tokenizer utilizes a task-guided vector quantization mechanism supervised by a frozen 3D detection head, which explicitly reallocates limited codebook capacity from pervasive static backgrounds to critical dynamic agents and effectively isolates spatial redundancy. Leveraging this purified spatial vocabulary, TPS-Drive employs a decoupled reasoning pipeline that sequentially performs scene understanding, future forecasting, and action generation. The framework is optimized via a progressive three-stage training paradigm, culminating in reward-driven refinement that surpasses pure imitation learning. Extensive experiments validate our approach: TPS-Drive achieves accurate agent spatial state forecasting and reduces collision rates in open-loop nuScenes evaluations, while establishing new safety records on the rigorous closed-loop NAVSIMv1 and NAVSIMv2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [338] [Learning to Balance Motor Thermal Safety and Quadrupedal Locomotion Performance with Residual Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27046)
*Yuhang Wan,Weixian Lin,Letian Qian,Yiqi Zou,Weiwei Wu,Shengwei Wu,Chuanlin Zhao,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的两阶段训练框架，将四足机器人全身热模型整合到训练流程中，通过标称策略+残差策略实现电机热管理，在保证运动性能的同时防止电机过热，仿真和实物实验均验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动驱动机器人（尤其是足式机器人）的电机热管理常被忽视，而电机过热是限制长时间运动的关键因素，尤其是在负载条件下。

Method: 1) 将四足机器人全身热模型集成到强化学习管线中实时更新电机温度；2) 两阶段训练框架：先预训练标称策略作为基础 locomotion 策略，再训练残差策略根据热状态提供修正动作。

Result: 仿真中策略有效平衡电机热安全与运动性能；宇树A1实物实验中，3kg负载下机器人可在多种地形稳定运动超过13分钟，而标称策略约5分钟即过热。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段热管理框架能显著延长四足机器人在负载下的持续运动时间，实现电机热安全与运动性能的有效平衡。

Abstract: Motor thermal management is often overlooked in the context of electrically-actuated robots, particularly legged robots, but motor overheating is a key factor that limits long-duration locomotion especially under payload conditions. This paper integrates a whole-body thermal model of a quadruped robot into the reinforcement learning pipeline to update motor temperatures, and proposes a two-stage training framework for motor thermal management. In this framework, a nominal policy is first pre-trained as a locomotion baseline capable of traversing diverse terrains. A residual policy is then trained on top of the nominal policy to provide corrective actions based on the robot's thermal state, ensuring high performance under low-temperature conditions and preventing motor overheating under high-temperature conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed policy achieves an effective balance between motor thermal safety and locomotion performance. Real-world experiments on a Unitree A1 quadruped robot further validate the approach: under a 3 kg payload, the robot achieves stable locomotion across multiple terrains for over 13 minutes, while the nominal policy alone leads to motor overheating in about 5 minutes.

</details>


### [339] [VR-DAgger: Immersive VR for Dexterous Data Collection and Uncertainty-Guided On-Policy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27114)
*René Zurbrügg,Tifanny Portela,Arjun Bhardwaj,Aravind Elanjimattathil Vijayan,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出机器人操作中通过示教学习（Learning from Demonstrations）虽然有效，但收集特定任务的数据是主要瓶颈。在分布偏移下，小误差会累积导致性能下降，而专家时间常被浪费在冗余、低价值的纠正上，而非少数关键的失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人操作中示教学习的数据收集瓶颈问题，尤其是分布偏移导致的误差累积和专家纠正效率低下的问题。

Method: （原文未明确给出具体方法，仅描述了问题背景和挑战）可能提出一种更高效的数据利用或纠正策略，专注于关键失败案例而非冗余纠正。

Result: （原文未明确给出结果）预期能减少数据需求，提升专家纠正的针对性，缓解分布偏移下的性能退化。

Conclusion: （原文未明确给出结论）强调需要更高效的数据收集和纠正机制，以突破示教学习在机器人操作中的瓶颈。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations is effective for robotic manipulation, but collecting sufficient task-specific data remains a major bottleneck. Under distribution shift, small errors compound, performance degrades, and expert time is often spent on redundant, low-value corrections instead of the few critical failure cases.

</details>


### [340] [TCBiRRT: Rapid Motion Planning for Tightly Coupled Dual-arm Space Manipulator Using Task-space Random Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27167)
*Jiawei Zhang,Xinhao Miao,Jifeng Guo,Qinghua Li,Chengchao Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务空间约束的双向快速随机扩展树算法（TCBiRRT），用于解决闭链约束下双机械臂运动规划难题。该方法直接在操作对象位姿定义的任务空间进行随机采样和节点扩展，并通过路径逆运动学映射到关节空间，结合双向RRT框架和重抓取机制，在复杂组装场景中实现了更高的成功率和数量级级别的规划时间提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭链约束显著降低了机械臂的可行构型空间，现有规划器在密集障碍物环境中难以高效生成无碰撞运动路径。

Method: 提出TCBiRRT算法：在任务空间（操作对象位姿）进行随机采样与节点扩展，通过路径逆运动学将候选对象运动映射为连续关节路径，集成双向RRT框架和重抓取机制实现两棵随机树的高效连接。

Result: 在多种复杂度级别的在轨组装场景仿真中，TCBiRRT相比现有最优规划器实现了显著更高的成功率和数量级级别的规划时间改进。

Conclusion: 所提方法为紧耦合双机械臂的运动规划提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Planning the motion path for a tightly coupled dual-arm space manipulator under closed-chain constraints is a fundamental yet challenging problem in on-orbit assembly of large-scale space structures. The closed-chain constraints significantly reduce the feasible configuration space, making it difficult for existing planners to efficiently generate collision-free motions, especially in cluttered environments. To address this issue, this paper proposes a task-space constrained bidirectional rapidly-exploring random tree algorithm, termed TCBiRRT. Unlike conventional methods that operate in the high-dimensional configuration space, the proposed approach performs random sampling and node expansion directly in the task space defined by the manipulated object pose. A task-space node expansion strategy is developed to generate candidate object motions, which are then mapped to continuous joint paths using a path inverse kinematics algorithm. The method is further integrated with a bidirectional RRT framework and a regrasp mechanism to efficiently connect two random trees. Extensive simulations are conducted in representative on-orbit assembly scenarios with varying levels of environmental complexity. The results demonstrate that TCBiRRT achieves significantly higher success rates and orders-of-magnitude improvements in planning time compared to state-of-the-art planners. The proposed method provides an efficient and robust solution for motion planning of tightly coupled dual-arm space manipulators.

</details>


### [341] [Towards Drone-based Mapping of Volcanic Gases using Gas Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27180)
*Marius Schaab,Niklas Karbach,Antonia Rabe,Thomas Wiedemann,Patrick Hinsen,Dmitriy Shutin,Thorsten Hoffmann,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的无人机气体断层成像方法，通过结合拉格朗日模型补偿风致平流，解决了无人机旋翼下洗气流对火山气体检测的干扰问题。在萨利内尔·代·卡普奇尼泥火山现场验证中，开放路径遥感成功实现了气体分布测绘，而传统机载原位传感器因气动干扰失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火山排放大量CO2影响人类生活，无人机监测可降低风险，但旋翼下洗气流会驱散气体羽流，导致原位传感器检测失效。现有技术难以在无人机扰动下准确测绘火山气体分布。

Method: 采用开放路径遥感代替原位传感器，并提出基于模型的气体断层成像方法，利用拉格朗日模型补偿风引起的平流效应，从遥感数据中重建气体分布图。

Result: 在萨利内尔·代·卡普奇尼泥火山现场，无人机原位传感器因气动扰动未能检测到CO2排放，而开放路径遥感成功实现了远程气体分布测绘。重建的气体分布图与手动采集的原位测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 基于模型的气体断层成像有效克服了无人机下洗气流的局限，能够实现火山排放的准确测绘，为火山监测提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Volcanoes emit large amounts of CO2, directly influencing human lives. Mapping volcanic gas emissions helps to forecast eruptions and understand the impact of volcanoes on climate and the environment. Drone-based gas sensing significantly reduces risks in volcanic monitoring but faces technical limitations when measuring gas, as rotor downwash disperses the gas plume before detection. Gas Tomography using remote gas sensing addresses this challenge. At the Salinelle dei Cappuccini mud volcanoes, we demonstrate that while drone-mounted in-situ sensors failed to detect CO2 emissions due to aerodynamic disturbance, open-path sensing successfully enabled remote gas distribution mapping. We present a novel model-based gas tomographic reconstruction approach that incorporates a Lagrangian model to compensate for wind-induced advection. The resulting gas distribution maps align with manually collected in-situ measurements, confirming that model-based gas tomography effectively overcomes downwash limitations and enables accurate mapping of volcanic emissions.

</details>


### [342] [FineVLA: Fine-Grained Instruction Alignment for Steerable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27284)
*Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Jinyu Zhang,Yutong Yao,Yuchong Sun,Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Yixuan Chen,Yingming Zheng,Shuai Bai,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FineVLA框架，通过细粒度动作级语言监督（如手臂选择、接近方向、接触区域等）增强VLA模型的任务执行能力。框架包含数据构建工具（整合10个开源数据集，构建47,159条细粒度轨迹）、基准测试（500视频，10,816原子事实，1,030 VQA问题）、专用VLM标注器和可控策略训练。实验表明：细粒度监督不牺牲目标级成功率（提升+1.4~+8.1%），与原始指令互补（最佳混合比FG:Raw=1:2~1:1），并在实际操控中显著提升可控性（姿态+23，颜色+18，接近方向+18）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集通常仅配以粗粒度的目标级语言描述，缺乏如执行手臂、接近方向、接触区域等关键执行细节，限制了策略的可控学习与机器人视频理解。

Method: (1) 数据构建工具，统一972,247条轨迹并构建经人工验证的FineVLA-Data (47,159条细粒度轨迹)；(2) 保留基准测试(500视频, 10,816原子事实, 1,030 VQA问题)；(3) 专用机器人VLM标注器；(4) 可控VLA策略，混合细粒度与原始目标级指令进行训练。

Result: 细粒度监督在多个设置下目标级成功率提升+1.4~+8.1%；混合指令呈倒U型趋势，最佳FG:Raw=1:2~1:1；混合设置达86.8%/82.5% (仿真)和62.7/100 (真实双臂操控)；真实场景中姿态(+23)、颜色(+18)、接近方向(+18)提升最大。

Conclusion: 细粒度语言应作为目标级指令的补充，同时指定如何执行(how)与达成什么(what)，可显著提升VLA模型的可控性与任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly expected to not only complete robot tasks, but also follow human instructions about how those tasks should be executed. However, existing robot datasets usually pair trajectories with coarse goal-level language, leaving execution-critical details such as active arm, approach direction, and contact region unspecified. This limits steerable policy learning and robotic video understanding. We introduce FineVLA, an open framework for action-aligned fine-grained VLA supervision. The framework includes: (1) a data construction tool that unifies 972,247 trajectories across 85K tasks from 10 open-source robot datasets and builds FineVLA-Data, a human-verified dataset of 47,159 fine-grained trajectories; (2) a held-out benchmark with 500 videos, 10,816 atomic facts, and 1,030 VQA questions; (3) a robotics-specialized VLM annotator for scalable fine-grained annotation; and (4) a steerable VLA policy trained with controlled mixtures of fine-grained and raw goal-level instructions. Our experiments yield three findings. First, fine-grained supervision does not sacrifice goal-level success: FG-only improves over Raw-only by +1.4 to +8.1 success-rate points across settings. Second, fine-grained and raw instructions are complementary, following a consistent inverted-U trend peaking at FG:Raw = 1:2 to 1:1. The best mixed setting reaches 86.8%/82.5% in RoboTwin simulation and 62.7/100 in real-world dual-arm manipulation (vs. 49.9 Raw-only). Third, fine-grained supervision improves steerable control: the largest real-world gains appear on pose (+23), color (+18), and approach direction (+18)--factors where goal-level instructions provide no guidance. Overall, fine-grained language should augment goal-level instructions: specifying how to execute alongside what to achieve. Project page: https://finevla.xlang.ai/

</details>


### [343] [Riding the Shifting Potential: When Reactive Control Suffices for Multi-Goal Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27314)
*Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图结构世界模型的零空间投影控制方法，通过动态优先级解决多目标任务中的冲突问题，在非凸障碍物导航和非凸物体平面推操作中取得100%成功率，远超基线方法，并可直接迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为反应式控制因目标冲突导致局部极小值，不足以处理多目标任务。作者认为这一限制并非固有缺陷，而是源于静态编码未能反映目标之间的动态交互关系。

Method: 基于图结构世界模型提取目标之间的交互结构，引入零空间投影机制：将低优先级目标的梯度投影到高优先级目标梯度的零空间中，优先级根据当前状态连续确定，从而在冲突发生处就地解决冲突。

Result: 在非凸障碍物导航（传统势场法根本失败）和非凸物体平面推操作中，方法在100个配置上实现100%成功率，而最速下降基线为0%，扩散策略为约55%。无需示范数据或重新训练，可直接迁移到真实机器人，额外感知和运动学约束通过相同机制处理。

Conclusion: 反应式控制通过动态优先级和零空间投影可以有效解决多目标冲突问题，打破了传统反应式控制局限性的认知。

Abstract: Reactive control is often considered insufficient for multi-objective tasks because conflicting objectives give rise to local minima. We argue this limitation is not inherent but arises from static encodings that fail to reflect how objectives currently interact. We exploit the interaction structure encoded in a graph-based world model by extending it with nullspace projections: conflicts are resolved where they arise by projecting lower-priority gradients into the nullspace of higher-priority ones, with priorities determined continuously from the current state. We demonstrate this in two domains where conflicts between objectives are central: navigation around non-convex obstacles, where static potential fields fundamentally fail, and planar pushing of non-convex objects, where our method achieves $100\%$ success across one-hundred configurations versus $0\%$ for the steepest-descent baseline and ${\sim}55\%$ for diffusion policy, without demonstrations or retraining. The same formulation transfers directly to a real robot with additional perceptual and kinematic constraints, accommodating them through the same mechanism.

</details>


### [344] [LAD-VF: LLM-Automatic Differentiation Enables Fine-Tuning-Free Robot Planning from Formal Methods Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18384)
*Yunhao Yang,Junyuan Hong,Gabriel Jacob Perin,Zhiwen Fan,Li Yin,Zhangyang Wang,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAD-VF是一种免微调框架，通过形式化验证反馈和自动提示工程，将LLM规划的安全合规成功率从60%提升至90%以上，实现了可扩展、可解释的正式验证控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在物理世界规划中常因幻觉或对齐不足违反安全约束；传统数据驱动对齐方法（如DPO）需要昂贵的人工标注，而形式化反馈方法仍依赖资源密集的微调。

Method: 提出LAD-VF框架：结合形式化验证信息文本损失与LLM-AutoDiff，通过迭代优化提示（而非模型参数）来提升合规性。

Result: 在机器人导航和操作任务中，成功从60%提升至超过90%。

Conclusion: LAD-VF提供了一条无需微调、可扩展且可解释的路径，通向可信赖的、经过形式化验证的LLM驱动控制系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can translate natural language instructions into executable action plans for robotics, autonomous driving, and other domains. Yet, deploying LLM-driven planning in the physical world demands strict adherence to safety and regulatory constraints, which current models often violate due to hallucination or weak alignment. Traditional data-driven alignment methods, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), require costly human labeling, while recent formal-feedback approaches still depend on resource-intensive fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose LAD-VF, a fine-tuning-free framework that leverages formal verification feedback for automated prompt engineering. By introducing a formal-verification-informed text loss integrated with LLM-AutoDiff, LAD-VF iteratively refines prompts rather than model parameters. This yields three key benefits: (i) scalable adaptation without fine-tuning; (ii) compatibility with modular LLM architectures; and (iii) interpretable refinement via auditable prompts. Experiments in robot navigation and manipulation tasks demonstrate that LAD-VF substantially enhances specification compliance, improving success rates from 60% to over 90%. Our method thus presents a scalable and interpretable pathway toward trustworthy, formally-verified LLM-driven control systems.

</details>


### [345] [AdaMorph: Unified Motion Retargeting via Embodiment-Aware Adaptive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07284)
*Haoyu Zhang,Shibo Jin,Lusong Li,Jun Li,Liang Lin,Xiaodong He,Zecui Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaMorph提出了一种统一的神经重定向框架，通过将人体运动映射到形态无关的隐式意图空间，并利用自适应层归一化(AdaLN)和课程学习策略，使单一模型能够将人体运动适应到多种不同的机器人形态上，在12种人形机器人上实现了跨异构拓扑的统一控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体运动重定向方法通常需要为每种机器人形态训练特定模型，扩展性差且无法利用共享的运动语义。解决不同机器人形态之间严重的运动学和动力学差异问题。

Method: 将重定向任务视为条件生成任务，将人体运动映射到形态无关的隐式意图空间；采用双用途提示机制；使用自适应层归一化(AdaLN)动态调制解码器特征空间；通过基于课程学习的训练目标强制执行物理合理性，确保方向与轨迹的一致性。

Result: 在12种不同的人形机器人上验证了有效性，能够统一控制跨异构拓扑结构的机器人，对未见过的复杂运动表现出强大的零样本泛化能力，同时保留源行为的动态本质。

Conclusion: AdaMorph通过单一模型实现了跨多种机器人形态的统一运动重定向，解决了现有方法扩展性差的问题，在泛化能力和运动保真度方面表现出色。

Abstract: Retargeting human motion to heterogeneous robots is a fundamental challenge in robotics, primarily due to the severe kinematic and dynamic discrepancies between varying embodiments. Existing solutions typically resort to training embodiment-specific models, which scales poorly and fails to exploit shared motion semantics. To address this, we present AdaMorph, a unified neural retargeting framework that enables a single model to adapt human motion to diverse robot morphologies. Our approach treats retargeting as a conditional generation task. We map human motion into a morphology-agnostic latent intent space and utilize a dual-purpose prompting mechanism to condition the generation. Instead of simple input concatenation, we leverage Adaptive Layer Normalization (AdaLN) to dynamically modulate the decoder's feature space based on embodiment constraints. Furthermore, we enforce physical plausibility through a curriculum-based training objective that ensures orientation and trajectory consistency via integration. Experimental results on 12 distinct humanoid robots demonstrate that AdaMorph effectively unifies control across heterogeneous topologies, exhibiting strong zero-shot generalization to unseen complex motions while preserving the dynamic essence of the source behaviors.

</details>


### [346] [Zero-Shot MARL Benchmark in the Cyber-Physical Mobility Lab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16578)
*Julius Beerwerth,Jianye Xu,Simon Schäfer,Fynn Belderink,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于CPM Lab的可复现基准平台，用于评估多智能体强化学习（MARL）策略在网联自动驾驶汽车（CAVs）中的仿真到实际迁移。该平台整合了仿真、高保真数字孪生和物理测试平台，支持零样本评估。通过部署SigmaRL训练的策略，揭示了性能下降的两个来源：仿真与硬件控制栈的架构差异，以及环境逼真度增加导致的仿真到实际差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏一个可复现的基准来系统评估MARL策略从仿真到实际环境的迁移效果，特别是对于CAV的运动规划策略。

Method: 基于CPM Lab构建了一个三层平台（仿真、数字孪生、物理测试），集成SigmaRL训练的策略，在所有三个域中进行零样本部署和评估。

Result: 发现了两种性能退化来源：仿真与硬件控制栈的架构差异，以及环境逼真度提升带来的仿真到实际差距。

Conclusion: 该开源平台能够在可复现的条件下系统分析MARL策略在仿真到实际迁移中的挑战，为未来研究提供了标准化评估工具。

Abstract: We present a reproducible benchmark for evaluating sim-to-real transfer of Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) policies for Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs). The platform, based on the Cyber-Physical Mobility Lab (CPM Lab) [1], integrates simulation, a high-fidelity digital twin, and a physical testbed, enabling structured zero-shot evaluation of MARL motion-planning policies. We demonstrate its use by deploying a SigmaRL-trained policy [2] across all three domains, revealing two complementary sources of performance degradation: architectural differences between simulation and hardware control stacks, and the sim-to-real gap induced by increasing environmental realism. The open-source setup enables systematic analysis of sim-to-real challenges in MARL under realistic, reproducible conditions.

</details>


### [347] [Vector Fields for Path Following on Lie Groups with Application in Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21450)
*Felipe Bartelt,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Weijia Yao,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对可在SE(3)上独立控制位置和姿态的机器人系统，提出了一种基于引导向量场的路径跟随通用框架。该框架在连通矩阵李群上设计向量场，保证从几乎所有初始条件收敛到期望参数曲线并实现连续运动，且控制输入表示更贴近工程应用。在SE(3)上的特化算法适用于实时控制，并通过机械臂实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器人系统（如全向飞行器、水下机器人、机械臂末端执行器）需要在三维空间中独立控制位置和姿态（位姿），而在跟踪连续位姿序列时，路径跟随相比轨迹跟踪具有重要实际优势。现有方法在矩阵李群（尤其是SE(3)）上的路径跟随问题缺乏通用的向量场框架，且控制输入表示不够贴近工程应用。

Method: 在连通矩阵李群上设计通用引导向量场框架，保证从几乎所有初始条件收敛到期望参数曲线并实现沿路径的连续运动；随后将该框架特化到SE(3)，得到适合实时机器人控制的高效算法。

Result: 该框架确保收敛性，控制输入表示更"最小化"且贴近工程应用（如SE(3)上的物体 twists）；在机械臂跟踪复杂位姿路径的实验中验证了方法的有效性，并提供了开源实现。

Conclusion: 提出的向量场框架为矩阵李群上的路径跟随问题提供了通用且有效的解决方案，在SE(3)上的特化算法兼具理论保证与实时实用性，对机器人位姿控制具有重要价值。

Abstract: Many robotic systems allow independent control of position and orientation (pose), including omnidirectional aerial vehicles, underwater robots, and manipulator end-effectors. In many applications, these systems must follow a continuous sequence of poses, leading to either trajectory-tracking or path following formulations. Compared to trajectory-tracking, path following offers important practical advantages. In particular, we focus on the problem of path following on Lie groups. Considering the robots as rigid bodies moving in the 3D space, this path-following problem can be posed as a problem of designing guiding vector fields on the matrix Lie group SE(3). In this paper, we develop a general vector-field framework for path following on connected matrix Lie groups, of which SE(3) is a prominent special case. The proposed vector field guarantees convergence to a desired parametric curve from almost all initial conditions while ensuring continuous motion along the path. Furthermore, another interesting feature is that, as opposed to previous works, the control input is "minimal" in terms of representation and closer to the engineering application (e.g., the body twist in the case SE(3)). After establishing the general case, the framework is then specialized to SE(3), of special interest in robotics, yielding an efficient algorithm suitable for real-time robotic control. Experiments with a robotic manipulator tracking complex pose paths demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach. An open-source implementation is also provided.

</details>


### [348] [RoboMME: Benchmarking and Understanding Memory for Robotic Generalist Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04639)
*Yinpei Dai,Hongze Fu,Jayjun Lee,Yuejiang Liu,Haoran Zhang,Jianing Yang,Chelsea Finn,Nima Fazeli,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RoboMME，一个用于评估和推进长程、历史依赖型机器人操作中视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型记忆机制的大规模标准化基准，包含16个任务和14种记忆增强VLA变体，实验表明记忆表示的有效性高度依赖于具体任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期VLA模型虽开始整合记忆机制，但评估局限于狭窄、非标准化的场景，缺乏系统性理解和比较。为此，作者提出RoboMME基准以统一评估标准。

Method: 基于精细设计的分类体系构建16个操作任务，评估时间、空间、物体和程序四种记忆类型；在π0.5骨干上开发14种记忆增强VLA变体，探索不同记忆表示和集成策略。

Result: 记忆表示的有效性高度任务依赖，每种设计在不同任务上各有优劣。

Conclusion: RoboMME为长程历史依赖型机器人操作中的记忆机制提供了标准化评估框架，有助于系统性理解和比较不同记忆增强VLA方法。

Abstract: Memory is critical for long-horizon and history-dependent robotic manipulation. Such tasks often involve counting repeated actions or manipulating objects that become temporarily occluded. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models have begun to incorporate memory mechanisms; however, their evaluations remain confined to narrow, non-standardized settings. This limits systematic understanding, comparison, and progress measurement. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboMME: a large-scale standardized benchmark for evaluating and advancing VLA models in long-horizon, history-dependent scenarios. Our benchmark comprises 16 manipulation tasks constructed under a carefully designed taxonomy that evaluates temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. We further develop a suite of 14 memory-augmented VLA variants built on the π0.5 backbone to systematically explore different memory representations across multiple integration strategies. Experimental results show that the effectiveness of memory representations is highly task-dependent, with each design offering distinct advantages and limitations across different tasks. Videos and code can be found at our website https://robomme.github.io.

</details>


### [349] [SOLE-R1: Video-Language Reasoning as the Sole Reward for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28730)
*Philip Schroeder,Thomas Weng,Karl Schmeckpeper,Eric Rosen,Stephen Hart,Ondrej Biza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SOLE-R1，一个专为在线强化学习设计的视频语言推理模型，通过时空链式推理生成密集任务进度奖励，在零样本条件下成功学习24个未见过的机器人操作任务，显著优于现有视觉语言奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最强的视觉语言模型在部分可观测性和分布偏移条件下，作为强化学习奖励函数时常失败，导致策略利用感知错误而非解决任务。急需一个能直接作为在线RL唯一奖励信号的模型。

Method: 构建大规模视频轨迹与推理合成流水线，生成时间对齐的链式推理轨迹和连续进度监督信号。采用混合训练框架，结合监督微调与可验证奖励的强化学习，融合基础空间推理和多帧时序推理能力。

Result: 在四个仿真环境和真实机器人场景中，SOLE-R1从随机初始化实现零样本在线RL，在24个未见任务上显著优于Robometer、RoboReward、ReWiND、GPT-5和Gemini-3-Pro等强基线模型，且对奖励破解具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SOLE-R1证明了仅基于原始视频观测和自然语言目标，通过专属的视频语言推理模型即可作为在线RL的唯一奖励信号，有效克服了部分可观测性和分布偏移问题，为机器人学习提供了一种无需真实奖励、任务调优或示范的通用方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, motivating efforts to leverage these models to supervise robot learning. However, when used as evaluators in reinforcement learning (RL), today's strongest models often fail under partial observability and distribution shift, enabling policies to exploit perceptual errors rather than solve the task. We introduce SOLE-R1 (Self-Observing LEarner), a video-language reasoning model explicitly designed to serve as the sole reward signal for online RL. Given only raw video observations and a natural-language goal, SOLE-R1 performs per-timestep spatiotemporal chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and produces dense estimates of task progress that can be used directly as rewards. To train SOLE-R1, we develop a large-scale video trajectory and reasoning synthesis pipeline that generates temporally grounded CoT traces aligned with continuous progress supervision. This data is combined with foundational spatial and multi-frame temporal reasoning, and used to train the model with a hybrid framework that couples supervised fine-tuning with RL from verifiable rewards. Across four different simulation environments and a real-robot setting, SOLE-R1 enables zero-shot online RL from random initialization: robots learn previously unseen manipulation tasks without ground-truth rewards, success indicators, demonstrations, or task-specific tuning. SOLE-R1 succeeds on 24 unseen tasks and substantially outperforms strong vision-language rewarders, including Robometer, RoboReward, ReWiND, GPT-5, and Gemini-3-Pro, while exhibiting markedly greater robustness to reward hacking. We release all models, data, code, and demos at the anonymous page: https://philip-mit.github.io/sole-r1/

</details>


### [350] [Governed Capability Evolution: Lifecycle-Time Compatibility Checking and Rollback for AI-Component-Based Systems, with Embodied Agents as Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08059)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对AI组件版本化演化中的全生命周期治理问题，提出了一种七阶段分级升级框架，引入接口、策略、行为、恢复四类兼容性检查，在PyBullet+ROS 2环境中验证：治理升级保持67.4%任务成功率且零不安全激活，而朴素升级虽达72.9%但最终不安全激活率达60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署模式（金丝雀发布、蓝绿部署、特征标记、MLOps流水线）主要针对无状态Web服务设计，无法有效处理状态化、受策略约束的AI组件运行时环境，缺乏对版本演化全生命周期的治理能力。

Method: 提出四类升级兼容性检查（接口、策略、行为、恢复）与七阶段流水线（候选验证→沙箱评估→影子部署→门控激活→在线监控→回滚→审计），将每个新版本视为受治理的部署候选而非直接可执行替换。

Result: 朴素升级任务成功率72.9%但第6轮不安全激活率60%；治理升级维持67.4%成功率且零不安全激活（Wilcoxon p=0.003）。影子部署可揭示40%沙箱评估无法发现的回归缺陷，回滚在79.8%的激活后漂移场景中成功。

Conclusion: 所提治理框架在不显著牺牲任务成功率的前提下有效消除不安全激活风险，验证了将AI组件版本演化作为一等软件生命周期问题的必要性。

Abstract: Software systems built from versioned AI components increasingly need lifecycle-time governance: when a capability module evolves into a new version, the hosting system must decide whether the new version may be activated safely, under what deployment conditions it should run, how it must be monitored, and when it should be rolled back. Existing software-deployment patterns (canary release, blue-green, feature flags, and MLOps pipelines) address parts of this loop but were designed for stateless web services rather than for stateful, policy-constrained runtimes that drive AI components in the field. We formulate governed capability evolution as a first-class software-lifecycle problem for AI-component-based systems and propose a staged upgrade framework in which every new capability version is treated as a governed deployment candidate rather than an immediately executable replacement. The framework introduces four upgrade compatibility checks (interface, policy, behavioral, recovery) and organizes them into a seven-stage pipeline (candidate validation, sandbox evaluation, shadow deployment, gated activation, online monitoring, rollback, audit). We implement a reference prototype on a PyBullet manipulation testbed with ROS 2 middleware and evaluate it over 6 rounds of capability upgrade with 15 random seeds. Naive upgrade achieves 72.9% task success but drives unsafe activation to 60% by the final round; governed upgrade retains comparable success (67.4%) while maintaining zero unsafe activations across all rounds (Wilcoxon p=0.003). Shadow deployment reveals 40% of upgrade regressions invisible to sandbox evaluation alone, and rollback succeeds in 79.8% of post-activation drift scenarios.

</details>


### [351] [Polymander II: an amphibious salamander-inspired robot with contact and flow sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24465)
*Qiyuan Fu,Sudong Lee,Andrea Grillo,Jonathan Arreguit,Louis Gevers,Josie Hughes,Auke J. Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于霍尔效应传感器的蝾螈仿生两栖机器人感知方案，可同时高速采集地面接触力、水动力等外部信息（>500Hz）与本体感知信息（100Hz），传感器体积小、灵敏度高且易于防水，实验验证了其两栖环境穿越能力及反馈控制潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两栖机器人缺乏同时感知陆地和水生环境交互的能力，需要一种能同时检测足部接触力和侧向水动力的传感方案。

Method: 采用霍尔效应传感器感知足部接触力和侧向水动力，通过两条总线同时获取外部信息（>500Hz）和本体信息（100Hz）；传感器体积紧凑可嵌入多个位置，且与被测对象分离放置便于防水封装。

Result: 实现了两栖环境下的穿越能力，展示了基于反馈控制的复杂运动控制潜力。

Conclusion: 霍尔效应传感器方案为两栖机器人提供了一种高效、灵敏、易防水的外感受感知手段，有助于提升机器人在复杂环境中的适应性和控制性能。

Abstract: Robots benefit from sensory information to coordinate body movement, gain robustness against perturbations, and transition between different modes to adapt to various terrains. However, few amphibious robots can sense interactions with both terrestrial and aquatic environments. In this paper, we present a solution that uses Hall-effect sensors to sense foot contact forces and lateral hydrodynamic forces on a salamander-inspired amphibious robot. With two bus lines, the robot can simultaneously acquire this exteroceptive information at more than 500 Hz and proprioceptive information, such as joint positions and loads, at 100 Hz. The Hall-effect sensors used are compact, making them suitable for embedding in multiple positions within a robot, and exhibit high sensitivity to small forces. Moreover, because the sensor can be positioned separately from the measured object, waterproofing can be implemented with relative ease. Our tests demonstrate the robot's capabilities in traversing amphibious environments and its potential in using feedback control for more complex locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [352] [ParkingWorld: End-to-End Autonomous Parking Reinforcement Learning from Corrective Experience in 3DGS Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25029)
*Zhengcheng Yu,Changze Li,Haoran Liu,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于校正循环的样本高效强化学习（CIL-SERL）框架，用于端到端自主泊车。该框架在基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）构建的高保真真实感泊车模拟器中训练，并设计了多级回放缓冲区机制，分层组织标准RL轨迹、人工校正干预、失败探索轨迹和回滚校正片段。在3DGS模拟环境和实车平台上的实验表明，该方法在泊车成功率、操作效率和安全性方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主泊车方法面临两大挑战：模仿学习需要大量高质量专家演示且泛化能力有限；传统强化学习方法存在训练开销大、探索效率低、在复杂场景中难以学到可行泊车策略等问题。迫切需要一种样本高效且鲁棒的泊车解决方案。

Method: 提出CIL-SERL框架：(1) 使用3D高斯泼溅构建真实感泊车模拟器进行全训练；(2) 设计多级回放缓冲区，将标准RL轨迹、人工校正干预、失败探索轨迹和回滚校正片段分层组织存储，实现结构化采样和针对性学习。

Result: 在3DGS模拟环境和实车平台上的实验表明，该方法在泊车成功率、操作效率和安全性方面相比基线方法均有实质性提升。

Conclusion: 本文验证了CIL-SERL框架在端到端自主泊车任务中的有效性和实际应用可行性，证明了结合人工校正循环与分层回放机制能够显著提升RL在复杂约束环境下的学习效率与策略性能。

Abstract: Autonomous parking demands precise low-speed maneuvering within narrow, cluttered, and highly constrained environments, where vehicles must navigate tight spaces while avoiding static obstacles and complex geometric boundaries. Unlike imitation learning, which typically requires massive volumes of high-quality expert demonstrations to converge to a stable policy and often suffers from limited generalization to unseen scenarios, traditional reinforcement learning (RL) methods face persistent challenges including excessive training overhead, inefficient exploration, and even failure to learn viable parking strategies in challenging settings. To address these limitations, this paper presents a correction-in-the-loop sample-efficient reinforcement learning (CIL-SERL) framework for end-to-end autonomous parking, which is entirely trained in a photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) parking simulator that enables high-fidelity digital reconstruction of real-world scenes. Inspired by error-correction notebooks used in learning practice, we design a novel multi-level replay buffer mechanism. These buffers hierarchically organize and store standard RL rollouts, human corrective interventions, failed exploration trajectories, and rollback-based correction segments in separate yet interconnected memory regions, facilitating structured sampling and targeted learning during training. The proposed framework is systematically evaluated in both the 3DGS simulation environment and a physical vehicle platform. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves substantial improvements in parking success rate, operational efficiency, and safety performance across diverse scenarios, validating the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed CIL-SERL-based end-to-end autonomous parking solution.

</details>


### [353] [ParkourFormer: Integrating Predictive Supervision and Sequence Modeling into Parkour Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25782)
*Yanheng Mai,Wenhao Xu,Zirui Huang,Yifei Fu,Shengwei Dong,Xinjue Wang,Kailun Huang,Yanzhe Xie,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ParkourFormer，一个基于Transformer的序列建模框架，通过显式预测未来本体感受状态来改进人形机器人的跑酷运动策略。在多样化地形上的仿真和真实机器人实验中，该方法相比MLP、MoE-MLP和Transformer基线取得了最高42.73%的提升，平均穿越成功率达93.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习策略大多是被动的反应式策略，直接将观测映射到动作，缺乏对未来身体状态的显式建模。在快速变化的复杂地形（如楼梯、间隙、斜坡）上，成功执行敏捷运动高度依赖于预测即将到来的接触转换和身体动力学，因此需要一种能够显式建模未来状态的运动策略。

Method: ParkourFormer采用Transformer架构进行序列建模。当前机器人状态通过交叉注意力机制查询历史传感器-运动轨迹；同时，一个轻量级预测头用于预测短时间范围内的未来本体感受状态；这些预测的未来状态通过监督信号进行训练，并与时序特征融合以生成动作，使策略能够同时推理运动历史和预期的未来动态。

Result: 在多地形人形机器人跑酷基准测试（包含楼梯、间隙、斜坡、粗糙地形和障碍物穿越）中，ParkourFormer在模拟和真实机器人实验中的平均穿越成功率达93.85%，相比MLP、MoE-MLP和vanilla Transformer基线方法最高提升42.73%，且使用单一统一策略覆盖所有地形类型。

Conclusion: 显式的未来状态建模能够显著提升敏捷全身运动控制的鲁棒性和泛化能力，验证了将人形机器人运动重新表述为未来条件决策问题的有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid parkour requires locomotion policies to coordinate whole-body dynamics across rapidly changing terrains such as stairs, gaps, slopes, and obstacles. Existing reinforcement learning policies are largely reactive, mapping observations directly to actions without explicitly modeling future body states. Such modeling becomes critical in agile locomotion tasks where successful motion execution depends strongly on anticipating upcoming contact transitions and body dynamics. We present ParkourFormer, a Transformer-based sequence modeling framework that reformulates humanoid locomotion as a future-conditioned decision-making problem. The current robot state queries historical sensorimotor trajectories through cross-attention, while a lightweight prediction head forecasts short-horizon future proprioceptive states. The predicted future states, trained with supervised signals, are fused with temporal features to generate actions, enabling the policy to jointly reason over motion history and anticipated future dynamics. We evaluate ParkourFormer on a diverse multi-terrain humanoid parkour benchmark including stairs, gaps, slopes, rough terrain, and obstacle traversal. Experiments in simulation and on a real humanoid robot show that ParkourFormer achieves a 93.85% average traversal success rate on highly challenging terrains, with improvements of up to 42.73% over strong MLP, MoE-based MLP, and vanilla Transformer baselines, while maintaining a single unified policy across all terrain types. These results demonstrate that explicit future-state modeling significantly improves robustness and generalization for agile whole-body locomotion.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [354] [Generative artificial intelligence and the marginalization of minoritized knowledges in higher education: the case of disability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26769)
*Fatiha Tali-Otmani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 生成式人工智能正在重塑高等教育的知识生产与验证方式，但其并非中立技术——以英语和西方为中心的训练数据集强化了认知殖民性。以残障群体为例，技术架构常将其简化为刻板印象或排除在设计之外，造成双重边缘化。文章探讨研究者与机器的杂交是否能在算法矫正仅作为 palliative 策略的结构限制下，维护认知多元性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示生成式人工智能在高等教育中并非中性工具，而是通过训练数据集的西方中心主义倾向，系统性地边缘化非霸权认识论；以残障群体的双重边缘化为典型案例，批判技术架构中的认知殖民性问题。

Method: 跨学科理论分析，融合教育科学、批判技术研究与残障研究，对训练数据集的语言偏向和西方中心主义进行批判性审视，并以残障群体作为典型案例进行阐释。

Result: 指出当前生成式AI技术架构强化了认知殖民性，残障群体被简化为刻板印象或排除在设计过程之外，面临技术与社会双重边缘化。

Conclusion: 提出研究者与机器的杂交可能作为维护认知多元性的路径，但同时指出算法矫正作为纯粹 palliative（姑息性）策略存在结构性局限。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence redefines higher education by restructuring the processes through which scientific knowledge is produced and validated. These systems are not neutral; they actively contribute to the marginalization of non-hegemonic epistemologies. This research draws upon educational sciences, critical technology studies, and disability studies to demonstrate that training datasets, which remain predominantly Anglophone and Western-centric, reinforce epistemic coloniality. The situation of persons with disabilities provides a particularly clear illustration of this phenomenon. Technological architectures frequently confine these individuals to reductive stereotypes or exclude them from the design process, leading to a double marginalization. This article examines whether a hybridization between the researcher and the machine might preserve epistemic plurality, while acknowledging the structural limitations inherent in algorithmic correction when used as a purely palliative strategy.

</details>


### [355] [Implementation of Big Data Analytics for Diabetes Management: Needs Assessment in the Rwanda Healthcare System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26786)
*Silas Majyambere,Tony Lindgren,Workneh Y. Ayele,Celestin Twizere*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究评估了卢旺达医疗系统采用大数据分析（BDA）管理糖尿病的准备情况，通过为期5天、涉及25位利益相关者的研讨会，识别了实施潜力与主要挑战，并提出了一个基于可解释机器学习模型的实用BDA框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病如不能及早诊断和管理会导致严重健康问题。大数据分析和机器学习为分析大规模健康数据、支持早期检测和优化治疗决策提供了实用工具，但其在常规临床实践中的应用仍然有限。本文旨在评估卢旺达医疗系统采用大数据分析管理糖尿病的准备情况。

Method: 开展为期5天的研讨会，邀请25位关键利益相关者（包括临床医生、数据管理员、政策制定者、医学研究人员、营养学家和技术提供商），评估BDA实施准备度并识别现存差距。

Result: 研究发现了BDA实施的潜力与主要挑战，并基于结果提出了一个实用的BDA框架。

Conclusion: 该框架利用可解释机器学习模型支持糖尿病管理策略，为卢旺达及其他类似发展中国家推进BDA在糖尿病管理中的应用提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Diabetes is a chronic metabolic disease that can lead to serious health problems if not diagnosed and managed early. Big Data Analytics (BDA) and machine learning offer practical tools for analyzing large health datasets and supporting early detection and better treatment decisions. However, their use in routine clinical practice is still limited. This study examines the readiness of Rwanda's healthcare system to adopt big data analytics for diabetes management. As the country continues to expand its use of electronic medical records and health information systems, new opportunities arise for improving prediction, monitoring, and clinical decision-making. A five-day workshop involving 25 key stakeholders, including clinicians, data managers, policymakers, medical researchers, nutritionists, and technology providers, was conducted to assess preparedness and identify existing gaps. The findings highlight both the potential and the main challenges of BDA implementation. Based on these results, the paper proposes a practical BDA framework to support diabetes management strategies using explainable machine learning models.

</details>


### [356] [How Students (Mis)understand Conditionals and Loops -- A Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26966)
*Dimitri Eckert,Christian Kautz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展分类法设计流程，构建了一个细粒度分类法，专门用于分类初学者在阅读和理解控制流结构（选择与迭代）时遇到的编程困难，旨在为计算教育研究者提供统一的错误分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解学生在编程中的困难是一个复杂挑战，因为涉及广泛的主题和多样的误解与错误。现有分类缺乏针对控制流结构阅读理解的细粒度系统框架。

Method: 基于先前研究及来自测验和访谈的实证数据，采用迭代式扩展分类法设计流程（ETDP）构建分类法。

Result: 建立了初学者在条件语句和循环方面常见误解的详细分析，清晰区分了不同困难类型。

Conclusion: 该分类法为计算教育研究者提供了统一的错误分类与分析框架，有助于深化理论洞察和指导教学策略；未来将应用于新学生数据并评估其在教育者和研究者中的可用性。

Abstract: Understanding student difficulties in programming is a complex challenge due to the wide range of topics and the abundant varieties of misconceptions and errors. This paper presents the design and development of a fine-grained taxonomy that categorizes novice programmers' difficulties specifically related to reading and understanding the control flow constructs selection and iteration. Building upon prior research and our own empirical data from quizzes and interviews with students, the taxonomy is constructed through the iterative methodology of the Extended Taxonomy Design Process (ETDP). Key contributions include clear distinctions between different student difficulties and a detailed analysis of common student misunderstandings concerning conditional statements and loops. The taxonomy aims to aid computing education researchers by providing a harmonized framework to classify and analyze student errors, fostering deeper theoretical insights and informing pedagogical strategies. Future work will involve applying the taxonomy to novel student data and evaluating its usability among educators and researchers.

</details>


### [357] [Building an Atlas of Social Experiments to Link Studies, Reconcile Conflicts, and Bridge Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27153)
*Jiawei Zhang,Honglin Bao,Pengda Wang,Alex Yan,Xiao Liu,James A. Evans*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: ExAtlas是一个将社会与行为科学实验档案转化为结构化地图的框架，通过分析目标研究与先前研究在治疗和结果空间上的局部邻近性，实现实验结果的链接、冲突调和与缺口桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学每年进行成千上万实验，但研究结果很少积累成连贯的知识地图，缺乏对已知、冲突和缺失内容的系统性理解。

Method: ExAtlas针对目标研究，在治疗和结果空间中搜索局部邻近的先前研究，通过组成效应预测目标结果。根据组成是否成功以及与观察结果是否一致，分为三种情况：链接一致证据、调和冲突（提出候选调节变量或高阶理论）、或提出桥接实验填补缺口。方法基于治疗-效应曲面的局部平滑性给出了误差界。

Result: 在经认证具有局部支持的留出目标上，ExAtlas在98.6%的情况下正确恢复了效应方向。人类评估显示其提出的桥接实验具有合理性和连通性，冲突解释对理论生成有用。

Conclusion: 社会实验档案中包含比当前实践所能提取的更多的潜在结构，将这种结构显式化可以指导未来的理论和实验设计。

Abstract: Social and behavioral science runs thousands of experiments each year, yet their findings rarely accumulate into a coherent map of what is known, what conflicts, and what remains missing. We introduce ExAtlas, a framework for turning an archive of experiments into an atlas: a structured map in which studies link, conflict, or leave bridgeable gaps. Given a target study, ExAtlas searches for prior studies that are locally close in treatment and outcome space and asks whether their observed effects can be composed to predict the target effect. This yields three cases. If the composition succeeds and agrees with the observed result, ExAtlas links the target to consistent prior evidence. If composition succeeds but disagrees, ExAtlas reconciles the conflict and proposes candidate moderators or higher-level theories that could explain it. If composition fails, ExAtlas proposes bridge experiments to close the gap. We provide an error bound for composition under local smoothness of the treatment-effect surface. On held-out targets certified as locally supported, ExAtlas recovers effect direction in 98.6% of cases. Human evaluations further suggest that its proposed bridge experiments are plausible and exhibit connectedness, and that its conflict explanations are useful for theory generation. These results suggest that the archive of social experiments contains more latent structure than current practice extracts -- and that making this structure explicit can guide both future theory and future experimentation.

</details>


### [358] [Faults and Pitfalls in Implementing the Right to be Forgotten](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27171)
*Chen Sun,Nikolas Guggenberger,Supreeth Shastri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法来解决法律与计算在"被遗忘权"实施上的矛盾，该方法可避免80%的GDPR第6年发生的RTBF违规，并识别了6个反模式，最后将RTBF能力引入Elasticsearch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管被遗忘权（RTBF）是历史悠久且重要的法定数据权利，但在实际计算实施中面临巨大挑战。GDPR前五年平均每9天发生一次RTBF违规，亟需弥合法律与计算之间的鸿沟。

Method: 首先从计算视角识别支持RTBF时的不确定性和风险，然后提出一种两阶段方法，搭建法律与计算之间内在二分法的桥梁。

Result: 该方法可完全避免GDPR第6年中80%的RTBF违规；发现6项已成为RTBF反模式的长期计算与数据管理实践；成功将RTBF能力引入Elasticsearch。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段方法有效桥接了法律与计算的矛盾，大幅降低了RTBF实施风险，并揭示了现有系统实践中阻碍RTBF的关键反模式。

Abstract: Right to be Forgotten (RTBF) in one of the oldest and prominent of the legal data rights. While its legal intention is straight forward (for example, the GDPR describes it in just 417 words), the computing community has found it challenging to implement this in practice. For example, regulators have issued 205 RTBF violations in the first five years of GDPR i.e., an RTBF failure once every 9 days, on average. In this work, we identify the uncertainties and risks in supporting RTBF from a computing perspective. Then, to mitigate these challenges, we propose a two-phase approach that bridges an intrinsic dichotomy between law and computing. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our technique by showing how it could have fully avoided 80% of RTBF violations that occurred in the year-6 of GDPR. We also discover six long-standing practices of computing and data management that have become anti-patterns for RTBF. Finally, to ground our research, we introduce RTBF capability into Elasticsearch, a popular open-source search engine.

</details>


### [359] [Queue & AI: When Faster Tasks Slow Down the Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27202)
*Silvia Bartolucci,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出，用平均任务完成时间衡量生成式AI的生产力效果具有误导性。AI虽能快速生成初稿，但若错误未被及时发现而进入下游返工，会导致系统级性能下降。作者将这种均值与系统延迟之间的偏差称为“方差楔子”，并通过排队模型证明：在高负载下，审核者会理性地提高检查阈值从而降低审查质量；AI只有在满足更严格的条件时才能稳定过载工作流。因此，AI部署应评估其对拥塞、返工和人工监督稳健性的整体影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用平均指标（如每工单完成量、平均处理时间）衡量AI的生产力提升，但这些均值指标可能掩盖AI在工作流中因错误返工和注意力竞争导致的系统级性能恶化。作者旨在揭示这种“方差楔子”现象及其对工作流拥堵的影响。

Method: 通过排队论（queueing model）对AI辅助工作流进行理论建模，分析AI助手下任务处理在“完全AI辅助”和“完全人工”两个工作流处理体制之间的相变条件，并推导出AI辅助稳定拥堵工作流所需满足的临界阈值条件。

Result: （1）在拥堵情况下，审核者会理性地提高检查AI输出的风险阈值，从而减少审查力度，恰恰在最需要审查时降低审查质量；（2）AI辅助稳定拥堵工作流需要满足两个条件：AI处理任务比例超过临界阈值，且人工用于审查和预期返工的注意力成本低于人工完成任务的注意力成本——这远比“生成初稿更快”这一条件严格。

Conclusion: 评估AI部署不能只看平均任务速度，而应综合考虑其对工作流拥堵、返工成本以及高负载下人工监督稳健性的整体影响。

Abstract: Quantifying the workplace productivity effects of Generative Artificial Intelligence is now central to economics, management, and public policy. The deployment of AI tools in customer service, writing, software development, and consulting operations has been reported to generate large per-task productivity gains, typically measured as tasks completed per worker-hour or reductions in mean handle time. We argue that such mean-based metrics can misrepresent AI's effects in workflows where tasks accumulate and compete for scarce human attention. AI assistance can generate a deceptive productivity signature: average completion times fall because AI tools typically supply a fast first draft, yet workflow-level performance deteriorates when a subset of AI errors escapes review and returns as costly downstream rework. We call this divergence between mean task speed and system-level delay the variance wedge. Depending on the operational parameters, the most time-efficient way to complete a workflow may undergo a transition between two task-processing regimes, a fully AI-assisted and a fully manual one. We formalize the mechanism as a queueing model and derive two main implications analytically. First, under congestion, reviewers rationally raise the risk threshold for checking AI outputs, reducing scrutiny precisely when it would matter the most. Second, AI assistance can stabilize an overloaded workflow only when (i) the fraction of tasks handled by AI exceeds a critical threshold, and (ii) the human attention required for review and expected rework is lower than the attention for manual completion, a requirement substantially more stringent than faster draft generation. These results suggest that AI deployment should be evaluated not only by average task speed, but by its overall effects on congestion, rework, and the robustness of human oversight under load.

</details>


### [360] [Authorship Attribution in the Era of LLMs: Problems, Methodologies, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08946)
*Baixiang Huang,Canyu Chen,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了LLM时代作者归属的四个核心问题：人类文本归属、LLM文本检测、LLM文本归属和人类-LLM合著文本归属，并讨论了泛化性和可解释性两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的快速发展模糊了人机写作边界，传统作者归属方法面临挑战，亟需系统梳理该领域研究现状以确保数字内容真实性和学术诚信。

Method: 通过文献综述方法，将相关研究归纳为四类代表性问题，并评估现有方法和基准的优缺点。

Result: 识别出四大核心问题领域及泛化性与可解释性两大关键挑战，指出了开放问题和未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为LLM时代作者归属研究提供了系统性路线图，为研究人员和从业者理解该领域最新进展提供了参考。

Abstract: Accurate attribution of authorship is crucial for maintaining the integrity of digital content, improving forensic investigations, and mitigating the risks of misinformation and plagiarism. Addressing the imperative need for proper authorship attribution is essential to uphold the credibility and accountability of authentic authorship. The rapid advancements of Large Language Models (LLMs) have blurred the lines between human and machine authorship, posing significant challenges for traditional methods. We present a comprehensive literature review that examines the latest research on authorship attribution in the era of LLMs. This survey systematically explores the landscape of this field by categorizing four representative problems: (1) Human-written Text Attribution; (2) LLM-generated Text Detection; (3) LLM-generated Text Attribution; and (4) Human-LLM Co-authored Text Attribution. We also discuss the challenges related to ensuring the generalization and explainability of authorship attribution methods. Generalization requires the ability to generalize across various domains, while explainability emphasizes providing transparent and understandable insights into the decisions made by these models. By evaluating the strengths and limitations of existing methods and benchmarks, we identify key open problems and future research directions in this field. This literature review serves as a roadmap for researchers and practitioners interested in understanding the state of the art in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [361] [Fixed Points and Stochastic Meritocracies: A Long-Term Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07478)
*Gaurab Pokharel,Diptangshu Sen,Sanmay Das,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过一个代际反馈模型研究功绩选拔制度（如大学录取）中的群体公平性问题，发现：完全对称条件下长期平均差距会消失但短期存在周期性波动；轻微不对称会导致永久性优势；项目稀缺性和有效性是关键调节因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨功绩选拔制度（如大学录取）产生的反馈循环中，即便初始条件对称，群体间的不公平现象如何产生、持续或消失，为算法公平性干预设计提供理论依据。

Method: 构建了一个新颖的代际反馈模型，通过数学分析刻画了完全对称、轻微不对称以及随机性条件下的群体差距演化动力学，并结合丰富模型进行仿真验证。

Result: 完全对称条件下长期平均差距消失但短期周期性波动；累积优势消散时间随项目相对重要性增加而延长；小群体中纯随机性可导致显著差距；轻微不对称导致随机出现并永久持续的差距；项目名额稀缺性和有效性调节差距动力学。

Conclusion: 功绩选拔制度的反馈循环中，即使初始条件完全对称，随机性和轻微不对称也能产生并固化群体间不公平；项目稀缺性和有效性是调节差距的关键因素，对算法公平性干预设计有重要启示。

Abstract: We study group fairness in the context of feedback loops induced by meritocratic selection into programs that themselves confer additional advantage, like college admissions. We introduce a stylized, yet novel inter-generational model for the setting and analyze it in situations where there are no underlying differences between two populations. When the benefit of the program (or the harm of not getting into it) is completely symmetric, we show that disparities between the two populations will vanish on average in the long term, although in the short term disparities will continue to arise and dissipate cyclically. Further, the time an accumulated advantage takes to dissipate can be significant, and increases as a function of the relative importance of the program in conveying benefits. Interestingly, significant disparities can arise purely due to randomness even from completely symmetric initial conditions, especially when populations are small. The introduction of even a slight asymmetry, where the group that has accumulated an advantage becomes slightly preferred, leads to a completely different outcome. In these instances, starting from completely symmetric initial conditions, disparities between groups arise stochastically and then persist over time, yielding a permanent advantage for one group. Our analysis precisely characterizes conditions under which disparities persist or diminish, with a particular focus on the role of the scarcity of available spots in the program and its effectiveness. We also present extensive simulations in a richer model that further support our theoretical results in the simpler, stylized model. Our findings are relevant for the design and implementation of algorithmic fairness interventions in similar selection processes.

</details>


### [362] [A Technical Policy Blueprint for Trustworthy Decentralized AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11878)
*Hasan Kassem,Orion Banks,Omar Benjelloun,Sergen Cansiz,Brandon Edwards,Patrick Foley,Inken Hagestedt,Taeho Jung,Peter Kairouz,Marco Lorenzi,Peter Mattson,Prakash Moorthy,Ann K Novakowski,Michael O'Connor,Bruno Rodrigues,Holger Roth,Micah Sheller,Dimitris Stripelis,Renato Umeton,Marc Vesin,Wenbin Zhang,Mic Bowman,Alexandros Karargyris*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出技术策略蓝图（Technical Policy Blueprint），通过策略即代码（policy-as-code）解耦策略验证与执行，实现去中心化AI系统中透明、可扩展且可验证的治理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化AI系统（如联邦学习）在隐私保护方面有巨大潜力，但现有治理依赖特定基础设施的定制策略，阻碍了资产互操作性和系统间信任，亟需透明、可扩展且可验证的治理方案。

Method: 将治理需求编码为策略即代码对象，分离策略验证与执行：策略引擎（Policy Engine）验证证据（身份、签名、支付、可信硬件证明等）并签发能力包（capability packages），资产守护者（Asset Guardians，如数据/模型/计算守护者）仅基于这些能力包执行访问或计算。

Result: 解耦策略处理与能力授权，使得治理可以独立演进而无需重新配置AI基础设施，实现了透明、可审计且抗变化的治理架构。

Conclusion: 该技术策略蓝图为去中心化AI资产市场提供了一种不依赖特定基础设施的通用治理方案，通过策略与执行的解耦提升了系统的互操作性、透明性和可演化性。

Abstract: Decentralized AI systems, such as federated learning, can play a critical role in further unlocking AI asset marketplaces (e.g., healthcare data marketplaces) thanks to increased asset privacy protection. Unlocking this big potential necessitates governance mechanisms that are transparent, scalable, and verifiable. However current governance approaches rely on bespoke, infrastructure-specific policies that hinder asset interoperability and trust among systems. We are proposing a Technical Policy Blueprint that encodes governance requirements as policy-as-code objects and separates asset policy verification from asset policy enforcement. In this architecture the Policy Engine verifies evidence (e.g., identities, signatures, payments, trusted-hardware attestations) and issues capability packages. Asset Guardians (e.g. data guardians, model guardians, computation guardians, etc.) enforce access or execution solely based on these capability packages. This core concept of decoupling policy processing from capabilities enables governance to evolve without reconfiguring AI infrastructure, thus creating an approach that is transparent, auditable, and resilient to change.

</details>


### [363] [Ethical Fairness without Demographics in Human-Centered AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13373)
*Shaily Roy,Harshit Sharma,Daniel A. Adler,Srijan Sen,Tanzeem Choudhury,Asif Salekin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出Flare，一种无需人口统计或异构属性的公平性框架，通过Fisher信息引导的潜在子群学习与无害正则化，在可穿戴和移动感知系统中提升伦理公平性，并引入BHE指标套件来超越统计奇偶性的伦理评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在普适医疗系统中，计算模型依赖可穿戴和生理传感数据推断人类状态，但仅追求高精度不够，模型需在不同人群、情境和设备间公平且符合伦理。然而，依赖人口统计或异构属性的公平方法难以实施，因这些属性不可用、隐私敏感或受监管；且传统的奇偶性公平可能牺牲子群性能，违反伦理原则。

Method: Flare（Fisher-guided LAtent-subgroup learning with do-no-harm REgularization）：利用Fisher信息（优化几何）正则化曲率，挖掘潜在性能差异；结合表征、损失和曲率信号识别隐藏的性能分层，通过协作但无害优化（do-no-harm）提升子群表现；提出BHE指标套件（Beneficence-Harm Avoidance-Equity）实现超越统计奇偶性的伦理公平评估。

Result: 在EDA、OhioT1DM、IHS和Percept-R等移动生理、行为和临床传感数据集上，Flare在伦理公平性上优于现有最先进基线。消融实验、可解释性和损失景观分析表明，性能提升源于更平坦的优化几何、更简单的决策规则以及无害的潜在子群适应。运行时分析支持其在资源受限传感部署中的实用性。

Conclusion: Flare作为不依赖人口统计/异构属性的公平框架，通过几何优化和潜在子群学习有效提升了普适传感系统的伦理公平性，且具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: In ubiquitous and mobile health systems, computational models infer human states from wearable, behavioral, and physiological sensing data. In these settings, high accuracy alone is insufficient; models must act ethically and equitably across diverse people, contexts, and devices. However, fairness methods that rely on demographic or heterogeneous attributes during training are difficult to enforce because such attributes are often unavailable, privacy-sensitive, regulated, or undesirable to collect. Conventional parity-based fairness can also violate ethical principles by trading off subgroup performance. To address this challenge, we present Flare, Fisher-guided LAtent-subgroup learning with do-no-harm REgularization, a demographic- and heterogeneous-attribute-agnostic framework that aligns human-centered fairness with ethical principles for ubiquitous and mobile sensing. Flare leverages optimization geometry, particularly Fisher Information, to regularize curvature and uncover latent disparities in model behavior without demographic or heterogeneous attributes. By integrating representation, loss, and curvature signals, it identifies hidden performance strata and refines them through collaborative but do-no-harm optimization, enhancing subgroup performance while preserving ethical balance. We also introduce BHE (Beneficence-Harm Avoidance-Equity), a metric suite that operationalizes ethical fairness beyond statistical parity. Across mobile physiological, behavioral, and clinical sensing datasets, including EDA, OhioT1DM, IHS, and Percept-R, Flare improves ethical fairness over state-of-the-art baselines. Ablation, interpretability, and loss-landscape analyses show that these gains arise from flatter optimization geometry, simpler decision rules, and do-no-harm latent-subgroup adaptation. Runtime analysis supports the practicality of Flare for resource-constrained sensing deployments.

</details>


### [364] [Auditing the Reliability of Multimodal Generative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00944)
*Erfan Samieyan Sahneh,Luca Maria Aiello*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对Gemini 2.5 Pro多模态搜索系统进行了大规模审计，分析了11,943个声称-视频对，发现3.7%至18.7%的视频引用声称未被来源支持，主要失败模式为不可验证的细节和夸大声称，而非直接矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型越来越多地作为生成式搜索系统使用，通过引用视频作为证据来回应查询，但这些引用是否真正支持生成的声称尚不清楚，现有研究缺乏对视频引用可信度的大规模系统评估。

Method: 使用三个独立的LLM裁判（87.7%交叉一致性，经人工标注验证）对Gemini 2.5 Pro在医疗、经济和通用三个领域生成的11,943个声称-视频对进行自动化验证，并通过逻辑回归进行事后探索性分析。

Result: 依据裁判严格程度不同，3.7%至18.7%的视频引证声称未获支持；主要失败模式为不可验证的具体细节和夸大声称；逻辑回归表明，偏离源词汇（β=-1.6至-3.1）和与视频转录语义相似度低（β=-2.1至-11.6）的声称更可能不被支持。

Conclusion: 视频生成式搜索存在可信度差距，系统注入来自参数知识的精确但无依据的细节并以视频作为引证，系统表现出的自信与其输出保真度之间存在鸿沟。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) increasingly function as generative search systems that retrieve and synthesize answers from multimedia content, including YouTube videos. Although these systems project authority by citing specific videos as evidence, the extent to which these citations genuinely substantiate the generated claims remains underexplored. We present a large-scale audit of the Gemini 2.5 Pro multimodal search system, analyzing 11,943 claim-video pairs generated across Medical, Economic, and General domains. Through automated verification using three independent LLM judges (87.7\% inter-rater agreement), validated against human annotations, we find that depending on the judge's strictness, between 3.7\% and 18.7\% of video-grounded claims are not supported by their cited sources. The dominant failure modes are not outright contradictions but rather unverifiable specificities and overstated claims, suggesting the system injects precise but ungrounded details from parametric knowledge while citing videos as evidence. Exploratory post-hoc analysis via logistic regression reveals properties associated with these failures: claims departing from source vocabulary ($β= -1.6$ to $-3.1$, $p < 0.01$) and claims with low semantic similarity to the video transcript ($β= -2.1$ to $-11.6$, $p < 0.01$) are significantly more likely to be unsupported. These findings characterize the current trustworthiness of video-based generative search and highlight the gap between the confidence these systems project and the fidelity of their outputs. The dataset is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/icwsm-gemini-audit-04DF .

</details>


### [365] [The ATOM Report: Measuring the Open Language Model Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07190)
*Nathan Lambert,Florian Brand*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文全面分析了截至2025年夏季开源大语言模型（如Qwen、DeepSeek、Llama等）的采用情况，发现中国模型在2025年夏季超越美国模型，并持续扩大领先优势，研究基于Hugging Face下载量、模型衍生品、推理市场份额和性能指标等多维度数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究人员、企业家和政策制定者提供开源大语言模型生态系统的全面采用快照，揭示中国模型与美国模型的竞争态势变化。

Method: 聚焦约1500个主线开源模型（来自Alibaba Qwen、DeepSeek、Meta Llama等），综合Hugging Face下载量、模型衍生品数量、推理市场份额和性能指标等多维度数据进行量化分析。

Result: 中国模型在2025年夏季在采用率上超越美国模型，并随后持续扩大差距。

Conclusion: 开源大语言模型生态的领先地位已从美国转移到中国，且差距呈扩大趋势。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive adoption snapshot of the leading open language models and who is building them, focusing on the ~1.5K mainline open models from the likes of Alibaba's Qwen, DeepSeek, Meta's Llama, that are the foundation of an ecosystem crucial to researchers, entrepreneurs, and policy advisors. We document a clear trend where Chinese models overtook their counterparts built in the U.S. in the summer of 2025 and subsequently widened the gap over their western counterparts. We study a mix of Hugging Face downloads and model derivatives, inference market share, performance metrics and more to make a comprehensive picture of the ecosystem.

</details>


### [366] [When VLMs 'Fix' Students: Identifying and Penalizing Over-Correction in the Evaluation of Multi-line Handwritten Math OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22774)
*Jin Seong,Wencke Liermann,Minho Kim,Jong-hun Shin,Soojong Lim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究多行手写数学公式的光学字符识别（OCR）问题，指出现有视觉语言模型（VLM）存在"过度纠正"的关键缺陷——即模型会"修正"学生手写中的错误，从而隐藏了教育评估本应检测的失误。为此，作者提出PINK（基于惩罚性INK的评分）语义评估指标，利用大语言模型进行基于评分标准的评判并显式惩罚过度纠正。在FERMAT数据集上对15个SOTA VLM的评估显示，与BLEU相比出现了显著的排名反转：GPT-4o因过度纠正受到严重惩罚，而Gemini 2.5 Flash成为最忠实的转录器。人类专家研究进一步表明，PINK与人类判断的一致性显著优于BLEU（55.0% vs 39.5%），为教育场景下的手写数学OCR提供了更可靠的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试未能正确评估手写数学公式转录能力：大多数先前研究聚焦于单行表达式，并依赖BLEU等词汇级指标，无法评估多行学生解答中的语义推理。教育AI系统需要对学生的真实书写进行准确转录，而非"修正"错误。

Method: 提出PINK（Penalized INK-based score）语义评估指标，利用LLM进行基于评分标准的评分，并显式惩罚VLM的过度纠正行为。在FERMAT数据集上对15个SOTA VLM进行全面评估，并与人类专家判断进行对比。

Result: 与BLEU相比出现显著的排名反转：GPT-4o因过度纠正被严重惩罚，Gemini 2.5 Flash成为最忠实的转录器。人类专家研究中，PINK与人类判断的一致性达55.0%，显著优于BLEU的39.5%。

Conclusion: PINK为教育场景下的手写数学OCR提供了更可靠的评估框架，能更好地反映真实转录质量，尤其适用于需要检测学生错误的教育评估系统。

Abstract: Accurate transcription of handwritten mathematics is crucial for educational AI systems, yet current benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability properly. Most prior studies focus on single-line expressions and rely on lexical metrics such as BLEU, which fail to assess the semantic reasoning across multi-line student solutions. In this paper, we present the first systematic study of multi-line handwritten math Optical Character Recognition (OCR), revealing a critical failure mode of Vision-Language Models (VLMs): over-correction. Instead of faithfully transcribing a student's work, these models often "fix" errors, thereby hiding the very mistakes an educational assessment aims to detect. To address this, we propose PINK (Penalized INK-based score), a semantic evaluation metric that leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) for rubric-based grading and explicitly penalizes over-correction. Our comprehensive evaluation of 15 state-of-the-art VLMs on the FERMAT dataset reveals substantial ranking reversals compared to BLEU: models like GPT-4o are heavily penalized for aggressive over-correction, whereas Gemini 2.5 Flash emerges as the most faithful transcriber.
