Furthermore, human expert studies show that PINK aligns significantly better with human judgment (55.0% preference over BLEU's 39.5%), providing a more reliable evaluation framework for handwritten math OCR in educational settings.

</details>


### [367] [Access Timing as Scaffolding: A Reinforcement Learning Approach to GenAI in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15850)
*Janne Rotter,Pau Benazet i Montobbio,Davinia Hernández-Leo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式AI（GenAI）的访问时机本身作为一种隐式脚手架，通过强化学习（RL）智能体决定学生何时可以访问GenAI（奖励函数基于元认知理论、认知负荷理论和生产性失败理论）。在105名高等教育学生的混合方法对照实验中，结果显示：与完全无限制和完全限制访问相比，策略性时机的GenAI访问（RL条件）显著提升了客观后测表现和元认知准确性，同时减少了任务错误和任务时间，且无需显式元认知提示或结构化脚手架；但在自我报告的元认知意识上无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中在如何教学法上脚手架化GenAI的使用，但关于何时允许学生使用现成GenAI的问题缺乏基于教学法的实证研究。本文旨在探索访问时机本身作为一种隐式脚手架的教学策略效果。

Method: 采用混合方法受控实验室研究，N=105名高等教育学生。通过强化学习（RL）智能体决定学生的GenAI访问时机，奖励函数基于元认知理论、认知负荷理论和生产性失败理论。比较RL条件与无限制访问和完全限制访问在客观后测表现、元认知准确性、任务错误和任务时间上的差异。

Result: RL条件下的策略性GenAI访问相比无限制访问显著提升了客观后测表现和元认知准确性；相比完全限制访问减少了任务错误和任务时间；在自我报告元认知意识上无显著条件间差异。

Conclusion: GenAI访问时机是一种可操作、有理论基础、可扩展的教学策略，优于完全无限制和完全限制访问，与现成工具兼容且采用门槛低。该研究开辟了探索教育者如何促进访问时机以及如何在人-AI学习系统设计中实现的新研究方向。

Abstract: In recent years, generative AI (GenAI) in educational settings has become ubiquitous in university students' daily lives, despite its potential to induce over-reliance, metacognitive disengagement, and diminished learning when used unrestrictedly. While most prior research has focused on how to pedagogically scaffold its usage, the question of when to allow off-the-shelf GenAI remains understudied and lacks pedagogically grounded empirical investigation. We treat access timing itself as a form of implicit scaffolding and operationalize it through a reinforcement learning (RL) agent that decides when students should access GenAI, with a reward function grounded in metacognitive theory, cognitive load theory, and productive failure. In a mixed-methods controlled lab study with N=105 higher education students, we compared the agent's effect on learning gains and metacognitive engagement to unrestricted and fully restricted use. Results show that strategically timed GenAI access under the reinforcement learning condition improved objective post-test performance and metacognitive accuracy compared with unrestricted access, while reducing task errors and time on task relative to complete withholding, thus outperforming both approaches without the need for explicit metacognitive prompts or structured scaffolding. However, no between-condition differences emerged on self-reported metacognitive awareness. Overall, timing of GenAI access therefore is a tractable, theoretically grounded, and scalable pedagogical strategy that improves over completely unrestricted and withheld access, compatible with off-the-shelf tools and potentially low adoption barrier. This opens up a new research area that explores how access timing can be facilitated by educators and implemented in human-AI learning system design.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [368] [MoDAl: Self-Supervised Neural Modality Discovery via Decorrelation for Speech Neuroprosthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00025)
*Yuanhao Chen,Peter Chin*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: MoDAl框架通过对比对齐与解相关损失联合优化，从多脑区（包括Broca区）发现互补神经模态，在Brain-to-Text Benchmark '24上将词错误率从26.3%降至21.6%，并验证了Broca区编码句法结构信息的神经语言学认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音神经假体系统主要从运动皮层解码，忽略了如Broca区（44区）等可能编码互补语言学信息的脑区，限制了解码性能。

Method: 提出MoDAl框架，通过对比损失将多个并行脑编码器与预训练LLM文本嵌入对齐，同时使用解相关损失防止编码器表示趋同，两者形成对抗性张力以发现多样化的神经语言学模态。

Result: 在Brain-to-Text Benchmark '24上，WER从26.3%降至21.6%；纳入44区信号的性能提升完全来自解相关机制；发现编码器功能分化：接收44区输入的编码器捕捉句子长度、语法语态、wh-词等结构句法属性。

Conclusion: 通过对比对齐与解相关损失的对抗性联合优化，MoDAl能够有效发现互补的多脑区神经语言学模态，提升解码性能，并验证了Broca区在句法结构编码中的神经语言学角色。

Abstract: Speech neuroprosthesis systems decode intended speech from neural activity in the absence of audible output, offering a path to restoring communication for individuals with speech-impairing conditions. Current approaches decode predominantly from motor cortical areas, discarding others -- such as area 44, part of Broca's area -- that may encode complementary linguistic information. We introduce MoDAl (Modality Decorrelation and Alignment), a framework that discovers complementary neural modalities through the interplay of two objectives in a shared projection space. A contrastive loss aligns each of several parallel brain encoders with the text embeddings of a pretrained large language model (LLM), while a decorrelation loss prevents the encoders from coalescing to duplicative representations. We prove that these objectives are in productive tension: Contrastive alignment induces transitive modality coalescence, which decorrelation must counteract for the framework to discover diverse neurolinguistic modalities. On the Brain-to-Text Benchmark '24, MoDAl reduces word error rate (WER) from 26.3% to 21.6% compared to the previous best end-to-end method, with the gain from incorporating previously discarded area 44 signals arising entirely from the decorrelation mechanism. Analysis of the discovered modalities reveals functional specialization: Encoders receiving area 44 input capture structural and syntactic properties (sentence length, grammatical voice, wh-words), consistent with the neurolinguistic understanding of Broca's area.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [369] [Mapping the gender attrition gap in academic psychology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13273)
*Xinyi Zhao,Anna I. Thoma,Ralph Hertwig,Dirk U. Wulff*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 利用78,216名心理学家的文献计量数据，研究发现女性在心理学领域虽占早期研究者多数，但退出研究的比例更高，性别差距在首次发表后约5年达到峰值；早期一作发表是保留的最强预测因素，且性别差异即使在控制多种职业决定因素后仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性在心理学领域占学生和早期研究者的大多数，但长期活跃于研究的比例较低，研究者希望明确女性在学术职业的哪个阶段不成比例地离开，以及是什么因素驱动了这一流失。

Method: 基于大规模文献计量数据，追踪2000-2014年间开始发表论文的78,216名心理学家，通过发表活动来操作化研究职业流失，并比较不同性别在完整职业轨迹中的差异。

Result: 女性占新入行者的60%以上，但流失率高于男性，性别差距在首次发表约5年后达到峰值；早期一作发表是后续保留的最强预测因子，而末位作者发表与后期持续活跃最相关；合作模式和机构背景对职业保留也有影响但较弱；控制这些因素后性别差异仍存在。

Conclusion: 心理学中的性别不平等更多源于差异性保留而非差异性招募；解决早期职业阶段的脆弱性对于实现该领域高级学术领导层的公平代表性至关重要。

Abstract: Women comprise the majority of students and early-career scholars in psychology, yet they are less likely to remain active in research over time. This pattern raises a central question: At what stages of academic careers do women disproportionately leave academia, and what factors drive their attrition? Using large-scale bibliometric data tracking 78,216 psychologists who began publishing between 2000 and 2014, we examine gender differences in research career attrition operationalized through publishing activity across the full trajectory from entry onward. Although women accounted for more than 60\% of new entrants, they experienced higher attrition rates than men, with the gender gap peaking approximately five years after first publication. Early-career performance, particularly first-authored publications, was the strongest predictor of subsequent retention, whereas last-authored publications were most closely associated with continued activity at later career stages. Collaboration patterns and institutional context also shaped career persistence, though less strongly than publication indicators. Notably, gender differences in research attrition persisted even after accounting for these career determinants, especially during early career stages. These findings suggest that gender inequality in psychology is driven less by recruitment than by differential retention over time. Addressing early-career vulnerability may therefore be essential to achieving equitable representation in senior academic leadership within the discipline.

</details>


### [370] [Rewarding Engagement and Personalization in Popularity-Based Rankings Amplifies Extremism and Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24354)
*Jacopo D'Ignazi,Emma Fraxanet Morales,Andreas Kaltenbrunner,Gaël Le Mens,Fabrizio Germano,Vicenç Gómez*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文识别并验证了一种机制，解释在线平台的排名算法如何基于用户的位置偏差、同质化偏好、极端用户更活跃以及算法偏好流行内容这四点假设，通过奖励活跃互动和个性化排名，不可避免地将用户推向更极端和两极分化的新闻消费。作者通过动态模型、仿真模拟和数百人参与的交互实验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究，但在线平台如何塑造极端主义和两极分化的具体机制仍不清晰。作者旨在识别并实证检验一种可解释该现象的机制。

Method: 基于四个经验支持的假设构建理论机制，将其形式化为动态模型，通过仿真模拟和数百名人类参与者的交互实验（排名根据用户活动动态更新）进行评估。

Result: 在平台奖励活跃互动并实施个性化排名的条件下，用户不可避免地走向更极端和两极分化的新闻消费。

Conclusion: 在线平台的排名算法通过特定机制会放大极端主义和极化现象，该机制具有坚实的经验基础。

Abstract: Despite extensive research, the mechanisms through which online platforms shape extremism and polarization remain poorly understood. We identify and test a mechanism, grounded in empirical evidence, that explains how ranking algorithms can amplify both phenomena. This mechanism is based on well-documented assumptions: (i) users exhibit position bias and tend to prefer items displayed higher in the ranking, (ii) users prefer like-minded content, (iii) users with more extreme views are more likely to engage actively, and (iv) ranking algorithms are popularity-based, assigning higher positions to items that attract more clicks. Under these conditions, when platforms additionally reward \emph{active} engagement and implement \emph{personalized} rankings, users are inevitably driven toward more extremist and polarized news consumption. We formalize this mechanism in a dynamical model, which we evaluate by means of simulations and interactive experiments with hundreds of human participants, where the rankings are updated dynamically in response to user activity.

</details>


### [371] [Grok in the Wild: Characterizing the Roles and Uses of Large Language Models on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11286)
*Katelyn Xiaoying Mei,Robert Wolfe,Nicholas Weber,Martin Saveski*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Grok在社交平台X上三个月的交互数据，发现Grok回应了62%的请求，51%为英语，半数回复在48小时内获得不超过20次浏览。研究归纳出LLM在社交互动中扮演的10种角色，其中信息提供者最常见，但相比私人一对一场景，Grok还承担了真理仲裁者、倡导者和对手等与争议管理相关的角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于大语言模型使用方式的研究聚焦于私人的一对一交互，而Grok在社交平台X上的部署代表了向公共社交空间的重大转变，因此需要系统研究这种新场景下的人机交互模式。

Method: 系统采样了三个月的交互数据，在平台层面分析使用模式，并通过归纳法构建了LLM在社交互动中扮演的10种角色分类体系，据此分析了41,735次Grok交互，同时刻画了提示Grok的用户群体特征。

Result: Grok回应了62%的请求，51%为英语，交互参与度低（半数回复48小时内浏览≤20次）。在角色分布上，信息提供者最常见，但出现了私人场景少见的争议管理角色（真理仲裁者、倡导者、对手）。用户的自述兴趣与模型承担的角色密切相关。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了对X平台上人类-AI交互的初始定量描述，以及对大语言模型在在线社交空间中可能扮演的多样化角色的更广泛理解。

Abstract: xAI's large language model, Grok, is called by millions of people each week on the social media platform X. Prior work characterizing how large language models are used has focused on private, one-on-one interactions. Grok's deployment on X represents a major departure from this setting, with interactions occurring in a public social space. In this paper, we systematically sample three months of interaction data to investigate how, when, and to what effect Grok is used on X. At the platform level, we find that Grok responds to 62% of requests, that the majority (51%) are in English, and that engagement is low, with half of Grok's responses receiving 20 or fewer views after 48 hours. We also inductively build a taxonomy of 10 roles that LLMs play in mediating social interactions and use these roles to analyze 41,735 interactions with Grok on X. We find that Grok most often serves as an information provider but, in contrast to LLM use in private one-on-one settings, also takes on roles related to dispute management, such as truth arbiter, advocate, and adversary. Finally, we characterize the population of X users who prompted Grok and find that their self-expressed interests are closely related to the roles the model assumes in the corresponding interactions. Our findings provide an initial quantitative description of human-AI interactions on X, and a broader understanding of the diverse roles that large language models might play in our online social spaces.

</details>


### [372] [CitePrism: Human-in-the-Loop AI for Citation Auditing and Editorial Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16000)
*Gowrika Mahesh,Budanur Madappa Darshan Gowda,Kavana Gopladevarahalli Papegowda,Prajwal Basavaraj,Binh Vu,Swati Chandna,Mehrdad Jalali*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: CitePrism是一个结合LLM推理、语义相似度、元数据验证和人工审核的混合决策支持框架，用于编辑部的引用审计。在单案例初步验证中，与人工标签的Cohen's kappa=0.429，在阈值tau=17时能识别所有无关引用但产生假阳性。目前仅为试点阶段，需广泛验证后方可投入实际使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前稿件级别的引用审计主要依赖人工、碎片化且难以规模化，编辑和审稿人难以高效确保文献引用的相关性、准确性、时效性和伦理适当性。

Method: CitePrism采用透明混合决策框架：LLM辅助上下文推理、基于嵌入的语义相似度计算、元数据验证、完整性标志检测以及人机协同的分析师审核。具体流程包括提取引用邻域、丰富引文元数据、计算融合相关性分数、生成元数据和自引审查提示，并支持可配置阈值的分类筛选。

Result: 在包含104条引用的路面工程案例手稿中，与人工二元相关性标签的Cohen's kappa=0.429。在阈值tau=17时，系统标记出所有人工标注的无关引用，但产生了需要分析师审核的假阳性。

Conclusion: CitePrism可能支持保守的编辑筛选和引用质量分类，但不具有普适的编辑性能。该系统属于试点阶段的决策支持工具，而非自主的不端行为检测器或自动编辑决策系统，需在更多手稿、领域、标注者、基线和部署环境中进行验证后方可投入实际使用。

Abstract: Editors and reviewers are expected to ensure that manuscripts cite relevant, accurate, current, and ethically appropriate literature, yet manuscript-level citation auditing remains largely manual, fragmented, and difficult to scale. Citation context, metadata quality, self-citation patterns, and bibliographic integrity all affect whether a reference appropriately supports a local claim. We present CitePrism, a transparent hybrid decision-support framework for editorial citation auditing that combines LLM-assisted contextual reasoning, embedding-based semantic similarity, metadata verification, integrity-oriented flags, and human-in-the-loop analyst review. CitePrism extracts citation neighborhoods, enriches reference metadata, computes fused relevance scores, surfaces metadata and self-citation review prompts, and supports configurable threshold-based triage. In a preliminary validation on a single case-study manuscript with 104 references from pavement engineering, agreement with human binary relevance labels reached Cohen's kappa = 0.429. At operating threshold tau = 17, CitePrism flagged all human-labeled irrelevant citations, while also producing false positives requiring analyst review. These results suggest that CitePrism may support conservative editorial screening and citation-quality triage, but they do not establish general editorial performance. CitePrism is intended as pilot-stage decision support, not as an autonomous misconduct detector or automated editorial decision system. Broader validation across manuscripts, domains, annotators, baselines, and deployment settings is required before operational use.

</details>
